### [1] [Tensor Probabilistic Model Checking of Finite-Horizon Markov Chains (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00374)
*Jianlin Li,Nick Guo,Peter Ye,Yizhou Zhang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出将马尔可夫链的步数有界可达概率验证重构为稠密张量计算，并利用现成编译器工具链和硬件加速器执行，从而改善传统显式或符号方法在稠密转移系统中的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率模型检测方法通常以显式矩阵或符号结构编码状态转移，在转移关系稀疏时较有效，但面对稠密状态转移矩阵时计算与存储效率较差。论文旨在利用张量计算生态及加速硬件的优化能力，解决该稠密场景下的性能瓶颈。

Method: 作者将步数有界可达概率的计算表达为稠密张量运算，并证明从概率模型检测问题到张量计算表示的映射是可靠的。随后实现了名为Tessa的工具，借助通用编译器工具链生成适合硬件加速器执行的优化计算。

Result: 实验表明，在文献中的部分选定基准上，Tessa相较当前先进方法获得了显著加速。摘要未给出具体基准规模、硬件配置、加速倍数或在稀疏模型上的性能表现，因此性能收益的适用范围仍需结合全文实验确认。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，稠密张量计算是进行稠密马尔可夫链概率模型检测的可行且经过正确性证明的实现范式。其核心贡献在于把模型检测计算连接到成熟的张量编译与加速器生态；该方法尤其适用于稠密转移动态，而非意图全面替代稀疏表示方法。

Abstract: We reexamine the problem of verifying Markov chains with respect to step-bounded reachability probabilities. Prevailing approaches rely on encoding the state-transition matrix using either explicit or symbolic representations. While these approaches are effective for sparse transition dynamics, they scale less favorably in the dense regime.
  Our insight is to cast probabilistic model checking of Markov chains as computations over dense tensors. This methodology enables the use of off-the-shelf compiler toolchains for optimized execution of these tensor computations on hardware accelerators. We prove the soundness of the methodology of mapping probabilistic model checking to tensor computations. We implement our approach in a tool called Tessa . Empirical evaluation shows that Tessa unlocks massive speedups over state-of-theart methods on selected benchmarks from the literature.

</details>


### [2] [Pretrain on Small Synthetic Data, Scale Large for Free: Symmetry-Aware Foundation Model for Logic Rule Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00383)
*Yin Jun Phua*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种满足多种对称性的逻辑规则归纳框架，通过从文字分数中规范化导出离散规则，使单个训练好的归纳器能够迁移到更大的命题模式，并保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑规则需要跨命题模式迁移，因此必须处理原子命名、样例顺序、极性翻转和标签交换等对称性。现有神经规则归纳器仅天然支持样例顺序对称，限制了其泛化能力与规则可解释性。

Method: 提出无需重新训练的规范化规则导出方法，将文字分数解码为离散规则；只要分数满足相应对称性，该导出过程就能严格保持等变性。方法基于Neural Rule Inducer（NRI）这一DNF基础模型，并通过模型架构、推理过程和训练机制补足其余对称性。

Result: 在合成压力测试中，支持集标签准确率在规模显著增大的模式上保持稳定，且新输入上的规则保真度高于未修改模型。在真实数据上，较大模式的准确率提升最明显。导出规则在合成全群测试和满足模式约束的真实数据测试中均表现出严格正确性。

Conclusion: 通过在结构上强制满足对称性，NRI可由小数据预训练模型转变为能够迁移到更大模式的可复用、可解释规则归纳器。严格等变性是规则导出方法本身的数学性质，而非特定模型的专属性质；本文仅在NRI上进行了实证验证。

Abstract: Logical rule induction seeks interpretable rules that transfer across propositional schemas. This requires respecting symmetries: atom naming, example order, polarity flips, and label swap. Enforcing exact symmetry by construction lets one trained inducer scale beyond its training schemas. Our central contribution is a canonical export that decodes a discrete rule from literal scores. It needs no retraining and is exactly equivariant whenever those scores respect the symmetries. We instantiate it on the Neural Rule Inducer, a disjunctive-normal-form (DNF) foundation model that natively respects only example order. We restore the remaining symmetries through architecture, inference, and training. On synthetic stress tests, accuracy on the support labels stays stable at much larger schemas, and rule fidelity on fresh inputs remains above the unmodified model. On real data, accuracy improves most on larger schemas. The exported rule is exact on synthetic full-group tests and on schema-valid real-data tests. This is a mathematical property of the export rather than of a specific model, and we validate it empirically only on the NRI. Enforcing symmetry by construction turns this small-data pretrained model into a reusable, interpretable inducer that transfers to larger schemas.

</details>


### [3] [Verifiable Checks for Business Rule Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00396)
*Joseph Tafese,Milad Hooshyar,Sam Bayless,Nick Feng,Arie Gurfinkel*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出SIRNA，一个结合大语言模型与SMT求解器的框架，用于检查业务规则的自然语言文档与其程序实现之间的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型系统中的业务规则会持续演化，自然语言文档与内部实现容易产生偏差。传统方法难以同时理解非结构化文本并严格验证逻辑等价性，因此需要融合语言理解与形式化验证的自动化方案。

Method: SIRNA采用三阶段流程：首先利用大语言模型将自然语言规则生成候选SMT公式；其次通过验证检查候选形式化结果的合理性；最后将程序中的对应业务规则转换为等价的SMT表示，并与由文档得到的形式化规则进行一致性验证。系统以成本计算规则为案例，但设计目标是适用于同时具有文本规范和程序实现的其他业务领域。

Result: 摘要声称，相比基线方法，SIRNA显著降低了误报和漏报数量，并能够为发现的不一致性提供可解释性。不过，摘要未给出具体数据集规模、评价指标数值、基线方法细节或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了LLM负责自然语言到逻辑表示的转换、SMT求解器负责严格一致性验证的混合范式。其主要贡献在于将文档—实现一致性检查自动化并增强可解释性；实际有效性仍需结合完整论文中的转换准确率、验证覆盖范围、对复杂规则的鲁棒性及人工审核成本进行评估。

Abstract: Maintaining consistency between natural language documentation of business rules and their evolving internal implementations is a significant challenge in large-scale systems. We present SIRNA, a tool and framework for checking such consistency using SMT solvers. Using the case study of cost calculations in tax domains, we demonstrate a three-part system that combines large language models (LLMs) with formal verification methods. SIRNA translates natural language documentation into candidate SMT formulas using LLMs, followed by checks to validate the translations. Then, corresponding business rules are converted into equivalent SMT representations and validated against the natural language formalizations. Our method is generalizable to domains where business logic exists in both natural language documentation and programmatic implementation. Compared to baseline evaluations, SIRNA significantly reduces the number of false positives and false negatives while offering explainability for its findings.

</details>


### [4] [Compact SAT and MaxSAT Encodings for Business-to-Business Meeting Scheduling with Idle-Time Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00614)
*Long Duc Nguyen,Tuyen Van Kieu,Khanh Van To*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出用于企业间会议排程的紧凑SAT与MaxSAT编码，通过解保持的域过滤、在部分优先级边界共享变量，以及最小化参与者内部空闲时段总量范围的目标函数，显著降低模型规模与内存开销，并在官方实例上整体优于Gurobi的中位总求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布尔编码保留了约束传播本可删除的“会议—时段”分配，并以成对子句表示优先级关系，导致变量和子句规模偏大。实际会议排程还需同时满足可用性、地点容量、冲突及先后关系等约束，因此需要更紧凑且能支持公平性优化的SAT/MaxSAT建模方法。

Method: 方法包括三部分：第一，对会议可分配时段进行解保持域过滤，预先删除不可能的分配变量；第二，在选定的优先级边界复用变量，以减少优先约束的编码冗余，并考察传递关系的作用；第三，构建MaxSAT目标，使参与者内部空闲时段总量的极差最小。实验覆盖126个官方实例及100个更高密度的派生实例，并比较域过滤、优先级表示、传递关系和不同优化方法。

Result: 相对于采用相同目标函数的改编既有MaxSAT公式，所提编码的子句数中位数减少40.3%，峰值内存中位数减少55.9%。仅使用域过滤即可使分配变量减少24.1%、子句减少16.2%。在含优先关系的官方实例中，边界变量共享使子句减少0.5%至1.0%；在最高密度派生实例中，最高可减少5.5%。所定义的空闲时间指标能够区分单时段中断与较长等待，而总空闲时间可作为补充指标。三种SAT/MaxSAT方法均在全部官方实例上取得低于Gurobi的中位总时间。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合预处理域过滤与结构化优先关系编码，可显著提升会议排程SAT/MaxSAT模型的紧凑性和求解效率。其公平性目标不仅优化总体等待，还关注参与者之间空闲时间负担的均衡；不过，变量共享在官方实例上的增益较小，说明其价值更依赖于问题密度和优先约束结构。

Abstract: Business-to-business meeting scheduling assigns requested pairwise meetings to time slots and locations under availability, capacity, conflict, and precedence constraints. The published Boolean formulation retains meeting-slot assignments that propagation can eliminate and encodes precedence relations with pairwise clauses. We present compact SAT and MaxSAT encodings based on solution-preserving domain filtering, variables shared at selected precedence boundaries, and an objective that minimizes the range of participants' internal idle-slot totals. Experiments on 126 official and 100 higher-density derived instances examine domain filtering, precedence representation, transitive relations, and optimization method. Compared with an adapted published MaxSAT formulation using the same objective, the proposed encoding reduces the median clause count by 40.3% and median peak memory usage by 55.9%. Domain filtering alone reduces assignment variables by 24.1% and clauses by 16.2%. Sharing variables at selected precedence boundaries reduces clauses by 0.5%-1.0% on official precedence instances and by up to 5.5% at the highest derived density. The idle-time measure distinguishes one-slot interruptions from longer waits, while aggregate idle time provides a complementary measure. Compared with Gurobi, a leading commercial solver, all three SAT and MaxSAT methods solve every official instance with lower median total times.

</details>


### [5] [Taming the Search Space: Solving and Generating Hitori and Binairo Puzzles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00768)
*Lukas Zandomeneghi,Rainhard Dieter Findling,Marc Kurz*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文比较了Hitori与Binairo逻辑谜题的优化回溯求解和基于CNF编码的SAT求解，并构建可生成有效且唯一可解实例的生成器。Binairo更适合SAT求解，Hitori则更适合结合约束传播的回溯求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hitori和Binairo具有不同的约束结构与求解难点，需要系统比较通用SAT建模和领域专用回溯优化的性能，并通过可控、唯一可解的实例生成支持公平基准测试。

Method: 研究实现两类求解器：一类为采用约束传播、变量排序及评分策略的回溯算法；另一类为将谜题约束编码为合取范式后调用SAT求解。实验在不同棋盘规模和难度的生成实例上，测量运行时间、搜索节点数及有效分支因子。

Result: 约束传播是回溯法中效果最显著的优化，可明显降低有效分支因子、搜索树规模和运行时间；变量选择启发式与评分策略带来进一步增益。对于Binairo，SAT方法在全部测试实例上均保持较低运行时间，而优化回溯在困难实例上超时。对于Hitori，基于传播的回溯表现最佳；SAT方法受迭代连通性检查开销主导，在困难实例上失败。

Conclusion: 不存在对两类谜题都最优的统一求解范式。Binairo的约束更适合SAT编码与求解，而Hitori应优先使用强约束传播的领域专用回溯；Hitori的SAT方案若要改进，关键在于降低连通性约束检查的迭代成本。

Abstract: This paper investigates solving and generation techniques for the logic puzzles Hitori and Binairo. Two solving paradigms are compared: backtracking with domain-specific optimizations, and SAT-based solving via conjunctive normal form encodings. An empirical evaluation analyzes runtime, explored search nodes, and branching factor across varying puzzle sizes. To support systematic benchmarking in the evaluation, generators capable of producing valid and uniquely solvable puzzle instances are developed. Results indicate that constraint propagation is the most effective backtracking optimization, substantially reducing the effective branching factor, search tree size, and thus runtime. Heuristic variable ordering and scoring strategies provide additional improvements. For Binairo, the SAT-based approach solves all evaluated instances within low runtime, while optimized backtracking fails to solve difficult puzzle instances within the timeout. For Hitori, propagation-based backtracking achieves the best results, while for the SAT-based approach the iterative connectivity check takes up the majority of the runtime, failing difficult puzzle instances.

</details>


### [6] [Alternating-Time Temporal Logic with Mean-Payoff Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01499)
*Muhammad Najib*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出带平均收益约束的ATL*扩展逻辑ATL*_mp，用于验证联盟是否存在单一策略，既满足时序目标，又在所有对手行为下保证长期平均收益阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ATL*擅长表达多智能体系统中的策略性时序目标，但难以同时刻画性能、资源或多指标收益保证。论文关注时序要求与定量平均收益要求的联合验证，且强调二者通常不能拆分为独立问题处理。

Method: 作者在带权并发博弈结构上定义ATL*_mp，使每个策略模态附带合取型长期平均收益约束；分别研究一维和多维约束、无记忆/有限记忆/完全记忆策略语义，以及纯定量、ATL和GR(1)等受限时序片段的模型检测复杂度与策略记忆需求。

Result: 一维约束下，完全记忆和有限记忆语义的模型检测均为2EXPTIME完全，与ATL*一致；纯定量片段及ATL、GR(1)受限片段具有更低复杂度。多维合取约束在有限记忆语义下仍为2EXPTIME完全。无记忆、有限记忆与完全记忆策略能力构成严格层级；有限记忆策略可达到任意严格低于完全记忆最优上确界的阈值。所需记忆量关于阈值分母具有紧确的线性上下界。

Conclusion: ATL*_mp为联合时序—性能保证提供了形式化且复杂度可控的验证框架，适用于带性能保证的时序综合、聚合目标和多准则目标。它还可表达相对固定收益基线的有利偏离，但不能直接复现二值偏好下ATL*对核心概念的标准编码。

Abstract: Alternating-time temporal logic and its extensions provide several ways of combining strategic and quantitative reasoning. We study a particular combination: whether a coalition has a single strategy that enforces a temporal objective while guaranteeing given long-run mean-payoff thresholds. We introduce ATL*_mp, an extension of ATL* over weighted concurrent game structures in which each strategic modality carries a conjunctive mean-payoff constraint. The temporal and quantitative requirements must hold against every behaviour of the remaining agents, and the existence of such a strategy cannot in general be reduced to the two requirements considered separately. For one-dimensional constraints, model checking is 2EXPTIME-complete under both perfect-recall and finite-memory semantics, matching ATL*. For the pure quantitative fragment and fragments restricted to ATL or GR(1) temporal objectives, model checking has lower complexity. With multi-dimensional conjunctive constraints, model checking under finite-memory semantics remains 2EXPTIME-complete. We show that memoryless, finite-memory, and perfect-recall abilities form a strict hierarchy, while finite-memory strategies still achieve every threshold strictly below the perfect-recall supremum. We give tight linear upper and lower bounds on the required memory as a function of the denominator of the threshold, even when the game and temporal monitor are fixed. We give several examples of properties expressible in the logic, including temporal synthesis with performance guarantees and aggregate and multi-criteria objectives. We also relate the logic to cooperative rational verification, showing that it can express beneficial deviations from fixed payoff baselines, but not directly reproduce the standard ATL* encoding of the core for dichotomous preferences.

</details>


### [7] [Isabelle/STARK: A Formalization of zk-STARK in Isabelle/HOL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01965)
*Diego Marmsoler*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文报告介绍了对一种 STARK 风格透明证明协议的 Isabelle/HOL 形式化验证，覆盖可执行的证明者与验证者模型、有限概率状态单子、最弱前置条件演算，以及完备性和可靠性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在以机器可检验的方式刻画透明证明协议的关键性质，降低复杂协议设计、概率推理与证明流程中因非形式化论证而产生的错误风险，并为具备形式化方法背景的读者提供可追踪的实现和定理位置。

Method: 作者在 Isabelle/HOL 中建立协议的可执行模型，将概率行为编码为有限概率状态单子，并在此基础上构建最弱前置条件演算。证明部分采用分阶段分解：首先证明诚实参与者条件下的零失败完备性，再逐步建立带有显式概率上界的可靠性结论。同时，报告给出主要定义和定理对应的 Isabelle 源码位置。

Result: 形式化开发得到两个核心结论：其一，协议满足零失败的诚实完备性，即诚实证明者生成的证明能够被验证者接受；其二，协议满足分阶段可靠性定理，并明确给出不诚实证明被接受的概率上界。开发还提供了可执行的证明者和验证者规格。

Conclusion: 该报告表明，STARK 风格透明证明协议可以在 Isabelle/HOL 中被系统化地建模和验证。其主要贡献不在于扩展密码学背景知识，而在于提供可执行协议语义、概率程序验证基础设施、模块化证明结构及可定位的源码证据，从而增强相关协议安全论证的可审计性和可信度。

Abstract: This report describes an Isabelle/HOL formalization of a STARK-style transparent proof protocol. The development contains an executable model of the prover and verifier, a finite probabilistic state monad with a weakest-precondition calculus, a zero-failure honestcompleteness theorem, and a staged soundness theorem with an explicit probability bound. The report is written for readers with a formal-methods background. It gives enough cryptographic context to explain the protocol, but its main emphasis is the formal model, the decomposition of the proofs, and the Isabelle source locations of the principal definitions and theorems.

</details>


### [8] [Octopus: Practical Equivalence Checking of P4 Packet Parsers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01982)
*Jort van Leenen,Tobias Kappé*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出Octopus工具，用于将P4数据包解析器转换为自动机，并以符号方式验证两个解析器的行为等价性。工具可输出双模拟证明或导致行为差异的反例比特流，并能在普通硬件上于数分钟内处理非平凡解析器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P4解析器定义输入比特流如何被识别、划分为报头和字段。解析器修改、重构或版本替换时，若行为发生非预期变化，可能导致报文处理错误。已有等价性验证方法难以高效处理复杂解析器，因此需要一种可用于实际网络开发场景、能够提供可验证证据的自动化工具。

Method: Octopus首先把P4包解析器编译或翻译为自动机模型，再采用符号化等价性检查。若两个自动机行为一致，工具构造双模拟关系作为等价证明；若不一致，则生成一个具体输入比特流，展示两个解析器在该输入上的可观察行为差异。论文还介绍工具实现，并通过网络相关用例评估其可用性与性能。

Result: 相较于先前工作，Octopus能够在消费级硬件上于数分钟内完成非平凡P4解析器之间的等价性检查。其输出不仅给出判断结果，还提供证明等价的双模拟证据，或用于定位问题的反例比特流，从而增强结果的可解释性和工程实用性。

Conclusion: Octopus将形式化自动机验证应用于P4解析器回归验证与演化检查，提升了复杂解析器等价性分析的可扩展性。其核心价值在于兼顾验证效率、证据生成和实际工具可用性；但摘要未说明支持的P4语言特性范围、自动机规模增长控制策略，以及在更大规模工业解析器上的性能边界。

Abstract: P4 is a domain-specific language for programming protocol-independent packet processors, where packet parsers describe how incoming bit-streams are structured into headers and fields. Building on work by Doenges et al. (2022), we present Octopus, a tool that translates P4 packet parsers into automata and then attempts to (symbolically) check their equivalence. Octopus produces evidence, either in the form of a bisimulation demonstrating equivalence, or a counterexample bit-stream witnessing a behavioral difference between the two parsers. In contrast with earlier work, our tool can check equivalence between non-trivial parsers within minutes, on consumer hardware. We report on the tool's implementation and evaluate its usability in networking contexts.

</details>


### [9] [The Triguarded Fragment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02013)
*Emanuel Kieroński,Sebastian Rudolph,Mantas Šimkus*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出三重守卫片段（TGF），将守卫片段（GF）与二变量一阶逻辑片段（FO2）统一到一个更具表达力且可判定的框架中，并系统刻画了其可满足性复杂度、有限模型性质及若干扩展的不可判定边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶谓词逻辑的可满足性通常不可判定，因此需要寻找兼具表达能力与可判定性的受限片段。GF和FO2是两类重要的可判定片段，并与模态逻辑、描述逻辑及验证和知识表示应用密切相关。论文旨在构造一个同时包含二者的统一片段，并明确其计算复杂度与表达能力边界。

Method: 作者定义TGF：仅当子公式含有三个及以上自由变量时，才要求量词受到守卫；自由变量不超过两个的量化则可不受标准GF守卫条件约束。随后通过可判定性与复杂度分析研究受限等词使用下的可满足性，证明有限模型性质并给出模型大小上界，同时考察放宽等词限制等自然扩展后是否仍保持可判定性。

Result: TGF同时包含GF和FO2。在限制等词使用时，TGF可满足性是N2ExpTime完全的；若谓词最大元数固定，则复杂度降为NExpTime完全。其数据复杂度为NP完全。TGF具有有限模型性质，且存在紧的双指数模型大小上界，因此有限可满足性与一般可满足性等价。另一方面，包括自由使用等词在内的多种自然扩展会导致不可判定性。

Conclusion: TGF提供了GF与FO2之间有效且严格的统一：它通过只对三变量及以上情形施加守卫约束，获得更高表达能力，同时保留可判定性和良好的有限模型理论性质。论文还清晰界定了复杂度及不可判定扩展的边界，使TGF成为研究模态逻辑、描述逻辑及相关知识表示形式化语言的潜在基础。

Abstract: A prominent research question in computational logic is how to restrict first-order predicate logic (FO) in such a way that the satisfiability problem becomes decidable. Among others, past efforts have identified two prominent decidable FO fragments of high expressivity: the guarded fragment (GF), and the two-variable fragment (FO2). These fragments are of high interest and crucial importance as they provide significant insights into decidability and expressiveness of other prominent (computational) logics like Modal Logics (MLs)} and various Description Logics (DLs)}, which play a central role in Verification, Knowledge Representation, and other areas. In this article, we show that GF and FO2 can be combined into a new fragment that subsumes both, while maintaining decidability of the satisfiability problem. This fragment, called the triguarded fragment (denoted TGF), is obtained by relaxing the standard definition of GF by requiring guardedness of quantification only for subformulae with three or more free variables. We show that, when restricting the use of equality, satisfiability in TGF is N2ExpTime-complete, dropping to NExpTime-complete when the maximum predicate arity is fixed (a natural assumption in the context of MLs and DLs). We further establish that the problem is NP-complete in terms of data complexity, which is again in line with data complexity results for basic expressive DLs. We observe that many natural extensions of TGF, including the liberal use of equality, lead to undecidability. We also establish that TGF has the finite model property (providing a tight doubly exponential bound on the model size), whence finite satisfiability coincides with satisfiability.

</details>


### [10] [Staying Productive Under the Palm Trees. On Graded Coeffect Typing in the Tropical Semiring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02596)
*Rémy Cerda,Ugo Dal Lago*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出以自然数上的热带半环作为分级余效应类型系统的分级空间，用类型等级精确表达参数何时可用，并在此基础上同时保证程序的生产性。作者构建了两种形式系统：一种支持递归与多态类型，另一种为带时间信息的交叉类型系统；后者的可类型化项恰好是具有遗传头部范式的项，且其类型判定在递归论意义下达到最优复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统类型系统通常难以统一刻画计算资源中的时间延迟、递归定义的生产性以及高阶程序的类型行为。论文旨在利用分级类型中的数值等级，将“某个参数经过多少时间步后可用”直接纳入静态语义，从而为流、递归程序及延迟计算提供既精确又可证明安全的类型化框架。

Method: 作者将自然数热带半环用作等级代数，其中等级a表示带注解参数最早在a个时间步后可用。首先，构造含递归类型和多态类型的分级类型系统，并施加一种针对递归类型的自然限制；证明该限制足以保证生产性，同时仍支持流及其递归程序的定义，并证明Nakano的later模态可直接嵌入该系统。其次，基于热带分级提出时间化交叉类型：类型环境不再以类型集合或多重集表示，而是以从类型A到最早可用时间的函数表示。随后证明该系统的生产性、类型化项与遗传头部范式之间的精确对应，并分析其算术层级复杂度。

Result: 第一套系统在允许递归和多态的条件下，通过对递归类型的限制保证了良定义程序的生产性，并能够表达流等典型无限数据结构及其递归计算；此外，later模态可被直接编码。第二套时间化交叉类型系统不仅保证生产性，而且给出完全刻画：一个项可类型化，当且仅当它具有遗传头部范式。论文进一步表明，该可类型性可直接证明为算术层级中的Π₀²性质，说明系统在递归论复杂度意义下是最优的。

Conclusion: 热带半环分级为“时间可用性”提供了自然且表达力强的类型级模型。该方法将时间延迟、递归生产性和交叉类型统一起来：既能对程序何时产生结果进行静态描述，又能以严格的语义与递归论结果刻画可类型化程序的边界。论文的核心贡献是证明时间分级不仅可用于保证生产性，还可通过时间化交叉类型精确表征具有遗传头部范式的程序。

Abstract: We show that the tropical semiring over the natural numbers, when used as the grading space in graded coeffect typing, faithfully models the passage of time while simultaneously guaranteeing productivity of well-typed programs. A grade a, when assigned to a function parameter, indicates that the parameter is not necessarily available immediately, but will become available after a time steps. We investigate this idea through two formal systems. We first introduce a graded type system featuring recursive and polymorphic types, and show that, in this setting, a natural restriction on recursive types is sufficient to guarantee productivity, while still allowing the definition of streams and recursive programs on them. In particular, we prove that Nakano's later modality can be embedded directly into our system. We then show that tropical grading naturally suggests a novel form of intersection typing, in which the role traditionally played by sets or multisets of types is instead taken by "timed" sets, i.e., functions assigning to each type A the earliest time, represented as a grade, from which the underlying term is available with type A. For the resulting system, we prove not only that productivity is guaranteed, but that it is also characterized: the typable terms are exactly those with hereditarily head normal forms. Remarkably, the system is recursion-theoretically optimal, i.e., typability can be directly proved to be a $Π_0^2$ property in the arithmetical hierarchy.

</details>

### [11] [Cross-Benchmark Generalization in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00181)
*Sushant Mehta,Logan Ritchie,Liudas Panavas,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究长程多工具强化学习后训练是否能产生跨基准迁移能力，而非仅利用训练环境中的工具模式、评分器规则或任务模板。作者在363个MCP任务上对Qwen3.5-122B-A10B进行SFT后RL训练，并报告其在5个未参与训练、奖励设计、模型选择或停止决策的外部评测上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭环境中的RL成绩可能来自对环境特定规律的投机性利用，因此同分布留出集不足以证明模型获得了可迁移能力。论文主张应以训练后智能体的实际行为及其跨基准表现为判据，检验后训练是否改变了通用的任务执行方式。

Method: 采用两阶段SFT-then-RL流程，在覆盖27类任务的363个长程MCP多工具任务上进行后训练。初始模型家族和SFT教师的选择参考了Toolathlon，但外部评测任务与评分器均未进入训练；外部得分也未用于奖励、超参数设定、检查点选择或训练停止。评估使用贪心解码下的pass@1，并通过配对轨迹开展探索性行为分析。

Result: 相对基础模型，训练模型在5项外部评测上获得增益：Toolathlon提升9.6个百分点、τ²-Bench提升5.3个百分点、BFCL-V4提升3.5个百分点、SWE-Bench Pro提升5.8个百分点、Terminal-Bench 2提升2.8个百分点。值得注意的是，两个软件工程评测均提升，而训练任务集不含软件工程任务。轨迹分析归纳出四类反复出现的行为差异：更谨慎地形成局部目标、构建与目标相关的工作状态、在局部修复中保持父目标稳定、以及验证任务是否完成。

Conclusion: 论文提供了描述性证据：长程、多工具的后训练可能促使模型形成可跨领域迁移的工作策略，而非仅适配训练环境。但结论主要建立在有限的外部基准增益和探索性轨迹归纳之上；要更强地证明因果机制与广泛泛化性，仍需更严格的消融实验、更多模型与训练域复现，以及对行为特征的可量化检验。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning (RL) in self-contained environments, a policy can get rewards by exploiting environment-specific regularities (tool schemas, grader parsing, task templates) rather than by acquiring transferable skill, and an in-distribution holdout shares those regularities. We argue that the discriminating question is behavioral, namely how a trained agent acts, and that cross-benchmark transfer is the right place to look for it. We post-train an open-weight mixture-of-experts model (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) on 363 long-horizon Model Context Protocol (MCP) tasks across 27 categories, using a two-stage SFT-then-RL pipeline. Toolathlon performance informed the initial base-family and SFT-teacher choices, but no external-benchmark task or grader entered training and no external score informed the reward, training hyperparameters, trained-checkpoint selection, or stopping. At greedy pass@1, the trained model improves over the base on five reported external evaluations: Toolathlon (+9.6 pp), $τ^2$-Bench (+5.3 pp), BFCL-V4 (+3.5 pp), SWE-Bench Pro (+5.8 pp), and Terminal-Bench 2 (+2.8 pp). Both software-engineering benchmarks improve despite the training collection containing no software-engineering tasks. An exploratory paired-trajectory analysis identifies four recurring behavioral differences (more careful local-goal formation, building goal-relevant working state, keeping parent goals stable through local repairs, and verifying completion) that appear in analogous forms across office workflows and code. These results provide descriptive evidence that long-horizon multi-tool post-training can change ways of working that transfer beyond its training domain.

</details>


### [12] [Source Code Authorship Attribution Does Not Generalize from Competitions to Classrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00227)
*Serhii Yemets,Marek Horváth*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估了基于CodeBERT的源代码作者归属在竞赛代码与课程作业两类场景中的泛化能力，发现其在GCJ竞赛数据上表现优异，但在真实课程作业数据上接近随机猜测，说明仅凭GCJ基准结果会显著高估教育场景中的实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码作者归属常被用于识别程序片段的编写者，但现有研究大量依赖Google Code Jam（GCJ）等竞赛数据集，尚不清楚这些基准上的高性能是否能迁移到课程作业等更具实际意义的教育环境。论文旨在检验这一迁移能力，并识别造成性能差异的数据与任务因素。

Method: 研究将预训练代码模型CodeBERT微调于三类数据：公开Kaggle来源的GCJ提交、整理后的GCJ档案，以及某技术大学收集的课程作业数据。评估采用Top-1和Top-10准确率，并覆盖10名、1000名、690名和812名作者等不同规模设置。此外，作者构建多模型对比基准，在更多数据集配置和模型家族上检验结论是否具有一致性。

Result: 在多轮GCJ数据上，CodeBERT对10名作者达到92.6%的Top-1准确率；在1000名作者设置下，Top-1仍为70.7%，Top-10为88.2%。然而，在课程作业数据上性能急剧下降：690名作者的封闭式作业中Top-1仅0.2%，812名作者的开放式作业中仅0.06%，均不高于相应随机基线。多模型实验也呈现一致的跨场景性能落差，表明该问题并非CodeBERT单一模型造成。

Conclusion: GCJ竞赛数据上的代码作者归属性能不能直接代表其在教育课程作业中的有效性。竞赛与课程场景可能在题目约束、代码风格稳定性、作者行为、协作与模板使用等方面存在关键差异，从而导致模型难以迁移。未来应在目标课程环境中进行验证，并使用更贴近实际应用的数据集与任务设计来评估作者归属系统。

Abstract: Source code authorship attribution aims to identify the author of a program fragment from its writing style. We fine-tune the pre-trained transformer CodeBERT on three sources of data: publicly available Google Code Jam (GCJ) submissions from an open Kaggle repository, a curated GCJ archive, and institutional coursework datasets collected at a technical university. On multi-round GCJ data, CodeBERT reaches 92.6% Top-1 accuracy for 10 authors and retains 70.7% Top-1 (88.2% Top-10) for 1000 authors. On the examined coursework datasets, the same pipeline performs at or below the corresponding chance baselines: 0.2% Top-1 on a closed-assignment dataset of 690 authors and 0.06% Top-1 on open-ended assignments evaluated over 812 authors. A companion multi-model benchmark provides consistent cross-model evidence across additional dataset configurations, indicating that the observed performance gap is not specific to CodeBERT and persists across the evaluated model families. We analyze dataset and task properties that plausibly explain this gap and argue that GCJ-based benchmarks overestimate the practical applicability of authorship attribution in educational settings unless they are validated on the target coursework context.

</details>


### [13] [Modelling Android applications through static analysis and systematic exploratory testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00228)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出DroidGraph框架，将静态分析与高效的系统化探索测试结合，为Android应用构建从方法语句到用户界面结构的综合控制流模型，以支持更有效的自动化测试生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用频繁发布，使自动化测试生成需求上升。现有静态分析难以完整处理Android的事件驱动特性及多样化开发机制；随机动态测试则缺乏应用知识，探索效率低、速度慢且覆盖有限。因此，需要一种能融合静态与动态信息的应用建模方法。

Method: DroidGraph首先采用传统静态分析提取应用组件、控制流和回调关系，再通过高效的系统化探索测试收集运行时信息，并将动态结果整合进静态模型。该框架生成跨越低层方法语句、高层UI结构及其交互关系的详细控制流模型，可作为模型驱动测试生成的基础。

Result: 在19个多样化Android应用上的评估显示，DroidGraph的探索测试相比常用随机探索平均多与应用18%的部分发生交互，同时所需交互次数少345次。动态分析结果还补充了静态分析：平均额外发现51个应用组件，以及49%更多的界面回调链接。

Conclusion: DroidGraph通过以较低交互成本进行系统化动态探索，有效弥补了Android静态分析的不完整性，并优于随机探索的效率与应用交互范围。其生成的综合控制流模型能够为更全面、可靠的Android自动化测试生成提供支持。

Abstract: Mobile application development is a fast paced industry with frequent releases. While the development pace increases, so too does the need for automated test generation. Model-based test generation is one of the most common and successful approaches to support this need. Understanding and modelling the application under test is integral to producing comprehensive, dependable and effective tests. Unfortunately, mobile platforms such as Android, introduce a host of difficulties. Static analysis struggles with Android's event-based nature and the growing variety of mechanisms available for developers to implement different features. Additionally, dynamic analysis, implemented by popular random test generators, is slow, inefficient, and limited by a lack of application knowledge.
  This paper introduces DroidGraph, a framework to generate a comprehensive control flow model of Android applications using traditional static analysis and efficient systematic exploratory tests. DroidGraph provides a detailed model of an Android application, from low level method statements to high level user interface structures. This model can be used to support automated test generation. We apply DroidGraph to 19 diverse apps and show that our efficient exploratory tests, on average, interact with 18% more of the app than commonly used random exploration in 345 less interactions. Integrating the dynamic analysis results provided by these tests complements our static analysis, and uncovers on average 51 more components and 49% more interface callback links in the application code.

</details>


### [14] [PADRAIG: Precise Android Automated Input Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00230)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PADRAIG，一种基于详细控制流模型的Android自动化测试输入生成框架。实验表明，相较三种先进工具，PADRAIG平均提升16%的应用代码行覆盖率，并减少84%的测试生成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机测试易维护但效率较低；系统化和搜索式方法通常生成效果较好，却需要较长运行时间；基于模型的方法虽然需要额外建模，但有望兼顾测试效果与生成效率。因此，本文旨在降低测试生成开销，同时提升Android应用的代码覆盖率。

Method: PADRAIG通过构建被测Android应用的详细控制流模型来生成测试输入。作者将其与三种采用不同测试输入生成技术的先进工具进行对比，评价指标包括应用代码行覆盖率和测试生成运行时间。实验使用来自F-Droid应用商店的19个随机选取的Android应用。

Result: 在19个应用上的实验结果显示，PADRAIG相较现有先进方法平均获得高16%的应用代码行覆盖率，并将测试生成运行时间平均降低84%。

Conclusion: PADRAIG能够在较低生成成本下实现更高的Android应用代码覆盖率，表明详细控制流模型有助于提升自动化测试输入生成的效率与有效性。

Abstract: Android automated test input generation has been a highly researched topic for over a decade and has shown promising results with a variety of approaches. Random input generation is commonly used and the easiest to maintain, but ultimately inefficient. Systematic and search-based approaches produce effective tests but require a disproportionally large generation runtime. Model-based approaches have the additional overhead of modelling the application under test (AUT) but they result in a faster test generation.
  In this paper we present Precise AnDRoid Automated Input Generation (PADRAIG), a model-based test input generation framework that uses a detailed control flow model of the AUT to generate tests that can achieve higher line coverage, with a lower test generation runtime than the state of the art. We compare the line coverage achieved, and the generation runtime of PADRAIG against 3 state of the art tools, each of which uses a different test input generation technique. Our results, using 19 randomly selected Android apps from the F-Droid application store, show that PADRAIG achieves, on average, 16% more coverage of the AUT than the state of the art and it can generate tests with, on average, 84% less runtime.

</details>


### [15] [Improving Mobile User Interface Testing with Model Driven Monkey Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00236)
*Jordan Doyle,Takfarinas Saber,Paolo Arcaini,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Monkey++：通过构建包含Android应用交互元素的新型控制流结构，并对该结构进行深度优先搜索，替代传统随机事件生成，从而显著提升自动化测试的速度与界面交互覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Exerciser Monkey不了解应用内部结构，只能随机或按人工设计脚本生成触摸、手势和按键等事件。因此测试存在大量重复操作、执行效率低、覆盖率不足以及缺陷发现能力较弱等问题。

Method: 作者设计了一种能够表示Android应用代码及其全部交互元素的控制流结构。Monkey++利用该结构获得测试环境知识，并采用深度优先搜索生成测试序列，以系统遍历用户交互元素、避免重复或冗余测试。实验将传统随机Monkey与基于该结构搜索的Monkey++进行接口覆盖率和执行效率比较。

Result: 结果表明，传统Monkey的随机性会产生执行缓慢且覆盖率较低的测试套件；Monkey++通过深度优先遍历生成的测试套件速度提高约一个数量级，并实现了用户交互元素的完全覆盖。

Conclusion: 利用应用结构信息指导事件生成能够明显改进Android自动化测试。该控制流结构及Monkey++不仅提高了测试覆盖率和效率，也为更有针对性的搜索式Android测试技术提供了基础。

Abstract: Testing mobile applications often relies on tools, such as Exerciser Monkey for Android systems, that simulate user input. Exerciser Monkey, for example, generates random events (e.g., touches, gestures, navigational keys) that give developers a sense of what their application will do when deployed on real mobile phones with real users interacting with it. These tools, however, have no knowledge of the underlying applications' structures and only interact with them randomly or in a predefined manner (e.g., if developers designed scenarios, a labour-intensive task) -- making them slow and poor at finding bugs.
  In this paper, we propose a novel control flow structure able to represent the code of Android applications, including all the interactive elements. We show that our structure can increase the effectiveness (higher coverage) and efficiency (removing duplicate/redundant tests) of the Exerciser Monkey by giving it knowledge of the test environment. We compare the interface coverage achieved by the Exerciser Monkey with our new Monkey++ using a depth first search of our control flow structure and show that while the random nature of Exerciser Monkey creates slow test suites of poor coverage, the test suite created by a depth first search is one order of magnitude faster and achieves full coverage of the user interaction elements. We believe this research will lead to a more effective and efficient Exerciser Monkey, as well as better targeted search based techniques for automated Android testing.

</details>


### [16] [SMS Opt-In/Opt-Out Consent Record Architecture in Enterprise CRM Systems: Compliance Patterns for Multi-Tenant Managed Packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00248)
*Devam Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向CRM原生企业短信托管包的生产级同意记录架构，用于满足短信通信中的显式订阅、退订即时生效、去重和发送前拦截等合规与工程需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRM虽具备联系人和潜客等丰富数据模型，但通常未将通信同意状态建模为独立的一等记录。对于通过企业应用市场分发的多租户托管包，软件包既不能假设客户组织的既有字段与对象结构，也不能随意修改其模式，因此需要可独立部署、可审计且跨租户适用的同意管理设计。

Method: 论文构建独立的同意记录数据模型，以关键词驱动的机制捕获订阅与退订事件；采用基于哈希的唯一性策略，将关键身份与通信维度编码为可去重的标识；在短信发送时执行抑制校验，阻止向退订或未满足许可条件的号码发送消息。设计同时考虑托管包在不同客户组织中的隔离性、模式约束和部署兼容性。

Result: 该架构将同意状态从CRM既有联系人模型中解耦，形成可跨多个企业组织复用的记录与执行机制。其核心能力包括：显式同意的持久化记录、关键词事件处理、重复记录抑制、退订状态的即时发送端强制执行，以及对客户自定义模式依赖最小化。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在多租户CRM托管包中，将通信同意设计为独立、可唯一识别且在发送时强制校验的领域模型，是兼顾合规、可审计性和产品可移植性的关键。哈希去重与发送时抑制共同降低了重复状态、历史数据不一致和误发消息的风险。

Abstract: Regulatory frameworks such as the Telephone Consumer Protection Act (TCPA) impose strict consent requirements on enterprise messaging systems: organizations must obtain and record explicit opt-in consent before sending SMS communications, and must immediately honor opt-out requests. While CRM platforms provide rich contact and lead data models, they do not natively model consent state as a first-class record type. This gap becomes architecturally significant in multi-tenant managed packages distributed via enterprise application marketplaces, where the package cannot assume or modify the installing organization's schema. This paper presents a production consent record architecture, generalized from patterns implemented within a CRM-native messaging managed package serving independent enterprise organizations spanning healthcare, financial services, and sales operations. We describe the data model design, keyword-based consent capture, a hash-based uniqueness strategy for deduplication, suppression enforcement at message send time, and the multi-tenant constraints that shaped these design decisions.

</details>


### [17] [LoopsBench: From Harness Engineering to Loop Engineering in Benchmarking Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00267)
*Han Li,Zhemin Fang,Rili Feng,Yingqi Zhao,Jiaheng Liu,Pengfei Gao,He Ye,Dayi Lin,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出LOOPSBENCH，用于评估编程智能体在长期、连续软件开发中的“循环工程”能力。该基准以具有依赖关系的开发单元DAG为任务结构，通过动态开放可执行测试并持续保留回归义务，衡量智能体的规划、执行、持续推进与回归维护能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程智能体基准大多聚焦局部修复、单次任务完成或最终状态，难以反映智能体在长期开发过程中处理前置依赖、迭代推进、测试反馈和回归问题的能力。随着编程智能体从单次调用转向持续运行，评测重点需要从工具调用与任务编排转向开发循环本身的工程质量。

Method: 作者构建LOOPSBENCH：每个任务由可独立测试的开发单元构成，并以源代码证据恢复单元间的前置依赖DAG。基准包含112个真实来源任务，覆盖8种编程语言和9个领域，累计超过5,300个开发单元。运行时采用流感知机制：仅释放当前依赖已满足的“就绪前沿”测试，同时将已完成单元持续作为回归测试义务。实验将多种前沿编程智能体与常见循环实现组合，比较其任务完成、计划对依赖图的恢复程度及回归表现。

Result: 最佳配置为Opus-4.7、Claude Code与外部续跑机制组合，完整解决率为25.00%。实验表明，智能体记录的计划只能部分恢复由源码证据得到的真实前置依赖DAG；同时，不同循环配置中仍普遍观察到回归事件，说明已完成工作在长期执行中容易被后续修改破坏。

Conclusion: LOOPSBENCH揭示了当前编程智能体在长时程开发上的核心短板：依赖建模不完整、持续规划不足，以及回归控制能力有限。该工作将评测对象从孤立任务成功转向开发循环中的持续可靠性，并通过开源任务、依赖结构和可执行测试，为后续研究循环策略、状态管理、规划机制和回归防护提供了可复现实验基础。

Abstract: Coding agent infrastructure is shifting from harness engineering toward loop engineering as coding agents are deployed for sustained long-horizon software development. Existing benchmarks often center on localized tasks or end-state outcomes, offering limited insight into sustained execution. We introduce LOOPSBENCH, a long-horizon benchmark for loop engineering in coding agent evaluation. Each task is a dependency DAG over separately testable development units with source-evidenced prerequisite edges. LOOPSBENCH comprises 112 tasks from authentic sources spanning 8 programming languages and 9 domains. Its flow-aware runtime releases tests along the ready frontier and retains completed nodes as regression obligations. We evaluate frontier coding agents paired with widely used loop implementations. The strongest configuration, Opus-4.7 with Claude Code and outer continuation, resolves 25.00% of tasks. Recorded plans recover only part of the source-recovered prerequisite DAG, and regression events remain visible across the evaluated loop profiles. We open source the benchmark data and code, including all tasks, more than 5,300 development units, and executable tests, at microsoft/Loopsbench.

</details>


### [18] [Algorithm-driven Development: A Proactive Approach to Improving Software Quality and Reducing Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01533)
*Philippe Jawish,Pierre Evrard,Alexandre Lemerle,Adrian Genin,Layal Dergham,Séverin Lanfranchi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“算法驱动开发”（ADD）方法：先将需求建模为算法流程图，再从流程图系统化导出验收测试，以连接需求、测试与实现。工业项目的四年评估显示，该方法与较高代码覆盖率、较低缺陷密度及稳定交付节奏相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统开发常面临需求理解偏差、边界场景遗漏、测试与需求脱节，以及在工期压力下难以同时保障质量和可维护性的问题。论文旨在建立一种可共享、可执行、可追溯的需求表示方式，使需求分析能够直接支撑测试设计与实现。

Method: ADD以算法流程图作为核心工件：流程图既描述功能行为和决策分支，也作为开发人员的技术蓝图；随后依据流程图中的路径、条件和异常分支生成验收测试，以覆盖正常场景与边界场景。评估在达索系统的一个工业项目中持续四年，涉及两个开发团队：团队1包含22,444行生产代码，团队2分析了157个API。研究结合生命周期管理系统和CI/CD数据，考察长期质量与交付指标，并按API复杂度比较ADD、测试驱动开发（TDD）和测试后置开发实践。

Result: 实证数据表明，采用ADD的项目保持了95%以上的代码覆盖率，在QA阶段和发布后的缺陷密度均较低，并维持了稳定的交付节奏。比较分析显示，ADD能够将功能需求中的决策逻辑和边界条件更早地映射为测试资产，从而提高功能场景覆盖的系统性。摘要未给出具体的缺陷率、统计显著性、各开发方式的完整对照数值或控制变量，因此其因果结论仍应谨慎解释。

Conclusion: ADD的主要贡献是以算法流程图作为需求、测试和实现之间的中间表示，并通过由模型导出验收测试来强化可追溯性和早期验证。该研究提供了工业场景下的长期应用证据，说明ADD具有改善质量保障与交付稳定性的潜力；但其外部有效性仍需通过更多项目、不同领域团队及严格控制的对比研究进一步验证。

Abstract: Ensuring software quality while meeting deadlines and adapting to evolving requirements is a persistent challenge in software engineering practice. This paper introduces Algorithm-Driven Development (ADD), a methodology developed from industrial practice to address recurring challenges in translating requirements into reliable, testable, and maintainable software behavior. ADD translates requirements into algorithmic flowcharts from which acceptance tests are systematically derived. These flowcharts serve both as specification artifacts and as technical blueprints, supporting shared understanding between stakeholders and developers. By linking requirement modeling with automated test generation, ADD provides systematic coverage of functional scenarios, including edge cases, from the outset of development. The approach was evaluated over a four-year period within an industrial project at Dassault Systèmes, involving two development teams, 22,444 lines of production code for Team 1, and 157 APIs analyzed for Team 2. The evaluation combined longitudinal quality and delivery indicators with a comparative analysis of ADD, TDD, and test-last development practices across API functions of different complexity levels. Empirical data collected from internal lifecycle management and CI/CD systems show that ADD supported sustained code coverage above 95%, low defect density in both QA and post-release phases, and a stable delivery cadence. These findings provide evidence of ADD's potential to strengthen the connection between requirements, testing, and implementation in industrial software development contexts.

</details>


### [19] [OrEdge: Efficient Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection in Distributed Software Systems via Orthogonal-Domain Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00309)
*Amr M. Zaki,Farhoud Jafari Kaleibar,Honggeun Ji,Komal Sarda,Marin Litoiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OrEdge提出一种面向边缘环境的轻量级多模态分布式软件异常检测框架，通过正交域时序重构替代高开销注意力与图模型，在保持有竞争力检测性能的同时显著降低参数规模与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态异常检测方法通常依赖注意力机制或图结构建模，计算与存储开销较高，难以部署于资源受限设备。该工作旨在联合利用日志、指标和链路追踪数据，并以更低复杂度捕获时序依赖和跨信号冗余。

Method: 框架核心为OrEdgeCore正交域重构模块：利用正交基表示和重构重复性时序模式，抑制短暂扰动；随后结合异构可观测性数据完成异常识别。论文还通过消融实验、敏感性分析、正交基对比和案例研究检验各组件作用。

Result: 在MSDS、SN和TT三个真实微服务数据集上，OrEdge取得有竞争力的检测效果。其重构模块参数量最高为9.6K，低于对比方法的20K至143K；在Raspberry Pi上可实现亚秒级推理，相比现有方法将推理延迟降低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，正交域时序建模能够以紧凑模型有效处理多模态软件可观测性数据，是高计算成本注意力和图模型的可行替代方案，尤其适合实时、资源受限场景。摘要中的结论主要强调效率优势；实际泛化能力仍需结合数据集覆盖范围、异常类型分布及与强基线的完整指标进行进一步评估。

Abstract: We introduce Orthogonal-Edge (OrEdge), a lightweight framework for real-time anomaly detection in multi-modal distributed software systems. Unlike existing approaches that rely on computationally expensive attention- and graph-based architectures, OrEdge leverages orthogonal-domain temporal representations to achieve accurate anomaly detection with substantially lower computational complexity and model size. It jointly analyzes heterogeneous monitoring data, including logs, metrics, and traces, to identify abnormal software behavior, capture temporal dependencies, and reduce redundancy across observability signals. At its core, OrEdge incorporates OrEdgeCore, a lightweight orthogonal-domain reconstruction module that captures recurring temporal patterns while suppressing transient variations. Evaluated on three real-world microservice datasets (MSDS, SN, and TT), OrEdge achieves competitive detection performance while reducing the reconstruction model size to at most 9.6K parameters, compared with 20K--143K parameters in existing methods. This compact design enables efficient deployment on resource-constrained edge devices: on Raspberry Pi platforms, OrEdge achieves sub-second inference and reduces inference latency by over an order of magnitude compared with existing approaches. Extensive ablation studies, sensitivity analyses, orthogonal basis evaluations, and qualitative case studies further validate the effectiveness of each design component. Overall, OrEdge demonstrates that orthogonal-domain temporal modeling provides an effective alternative to computationally intensive attention- and graph-based architectures, achieving a favorable balance between detection accuracy and computational efficiency for real-time multi-modal anomaly detection in edge environments. The code is available at https://github.com/theamrzaki/MicroService_Twin_Original.

</details>


### [20] [Artificial Intelligence and Modeling & Simulation: An Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00366)
*Niclas Feldkamp,Philippe J. Giabbanelli,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该报告系统梳理了人工智能与建模仿真（M&S）的双向融合：AI可增强或替代仿真流程中的部分环节，仿真则可为AI提供数据、训练环境与评测平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI等技术快速发展，以及数据与计算资源日益丰富，使AI与M&S的研究需求和技术路径显著交汇。报告旨在为研究者提供结构化视角，以理解这一快速演化领域的主要方向、应用机会与挑战。

Method: 作者按照M&S全生命周期组织相关研究，包括模型规约、输入建模、仿真执行、实验设计、验证与确认，以及输出分析；在各阶段选取代表性研究，说明大语言模型等AI技术对仿真实践的影响，并总结局限性与开放问题。

Result: 报告表明，AI能够在模型构建、参数与输入生成、代理执行、实验优化、结果分析等环节提升自动化程度和效率；与此同时，仿真环境能够生成可控数据、支持AI训练，并为模型评估提供可重复的测试条件。大语言模型正在重塑若干仿真工作流，但其可靠性、可解释性、验证难度及适用边界仍需重点关注。

Conclusion: AI与M&S并非单向工具关系，而是相互促进的技术生态。该报告提出的分阶段框架与概念路线图有助于定位具体研究问题，并提示未来工作应重点解决可信验证、方法集成、评测规范和实际部署等挑战。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and Modeling & Simulation (M&S) are increasingly intertwined, reflecting converging research needs across both communities, rapid technological advances such as the rise of generative AI, and the growing availability of data and computational resources. This report provides a structured overview of the intersections of AI and M&S. The relationship goes both ways: AI can support, augment, or even replace components of simulation studies, while simulations can serve as data generators, training environments, and evaluation platforms for AI. We organize this landscape along the stages of M&S from model specification and input modeling to execution, experimentation, verification and validation, and output analysis. Selected studies at each stage illustrates how techniques such as Large Language Models have reshaped simulation practices, while highlighting limitations and open challenges. This report also provides a conceptual roadmap that helps readers navigate a rapidly changing ecosystem.

</details>


### [21] [CASPER-Change-Aware Slice Prioritization for Efficient Regression Testing of LLM-based systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00378)
*Biruk Asmare Muse,Lionel Briand,Yiwei Lu,Keheliya Gallaba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CASPER，一种面向大语言模型系统回归测试的变更感知切片优先级框架，通过识别语义相关且性能一致的测试切片，并根据执行日志预测回归可能性，从而提升回归测试效率与定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个回归实例的信息有限，单次失败未必代表系统层面的有意义回归，也难以诊断受影响的行为；仅依赖整体性能变化又过于粗粒度，无法定位具体受影响行为。因此，需要在实例与系统整体之间引入测试切片这一中间分析层次。

Method: CASPER首先采用进化式切片识别方法，从回归实例中构建包含语义相关实例且性能特征一致的回归切片。针对提示级和模型级变更，框架进一步利用执行日志中的行为信息评估各切片的回归可能性，并据此进行优先级排序。实验在软件问题解决领域开展，分别与基于聚类的切片识别方法和随机排序方法进行比较。

Result: 实验结果表明，CASPER能够生成一致性更高的测试切片，同时保持相当或更好的语义连贯性。在不同类型的大语言模型系统变更和不同测试预算下，CASPER均能提升回归切片的优先级排序效果。

Conclusion: 通过以测试切片为中间分析单元，CASPER有效弥补了单实例分析过细与系统级指标过粗之间的不足，能够更高效地识别和优先测试潜在回归行为，为大语言模型系统的提示和模型变更测试提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Regression testing for LLM-based systems poses unique challenges because individual regression instances provide limited information about system-level regressions. A failure in a single instance does not necessarily indicate a meaningful regression or provide sufficient information to diagnose affected behaviors. Conversely, detecting regressions based only on overall system performance changes is too coarse-grained, as it does not identify which behaviors are affected. This motivates analyzing regression instances at an intermediate level through test suite slices.
  To address this challenge, we propose CASPER, a change-aware slice prioritization framework for efficient regression testing of prompt-level and model-level changes in LLM-based systems. CASPER first identifies regression slices containing semantically related instances with consistent performance characteristics using an evolutionary slice identification approach. Given a change to an LLM-based application, CASPER prioritizes slices according to their likelihood of regression using behavioral information extracted from execution logs. We instantiate CASPER in the software issue resolution domain and evaluate slice identification against clustering-based baselines and regressed slice prioritization against a random ranking baseline. Results show that CASPER generates more consistent slices while maintaining comparable or better semantic coherence and improves regressed slice prioritization across different LLM-based system changes and testing budgets.

</details>


### [22] [Lossless Tensor Compression as Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02162)
*Jieke Shi,Junda He,Wenjia Jiang,Weifeng Sun,Shidong Pan,Zhensu Sun,Chengran Yang,Peixin Zhang,Yifan Jia,Zhou Yang,Thong Hoang,Xiwei Xu,Zhenchang Xing,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Brevis将无损张量压缩建模为程序合成问题，通过自动生成可逆DSL程序，实现模型检查点的按位精确重构，并在压缩率上优于通用与现有张量专用压缩器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型检查点的数量和体积持续增长，导致存档、传输与部署成本上升。通用压缩器未利用张量内部结构，而既有张量压缩方法通常依赖固定、格式绑定的处理流水线，适应性有限。

Method: 作者设计了用于描述重复区域、浮点字段等张量规律的类型化领域专用语言，并以可逆算子保证无损恢复。系统从少量代表性张量中学习检查点特定的生成先验，再使用有界A*搜索合成紧凑、自包含的DSL程序；解压时直接执行该程序以按位重建原始张量。

Result: 在覆盖语言、音频和图像生成模型的10个公开检查点上，Brevis将2.13 TB数据压缩至1.41 TB，存储量减少33.93%。其归档结果相较zstd、gzip等4种通用压缩器最多小30.87%，也优于ZipNN和DFloat11。在实际并发配置下，压缩吞吐量为3.60 GB/s，解压吞吐量为6.61 GB/s，且保留全部源字节。

Conclusion: Brevis表明，利用程序合成自动发现张量结构，可在严格无损条件下获得更高的检查点压缩效率。其检查点特定先验与可执行DSL表示兼顾了压缩率、通用性和解压性能，为大规模模型检查点的存储与分发提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Model checkpoints are growing in both number and size, which makes archival, transfer, and deployment increasingly costly. General-purpose compressors can reduce storage requirements but ignore tensor structure, whereas existing tensor-specific compressors rely on fixed and format-specific pipelines. We present Brevis, which formulates lossless tensor compression as program synthesis. We design a typed domain-specific language (DSL) that captures recurring tensor structures, such as repeated regions and floating-point fields, through a set of reversible operators. Given a tensor, Brevis synthesizes a self-contained DSL program that reconstructs it bit-exactly. A checkpoint-specific production prior, learned from a small representative sample of tensors, guides a bounded A* search to synthesize compact programs, which can later be executed directly for bit-exact decompression. On 10 public checkpoints spanning language, audio, and image generation models, Brevis reduces 2.13 TB of checkpoint data to 1.41 TB, a 33.93% storage reduction. It produces archives up to 30.87% smaller than those of four general-purpose compressors, including zstd and gzip, and smaller archives than the tensor-specific compressors ZipNN and DFloat11. Under a practical concurrency configuration, Brevis achieves 3.60 GB/s compression and 6.61 GB/s decompression while preserving every source byte.

</details>


### [23] [Distilling Reasoning Traces into Advisory Prompts for Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00437)
*Faizan Faisal,Prem Devanbu,Toufique Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需额外训练、仅在推理阶段使用的错误缓解方法：从低资源模型在“思考模式”下成功避免的代码错误中提炼经验，再将经验压缩为简短的建议提示词，以提升普通模式下的代码任务表现并减少持续使用思考模式的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码大模型训练数据主要来自人类编写的代码，难以完全避免缺陷，因此模型在代码生成、输入推断、输出预测等任务中可能产生严重错误。虽然可切换的“思考/推理模式”通常能降低错误率，但会带来额外计算与时间成本。论文希望借鉴人类学习编程时“识别错误—反思原因—归纳规则—内化规则”的过程，使模型并非每次都进行高成本推理，仍能获得部分推理模式带来的收益。

Method: 方法首先筛选低资源语言模型在普通模式下出错、但在同一模型思考模式下能够正确解决的样例。随后，使用更大模型分析这些错误及思考模式为何能避免它们，生成具有解释性的反思总结；再由大模型将这些总结进一步压缩为简洁、可复用的咨询式提示词，例如在编码前重述需求以澄清约束。最后，将这些提示词加入目标模型的推理上下文，评估其对原模型及其他模型代码任务性能的影响，并分析错误类型和该方法适用的条件。

Result: 实验表明，该策略可在多种中小规模模型上发挥作用：通过从思考模式成功案例中提炼出的建议提示词，能够改善模型在非持续思考模式下的表现。部分提示词还具有跨模型迁移价值，即由一个模型的错误经验归纳得到的规则可帮助其他模型。论文同时对模型常见代码错误进行了考察，并刻画了该方法更可能有效的场景。

Conclusion: 思考模式的价值不仅在于即时执行更长的推理，还可被转化为简短、可复用的行为规则。通过“错误驱动的反思—规则提炼—提示注入”流程，可以在不重新训练模型、且不总是承担思考模式开销的条件下减少代码错误。不过，该方法的效果依赖于能否获得普通模式与思考模式表现不同的代表性错误样例，以及提炼出的建议是否与目标任务和目标模型相匹配。

Abstract: Language models are widely used for generating and otherwise processing code (e.g., identifying code hallucinations, possible inputs, or predicting outputs); however, LLMs can make mistakes, which can be serious. One key issue is that models are trained on (still) largely human-written, and thus imperfect, code; it's not easy to find sufficiently large code corpora that are entirely free of bugs. Thus, other inference-time ways of reducing LLM errors, without additional training, are desirable. "Reasoning" or "thinking" modes, exposed as a togglable feature by hybrid reasoning models, do reduce errors; however, reasoning consumes additional resources. This paper asks if better performance can be achieved without always incurring the cost of reasoning. Human students of programming learn to avoid mistakes by (a) identifying them, (b) reflecting upon the cognitive lapses that led to them (essentially, "thinking through" the errors), (c) inferring general rules or lessons from these reflections, and (d) internalizing these lessons into rules. In tutorial sessions with an instructor, this is a common Socratic interaction. Examples of such internalizable rules might include the nugget "Before coding, restate the requirements to clarify them." Inspired by this process, this paper describes an approach where we first identify examples in which "thinking mode" in a (low-resource) LLM avoids errors. These errors, and their avoidance via "thinking" in the same LLM, are then examined by a bigger LLM to generate summary explanations; these are then summarized by a large LLM into brief advisory prompts. This approach works on many modest-sized models; in some cases, the "advisory prompts" thus learned can also be gainfully transferred to other models. We also present investigations into the nature of coding errors that language models make, and a characterization of when this approach can be helpful.

</details>


### [24] [AiFlow: Token-Native Reactive Orchestration with Bounded Backpressure for Streaming LLM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00558)
*Qunhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出AiFlow，一种面向大语言模型流式应用的令牌级响应式编排模型。其核心是在工作流图中将模型输出增量统一为带类型的Context<T>事件，并通过Node Guardian对每个节点的队列、并发、顺序、溢出、取消和重试进行显式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编排框架虽能暴露流式输出增量，但多数工作流节点仍以粗粒度请求—响应方式处理生成过程。队列管理、工作线程分配、顺序保持及背压控制通常依赖临时回调实现，导致延迟高、内存边界不清晰、并发行为难以验证。

Method: AiFlow构建有向流式图，将提供方输出的增量事件规范化为类型化Context<T>。每个节点由Node Guardian管理，并声明本地队列上限、工作线程并发度、事件顺序策略、溢出策略、取消传播和重试规则。论文还给出紧凑DSL及JSON图定义到运行图的编译方式，并提供类型安全、状态并发安全与依赖注入兼容性的静态验证；实验覆盖微基准、30次真实轨迹回放、在线描述性运行、LangGraph对比、流式RAG任务和本地后端验证。

Result: 实验显示，AiFlow不会改变模型服务端的首令牌时间，但相较于聚合式处理，可将应用侧首个可见令牌时间降低70.9%至94.7%。在无界队列策略对比中，其运行时自有队列深度降低93.7%至96.5%，并保持在声明的队列上限内。补充材料提供脚本、原始轨迹、结构化结果表、校验信息及无需API的冒烟测试。

Conclusion: AiFlow的主要贡献是将流式令牌传递、背压和节点级资源治理提升为编排模型的一等概念，从而为复杂LLM工作流提供更低的应用层流式延迟和可验证的有界内存行为。论文的结论主要由受控实验和轨迹回放支持，但摘要未充分说明不同负载规模、复杂拓扑及真实生产环境下的通用性与额外调度开销。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly operate as streaming workflows combining retrieval, tool calls, safety filters, and multi-agent coordination. Although contemporary frameworks expose provider deltas, workflow nodes often treat generation as coarse request-response steps, leaving queue management, worker allocation, ordering, and backpressure to ad hoc callback code. This paper presents AiFlow, a token-native reactive orchestration model that normalizes provider deltas into typed Context<T> events propagated through a directed streaming graph. Each node is managed by a Node Guardian that declares and enforces local queue bounds, worker concurrency, ordering, overflow policy, cancellation propagation, and retry discipline. We formalize the bounded-memory property, present the compilation from a compact DSL and JSON graph form, and provide static validation for type safety, state concurrency, and injection compatibility. Controlled microbenchmarks, captured DeepSeek trace replay (30 runs), descriptive online runs, LangGraph baselines, a streaming RAG workload, and an Ollama local-backend check show that AiFlow does not alter provider-side Model TTFT but reduces Application TTFPT by 70.9-94.7\% versus aggregation and keeps runtime-owned queue depth within declared bounds (93.7-96.5\% MaxQ reduction versus unbounded policies). The supplementary artifact contains scripts, raw traces, machine-readable tables, checksums, and an API-free smoke test; the public implementation is available through the FIT Framework repository.

</details>


### [25] [Can Perplexity Serve as a Cognitive Signal for Code Understandability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00624)
*Xiaokai Rong,Mohammadali Sefidi Esfahani,Aashish Yadavally,Rigby Peter,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究发现，尽管大语言模型的词元级困惑度可反映局部代码理解困难，但将其简单聚合为片段级指标后，无法稳定预测人类对整段代码可理解性的判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究显示，词元级困惑度与人类在代码阅读中的局部困惑存在对应关系。本文进一步检验：困惑度能否作为方法或代码片段层面的自动化可理解性度量，从而支持代码质量评估与改进。

Method: 研究在多类以人为依据的数据集上进行实证分析，包括方法级可理解性评分、代码输出预测任务和被接受的可理解性改进补丁。作者测试了平均值、中位数和峰值等词元困惑度聚合方式，并分析困惑度分布、标注一致性，以及不同模型和分词器之间的差异。

Result: 简单的片段级困惑度聚合与人类可理解性评价之间未呈现可靠相关性。其原因包括：困惑度分布高度偏斜且具有重尾特征，高值可能由标识符、字面量、类型、分隔符和分词伪影引起，而不一定对应语义困难；人类对整体代码难度的标注常缺乏一致性；不同模型及分词器产生的困惑度分布及其与人工难度的对应关系差异显著。

Conclusion: 词元级困惑度可以作为定位局部代码困惑点的潜在认知信号，但不能直接通过普通统计聚合用于可靠的片段级可理解性测量。未来需要采用面向代码结构的聚合方法、考虑标注共识的评估协议，并系统分析模型与分词器敏感性。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that token-level perplexity from large language models can align with localized human confusion during code comprehension. This raises a natural question: can perplexity also serve as a snippet-level signal for code understandability? We conduct an empirical study of this question across multiple human-grounded datasets, including method-level understandability judgments, output-prediction tasks, and accepted understandability-improvement patches. Despite prior token-level evidence, we find that simple snippet-level aggregations of token perplexity, such as average, median, or peak perplexity, do not reliably correlate with human understandability. We then investigate why this happens. First, token perplexity is highly skewed and heavy-tailed across code structures; extreme spikes arise not only from semantically meaningful constructs, but also from identifiers, literals, types, separators, and tokenization artifacts. Second, human understandability labels often lack consensus, making whole-snippet difficulty a noisy target. Third, perplexity distributions and their alignment with human difficulty vary substantially across models and tokenizers. These findings explain why prior token-level perplexity--confusion alignment does not directly transfer to snippet-level understandability. Overall, our study positions perplexity as a promising but delicate cognitive signal: useful for localized code confusion, but requiring code-aware aggregation, consensus-aware evaluation, and model-sensitivity analysis before it can support reliable code-understandability measurement.

</details>


### [26] [Understanding Online Failure Prediction in Linux Through Complementary Multi-View Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00651)
*Diogo Dória,João R. Campos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估了一个面向 Linux 操作系统的可解释在线故障预测（OFP）管线。该方法在未见工作负载、冻结训练产物且不重训练的严格条件下实现了91%至94%的故障检测率，并将误报率控制在1%以下；但故障诊断对工作负载变化更敏感，对未见故障模式的诊断完全失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅提供故障预测告警不足以支持实际运维：操作人员需要理解告警依据、故障发生过程及可采取的响应措施。同时，高预测精度可能来自工作负载特有噪声或偶然遥测相关性，而不一定反映可迁移的真实故障机理。因此，论文旨在构建兼具高检测性能与诊断解释能力的在线故障预测系统，并评估其跨工作负载泛化边界。

Method: 作者构建了用于 Linux 的可解释 OFP 管线。检测阶段采用基于共识的特征选择；解释与诊断阶段结合时间起始点分析、子系统级因果分析以及其他互补诊断机制。评估采用严格跨工作负载设置：训练完成后冻结模型及相关训练产物，在未见工作负载上直接测试，不进行再训练；同时通过留一故障模式法（LOMO）考察系统对未见故障模式的诊断能力。

Result: 在未见工作负载上，系统故障检测率达到91%至94%，误报率低于1%，表明检测模型具有较强的跨工作负载泛化能力。早期预警时间高度依赖故障模式，在38秒至215秒之间变化。相比检测，故障模式诊断更易受工作负载漂移影响，部分诊断机制对特定故障类型效果有限。在 LOMO 测试中，模型仅依据相关但不同的训练故障模式时，对未见故障模式的诊断准确率为0%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，可解释机制能够补充高准确率故障预测，为判断预测信号是否对应可迁移的故障结构提供依据。核心结论是：故障检测通常比故障诊断更能跨工作负载泛化；预警提前量由故障模式决定；相关训练故障模式不足以可靠诊断未见故障模式。实际部署中应将检测与多种互补解释机制结合，并明确区分可泛化的检测能力和更脆弱的诊断能力。

Abstract: Accurate Online Failure Prediction (OFP) has been shown to be feasible in Operating Systems (OSs) settings, but prediction alone is not sufficient for practical adoption. Without diagnostic insight, operators have limited basis to trust alerts or decide how to respond. Moreover, even when predictive accuracy is high, it is often unclear whether models are capturing meaningful failure processes or merely exploiting workload-specific noise and incidental correlations in telemetry. This paper reports a practical experience building and evaluating an explainable OFP pipeline for Linux OSs. We combine consensus-based feature selection for detection with temporal onset analysis, subsystemlevel causal analysis, and complementary diagnostic mechanisms to support failure interpretation. Evaluated under strict crossworkload conditions with frozen training artifacts, it achieved 91-94% detection on unseen workloads without retraining, while maintaining false alarm rates below 1%. However, failure mode diagnosis proved substantially more sensitive to workload shift, and several diagnostics mechanisms showed limited effectiveness for specific failure types. Our experience highlights three main lessons: i) detection generalizes more robustly than diagnosis across workload changes; ii) early-warning capability depends strongly on the failure mode, ranging from 38 to 215 seconds in our study; and iii) unseen failure modes are not reliably diagnosable from related training modes alone, providing 0% accuracy under Leave-One-Mode-Out (LOMO) evaluation. Taken together, these results show the value of complementary explainability mechanisms for interpreting accurate failure predictions, revealing when predictive signals reflect transferable failure structure and when diagnostic generalization breaks down under workload variation.

</details>


### [27] [Unreliable in Practice? A Comprehensive Study of Errors in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00661)
*Rodrigo Pato Nogueira,Marco Vieira,João R. Campos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了七种大语言模型在四种编译型语言中生成的86,726个含编译或运行时错误的代码样本，发现错误模式显著受模型和编程语言影响，但大型模型仍频繁出现基础性错误，并常遗漏输入验证与内存安全检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于大模型生成代码错误的研究通常仅覆盖单一语言、少量任务或有限模型，难以区分普遍错误与模型或语言特有错误。该研究旨在建立更全面的错误图谱，为评估代码生成能力、设定可靠性预期及设计缓解措施提供依据。

Method: 研究者收集七个大语言模型在四种编译型编程语言中生成的、具有编译或运行时错误的代码样本；使用大语言模型按根本原因对错误进行分类，并通过人工验证分类质量；随后从模型、语言和题目难度三个维度比较错误发生率与错误模式。

Result: 错误类别在不同模型和语言之间存在明显差异，说明错误来源并非完全通用。尽管模型规模较大，生成代码仍会出现简单的语法、逻辑或运行时问题。此外，代码经常缺少基本输入校验和内存安全保护，从而可能导致溢出、资源耗尽及其他可靠性问题。

Conclusion: 大模型代码生成的质量评估不能仅依赖总体成功率，而应结合模型、语言和任务难度分析具体错误类型。研究提供的大规模标注数据有助于定位高频缺陷，并支持针对输入校验、资源管理和内存安全等薄弱环节开发测试、提示、静态分析与后处理机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being widely used for coding, with reports indicating that AI now generates an increasing share of production code. Studies show that LLMs can significantly improve developer productivity, yet they still struggle with more complex coding tasks. Just as understanding error modes in human-written code has been central to improving software quality, identifying and characterizing the errors in LLM-generated code is critical for setting realistic expectations and designing mitigation strategies. Prior research has been limited in scope, often focusing on a single language, a small number of problems, or a limited selection of models. As a result, there is still no comprehensive understanding of which errors are common and which are specific to certain models or languages. To address these gaps and develop a deeper understanding of the quality of LLM-generated code, we analyzed a corpus of 86,726 code samples that contained compilation or runtime errors. These samples were generated by seven LLMs across four compiled languages. We classified errors by their underlying causes using an LLM, manually validated these classifications, and performed a comparative analysis. This labeled data is then used to measure error prevalence by model, language, and problem difficulty, to identify common error patterns. Results show that, although error types vary strongly across languages and models, even the largest models frequently make simple mistakes. We also observe that generated code often omits basic input validation or memory-safety checks, which can lead to overflows, resource exhaustion, or other reliability/security issues.

</details>


### [28] [Vul4Py: Benchmarking Automated Vulnerability Repair in Python with Paired Exploit and Functional Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00692)
*Tan Bui,Ting Zhang,Ferdian Thung,Yunpeng Xiong,Penghao Jiang,Xin Zhou,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Vul4Py，这是一个面向Python自动化漏洞修复的可复现基准，采用漏洞利用与功能回归配对验证，并比较了专用修复工具、直接提示的大语言模型和软件工程智能体三类方法。结果显示，OpenHands修复了100个漏洞中的41个，显著优于直接提示的大语言模型和专用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Python自动化漏洞修复评测通常仅依赖概念验证漏洞利用，或只在少数项目提供功能测试时进行验证，因此可能接受能够阻断攻击但破坏正常功能的修复补丁。论文旨在建立统一、严格且可复现的Python漏洞修复比较框架。

Method: 构建Vul4Py基准，包含来自60个开源项目的100个真实漏洞，覆盖60种CWE，时间跨度为2017至2025年。每个实例均提供固定且可复现的环境，并配备成对的验证预言机：漏洞利用预言机要求漏洞版本可被利用而修复版本不可被利用；项目原生pytest功能预言机要求漏洞版本和修复版本均通过功能测试。基于该基准比较三类共六种方法：专用漏洞修复工具、直接提示的大语言模型和软件工程智能体。

Result: OpenHands成功修复41个漏洞，最强的直接提示大语言模型修复4个，专用工具修复2个，尽管三者使用相同的基础模型。配对预言机拒绝了仅依赖漏洞利用测试时可能接受的119个补丁中的15个，说明其能够识别功能回归。在其接受的104个补丁中，98个经人工确认与开发者补丁具有语义等价性。

Conclusion: Vul4Py通过同时验证安全性和功能保持性，提高了Python自动化漏洞修复评测的可信度与可比性。实验表明，具备软件工程能力的智能体明显优于直接提示的大语言模型和专用修复工具；未来研究应重视项目级环境交互、功能回归检测以及严格可复现的双重验证机制。

Abstract: Automated Vulnerability Repair (AVR) has advanced rapidly across program analysis, machine learning, and Large Language Models (LLMs), but a verifiable, head-to-head comparison of AVR approaches on Python is still missing. Python underpins critical web, data, and machine-learning infrastructure, yet existing Python benchmarks accept a patch on the strength of a proof-of-concept exploit alone, or apply a functional test only on the subset of entries whose upstream project happens to ship one. Both therefore miss functional regressions, in which a patch defeats the exploit but breaks unrelated behavior. We present Vul4Py, a Python AVR benchmark in which every entry carries a paired oracle: an exploit oracle that must fail on the vulnerable revision and pass on the fixed one, together with a project-native pytest functional oracle that must pass on both. Vul4Py comprises 100 real vulnerabilities from 60 open-source projects, spanning 60 distinct CWEs and the years 2017 to 2025, each packaged with a pinned, reproducible per-instance environment. Using Vul4Py, we compare six approaches in three categories: a specialized vulnerability repair tool, directly prompted LLMs, and software engineering agents. The agents dominate: OpenHands repairs 41 of 100 vulnerabilities, against 4 for the strongest directly prompted LLM and 2 for the specialized tool, despite all three sharing the same backbone model. The paired oracle is what makes these counts trustworthy: it rejects 15 of the 119 patches that an exploit-only oracle would accept, and 98 of the 104 patches it admits are manually confirmed to be semantically equivalent to the developer's patches

</details>


### [29] [EduPluginBench: Executable Assurance for AI-Generated Educational Plugins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00739)
*Nizam Kadir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出EduPluginBench：面向受治理软件生态中自动生成插件的可执行基准与分阶段准入方法，用于验证传统编译与功能测试无法覆盖的权限、遥测同意、来源、特权写入、生命周期和失效边界等合规约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成模型生成的组件即使能够编译并通过功能测试，仍可能违反最小权限、数据遥测同意、来源可追溯性、特权操作授权及生命周期约束。作者旨在建立可执行、可审计的评测与准入机制，以识别这类发布阻断型缺陷，并检验其向真实模型生成代码和外部漏洞数据的迁移能力。

Method: 作者构建包含30份规格、1,440个经激活检查的一阶变异体的EduPluginBench，并采用P0-P4分阶段验证流程。实验比较完整流程P0-P4相对于较短流程P0-P2的缺陷召回率；使用120个干净参考实现估计误拒率；在两个当前代码模型的600个冻结原始生成结果上进行迁移测试；另以独立标注的Moodle漏洞/修复版本对评测器进行外部检验。研究还分析了540个生成样本中后处理的有界修复效果。

Result: 在受控变异体实验中，P0-P4相较P0-P2将发布阻断缺陷召回率提高74.7个百分点，按规格聚类的95%置信区间为73.4至75.8个百分点；120个干净参考实现中未观察到拒绝，Wilson 95%上界为3.1%。在600个冻结模型生成样本中，300个可解析，但没有样本通过P0或满足P0-P4一致性，合格率95%上界为0.64%，因此后续保证性估计无法定义。Moodle外部研究最终保留16组漏洞/修复对，冻结的通用PHP检测器未发现任何漏洞版本。此前540个样本的诊断显示，有界后处理修复使P0通过数达到112，但这些样本均不合规；缺陷召回率则从13.4%升至100%。

Conclusion: EduPluginBench在其受控、契约驱动的变异测试环境中能显著提高发布阻断缺陷的识别能力，并显示较低的观测误拒风险。然而，模型原始生成代码与Moodle真实漏洞上的迁移结果均为负，表明受控契约一致性不能被解释为对独立真实缺陷的有效检测证据。论文的主要贡献因而是可复现、具备完整来源与审计记录的准入评测框架，而非已经证明具有广泛真实世界漏洞检测能力的工具。

Abstract: Code-generation models can produce executable components, but compilation and functional tests do not establish compliance with least privilege, telemetry consent, provenance, privileged-write authority, lifecycle constraints, or bounded failure. We introduce EduPluginBench, an executable benchmark and staged admission method for generated plugins in governed software ecosystems. Across 1,440 activation-checked first-order mutants from 30 specifications, P0-P4 increased release-blocking-defect recall by 74.7 percentage points (specification-clustered 95% CI 73.4-75.8) over P0-P2, with no observed rejection among 120 clean references (95% Wilson upper bound 3.1%). A frozen transfer study of 600 unmodified generations from two current coding models found that 300/600 parsed, but none passed P0 or achieved P0-P4 conformance (95% upper bound 0.64%); downstream assurance estimands were undefined. An independently labelled Moodle study retained 16 vulnerable/fixed pairs; the frozen generic PHP detector found no vulnerable revisions. These negative transfer results prevent controlled contract consistency from being read as independent real-defect effectiveness. An earlier 540-generation diagnostic found that post-hoc bounded repair yielded 112 P0 passes, all nonconforming, with recall increasing from 13.4% to 100%. The artifact retains protocols, public-source provenance, raw generations, row-level decisions, audits, analysis code, and reproduction instructions.

</details>


### [30] [Less Is More: Tuning Configurable Systems with Imperfect Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00759)
*Yulong Ye,Miqing Li,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出MFTune，通过先在成本更低但与目标环境相似的不完美保真度环境中进行配置探索，再将优质配置种子迁移到目标完美保真度环境中深度调优，从而提升可配置系统的调参效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高可配置系统通常包含大量待调选项，而目标环境中的性能测量成本很高，导致调参预算有限。论文旨在利用更便宜且具有代表性的近似环境，提高预算利用率并减少目标环境中的测量开销。

Method: 论文建立了可配置系统的保真度概念框架，并提出MFTune。该方法主动探索超过10^4种不完美保真度设置，寻找兼顾环境相似性与探索广度的有用设置；随后利用这些设置产生高质量配置种子，并在完美保真度环境中进行更深入的调优，以结合搜索广度和调优深度。

Result: 在多种真实系统上进行了为期19个月、每周7天每天24小时的实验，并与10种先进调参器比较。MFTune在83.33%的测试案例中表现更优，最高性能提升达到19.34%，同时通常可节省数小时的调参预算。

Conclusion: 在低成本的近似环境中进行部分调参能够实现“少即是多”，显著改善配置调优的预算利用率和最终性能。MFTune证明了多保真度调优在真实可配置系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Configuration tuning is essential for optimizing the performance of highly configurable systems, e.g., throughput or runtime, under a given environment. Yet, this is a challenging process as there can be many options to tune, and configuration measurement is often highly expensive. In this paper, we demonstrate the phenomenon of ``less can be more'': system configuration tuning can be greatly improved with much superior budget utilization by partially tuning under the imperfect-fidelity---an environment that is similar, but cheaper to measure, compared with the concerned perfect-fidelity of environment under which the system should be tuned. We codify a conceptual framework of fidelity for configurable systems, drawing on which allows us to propose MFTune, a tuner that proactively explores in the space of $>10^4$ possible imperfect-fidelity settings to approximate a useful one, which strikes for the wideness of tuning. This creates high-quality seeds for the perfect-fidelity, which in turn ensures the tuning depth. Experiment results against $10$ state-of-the-art tuners, obtained from running diverse real-world systems for $19$ months $24 \times 7$, show that MFTune performs considerably better on $83.33$\% cases with up to $19.34\%$ improvement while achieving hours of budget saving in general.

</details>


### [31] [Turning Interaction History into Execution State: A Runtime Layer for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00808)
*Zehao Wang,Yisen Xu,Chenglin Li,Chao Peng,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Ledger，一种面向长程代码智能体的确定性运行时层，用显式执行状态替代模型对原始历史轨迹的隐式推断，从而提升SWE-bench Verified上的修复成功率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程代码智能体的轨迹包含大量操作与观测，但历史记录未明确标识哪些文件观察仍然有效、哪些文件已被修改、哪些操作已执行。模型因此可能基于过期内容决策，或重复执行已有有效结果的命令，造成性能与效率损失。

Method: Ledger维护在线执行账本，记录已观测内容、已修改内容和已尝试操作，并在每一步设置两个边界机制：inform在模型行动前向提示词追加紧凑的运行时状态视图；govern在命令执行前依据账本检查其有效性，对仍有效的历史结果直接复用，并标记可能冗余的重复操作。该机制不增加额外语言模型调用，可封装于原有智能体之外。

Result: 在500个SWE-bench Verified实例上，Ledger将GPT-5 mini的Pass@1由56.2%提升至64.2%，将MiniMax M2.5的Pass@1由75.8%提升至81.0%；总成本分别降低28.9%和31.8%。接入OpenAI Codex后，Pass@1额外提升3.4个百分点，成本降低24.4%。消融实验显示，govern主要贡献成功率提升，inform主要贡献效率改善，二者组合效果最佳。

Conclusion: 论文表明，长程代码智能体的关键瓶颈并非仅是上下文历史过长，而是缺少对自身执行状态的显式、可验证表示。通过确定性账本管理观测、修改和执行记录，可在不改动基础智能体且不增加模型调用的条件下，同时改善任务解决能力与运行成本。

Abstract: Long-horizon coding agents accumulate hundreds of actions and observations in their trajectories, yet nothing in this record indicates which observations still describe the repository as it currently stands. Before every decision, the model must implicitly infer the execution status from raw history, and when this inference falls short, the agent acts on outdated file contents or re-executes work whose results are still valid. We propose Ledger, a deterministic runtime layer that distills an agent's completed interactions into an explicit execution state: what has been observed, what has been modified, and what has been attempted. Ledger keeps this state in an online execution ledger and applies it at two boundaries of every step. Before the model acts, an inform path appends a compact runtime state view to the prompt; before a proposed command runs, a govern path checks it against the ledger, returning still-valid earlier results in place of re-execution and flagging likely-redundant repetition. The layer adds no language-model calls and wraps an otherwise unmodified agent. Across all 500 SWE-bench Verified instances, Ledger raises Pass@1 from 56.2% to 64.2% with GPT-5 mini and from 75.8% to 81.0% with MiniMax M2.5, while cutting total cost by 28.9% and 31.8%. Attached to OpenAI Codex, it adds 3.4 percentage points of Pass@1 at 24.4% lower cost. Ablations attribute most of the resolution gain to govern and most of the efficiency gain to inform, with their combination performing best. What long-horizon agents lack, we conclude, is not a shorter view of their history but an explicit account of their own execution state.

</details>


### [32] [Structure-Aware Semantic Chunking with Title-Chain Prefixes: A 1600-Query Evaluation and the Measurement Trap in Text-Transform Ablations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00824)
*Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出无需额外LLM调用的三阶段语义分块方案，并在生产知识库检索评测中显著提升MRR@5；同时指出，移除标题链前缀会使人工相关性标注一致性严重下降，从而暴露常见分块消融评测协议的有效性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中的分块边界决定后续检索单元及其语义完整性。固定长度或缺少层级上下文的分块可能破坏主题连续性、降低可检索性，并使短片段缺乏消歧信息。论文旨在以低成本方式改善分块语义结构，并检验标题上下文在检索与评测中的作用。

Method: 方法包括三个仅作用于文档块侧的阶段：先按Markdown标题切分；再对语义连续的相邻片段进行合并；最后为每个块添加由原始标题层级构成的标题链前缀。作者在1,600个分层查询上评测，并对可回答子集进行单独报告；使用双标注者评分及Cohen's kappa衡量一致性。另进行同一候选池下的标题链前缀开关消融，并讨论若干未形成可比运行记录的后续设想及一项前缀权重衰减失败实验。

Result: 完整查询集上的MRR@5从0.374提升至0.463，相对提升23.8%；563个可回答查询上的MRR@5从0.828提升至0.925，相对提升11.7%。主评测中双标注者未加权Cohen's kappa为0.45，基于16,000对评分。关键消融显示，在相同提示词和相同候选池条件下，去除标题链前缀后标注一致性由0.45骤降至0.04，说明前缀提供了标注相关性所需的关键消歧上下文。

Conclusion: 标题层级可作为零额外LLM成本的有效上下文来源，结合标题切分、语义合并和标题链前缀可提升RAG检索质量。更重要的是，若评测时移除检索时实际存在的标题上下文，人工相关性判断本身会失去稳定性，因此此类前缀开关消融不能简单归因于模型或分块效果。论文建议采用检索时按候选块保留并评估前缀的协议。需要注意的是，部分被称为“设计死路”的方案未有可比实验记录，不能据此得出强实证结论；此外，kappa=0.45仅属中等一致性，也限制了结果解释的确定性。

Abstract: Chunking is the first and most consequential step in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG): every downstream retrieval decision inherits the chunk boundaries. We present a three-stage, chunk-side-only semantic chunking pipeline---header-split, semantic merge, and title-chain prefixing---that costs zero additional LLM calls: the title chain reuses the document's own header hierarchy instead of a generated summary. On a 1600-query stratified evaluation over a production Markdown knowledge base, the pipeline improves MRR@5 from 0.374 to 0.463 (+23.8%) on the full set and from 0.828 to 0.925 (+11.7%) on the answerable subset (n=563), with dual-annotator Cohen's kappa 0.45 (unweighted, 16,000 score pairs). We then report what we tried and what failed: three query-side or architecture-level follow-ups are design dead ends (unevaluated---no comparable run artifacts), one measured failure (prefix weight decay), and one protocol-level failure that is the paper's central methodological finding. In a same-pool prefix on/off ablation, dual-annotator agreement collapsed from kappa 0.45 to 0.04 under identical prompts---stripping the title-chain context strips the disambiguation signal annotators need to agree on relevance. This measurement trap invalidates a common evaluation practice in chunking research and motivates retrieval-time per-candidate prefix evaluation, the direction we recommend from all our evidence.

</details>


### [33] [Documentation vs. Code Patterns: What Drives LLM-Based Exception Oracle Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00884)
*Soneya Binta Hossain,Matthew B. Dwyer,Tasfia Tasnim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过干预实验发现，LLM 测试预言生成系统在异常预言预测中的高准确率并非主要依赖 Javadoc 的 @throws 文档，而可能依赖代码、测试和文本中的表面模式或捷径特征。因此，仅以预测准确率评估异常预言生成的可靠性并不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的测试预言生成方法在异常预言任务上报告了较高准确率，但其决策依据尚不透明。论文旨在澄清模型是否真正利用了显式的异常行为说明，尤其是 Javadoc @throws 子句，还是仅从数据中学习了重复出现的结构性或词汇性关联模式。

Method: 研究对三种覆盖分类式与生成式架构、参数规模约为 1.1 亿至 70 亿的 TOG 系统开展大规模干预研究，并在三个真实世界基准上评测，包括两个自动生成测试数据集和一个新构建的开发者手写测试基准。首先，研究移除 Javadoc @throws 子句，比较模型预测准确率变化；随后，采用归因引导的替换消融方法，定位并替换对预测影响较大的输入信号，以分析模型实际依赖的特征。

Result: 移除 @throws 子句后，所有模型的准确率仅发生边际变化，最大下降不足一个百分点，表明结构化异常文档不是异常预言预测的主要证据来源。归因引导消融进一步显示，不同模型可能依赖不同类型的捷径信号：部分模型对少量结构性标记高度敏感，另一些模型则将依赖分散在大量词汇线索上。高准确率因而可能来自统计关联，而非对异常触发条件及其语义的稳健理解。

Conclusion: 异常预言生成系统的评估不应局限于预言类型是否预测正确，还应检验预测是否建立在有意义、与异常触发机制相关的证据之上。论文挑战了“高准确率等价于有效理解异常语义”的隐含假设，并建议未来 TOG 研究引入证据归因、干预鲁棒性和语义基础性评估。

Abstract: LLM-based test oracle generation (TOG) methods report high accuracy on exception oracle generation, but it remains unclear what evidence drives these predictions. In particular, do models use explicit exceptional-behavior documentation such as Javadoc @throws clauses, or do they rely on recurring patterns in tests, code, and documentation?
  We investigate this question through a large-scale intervention-based study of three TOG systems spanning classifier-based and generative architectures and model sizes from roughly 110M to 7B parameters, evaluated on three real-world benchmarks comprising two generated-test datasets and a new benchmark of developer-written tests. We first remove Javadoc @throws clauses and find that accuracy changes only marginally, with the largest drop below one percentage point. This indicates that structured exception documentation is not the primary driver of exception-oracle prediction. We then apply attribution-guided substitution ablations to identify the signals that predictions depend on. The results show that high accuracy can be driven by shortcut signals: some models are highly sensitive to a small number of structural tokens, while others distribute reliance across many lexical cues. These findings challenge the assumption that strong exception-oracle accuracy reflects robust use of exception semantics. Future TOG systems should therefore be evaluated not only by whether they predict the correct oracle type, but also by whether their predictions are grounded in meaningful exception-triggering evidence.

</details>


### [34] [Joint Optimization of Human Headcount and Stochastic AI Resource Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00886)
*Marco Montes de Oca*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文构建了一个在人力规模与生成式AI令牌容量之间进行联合配置的随机优化模型，核心结论是：在使用量方差与均值独立时，令牌使用波动上升会提高人均令牌配置、降低最优雇员数量；若波动随均值同比例增长，则该替代关系可能反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI能提升生产率，但令牌成本具有高波动和右偏特征，传统确定性预算规划无法处理超支风险。同时，审核AI输出带来的认知摩擦会削弱令牌投入的实际产出。因此，组织需要在固定预算下同时优化人员配置和AI资源配置，并控制预算超限概率。

Method: 作者将含认知摩擦的生产函数与机会约束随机预算前沿结合。设每位工程师的令牌使用量独立同分布、非负且右偏，不预设具体分布，仅使用均值、方差和偏度信息。预算机会约束通过中心极限定理近似，并以Cornish--Fisher校正纳入偏度影响，转化为确定性约束；随后利用拉格朗日法求解，得到最优人数的闭式表达、最优人均令牌强度的超越方程，并使用加边海森矩阵验证二阶充分条件。

Result: 在个体令牌使用离散度不随计划均值变化的前提下，更高的个体使用波动会使组织增加人均令牌强度并缩减人员规模。尽管人员与令牌在生产函数中互补，随机预算约束使二者在边际配置上表现为替代品。个体使用偏度仅通过压缩有效预算产生无谓损失，不改变人员与令牌之间的边际替代方向。若离散度随均值同比例扩大，则比较静态结果会反转。

Conclusion: 该研究指出，生成式AI资源规划不能仅依赖平均成本，而应显式管理使用量的方差、偏度及审核摩擦。决定最优配置方向的关键经验问题是：实际令牌使用的波动是否随目标人均配置同步扩大。组织可据此估计使用分布特征，并选择偏向扩充人员或提高人均AI容量的预算策略。

Abstract: Generative AI tools are rapidly becoming standard for coding and other business operations. However, while these tools can drive productivity, their unpredictable, and often significant, costs are putting severe financial strain on organizations. The leadership of such organizations must split a fixed budget between human headcount and generative-AI token capacity, yet standard deterministic planning ignores both the heavy-tailed volatility of token consumption and the cognitive cost of auditing AI-generated output. We model the joint allocation by intersecting a cognitive-friction-adjusted production function with a chance-constrained stochastic budget frontier. Per-engineer token usage is treated as i.i.d., non-negative, and right-skewed; no parametric family is assumed, as only its mean, variance, and skewness enter the analysis.
