The chance constraint is reduced to a deterministic equivalent via the Central Limit Theorem with a Cornish--Fisher skewness correction. Solving the resulting Lagrangian yields a closed-form optimal headcount, a transcendental condition for optimal per-capita token intensity, and a bordered-Hessian second-order condition that holds for any reasonable volatility-to-headcount ratio. Comparative statics show that rising individual token volatility raises optimal per-capita token intensity while contracting headcount: although human labor and tokens are complements in production, the stochastic budget makes them substitutes at the margin. Individual usage skewness acts as a pure deadweight tax that shrinks the budget without altering the substitution dynamics. Both conclusions presuppose that usage dispersion is invariant to the planned mean allocation. In the case where dispersion instead scales proportionally with the mean, the substitution reverses, so which regime applies is a sharp, empirically testable question.

</details>


### [35] [RefactorAssist: Agentic Refinement for Reliable Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00924)
*Jonathan Cordeiro,Shayan Noei,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出RefactorAssist智能体，用于修复大语言模型生成的代码重构中导致测试失败的错误。该方法结合静态修复、单元测试日志、错误解释、项目上下文检索和代码差异分析，在最佳配置下将累计测试通过率提升至94.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能够自动生成重构代码，但其输出常因上下文误解、重命名不一致、引入额外功能、代码不完整或类型处理错误等问题改变原有行为，导致单元测试失败。这一可靠性问题阻碍了LLM重构工具在实际开发流程中的应用，因此需要一种能够依据项目证据迭代修复重构结果的方法。

Method: 研究使用10个开源Java项目及其原生测试套件，人工分析LLM生成重构失败的根因，并据此设计RefactorAssist。该系统首先通过非LLM静态修复处理缺失导入、括号不平衡和编译错误；随后针对剩余失败，利用单元测试日志、错误说明、项目上下文检索结果以及重构前后的代码差异，驱动智能体进行迭代式修复和验证。

Result: 测试失败的主要原因包括：上下文误解或幻觉占24.3%，错误或不一致的重命名占15.3%，新增功能或变量占13.7%，代码不完整占11.3%，语法与结构错误占9.7%，遗漏边界情况占9%，类型处理不当占8.7%，以及变量超出作用域占8%。对于静态修复后仍失败的样本，RefactorAssist最高可修复70.8%；在最佳配置下，累计测试通过率达到94.2%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，单纯依赖LLM生成重构代码难以保证行为等价性，而将静态检查与测试驱动、上下文感知的智能体修复结合，能够显著提高重构结果的功能正确性。该方案为将LLM辅助重构更可靠地集成到开发者工作流提供了实证支持，但其结论目前主要基于Java项目和既有测试覆盖，跨语言泛化能力及未被测试覆盖行为的可靠性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Code refactoring aims to enhance the internal structure of source code without affecting its functional behavior. The recent advancements of Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated potential for automating software engineering tasks, such as code refactoring. However, the refactorings produced by LLMs often introduce subtle errors, leading to functional behavior changes and failed unit tests, which limit their practical adoption. To address the limitations of LLM-generated refactorings, we analyze the root causes of their failures and develop the RefactorAssist agent to improve the functional correctness of LLM-generated refactorings. To this end, we use 10 open-source Java projects with their native test suites and manually evaluate why LLM-generated refactorings fail unit tests. We then design an agentic approach that leverages unit-test logs, error explanations, project context retrieval, and code diffs to guide the iterative refactoring. Our findings show that the main reasons for failure are context misunderstanding/hallucination (24.3%), incorrect or inconsistent renaming (15.3%), adding new functionality or variables (13.7%), code incompleteness (11.3%), syntax and structural errors (9.7%), edge cases not handled (9%), improper type handling (8.7%), and variables outside scope (8%). To make our approach cost-effective, RefactorAssist first applies a static repair step for missing imports, unbalanced brackets, and compilation errors without LLMs. For remaining failures, RefactorAssist incorporates error logs and code diffs, achieving up to a 70.8% repair rate on the remaining failures and a 94.2% cumulative pass rate under the best-performing configuration. These results indicate that static checks and test-guided, context-aware agentic repair can increase the reliability of LLM-generated refactorings, bringing them closer to practical integration within developer workflows.

</details>


### [36] [Claim Plane: Reliability Gains and the Limits of Selective Concurrency for Parallel Coding Agents: A 30-Pair, Three-Seed Confirmatory Study of Deterministic Pre-Write Admission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00947)
*Maxim Nikolaev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究评估了 Claim Plane 这一针对并行编码代理集成失败的“写入前准入”机制。静态准入显著提高任务通过率和集成成功率，但代价是几乎将全部任务串行化；动态准入选择性更强，却因声明范围不足而频繁拒绝执行。该研究证明了可靠性收益，但尚未证明实际并行加速收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行编码代理分别产生的修改可能在局部有效，却在合并后发生冲突或破坏集成。Claim Plane 试图通过对版本化变更意图进行确定性的预写入审查，在代码写入前识别并协调潜在冲突，从而提升并行开发的集成可靠性。

Method: 研究在30个冻结的 CooperBench 特征对上进行验证，其中15个标注为冲突样本、15个为干净样本。实验采用3个编码器随机种子和4种协调策略，共完成360次执行。DeepSeek V4 Pro 预先生成60份特征级规划声明，并在所有策略和种子间保持冻结；DeepSeek V4 Flash 负责实际编码。研究比较了无约束并行、静态 Claim Plane 准入及动态准入，并采用按任务簇配对的自助法估计差异及95%置信区间。

Result: 无约束并行的特征对通过率为23.3%，静态 Claim Plane 将其提高至50.0%，提升26.7个百分点，95%自助法置信区间为9.6至60.0个百分点；集成成功率由65.6%升至96.7%。在冲突标注样本中，通过率从6.7%升至60.0%。但静态策略使96.7%的执行被串行化，且干净样本中也有93.3%被串行化，说明可靠性提升主要来自趋近串行执行。动态策略对冲突样本串行化66.7%，对干净样本仅串行化13.3%，选择性更好；但90次执行中有46次因未声明范围而保守拒绝，导致通过率降至22.2%。其中45次拒绝涉及声明中已包含的文件，表明主要问题是文件内区域覆盖不足和缺乏声明修订机制，而非完全未知的文件访问。

Conclusion: 写入前准入能够有效提升并行编码代理的集成可靠性，尤其能缓解冲突任务的失败；然而，静态保守策略以大规模串行化换取可靠性，不能据此证明并行场景具有有效的墙钟时间加速。动态策略显示出更高的选择性潜力，但需要改进变更区域声明的粒度、覆盖率以及声明更新机制，才能减少误拒绝并同时保持可靠性与并行效率。

Abstract: Parallel coding agents can produce locally valid changes that fail when combined. Claim Plane addresses this failure mode as deterministic pre-write admission over versioned change intents. This paper reports a confirmatory study on 30 frozen CooperBench feature pairs, balanced between 15 conflict and 15 clean labels, with three coder seeds, four coordination arms, and 360 completed executions. DeepSeek V4 Pro generated 60 feature-level planner declarations once; the declarations were frozen across all arms and coder seeds, while DeepSeek V4 Flash performed the coding work. Static Claim Plane raised pair pass from 23.3% under unconstrained parallel execution to 50.0%, a paired task-cluster difference of +26.7 percentage points (95% bootstrap CI 9.6 to 60.0), and raised integration success from 65.6% to 96.7%. On conflict-labeled pairs, pair pass rose from 6.7% to 60.0%. However, static admission serialized 96.7% of executions, including 93.3% of clean cases, and therefore recovered reliability largely by collapsing toward serial execution. Dynamic admission was more selective, serializing 66.7% of conflict cases and 13.3% of clean cases, but 46 of 90 executions failed closed on undeclared scope, reducing pair pass to 22.2%. Forty-five of those 46 blocks targeted files already present in the frozen declarations, indicating region undercoverage and insufficient amendment handling rather than wholly unknown files. The results support pre-write admission as a reliability mechanism, but they do not establish useful wall-clock parallel speedup: provider calls were physically sequential, and the conservative policy largely serialized the workload. The complete study artifacts, hashes, and clustered bootstrap analysis are publicly released for reproduction.

</details>


### [37] [AgenTag: Attribution of AI Coding Agents from Behavioral Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00966)
*Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出AgenTag，用于开放世界场景下识别拉取请求由何种AI编程代理或人类作者生成。实验表明，多模态归因具有较高准确性，且最关键的证据来自PR描述与提交信息中的沟通风格，而非代码差异本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程代理常以开发者账户提交PR，导致实际作者身份难以辨别。这会影响代码库治理、AI辅助开发实证研究及代理影响评估。现有研究主要假设候选代理集合已知，尚未充分解决未知新代理出现时的开放集归因问题。

Method: 作者在包含33,580个AI代理PR和6,618个人类PR的AIDev数据集上评估AgenTag。该框架融合文本、行为和代码三类特征，并比较传统监督分类与监督式对比学习；后者用于开放集未知代理检测，以及对新代理进行少样本注册。研究还通过移除显式自我披露标记，并采用多种代码表示，检验归因信号的来源与稳健性。

Result: AgenTag在已知代理识别上达到加权F1为0.96、宏平均F1为0.84；在人类与AI生成PR二分类上取得0.89的平衡F1；对未见代理的检测达到0.84 AUC。消融分析显示，PR描述和提交消息几乎包含全部有效归因信号，代码diff在不同表示方式下贡献均有限。即使去除显式代理标记，模型仍能保留有效识别能力，说明其主要利用潜在的写作和沟通风格特征。

Conclusion: 该研究证明开放世界AI编程代理归因在实践中可行，并显示监督式对比学习适合处理未知代理和少样本扩展。其核心发现是：代理之间的差异主要体现于变更说明与提交沟通方式，而非生成代码的表面特征。该结论也提示实际部署需权衡归因精度与可获得的文本元数据，并关注文本模板、代理版本变化和跨项目泛化可能带来的性能漂移。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly author pull requests (PRs), often under developers' own accounts, obscuring who actually produced a change. Reliable attribution is important for repository governance, empirical studies of AI-assisted software development, and measuring the impact of AI coding agents. Existing work focuses on closed-set identification of known agents, leaving the practical limits of open-world AI coding agent attribution largely unexplored. In this paper, we present AgenTag, a multimodal framework for open-world AI coding agent attribution, evaluated on AIDev, comprising 33,580 PRs from five AI coding agents and 6,618 human-authored PRs. We represent each PR using textual, behavioral, and code-based modalities, and compare conventional classification with supervised contrastive learning for open-set recognition and few-shot enrollment of previously unseen agents. AgenTag identifies authoring agents with a weighted F1 of 0.96 (macro F1 of 0.84), distinguishes AI- from human-authored PRs with a balanced F1 of 0.89, and detects previously unseen agents with an AUC of 0.84. We further show that PR descriptions and commit messages provide nearly all of the attribution signal, whereas code diffs contribute little across multiple representations, indicating that coding agents are distinguished primarily by how they communicate changes rather than by the code they generate. Moreover, these behavioral fingerprints persist after removing explicit self-disclosed markers, demonstrating that attribution relies largely on latent stylistic characteristics. These findings show that reliable attribution of AI coding agents is feasible and clarify the practical trade-offs between attribution accuracy and the information required to achieve it.

</details>


### [38] [Registry Descriptions Go Stale Unevenly: An 89-Day Measurement of Model Context Protocol Drift, and Why Drift-Ranked Re-Auditing Under-Covers It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00997)
*Gautam Bharti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文基于88.6天内官方MCP注册表的120次观测，研究服务器描述文本的“保质期”与漂移规律。核心发现是：仅按历史漂移程度优先复审高风险服务器，无法有效保持描述级安全审计结论的时效性；更合适的机制是描述内容哈希变更时立即复验，并辅以定期全量扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP生态安全研究通常只在单一时点审计注册表，默认描述信息在后续仍然有效。论文指出，描述级发现是否仍可适用，首先取决于被审计描述是否保持不变。因而作者关注的不是安全漏洞结论本身是否持续成立，而是注册表描述文本随时间变化的频率、分布及其对复审策略的影响。

Method: 作者重建了官方MCP注册表的纵向面板数据，共120个时间点、覆盖19,099个不同服务器；期间注册表规模从3,510增长到18,966。研究比较了基于既往漂移历史的排序复审策略与描述符整体漂移情况，采用留出时间窗口评估其对未来描述变更服务器的捕获能力；同时统计持续观测服务器的变更比例、变更事件集中度、新到达服务器贡献以及30天内描述变更率，并将朴素独立复利估计作为重尾分布诊断。

Result: 在前5%的复审预算下，按既往描述漂移排序仅能捕获留出窗口中约20%的既有描述变更服务器；相较之下，对描述符整体漂移可捕获约27%，而对所有描述变更者仅覆盖约10%。该排序并非无效，其仍提供约4倍提升，但覆盖率受两项结构性因素限制：仅8.6%的服务器曾改写描述，而24.8%的服务器发生过描述符漂移；且约一半描述变更发生于新加入服务器，历史排序无法覆盖。对至少经历10个观测区间的服务器，约75%从未变更；最活跃的5%贡献61%的全部变更事件；一个队列中仅11.9%的描述符在30天内变化。朴素复利模型则预测30天35.8%、89天73%，显著高估真实变化，反映出明显的重尾和异质性。

Conclusion: 描述级审计的维护不能主要依赖“优先复审最常漂移的服务器”。历史漂移排序可作为有限补充，但天然无法处理新到达服务器，且描述变更本身较稀疏。推荐的控制方案是内容绑定式复验：当描述哈希发生变化时立即重新验证，并按合适周期执行全注册表扫描。该结论定位为描述级扫描与审计卫生实践，不应被解释为运行时信任或安全状态信号。

Abstract: Security studies of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) ecosystem share a design: each audits a registry at a single point in time. None reports how long the registry descriptions those audits judged stay current - a necessary condition for any description-level finding to still apply, though not a sufficient one: we measure the shelf-life of the audited text, not the validity of a security finding itself (Sec. 7.1). We reconstruct 120 observations of the official MCP registry over 88.6 days, covering 19,099 distinct servers as it grew from 3,510 to 18,966. Our central result is a policy one: you cannot keep description-level findings current by re-auditing the servers that drift most. At a top-5% re-audit budget, ranking by prior drift catches only ~20% of the previously-seen servers whose description changes in a held-out window - versus ~27% for descriptor drift overall - and only ~10% of all description changers. The limit is not unpredictability: the ranking still buys ~4x lift. It is that the description surface is sparse - 8.6% of servers ever rewrite one, against 24.8% for descriptors - and that roughly half of all description changes land on new arrivals a history ranking cannot reach, so the same lift buys far less coverage. The control that fits is content-binding - revalidate the moment a description's hash moves - plus a sized periodic full-catalog sweep; a drift-history ranking is at best a partial, blind-to-new-arrivals control. This is scanner hygiene for a description-level auditor, not a runtime trust signal. Of servers observed across at least ten intervals, three-quarters never change, the most active 5% generate 61% of all change events, and only 11.9% of a cohort's descriptors change within 30 days; naive compounding predicts 35.8% at 30 days (73% at 89), a heavy-tail overestimate we use only as a diagnostic. We release the panel, figure generator, and analysis code.

</details>


### [39] [What Could the Agent See at 19:05? Generating Temporal Enterprise Scenarios from Real Research and Replaying Them to Evaluate Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01042)
*Tezan Sahu,Himani Arora*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向企业AI智能体的时间感知评测系统，可在任意历史时刻重建多应用企业环境，并以快速、可复现的方式评估智能体答案是否符合当时的数据状态与访问权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线评测通常基于单一静态数据快照，往往对应任务流程结束时的最终状态。这无法覆盖流程中较早时刻的真实问题，因为不同时间点具有不同的数据、权限可见性和正确答案。为每个时刻单独部署完整租户成本过高；单一快照还可能泄露未来状态，并难以表达真实企业工作中跨应用、按时间顺序演化的数据交互。

Method: 系统首先基于真实研究材料生成由角色驱动、随时间演化的多应用企业世界。随后，通过模式推断得到时间描述，并结合确定性逻辑与大语言模型，重建每条记录在历史时刻的状态。针对有限的可查询时间点，系统预先计算所有重建结果并保存为紧凑的差分缓存；评测时仅进行缓存查找，而不再调用模型。该架构同时覆盖企业世界生成流程与任意时刻的环境回放流程，并支持接入不同的智能体进行评测。

Result: 该方法能够在指定历史时刻复现与当时数据和权限相一致的企业环境，避免以最终快照评测早期任务时产生的未来信息泄露。预计算差分缓存使评测过程成为快速、确定性的查询操作，提升了可复现性，并降低了逐时刻重新部署完整企业环境的成本。论文还报告了使用该系统评估企业智能体的初步经验。

Conclusion: 该工作将企业智能体评测从静态快照扩展为时间感知的动态环境回放。其核心贡献是通过历史状态重建和差分缓存，在不为每个时间点重复配置完整租户的前提下，支持多应用、权限相关且可复现的历史情境评测。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents act across many apps whose data changes continuously, so an answer is correct only relative to what data existed and who could see it at the moment it was asked. Offline evaluation today grades against a single static snapshot, effectively the end of the episode. So, it can only evaluate one situation, the final one, even though every earlier moment of the episode is a different situation that invites its own realistic questions with its own correct answers. Recreating each of those moments as a separate snapshot would mean re-provisioning a whole tenant per instant, which is prohibitively costly; and even a single snapshot leaks future state hidden inside records and cannot represent the multi-app, time-ordered way real work happens. Our system closes two gaps at once: it generates a realistic, persona-driven, temporally-evolving enterprise world from real research, and replays that world at any chosen moment to evaluate any pluggable agent. A schema-inferred temporal description drives a deterministic-plus-LLM rebuild of each record's past state; because the queryable moments are finite, all rebuilds are precomputed into a compact difference cache, making evaluation a fast, reproducible lookup with no model in the path. We describe the design, an architecture spanning both flows, and early experience evaluating enterprise agents.

</details>


### [40] [MADE: Belief-Driven Dual-Agent Coordination for Autonomous Model Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01189)
*Yicheng Liu,Bolin Zhang,Weiran Liu,Yakun Zhang,Yangqin Jiang,Zhiying Tu,Dianhui Chu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出MADE（Model Automated Deployment Engine），通过双智能体协作自动将开源模型资源转化为可调用API，并发布M2ABench评测基准。MADE在122个真实模型上的部署成功率为68.85%，显著优于SWE-agent和OpenHands。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量开源AI模型以异构研究产物形式发布，实际部署为稳定、可调用的API通常需要处理环境配置、依赖安装、代码修复、服务启动和接口验证等多阶段任务，人工成本高。现有智能体虽具备通用能力，但对这种长链路、反馈驱动的模型部署流程支持不足。

Method: MADE采用双智能体协调机制：针对给定模型资源，系统迭代生成部署所需工件，如环境配置、依赖、启动脚本和服务接口；执行并验证这些工件；依据运行反馈更新“部署信念”；若发现下游步骤失败，则回溯并修正无效的上游工件，直至模型成功以API形式提供服务。论文同时构建M2ABench，包含122个真实世界模型及标准化测试用例，用于评估从模型资源到可调用API的端到端部署能力。

Result: 在M2ABench上，MADE取得68.85%的部署成功率，分别比SWE-agent和OpenHands高13.93个和44.26个百分点。该结果表明，面向部署任务的双智能体协作、执行反馈利用与上游工件回溯机制，能够有效提升复杂模型自动部署的完成率。

Conclusion: 论文将自动模型部署定义为一项独立且具有挑战性的长时程智能体任务，并以MADE和M2ABench提供了系统化解决方案与评测基础。其核心贡献在于将工件构建、执行验证、状态信念更新和错误回溯整合为闭环流程，从而缩小开源模型资源与实际工具可用性之间的差距。

Abstract: LLM-based agents now have strong general capabilities. However, they still struggle with domain-specific tasks, motivating the integration of external tools to broaden their capabilities. The open-source community offers a vast array of AI models typically released as heterogeneous research artifacts, whereas transforming them into ready-to-call APIs is costly and labor-intensive. Automated model deployment is therefore essential for bridging the gap between model resources and tool usability, yet it remains a long-horizon, multi-stage task that has not been sufficiently explored. To tackle this challenge, we introduce Model Automated Deployment Engine (MADE), a dual-agent coordination system. Specifically, given a model resource, MADE iteratively constructs and validates the deployment artifacts, updates its deployment belief based on execution feedback, and revisits invalid upstream artifacts until the model is successfully served as a ready-to-call API that can then be used by other agents. We further introduce M2ABench, a benchmark for the task of transforming Models to ready-to-call APIs. M2ABench comprises 122 real-world models with standardized test cases for evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that MADE achieves a deployment success rate of 68.85%, outperforming SWE-agent and OpenHands by 13.93 and 44.26 percentage points, respectively. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/HITDiSC/MADE.

</details>


### [41] [Doc2CI: A Multi-Service Study of CI Configuration Generation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01451)
*Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了大语言模型根据自然语言生成持续集成（CI）YAML配置的能力，发现模型很少能精确复现文档示例，但多数可生成可解析且部分符合服务Schema的配置；基于Schema的无训练修复可将有效性显著提升至94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI配置通常以YAML编写，易出错且维护成本高。尽管LLM已广泛用于软件工程，其跨CI服务、跨模型家族生成可靠CI配置的能力尚不明确，因此需要统一基准和结构化评估来衡量其实际可用性。

Method: 作者构建DOC2CI基准，包含从4种CI服务官方文档收集的3,363个“自然语言描述—YAML配置”样本。实验评估14个参数量为7B至34B的开放权重模型，以及GPT-4o和GPT-4.1，共生成超过53,000份配置。评估指标包括与参考配置的一致性及服务Schema有效性；此外，作者人工分析385份配置以建立失败分类体系，并分析模型输出分歧原因。最后比较了无训练Schema引导修复与微调两类改进方法。

Result: 所有模型的精确参考复现率均不超过3.1%。约97%的输出能够被解析为YAML，但仅71%满足对应CI服务的Schema。更大模型通常具有更高的结构有效性，但代码专用模型相对同等规模通用模型没有稳定优势。模型间差异主要来自输出完整度：面对相同请求，部分模型只输出目标片段，另一些则生成完整工作流。无训练的Schema引导修复将Schema有效率提升至94%；微调可提高与文档参考答案的相似度，但会降低独立生成时的有效性。

Conclusion: CI配置生成中，参考答案相似度与Schema有效性是不同且可能相互冲突的优化目标。仅依赖文本匹配不足以评估LLM生成CI配置的质量；未来应采用Schema感知的评估方法，并结合自动修复等工具提升生成配置在实际CI服务中的可执行性。

Abstract: Adopting Continuous Integration (CI) often requires writing YAML configurations that are error-prone and challenging to maintain. Despite increasing LLM use in software engineering, their ability to generate CI configurations from natural language across services and model families remains unclear. This paper presents a large empirical study on using LLMs to generate CI configurations. We introduce DOC2CI, a benchmark of 3,363 description-to-YAML pairs collected from the official documentation of four CI services, and evaluate 14 open-weight models from 7B-34B parameters together with GPT-4o and GPT-4.1, producing over 53,000 configurations. We assess both reference alignment and schema validity to determine whether the generated configurations are structurally valid. We further develop a failure taxonomy from a manual analysis of 385 configurations and examine why LLMs disagree. Across models and services, exact reference reproduction never exceeds 3.1%, and while 97% of outputs parse as YAML, only 71% satisfy service schemas. Larger models improve structural validity, but code specialization provides no consistent advantage over comparable general models. Model differences are driven largely by output completeness: for the same request, some models generate the expected fragment while others produce a full workflow. Finally, a training-free schema-guided repair method improves schema validity to 94%, while fine-tuning improves similarity to documentation but reduces standalone validity. This suggests that similarity and validity are distinct objectives for CI generation and motivate schema-aware evaluation and tooling for LLM-based configuration generation.

</details>


### [42] [An Empirical Investigation of Soft Skills and Corresponding Challenges in International Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01469)
*Sajid Ibrahim Hashmi,Jouni Markkula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨国际软件工程高等教育中的学生小组作业，重点识别团队协作所需的软技能及其面临的挑战，并为教师设计更有效的小组学习活动提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发不仅依赖技术能力，也高度依赖团队合作等软技能。软件工程教育中的小组作业是学生练习协作、沟通和职业实践能力的重要场景，因此需要理解学生在国际化协作环境中的技能需求与困难。

Method: 研究通过开展两个关于小组作业的焦点小组访谈，收集学生协作经验相关的实证材料，并围绕国际软件工程教育中的团队合作软技能与实践挑战进行归纳分析。

Result: 研究发现，学生在课程作业和练习中需要学习并实践多项团队协作软技能，同时国际化小组工作会带来额外挑战。摘要未具体列出技能类别、挑战细节、样本规模或分析方法。

Conclusion: 高校教师和教学设计人员应将团队软技能培养纳入软件工程课程的小组作业设计，并针对国际化协作中的困难提供明确的组织、指导与支持机制，以提升学生进入软件行业前的职业准备度。

Abstract: Joint software development work requires the application of technical skills and professional practices. Soft skills, such as teamwork, remain essential to succeeding in a professional software development environment. The phenomenon is reflected by group work in SE (software engineering) education, where students can learn and practice teamwork-related soft skills. Therefore, SE education requires students to practice those skills so that they can actively embark on their professional careers in the software industry. In this research study, empirical findings from two focus groups we conducted on group work are presented, with emphasis on the soft skills necessary for teamwork in international software engineering education, specifically student collaboration in higher education. There are several soft skills students should learn as they practice teamwork in their course-related assignments and exercises, as well as the challenges that come with it in international settings. The findings serve as a guideline to university teachers and pedagogy experts to plan student group work more effectively.

</details>


### [43] [Deep Agentic Search for Repository-Level Code Question Answering: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01507)
*Amirkia Rafiei Oskooei,Bora Ilci,Alperen Kayim,Mehmet Egemen Uzun,Berat Can,Kaan Emre Kara,Ozan Orhan,Mehmet S. Aktas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文比较了代码智能体中的语义检索与深度智能体搜索两种代码定位方案。基于SWE-QA评测，语义检索的正确率为65.2%，显著高于深度智能体搜索的46.2%，且每个正确答案的成本不足后者一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体需要在大型仓库中定位相关代码。深度智能体搜索通过将探索任务交给隔离上下文中的子智能体，旨在减少主智能体的上下文污染，但该设计是否真正提升问答质量与成本效率，缺乏系统实证。

Method: 作者在SWE-QA仓库级代码问答基准上，对预先构建向量索引的语义检索方法与“规划器—子智能体”式深度智能体搜索进行对比；随后对所有失败运行进行人工编码，建立失败模式分类体系，以解释性能差异。

Result: 语义检索答对65.2%的问题，深度智能体搜索答对46.2%；同时，语义检索获得每个正确答案的成本低于后者的一半。失败分析表明，深度智能体搜索并未消除失败，而是新增了规划器与子智能体交接导致的失败；该类问题占其失败案例的41.8%，且常以表面流畅、自信但错误的答案形式出现。

Conclusion: 对于可被预先索引的仓库中的只读代码问答任务，语义检索在准确性和成本上均优于深度智能体搜索。隔离子智能体虽可缓解上下文污染，但其信息交接损失构成显著代价；代码智能体设计应审慎权衡上下文保护与检索完整性。

Abstract: Code agents spend much of their effort simply locating the right code inside a repository. Two approaches dominate current practice. In Semantic Search, the agent retrieves code blocks from a vector index built from the repository in advance. In Deep Agentic Search (also known as grep-search by subagent), a planning agent delegates the exploration to a separate subagent that works in an isolated context window and returns only a condensed result. The second design, which is considered good context engineering practice, exists to protect the main agent from context pollution (also known as context rot), the loss of accuracy that occurs as unrelated material accumulates in the context window. Recent code agents (such as Claude Code, Codex, Antigravity, etc) have adopted it quickly, but there is little evidence on whether it produces better answers. We compare the two approaches on SWE-QA, a benchmark for repository-level code question answering. Semantic search answered 65.2% of questions correctly against 46.2% for deep agentic search, and it produced each correct answer at less than half the cost. To explain the gap, we then coded every failed run into a taxonomy of failure modes. The taxonomy shows that deep agentic search did not remove failures but introduced a new class of them: the single largest share of its failures, 41.8%, occurred at the hand-off between the planner and its sub-agent, and these were usually silent, ending in a fluent and confident answer that was wrong. Deep agentic search addresses a real problem and is now the preferred design in many code agents. However, our results show that the protection it offers may not be free, and that for read-only questions over a repository that can be indexed, retrieval was the stronger and cheaper option.

</details>


### [44] [How Well Do LLMs Generate Taxonomies in the SE Domain? A Multi-perspective Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01592)
*Sota Nakashima,Yuta Ishimoto,Masanari Kondo,Tao Xiao,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了自动化分类体系生成方法在软件工程（SE）工件上的适用性，发现TnT-LLM质量较高但成本和耗时显著更大，CLIMB则更快、更便宜但面对需要技术推理的任务时质量较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程研究中的分类体系能够统一组织异构观察结果，但人工构建依赖领域专家且耗费大量人力。尽管大语言模型已被用于其他领域的自动分类体系生成，现有方法能否处理技术复杂、语义专业的软件工程工件尚不明确，因此需要系统性的实证验证。

Method: 研究者收集了7篇具有公开工件及人工定义分类体系的软件工程论文，使用TnT-LLM和CLIMB两种自动化方法，并结合5个先进大语言模型开展实验。评估框架从四个维度考察方法表现：分类体系质量、与人类专家分类体系的一致性、独立标注下的可靠性，以及运行效率与成本。

Result: TnT-LLM生成的分类体系质量较高，与人工定义体系具有可比性，但通常会产生过于复杂的分类结构，并带来显著更高的推理成本和运行时间。CLIMB的运行速度快15至40倍、成本低8至49倍，但在不能仅依赖表层相似性、而需要深入技术推理的场景中，其质量评分较低。

Conclusion: 自动分类体系生成方法可用于软件工程研究，但不应直接替代人工专家。实践中应先在目标数据的一个子集上评估分类问题复杂度、生成结构及成本：若强调高质量和与专家体系的一致性，可考虑TnT-LLM；若资源受限且任务主要依赖表层语义相似性，则CLIMB更具效率优势。该研究为理解和选择SE领域自动分类体系生成工具提供了初步实证依据。

Abstract: Taxonomies provide a shared conceptual framework for organizing heterogeneous observations in software engineering (SE) research. Manually constructing such taxonomies is labor-intensive and requires annotators with expertise in the SE domain. While advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have led to the emergence of automated taxonomy generation methods outside the SE domain, their applicability to technically complex SE artifacts remains unclear. In this experience paper, we present the first comprehensive empirical evaluation of how state-of-the-art automated methods perform on SE artifacts through a multi-perspective evaluation framework, including taxonomy quality, alignment with taxonomies defined by human experts, reliability under independent annotation, and efficiency. To support this evaluation, we systematically collect seven SE papers with publicly available artifacts and human-defined taxonomies, and conduct experiments using two automated methods (TnT-LLM and CLIMB) with five state-of-the-art LLMs. Our evaluation reveals a clear trade-off: TnT-LLM constructs high-quality taxonomies comparable to human-defined ones but incurs substantially higher cost and runtime and tends to generate overly complex taxonomies, whereas CLIMB is 15--40$\times$ faster and 8--49$\times$ cheaper but tends to score lower on quality when technical inference beyond surface-level similarity is required. These findings suggest that TnT-LLM and CLIMB can be used in practical situations in the SE domain, while researchers should first assess the complexity of the generated taxonomies and their cost using a subset of the target data to decide whether to use automated methods or human experts. Our work represents a first step toward a systematic understanding of automated taxonomy generation in SE, offering actionable insights for future research and practice.

</details>


### [45] [AI-assisted Script Management for Requirements Elicitation Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01640)
*Anmol Singhal,Paulo Carvalho,Travis Breaux*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估了一套AI辅助需求获取工作流，将基于业务目标的访谈脚本生成、实时主题覆盖跟踪和按需追问生成整合到访谈过程中。相较于仅接受培训的人工访谈，AI辅助组生成的脚本质量更高、对脚本问题的覆盖更充分、每个主题的追问更多，并产出更细化的目标模型，但其覆盖的主题总数较少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求获取访谈要求访谈者同时管理主题覆盖、倾听利益相关者反馈并进行适应性追问。既有研究多聚焦脚本生成或追问生成等单项AI能力，尚不清楚整合式AI支持如何改变访谈过程及最终需求工件。尤其是实时脚本管理，即帮助访谈者掌握主题覆盖并决定何时深入追问，仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 研究设计了一种AI辅助需求获取流程：首先依据业务目标和理论指导生成访谈脚本；随后在实时访谈中提供主题覆盖追踪与按需追问问题生成。作者采用组间准实验，将“无培训、使用AI辅助”的参与者与“接受培训、不使用AI”的参与者比较。评估指标包括依据需求获取最佳实践制定的脚本评分量表、访谈主题覆盖、脚本问题覆盖率、各主题追问数量、目标模型细化程度，以及参与者对功能实用性的主观评价。

Result: AI生成脚本的平均评分高于仅培训组脚本，分别为92.8分和74.8分（满分100）。AI辅助访谈覆盖的主题数较少，分别为9.6和14.5；但AI辅助组覆盖了更多脚本中的问题，比例为86%对69%；每个主题的平均追问次数更多，为3.43对1.15；并形成更细化的目标模型，最底层目标占比分别为0.653和0.598。参与者普遍认为脚本管理有用，其中主题追踪被认为是最有用的功能，86%的参与者表示认同。

Conclusion: 整合式AI支持并非单纯提升传统访谈的所有指标，而是使访谈呈现出不同的推进轨迹：减少横向主题扩展，增强对既定主题的脚本执行和纵向探究，从而生成更细化的需求目标模型。研究表明，AI辅助工作流可作为需求获取的过程支架；但主题数下降也提示后续研究需进一步考察访谈广度与深度之间的权衡，以及不同应用场景下的适用性。

Abstract: Requirements elicitation interviews require interviewers to balance topic coverage, active listening, and adaptive probing while responding to stakeholders in real time. Although prior work has explored AI support for isolated interviewing tasks, such as script generation and follow-up question generation, little is known about how integrated support affects the interview and what requirements artifacts emerge. Furthermore, script management---which helps the interviewer track topic coverage in real time and decide when to probe further---remains underexplored. This paper presents an AI-assisted elicitation workflow that combines theory-guided script generation grounded in business goals with live support for topic coverage tracking and on-demand follow-up question generation. We evaluate the workflow in a between-subjects quasi-experimental study comparing a no-training, AI-assisted condition with a training, AI-unassisted condition. Based on a rubric derived from elicitation best practices, the AI-generated scripts score higher than training-only scripts (92.8 vs. 74.8 out of 100). AI-assisted interviews cover fewer topics (9.6 vs. 14.5), cover more scripted questions (86% vs. 69%), ask more follow-ups per topic (3.43 vs. 1.15), and produce more refined goal models (lowest-level goal fraction 0.653 vs. 0.598). Participants find script management useful, rating topic tracking as the most useful workflow feature (86% agreement). Collectively, these results show that the AI-assisted condition is associated with a different interview trajectory and different elicited requirements than a training-only condition, positioning AI-assisted workflows as elicitation scaffolds for future studies.

</details>


### [46] [Coding Agents as Test-Suite Auditors: Finding What Official Suites Miss While Approaching What They Catch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01715)
*Shuyang Xie,Shuxiao Xie,Feng Zhu,Yanli Ji,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向代码大模型评测的数据质量审计框架：利用现成编码智能体自动构造对抗测试，并通过独立解答、暴力求解与输入合法性验证组成认证链，识别在线评测中“被官方测试接受但实际有缺陷”的提交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线评测判题结果通常被当作代码模型训练和评测的真实标签，但已有审计表明官方测试集可能漏检逻辑错误。现有工作主要指出问题，缺少可规模化、无需依赖官方判题器的修复与验证方案；对于没有公开官方测试集的题目，该问题尤其突出。

Method: 方法包含两部分。第一，使用现成编码智能体作为测试集审计器，为每道题生成能够暴露缺陷提交的对抗测试；若不存在官方测试集，则直接提供替代测试集。第二，对智能体标记为错误的已通过提交进行认证：多个独立编写且已接受的参考解对每个测试输入的输出达成一致；出现分歧时用暴力解确定正确输出；再由每题验证器确认失败输入满足题目约束。实验在AtCoder已通过提交上审计，并比较五个智能体在官方测试存在与不存在两类场景下的表现。

Result: 单个智能体在20,375份经审计的AtCoder已接受提交中发现589份经验证的实际错误提交；对五个智能体结果取并集后，至少发现906份此类提交。对于官方测试本身能够捕获的逻辑错误，五个智能体各自的覆盖率与官方测试集相差不超过1.7个百分点。在没有公开官方测试集的后截止日期Codeforces题目上，该测试生成方法在所有测试输入预算下均优于被复现和测试的五个基线。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在线评测的接受结果不应被无条件视为可靠真值。基于编码智能体的对抗测试生成，加上不依赖官方判题结果的认证链，能够有效审计官方测试集遗漏，并在缺少官方测试集时提供更强的缺陷检测能力。这一框架有望提升代码数据集、基准及代码大模型训练标签的可信度。

Abstract: Online-judge verdicts and the datasets and benchmarks built on them are treated as ground truth for evaluating and training large language models for code. Yet prior audits have sounded a warning: official suites accept buggy submissions. These audits, however, stop at the warning and offer no practical remedy. Our remedy has two parts: an off-the-shelf coding agent, serving as a test-suite auditor, both builds adversarial test suites to expose what official suites miss and supplies these suites where no official suite exists; a certification chain determines whether each agent-flagged submission is genuinely buggy without relying on the official judge: multiple independently written accepted solutions agree on the expected output for every test, brute-force solutions settle disagreements, and a per-problem validator certifies each failing input legal. One such agent identifies 589 verified accepted-but-buggy submissions among AtCoder's 20,375 audited accepted submissions; extending the same certification to all five agents yields a union floor of 906 such submissions. Five agents, scored separately, each stay within 1.7pp of official-suite coverage on logic bugs those suites catch. On post-cutoff Codeforces problems with no available official suites, the same test-building method leads all five reproduced baselines at every tested input budget. Where an official suite exists, the agent audits suite adequacy instead of assuming it; where none exists, agent suites catch the most buggy submissions among methods we reproduced and tested.

</details>


### [47] [Effective and Efficient Context Retrieval via Partial Dependency Graph for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01927)
*Zhongxin Liu,Zhonghao Jiang,Zhifan Ye,Haoye Wang,Jiakun Liu,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出DyRetriever，一种面向仓库级代码生成的动态依赖检索方法；其按需构建局部依赖图，并结合LLM进行多跳语义验证。与相似度检索及静态图检索相比，该方法在CoderEval和DevEval上显著提升Pass@1，同时降低图构建与维护开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级代码生成需要理解跨文件、跨函数的复杂依赖，但LLM受上下文窗口和仓库知识不足限制，通常依赖RAG补充上下文。传统相似度检索难以找到目标函数真正依赖的代码；现有图检索依赖人工规则和静态全局依赖图，灵活性不足，且构建、维护成本较高。作者观察到开发者通常从若干入口出发，沿依赖关系逐步检查相关代码，因此希望模拟这一动态检索过程。

Method: DyRetriever首先利用LLM选择与待生成目标相关的一组入口函数；随后沿代码依赖关系执行多跳搜索。在每一跳中，LLM依据目标生成任务和候选函数语义，判断该函数是否具有实际帮助，从而替代人工设计的筛选规则。系统不预先构建完整仓库级依赖图，而是在请求时按需构建局部依赖图，任务结束后丢弃。作者将DyRetriever与相似度代码检索器结合，形成DyCoder，以同时覆盖语义相似上下文和显式依赖上下文。

Result: 在CoderEval和DevEval上的实验表明，相较已有RAG方法，DyCoder的相对Pass@1分别提升25.63%和59.73%。相较依赖静态依赖图构建的基线方法，DyCoder速度提高7.4倍。结果说明，动态构建局部依赖图与LLM语义验证能够更有效地检索生成任务所需的依赖代码，并减少静态图方案的工程成本。

Conclusion: 该工作将仓库级代码检索从静态、规则驱动的全局图方法，转向按需、语义驱动的局部依赖探索。其主要贡献在于：以LLM选择入口并验证多跳依赖相关性，避免大量人工规则；按需构建和释放局部图，降低维护成本；结合相似度检索后，在代码生成正确率和检索效率上均取得明显改进。

Abstract: LLM-based repository-level code generation aims to generate code using the context available in a software repository, requiring LLMs to reason over complex code dependencies. Due to limited context windows and insufficient repository-specific understanding, LLMs typically rely on retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to incorporate relevant code. Early RAG approaches primarily employ similarity-based retrieval, which often fails to retrieve code snippets that the target function depends on. Recent work introduces graph-based retrieval to model such dependencies, but typically relies on manually designed rules and static global graphs, leading to limited flexibility and high construction and maintenance costs. In contrast, human developers collect helpful context by implicitly constructing a partial dependency graph and iteratively inspecting along it. Inspired by this behavior, we propose DyRetriever, an efficient context retrieval method via partial dependency graphs. DyRetriever uses an LLM to first select a set of entry-point functions and then perform multi-hop reasoning along the code dependency graph. During multi-hop reasoning, it uses the LLM's semantic understanding to validate whether a function can help generate the target function, eliminating manually designed rules and enabling flexibility across scenarios. Instead of statically constructing a global dependency graph, DyRetriever builds a partial graph on demand and discards it after use, reducing construction and maintenance costs. We integrate DyRetriever with a similarity-based code retriever to build DyCoder and evaluate it on CoderEval and DevEval. Experimental results show that DyCoder achieves relative Pass@1 improvements of 25.63% and 59.73% on CoderEval and DevEval, respectively, compared with existing RAG-based methods, while being 7.4x faster than baselines based on static dependency graph construction.

</details>


### [48] [TELLER: Non-intrusive Cross-Layer Root-Cause Analysis for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01975)
*Ruilin Xu,Junyi Li,Pengfei Chen,Zongxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TELLER是一个面向大语言模型推理服务的非侵入式根因分析框架。它联合GPU追踪数据与服务日志，重建请求级跨层调用链，并通过结构化压缩表示和多模态诊断模型定位异常步骤、可疑算子及其原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理已成为持续运行的在线服务，但单个请求横跨推理引擎、Python/C++后端、CUDA接口、GPU内核与分布式通信，故障链路复杂。现有时间线分析工具提供的信息过于原始，而日志诊断又缺少跨层执行语义和请求级关联，因此难以高效完成根因定位。

Method: TELLER在无需修改模型二进制文件的条件下采集NVTX/CUPTI追踪与服务日志；随后重建每请求调用链树，将日志行对齐到对应执行步骤。框架提出依赖感知因果上下文切片，以保留父子关系、时间顺序和通信关联；再使用Trace Pair Encoding将切片编码为包含父节点、深度和持续时间属性的紧凑结构化标记序列。最后，系统结合数值候选定位与多模态根因模型，共同完成异常步骤预测、可疑算子定位和自然语言解释生成。

Result: 多节点GPU推理实验表明，TPE存在明显的压缩率与诊断准确率权衡：中等规模词表可将每步骤追踪长度压缩超过80%，同时在跨节点通信的横向视图和节点内执行栈的纵向视图中取得最佳综合表现。过度压缩则会显著降低诊断质量。低故障先验、增强基线、模态消融、解释质量和追踪开销实验进一步支持其有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作将请求级追踪、日志语义与结构化因果上下文整合为统一的LLM推理诊断表示，弥补了纯时间线工具和纯日志方法的不足。其核心价值在于提供可部署的故障分诊与证据定位能力；不过，性能高度依赖合适的编码压缩粒度，实际部署中需要在存储、开销和诊断精度之间进行调优。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference has evolved from an offline workload into a continuously operated software service, yet root-cause analysis remains difficult because a single request spans the inference engine, Python/C++ backend, host CUDA APIs, GPU kernels, and distributed communication. Existing profilers expose raw timelines, while log-based diagnosis often misses cross-layer execution semantics and request-level structure. We present TELLER, a non-intrusive Trace- and Log-aware LLM inference Root-cause analysis framework. TELLER first collects NVTX/CUPTI traces and service logs without modifying model binaries, then reconstructs per-request call-chain trees and aligns log lines with the corresponding execution steps. We introduce a dependency-aware causal-context slice that preserves parent-child structure, temporal order, and communication relations, and a Trace Pair Encoding (TPE) tokenizer that compresses such slices into compact structural token sequences with parent, depth, and duration attributes. On top of these representations, TELLER combines numeric candidate localization with a multimodal root-cause model that jointly predicts abnormal steps, localizes suspicious operators, and generates natural-language explanations. Experiments on multi-node GPU inference workloads show a clear compression-accuracy trade-off: a moderate TPE vocabulary reduces per-step trace length by more than 80% while achieving the best overall performance on both horizontal (cross-node communication) and vertical (within-node execution stack) views, whereas more aggressive compression substantially degrades diagnosis quality. Further analyses under low-fault priors, strengthened baselines, modality ablations, explanation-quality checks, and tracing overhead show that TELLER provides a practical triage and evidence-localization substrate for LLM inference RCA.

</details>


### [49] [VulnGym: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Repository-Level Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02001)
*Kexing Ji,Jiachen Liu,Enze Hu,Cuiyun Gao,Keke Lian,Yongheng Liu,Lei Zhang,Tian Dong,Hao Chen,Wang Bin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出VulnGym，一个面向代码智能体的真实仓库级漏洞检测基准，用于弥补现有基准仅评估预选代码片段、难以衡量仓库自主探索与证据构建能力的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞检测评测通常将候选函数或代码片段预先提供给模型，因此无法反映代码智能体在完整仓库中自主定位漏洞相关代码的能力。同时，缺少细粒度漏洞传播路径标注，使研究者难以区分智能体失败究竟源于代码定位错误、关键操作识别不足，还是证据链构建不准确。

Method: 作者将已审查的GitHub安全公告与对应的漏洞版本仓库对齐，构建包含23个仓库、184条公告和408个漏洞条目的数据集。每个漏洞条目提供行级标注，包括漏洞入口点、关键操作及完整漏洞追踪路径。基准设计了一个端到端仓库级检测任务，以及三个基于标准答案信息的诊断子任务，用于分别评估漏洞发现、代码定位和证据构建能力。

Result: 实验表明，当前代码智能体在端到端仓库级漏洞检测上的能力仍然有限；即使能够发现部分相关线索，也难以稳定生成准确、完整且能够支撑漏洞结论的追踪证据。细粒度标注能够帮助进一步诊断性能瓶颈所在。

Conclusion: VulnGym将漏洞检测评估从预选片段分类推进到真实仓库级自主分析，并通过行级漏洞轨迹提供可诊断的评测基础。该工作指出，未来代码智能体需要重点提升仓库探索、漏洞相关代码定位以及可验证证据链构建能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-based vulnerability detection have shown promising results, while coding agents further extend this capability from isolated code snippets to complete repositories. This shift requires agents to autonomously explore repositories and locate vulnerability-relevant code, instead of performing detection on preselected functions. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on vulnerability classification over preselected code snippets, limiting their ability to evaluate coding agents in repository-level vulnerability detection. Moreover, without fine-grained vulnerability trace annotations, the capability limitations underlying the detection process remain difficult to explore. To address these limitations, we present \textbf{VulnGym}, a real-world repository-level benchmark for evaluating vulnerability detection by coding agents. VulnGym aligns reviewed GitHub advisories with their corresponding vulnerable version repositories. It contains 184 advisories and 408 vulnerability entries across 23 repositories, with each entry annotated with line-level entry points, critical operations, and vulnerability traces. Using this fine-grained ground truth, VulnGym defines an end-to-end detection task and three oracle-based subtasks to jointly evaluate vulnerability detection and diagnose limitations in code localization and evidence construction. Our evaluation indicates that current coding agents remain limited in both end-to-end repository-level vulnerability detection and the construction of accurate supporting traces.

</details>


### [50] [Pretraining on Call Graphs: When Binary Analysis Tasks Profit From Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02084)
*Samuel Valenzuela,Johannes Kinder*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估了将调用图等跨过程上下文融入二进制函数嵌入的效果，发现其虽能提升二进制代码相似性检测，但该收益不能稳定迁移至其他下游任务，且语义相似性优化可能损害句法任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制函数嵌入通常仅依赖函数内部信息；调用图增强方法已显示出相似性检测收益，但其实际作用机制、跨任务泛化能力及适用场景尚不明确。

Method: 作者以两种先进的二进制函数嵌入模型为基础，结合多种图模型引入调用图上下文，并在二进制代码相似性检测及语义、句法类下游任务上进行对比实验；同时通过数据集解释性分析，考察增强效果与函数类型、初始模型困难程度之间的关系。

Result: 调用图增强可改善二进制代码相似性检测，却未必提升语义或句法下游任务。面向语义相似性的性能优化与句法任务性能下降呈相关关系。调用图信息尤其能提高基础嵌入模型在困难样本上的鲁棒性；对于依赖命名空间和外部上下文的函数，其帮助大于主要实现独立局部逻辑的函数。

Conclusion: 调用图并非对所有二进制分析目标都普适有效，其价值主要体现为补充上下文依赖信息和提升困难场景鲁棒性。模型设计与评估不应仅依据相似性检测指标，而应针对目标下游任务分别验证，并在语义表征与句法特征保留之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Binary function embedding models are trained to encode the semantics of binary code in such a way that they can be generalized to a variety of reverse engineering tasks, such as binary code search, vulnerability detection, or malware classification. While many models only take the function in question as contextual input, there have been successful attempts to improve function embeddings by leveraging information from the call graph. In this study, we dissect the implications of these embedding refinements. We conduct experiments using a range of graph-based models on the embeddings generated by two state-of-the-art binary function embedding models. Integrating inter-procedural context, we show that improvements on binary code similarity detection (BCSD) will not necessarily generalize to downstream tasks, neither of semantic nor of syntactic nature. More generally, we find that optimizing for semantic similarity tasks correlates with worse performance on syntactic tasks. By conducting an explanatory analysis on the dataset, we find that the call graph-based enhancements significantly enhance the robustness of embeddings, particularly in scenarios where the initial models struggle. Furthermore, we observe that the added context is more beneficial for namespace-related functions than for those focused on individual logic, confirming that the call graph can be leveraged most effectively in context-dependent scenarios.

</details>


### [51] [ECLAIR: A Causally-Grounded AI Framework for Scientific Discovery in Empirical Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02323)
*Alejandro Velasco,Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas,Dipin Khati,David N. Palacio,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出ECLAIR：一个以因果推断为约束、将大语言模型嵌入软件工程科学研究全过程的人工智能框架，并通过提示设计影响代码生成准确率的案例展示其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统复杂，传统科学方法在软件工程实证研究中的系统化执行面临困难。尽管大语言模型可辅助研究，但其自动推理可能缺乏可靠性，因此需要一种满足实证软件工程认识论要求、具备人类监督和因果解释能力的方法论。

Method: ECLAIR将大语言模型定义为受因果推断原则约束的“科学智能体”，覆盖假设生成、研究设计、数据分析与结果解释等阶段。框架采用人类在环机制，对模型生成的推理、假设和结论进行审查，以降低不可靠自动化推理风险。案例研究比较两种大语言模型，并估计不同提示设计因素——指令式提示、较长的少样本示例及函数签名增强——对代码生成准确率的因果效应。

Result: 在案例中，两种模型均表现出一致趋势：指令式提示、较长的少样本提示和签名增强提示对代码生成准确率产生了较小的负向因果效应。该结果表明，仅观察提示策略与准确率的相关关系并不足够；因果分析可用于识别提示因素变化所带来的实际影响，并支持对软件现象成因的解释。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是提出面向实证软件工程的、因果驱动且人类监督的LLM辅助科学研究框架。ECLAIR为将LLM用于严谨研究提供了结构化方法，但其当前实证证据主要来自单一提示工程案例；其通用性、不同任务上的稳健性以及人类监督成本仍需通过更广泛的研究进一步验证。

Abstract: The scientific method has long guided empirical research in Software Engineering (SE), but the complexity of modern software systems often hinders its systematic application. This paper introduces _ECLAIR_, a causally grounded AI framework that integrates Large Language Models (_LLMs_) into every stage of the scientific process, from hypothesis generation to analysis and interpretation. _ECLAIR_ treats _LLMs_ as active **scientific agents** operating under the principles of causal inference, within a human-in-the-loop design that safeguards against the risks of unsound automated reasoning. We demonstrate the framework through a case study examining how prompt design influences code generation accuracy in two _LLMs_. Results show that, for both models, instruction-style, longer few-shot, and signature-augmented prompts yield small negative causal effects on accuracy, illustrating how causal reasoning provides a principled foundation for explaining _why_ software phenomena occur. This study presents the first causally grounded structured methodology for embedding _LLMs_ within the scientific method in SE, designed around the epistemological demands of empirical SE research, establishing a basis for rigorous AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [52] [On Automated and Explainable Provenance of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02329)
*Alejandro Velasco,Nathan Wintersgill,Trevor Stalnaker,Oscar Chaparro,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“可解释溯源”研究愿景：CodeGenAI不仅应生成代码，还应自动追溯代码生成的原因与来源，包括提示词、训练数据、数据全局特征和模型内部组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成工具的输出来源不透明，开发者、部署组织与合规人员难以判断生成代码为何出现、是否可靠及如何改进。现有方法多在事后标记风险，却不能解释成因或支持后续生成优化。

Method: 作者基于对开发者、模型使用者及合规专业人员的实证研究，归纳当前工具的溯源缺口；进一步从四个可追溯性维度刻画问题，并提出结合大规模经验研究、事后因果分析和模型可解释性技术的研究计划。

Result: 研究表明，溯源信息并非附加功能，而是实际开发与合规工作中的必要能力；当前CodeGenAI工具尚无法系统地将输出代码关联到具体提示词成分、训练样本、数据特征及模型机制。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是明确了面向代码生成模型的“可解释溯源”方向及其关键挑战。未来工具应提供可验证、可操作且多层次的生成归因，以提升代码生成过程的透明度、质量控制能力和合规支持能力。

Abstract: Generative AI for code generation has transformed software development, but it has also introduced a critical transparency problem: the origins of AI-generated code are opaque to the developers who use it, the organizations that deploy it, and the compliance professionals responsible for ensuring its legal and quality standards. Existing mitigations flag problematic outputs after the fact without explaining why a model produced them or how future generation could be improved. We present a research vision, grounded in a U.S. NSF-funded research grant, that argues that the next generation of CodeGenAI tools must be built on a foundation of explainable provenance: automated, post-hoc traceability that links generated code back to the prompt components, training data instances, global data features, and internal model components that caused its generation. We grounded this vision in empirical evidence from studies of software developers, model users, and compliance/legal professionals, which show that provenance information is a practical necessity that current tools do not provide. We characterize the problem across four traceability dimensions, outline a research program combining large-scale empirical studies with post-hoc causal and interpretability techniques, and identify the key open challenges that the community must address to realize this vision.

</details>


### [53] [Profitability of Open-Source Software Product Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02398)
*Shivendu P. Singh,Narayan Ramasubbu,Chris F. Kemerer,M. Zia Hydari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明，企业采用开源产品开发与平均4%—5%的毛利率提升相关，主要机制是外部贡献者带来的知识整合提高劳动生产率，而非单纯降低用工成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注企业向外部开发者开放产品开发是否能够带来经济回报，并试图区分开源的知识创造价值与低成本外包效应，为企业资源配置和开源生态建设提供依据。

Method: 研究使用2001—2025年977家美国高科技企业的样本，估计开源采用与毛利率之间的关系，并考察劳动生产率和劳动成本等潜在传导机制。研究进一步识别外部志愿贡献占比约35%的有效阈值，并通过配置分析比较开源开发、内部研发及其他资源条件的组合。

Result: 开源采用企业的毛利率平均提高4%—5%。收益主要来自劳动生产率上升：企业将外部开发者的多样化知识纳入内部流程，扩大知识基础，而劳动成本未同步显著增加。只有外部志愿者承担足够比例工作时，开源收益才会显现；在高盈利的所有资源配置中，开源产品开发与持续内部研发的组合均为关键条件。

Conclusion: 开源应被视为与企业资源能力相匹配的知识整合战略，而不是获取廉价外部劳动力的工具。论文的核心贡献在于揭示开源价值具有阈值性和配置依赖性；但摘要未说明开源采用的识别策略、内生性处理及生产率变量的具体测量，因而对“提高”利润的因果解释仍需结合全文方法进一步评估。

Abstract: Many technology firms now build software products in the open, inviting outside developers to contribute alongside their employees on platforms like GitHub. Does this openness in product development pay off? Analyzing 977 U.S. high-tech firms from 2001 to 2025, this study finds that open-source adoption raised firms' gross margins by 4-5% on average. These gains flow largely through higher labor productivity, as firms integrate external contributors' diverse knowledge into internal workflows, broadening the organizational knowledge base without a commensurate rise in labor costs. However, the payoff emerges only when outside volunteers supply a meaningful share of the work (around 35% in this sample), and hinges on the firm's resource configuration. While there are multiple pathways to profitability, pairing open-source product development with sustained internal R&D is a core condition present in all high-profitability configurations. For managers, the lesson is to treat open source as a knowledge-integration strategy matched to the firm's resources, not merely a means of sourcing external effort at lower cost. For policymakers, the findings underscore the broad economic value of open-source ecosystems and the need for policies that foster skilled volunteer communities and public-private open-source infrastructure.

</details>


### [54] [SWE-Touch: Benchmarking Coding Agents When Users Touch the Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02499)
*Yuqiao Tan,Jinxiang Meng,Fangyu Lei,Minzheng Wang,Shizhu He,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Touch评估编码智能体在共享工作区中面对用户并发修改时的适应能力。实验表明，具有任务冲突性的用户编辑会使智能体的任务解决率平均下降7.7个百分点，暴露出其工作区状态感知、冲突协调和针对性验证能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级编码智能体基准通常假设智能体独立工作，或仅通过消息模拟用户参与，未充分评估用户直接修改代码时的协作能力。研究旨在考察智能体能否识别、理解并处理共享工作区中的代码变化。

Method: 提出SWE-Touch框架，通过Counter-Edits模拟与任务完成目标相冲突但合理的用户代码修改。方法从多条修复轨迹中挖掘任务关键区域，由独立的User Patch Generator生成编辑，并在智能体到达相关代码时结合上下文用户消息注入修改。研究在SWE-bench Verified上评估9个编码模型，并在SWE-Bench Pro和DeepSWE的长周期任务上进行补充实验。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，Counter-Edit使平均任务解决率下降7.7个百分点，且在两个长周期基准上仍存在性能退化。轨迹分析显示，智能体常未能充分感知工作区的演化：有时保留与新编辑冲突的代码，有时直接覆盖这些编辑，却未重新检查仓库状态，也未通过针对性测试验证修改后的行为。

Conclusion: 自主编码性能强并不意味着具备共享工作区协作所需的状态感知和自适应能力。未来优化应重点提升工作区变更检测、冲突编辑与任务目标的协调，以及对受影响行为进行针对性验证的能力。

Abstract: Real-world software development requires coding agents to operate in shared workspaces where users may inspect and modify code during an ongoing task, yet existing repository-level benchmarks typically evaluate agents working alone or restrict user participation to messages. This leads us to ask: how do coding agents understand and respond to code changes in a shared workspace? We introduce SWE-Touch, a framework that stress-tests this setting through validated Counter-Edits: plausible edits to task-relevant code that conflict with task completion. SWE-Touch mines task-critical regions from multiple repair trajectories, uses a separate User Patch Generator to construct the edits, and injects them with contextual user messages when agents reach the relevant code. We evaluate nine coding models on SWE-bench Verified, with additional experiments on longer-horizon tasks from SWE-Bench Pro and DeepSWE. Counter-Edit lowers average resolve rate by 7.7 percentage points on SWE-bench Verified, with degradation also persisting on both longer-horizon benchmarks. Trajectory analysis links these failures to limited awareness of the evolving workspace: agents may retain conflicting code or replace it without sufficiently re-inspecting the repository and validating the revised code with targeted tests. These findings show that strong autonomous performance does not yet ensure the state awareness and adaptive behavior needed for shared-workspace collaboration, and point to detecting workspace changes, reconciling conflicting edits with the task, and verifying the affected behavior as key capabilities for future optimization.

</details>


### [55] [Decomposing the Doer Effect in Programming Practice: Code Writing Stands Out Among Active Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02541)
*Arun Balajiee Lekshmi Narayanan,Gillian Gold,Jordan Barria-Pineda,Quinn K Wolter,Peter Brusilovsky,Paulo Carvalho*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究检验“做中学效应”在计算机科学教学中的适用性，并比较不同主动练习形式的效果。结果显示，主动练习与学习成效的关联约为被动活动的3.2倍；在各类主动练习中，代码编写与后测成绩提升的关联最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有“做中学效应”研究通常将各种主动练习统一归类，未充分区分不同练习形式的相对价值。该研究旨在确认该效应是否适用于Java课程，并识别何种主动练习最有利于学习。

Method: 研究分析了11个学期共334名Java入门及中级课程学生的交互式练习系统日志数据。系统内容分为代码编写、代码跟踪、代码补全、代码可视化和代码解释五类；研究比较主动练习与被动活动同学习结果的关联，并进一步比较不同主动练习类型与后测表现的关系。

Result: 主动练习活动与更好的学习结果显著相关，其关联强度约为被动活动的3.2倍。在主动练习类型中，代码编写与后测成绩改善的关联最为显著；代码跟踪、代码补全、代码可视化和代码解释未表现出同等强度的关联。

Conclusion: “做中学效应”可扩展至计算机科学教育，但并非所有主动练习同样有效。教学设计应优先采用具有较高挑战性且能提供反馈支持的代码编写练习，而不应仅以“主动参与”作为判断练习质量的标准。

Abstract: The "doer effect" suggests that actively doing practice activities is more strongly associated with learning outcomes than passively viewing content. In the doer effect literature, "doing" refers specifically to active practice. However, this categorization treats different forms of active practice as equivalent, leaving open whether some types of active practice are more effective than others. In this paper, we investigate whether the doer effect extends to computer science instruction and whether some forms of doing stand out compared to other forms. We analyze log data from 334 students across 11 semesters of introductory and intermediate Java who used an interactive practice system with five content types: Code Writing, Code Tracing, Code Completion, Code Visualizations, and Code Explanations. Consistent with prior doer effect work, we find that active practice activities were associated with 3.2 times better learning outcomes than passive activities. Interestingly, among the active practice, code writing was the most strongly associated with improved posttest performance, while no other activity type showed a comparable association. These results highlight the importance of challenging, feedback-supported practice activities, such as code writing problems.

</details>


### [56] [ACEM: A Cost Estimation Model for Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02582)
*Mohammad El-Ramly*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ACEM（Agentic Cost Estimation Model），用于估算自主智能体软件工程中的总成本，将成本分解为LLM、人在回路（HITL）和基础设施三部分，并引入Revision Factor、Context Factor与HITL Intensity Score以刻画非确定性开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统COCOMO II、功能点和故事点模型主要依据人工设计、编码与测试工作量，难以反映AI智能体开发中的Token消耗、人工监督、重试修订、上下文增长及工具编排等新成本。因此，研究旨在建立一种能够连接传统规模度量与智能体成本结构的估算框架。

Method: ACEM将总成本建模为LLM成本、HITL成本和基础设施成本之和。模型通过Revision Factor（RF）描述输出被拒绝和重试导致的Token开销，通过Context Factor（CF）描述累积上下文带来的Token增长，并以四级HITL Intensity Score（HIS）刻画人工监督强度。此外，模型将Use Case Points、Story Points和Function Points映射至预计Token消耗，使现有项目规模数据能够用于智能体成本预测。模型结构和校准流程已形式化，但具体常数暂以符号表示。

Result: 论文产出的是一个完整的概念模型与校准方法，而非经过实证验证的预测系统。ACEM明确了三类主要成本、关键动态因素及传统规模指标到Token消耗的映射路径，但尚未报告真实项目数据、预测误差、基准比较或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: ACEM为自主智能体软件工程的成本估算提供了可扩展的初步框架，弥补了传统人工劳动导向模型的不足。其实际有效性取决于后续基于真实项目数据对RF、CF、HIS及相关成本参数进行校准、验证和持续扩展。

Abstract: Traditional software cost estimation models, such as COCOMO II, Function Points, and Story Points, assume that development effort is primarily driven by human labor in design, coding, and testing. Agentic software engineering, where autonomous AI agents perform substantial implementation work and humans focus on planning, specification, and validation, challenges this assumption. New cost dimensions arise: large language model (LLM) token consumption across agent actions, Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) oversight effort, and infrastructure costs for agent orchestration and tooling. These costs are nondeterministic: identical tasks may consume different tokens, follow divergent reasoning paths, and require varying human correction, phenomena absent in traditional development. A new framework is needed to bridge standard sizing metrics with this cost structure.
  This paper proposes ACEM (Agentic Cost Estimation Model), which decomposes total agentic development cost into three additive dimensions: LLM, HITL, and infrastructure cost. ACEM introduces three constructs for agentic dynamics: the Revision Factor (RF), modeling token overhead from output rejection and retries; the Context Factor (CF), capturing rising token consumption as context accumulates; and the HITL Intensity Score (HIS), a four-level oversight classification scheme. It further maps Use Case Points, Story Points, and Function Points to estimated token consumption, enabling organizations to reuse existing project-scoping data for agentic cost forecasting.
  ACEM is presented as a fully specified model structure and calibration methodology, with constants left symbolic pending empirical grounding. As an early-stage proposal, it invites the research community to calibrate, test, and extend the model through real project data.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [57] [A Fortran General-Purpose Transpiler: Proof of Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00130)
*Shivamshan Sivanesan,Kazem Ardaneh*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: FGPT 是一个面向遗留 Fortran 科学代码现代化的编译框架，可系统地将 Fortran 转换为适配 GPU、支持自动微分的 Fortran，或转换为 NumPy/JAX 模块，并在代表性气候建模内核上验证了正确性与可微性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fortran 仍是高性能科学计算的重要语言，但新一代研究人员对其不熟悉，而资深开发者向 JAX 等现代生态迁移时又容易保留传统 Fortran 编程范式，难以充分利用 GPU 加速和自动微分能力。针对大规模社区科学代码，基于大语言模型的逐段转换难以保证跨模块一致性、数值保真和生产测试验证，因此需要可靠、可扩展的编译式迁移方案。

Method: FGPT 采用三阶段编译流水线：前端解析 Fortran 源码，提取目标过程及其跨模块依赖；中端将程序降级为中间表示，并生成 GPU 适配 Fortran、经 Tapenade 自动微分的 Fortran 或 NumPy 类；后端重写控制流和表达式，生成可在 GPU 上运行且支持自动微分的 JAX 模块。框架强调在全流程中保持原程序语义，以支持大规模代码库的稳定转换。

Result: 作者在具有代表性的气候建模计算内核上验证了 FGPT。结果表明，该框架能够无需人工干预地生成正确的、可微分的 Python 实现，并可输出面向 GPU 加速和自动微分的 JAX 代码。摘要未提供具体的性能提升比例、转换覆盖率或数值误差统计。

Conclusion: FGPT 将传统编译技术与现代加速器及自动微分生态结合，为大规模遗留 Fortran 科学软件迁移到 NumPy/JAX 提供了可扩展且可信的路径。其核心价值在于依赖分析、语义保持和自动化代码生成；后续仍需通过更大规模应用、性能基准和数值一致性指标进一步评估其实用性。

Abstract: Fortran has been the cornerstone of high-performance computing for decades and remains unmatched in many domains. Yet the language faces an expertise gap: a new generation of scientists is barely familiar with it, while many experienced Fortran developers are only now transitioning to modern ecosystems such as JAX. This gap often results in "Fython"--Python code written with a Fortran mindset-- that fails to leverage modern frameworks. We present FGPT, a Python-based compiler framework designed to bridge this divide. It provides a systematic pipeline that transpiles Fortran into GPU-adapted Fortran, auto-differentiable Fortran via Tapenade, or NumPy and JAX modules. Its architecture comprises three stages: (i) a frontend that parses Fortran and extracts target procedures along with their cross-module dependencies; (ii) a middle-end that lowers the code into an intermediate representation, then into GPU-adapted or auto-differentiable Fortran, or a NumPy class; and (iii) a backend that rewrites control-flow and expressions to produce JAX modules ready for GPU acceleration and automatic differentiation. While large language models hold promise for small snippets, they fail at the scale of community scientific codes--often spanning hundreds of thousands of lines--where consistent transformations, strict numerical fidelity, and validation against production tests are non-negotiable. FGPT addresses these challenges by preserving program semantics throughout the entire translation. We verified the framework on representative climate modeling kernels and demonstrated that it produces correct, differentiable Python implementations without requiring manual intervention. By combining rigorous compiler techniques with modern accelerator support, FGPT offers a scalable, trustworthy path for modernizing legacy Fortran code.

</details>


### [58] [Triton for MTIA: Bridging the Programming Model Gaps for Custom AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00325)
*Haishan Zhu,Domi Yan,Michael Levesque-Dion,Changxu Zhang,Mitch Gamburg,Kirsten Lee,Giancarlo Colmenares,Aditya Bhagwat,Arnab De,Markus Le Roux,Victor Perez Carrasco,Xin Tong,Will Cromar,Simran Barnwal,Andrew Uderian,Blaine Burton Rister,Jordan Fix,Jazlyn Li,Zejun Huang,Lite Ye,Nan Zhang,Xinchen Guo,Andiry Xu,Michael Roberts,Kunming Ho,Site Cao,Suryadev Sahadevan Rajesh,Tristan Trouwen,Mike Tsai,Jake Lee,Wayne Su,Yuhan Chen,Xiaolong Xie,David Eklov,Aaron Barnes,Max Bremer,Adam Belay,Shintaro Iwasaki,Roman Levenstein,Ajit Mathews*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文介绍了将Triton首次以生产级规模部署到Meta自研ML加速器MTIA-2i上的工作，证明Triton这类领域专用语言能够适配非GPU加速器，并在性能与开发效率之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定制ML加速器通常具有区别于GPU的编程模型，导致算子覆盖不足、内核开发成本高，难以快速支持多样化模型。虽然Triton和TorchInductor已改善GPU上的内核开发与自动生成流程，但其是否能有效迁移至自定义加速器尚未得到验证。

Method: 作者为MTIA-2i实现了新的Triton编译器后端，扩展TorchInductor的代码生成能力，并以最小化语言扩展暴露MTIA特有的体系结构能力。在此基础上，既支持人工编写Triton内核，也支持由Inductor自动生成的Triton内核，并将其用于实际生产模型。

Result: Triton-MTIA内核的性能可与专家手工优化的C++实现竞争。该方案已在约60类模型中投入生产，覆盖这些模型50%的层，并占据47%的非GEMM执行时间，表明其具有较高的实际算子覆盖率和部署价值。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，Triton等DSL可以作为ML框架、内核实现与定制加速器之间的抽象桥梁。通过编译器后端、框架代码生成和少量硬件特性扩展的协同设计，可以在保持接近手工优化性能的同时，显著提升定制加速器上的内核开发、模型迭代和规模化部署效率。

Abstract: The rapid growth in machine learning workloads has fueled the proliferation of custom accelerator architectures. Designed from the ground up, these accelerators often expose programming models that are distinct from GPUs. While hyperscalers and AI chip startups continue to innovate in this space, achieving broad operator coverage to support diverse models remains a major challenge. Additionally, an easy-to-use, high-level kernel programming language is important for rapid iteration of models and kernels. Triton, together with TorchInductor, addresses these issues on GPUs, but its viability on accelerators with different programming models has yet to be established. In this work, we present the first production-scale application of Triton on a custom ML accelerator, MTIA-2i, developed by Meta. To support MTIA-2i, we develop a new compiler backend that targets it, introduce enhancements to TorchInductor code generation, and propose minimal language extensions that expose MTIA-specific architectural features. We demonstrate that Triton-MTIA kernels achieve performance competitive with expert-tuned C++ implementations. Leveraging these development efficiency gains, we successfully deployed manually written and Inductor-generated Triton kernels in production across approximately 60 different model types, accounting for 50% of layers and 47% of non-GEMM execution time for these models. Our results provide compelling evidence that DSLs like Triton can bridge the programming model gaps between ML frameworks, kernels, and custom accelerators, enabling rapid innovation and efficient deployment at scale.

</details>


### [59] [P4-SpecTec: Integrating a Language Mechanization Framework into the Real-World P4 Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00639)
*Jaehyun Lee,Seokhun Jeong,Sehyuk Ahn,Haechan Kwon,Sukyoung Ryu*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出 P4-SpecTec：面向 P4 语言的机制化规范框架。该框架以可执行的算法化推理规则形式统一描述 P4 的静态与动态语义，可生成类型检查器、解释器和自然语言风格的规范文档，并已用于发现官方规范及参考编译器中的 24 个缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程语言规范常存在歧义、不一致或实现偏差，导致规范文本、编译器实现和形式化模型难以同步。已有机制化框架虽已成功应用于其他语言，但无法直接满足 P4 的核心需求：其类型系统的形式化必须可执行，以便实际充当类型检查器。论文旨在验证语言机制化方法在 P4 这一真实工业语言中的适用性，并解决可执行类型系统机制化问题。

Method: 作者构建 P4-SpecTec，并将算法化推理规则作为规范机制化的主要表示手段。该表示既保留推理规则的形式化结构，又支持按算法执行：静态语义可执行为 P4 类型检查器，动态语义可执行为解释器。框架还从同一机制化规范派生出以散文算法呈现的规范文档，从而使形式化定义对 P4 开发者更易阅读和审阅。作者据此机制化最新版本的 P4 规范，并将生成工具用于验证规范与参考实现。

Result: P4-SpecTec 完成了对最新 P4 规范的静态和动态语义机制化；生成的可执行语义能够作为类型检查器和解释器运行；框架可自动导出开发者可读的规范文档。通过执行和比对机制化语义，作者在官方 P4 规范与参考编译器中共发现 24 个错误。该工具链已获得有条件的官方采纳，成为 P4 规范编写流程的候选基础设施。

Conclusion: 论文表明，机制化规范不仅可用于形式化验证，还能作为语言规范、实现和文档的统一事实来源。通过引入算法化推理规则，P4-SpecTec 解决了可执行类型系统这一关键难题，并在真实规范维护中发现了大量缺陷。其官方采纳进展说明该方法具有实际工程价值，同时为将类似框架推广至其他编程语言提供了经验案例。

Abstract: Programming languages evolve, but often without a complete and unambiguous definition of their syntax and semantics. Ambiguities and inconsistencies are silently introduced into specifications, and manifest as divergences between the specification, implementations, and formalizations that constitute the language ecosystem. Even in rare cases when a normative specification exists, keeping the ecosystem in sync is a daunting task. Language mechanization frameworks address this problem by treating a mechanized specification as the single source of truth, from which implementations and documents are generated. Recently, this approach has been integrated into the actual JavaScript and WebAssembly (Wasm) specifications with ESMeta and Wasm-SpecTec, respectively. Despite these successes, it remains an open question how to extrapolate ESMeta and Wasm-SpecTec to other language specifications. As a first step towards addressing this question, we present P4-SpecTec, a language mechanization framework for the P4 programming language, as a case study of real-world adoption of language mechanization. P4 introduces unique challenges, in particular the requirement that its type system mechanization should be executable, which is not supported by either ESMeta or Wasm-SpecTec. To address this challenge, we introduce algorithmic inference rules as the primary instrument for mechanization, enabling the mechanized P4 static and dynamic semantics to be executed as a P4 type checker and interpreter, respectively. We mechanized the most recent P4 specification, and utilizing its executability, identified 24 bugs across the official P4 specification and the reference compiler. Furthermore, P4-SpecTec derives a specification document as prose algorithms, making it accessible to P4 developers. P4-SpecTec is conditionally adopted as the official P4 specification authoring toolchain.

</details>

### [60] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [61] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM提出一种面向OpenFOAM的自进化大语言模型智能体，可根据自然语言指令自动生成、执行、评估并迭代CFD仿真配置，以降低开源CFD工具的使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenFOAM虽然功能强大，但其求解器选择、网格设置、边界条件与配置文件编写高度依赖专业知识，且准备过程耗时。论文旨在通过LLM自动化这些环节，加速工程原型验证并扩大CFD工作流的可及性。

Method: 方法以Qwen-Coder 2.5-14B为基础模型，在252条覆盖7类OpenFOAM求解器、13种参数化网格模板及考虑y+约束的数值策略的文本提示上微调。核心是包含7个阶段的演化闭环，使智能体能够创建、运行、评估和改进仿真；同时采用RAG增强的重试上下文、字典级定向修补和提示多样化改写三种抗坍塌机制，缓解重复自训练导致的性能退化。

Result: 摘要声称该系统能够仅依据自然语言完成OpenFOAM仿真工作流，并通过自演化机制持续改进生成结果。其主要贡献在于将生成式AI与可执行、可评估的流体仿真闭环结合；但摘要未报告定量准确率、仿真成功率、计算成本、基准对比或复杂案例上的泛化结果。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了LLM代理自动化CFD配置与迭代的潜力，尤其适用于快速原型开发和降低OpenFOAM操作复杂度。论文价值主要取决于演化闭环的实际稳定性、生成配置的物理可靠性，以及相对于人工专家和现有自动化工具的可验证性能优势。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [62] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究面向中小企业问答与决策支持场景的大语言模型幻觉问题，提出并比较VectorRAG与GraphRAG两类检索增强生成方法，以提升回答的可靠性、上下文相关性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业采用大语言模型辅助业务决策时，模型幻觉和错误信息会削弱用户信任，并可能影响决策质量。仅依赖模型内部知识难以保证事实准确性，因此需要引入外部、可追溯的知识来源来约束生成结果。

Method: 论文构建VectorRAG和GraphRAG方案，并在LLaMA、Mistral和Qwen等多种模型上开展实验。评估维度包括有用回答生成能力、幻觉风险、上下文相关性以及人工可解释性，以比较不同RAG建模方式在中小企业环境中的适用效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用RAG增强后的模型能够显著改善回答质量，降低幻觉与错误信息风险，并提升回答与检索上下文的关联程度。摘要指出两种RAG方案均对提高中小企业场景下模型输出的可信度具有积极作用，但未给出各模型、各方法之间的具体量化差异。

Conclusion: RAG是缓解中小企业大语言模型应用中幻觉问题的有效路径。通过整合外部知识，VectorRAG和GraphRAG可为业务问答和决策支持提供更可靠、可信且具备上下文依据的输出；后续仍需报告更细致的基准数据、知识库构建成本及不同业务任务下的效果差异。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [63] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nova 是一种端到端 JIT 深度学习编译器，通过全图捕获、跨算子融合、内存层级优化和寄存器级调度生成，实现高效硬件映射；其在 RTX 3060 上可在多数 TF32 矩阵乘形状中达到或略超 cuBLAS 与 XLA 的性能，并显著降低训练内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高层张量框架的即时执行模式缺乏完整计算图可见性，因而难以实施跨算子优化、精细内存管理及硬件级调度，导致硬件利用率和大模型训练容量受到限制。论文旨在弥合高层数学表达与底层硬件执行之间的映射鸿沟。

Method: Nova 捕获即时执行过程，并将前向与反向计算统一到值语义中间表示，以支持全图优化。系统执行跨边界算子融合、复杂内存层级优化及寄存器级执行调优；通过基于算术强度的解析式配置器确定性推导执行调度，避免传统自动调优的搜索开销；运行时利用结构哈希，根据计算结构直接合成细粒度内核。

Result: 在 RTX 3060 上，Nova 在多数 TF32 矩阵乘尺寸下达到或略优于 cuBLAS 和 XLA，同时将相对误差控制在 5e-4 以下。对于一个 4200 万参数模型，Nova 相比 PyTorch 的吞吐量最高提升 10.6%，相比 XLA 最高提升 4.4%。其相对 PyTorch 最多减少 29% 的内存占用，并能在 12 GB 消费级 GPU 上以 17,900 tokens/s 训练 1.44 亿参数模型，而 PyTorch 在相同条件下发生显存不足。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将动态图执行转换为可进行全图分析的统一表示，并结合解析调度、结构驱动内核生成与细粒度内存优化，能够同时提升训练吞吐、数值保真度和显存效率。Nova 的主要贡献在于以零搜索代价实现硬件感知调度，并将节省的显存转化为更大的可训练模型规模。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models at scale relies heavily on how effectively high-level mathematical operations are mapped to underlying physical hardware. While high-level tensor frameworks provide flexible abstractions for model design, their eager execution models inherently lack the whole-graph visibility and granular control over hardware and memory required to maximize physical hardware utilization natively. To bridge this gap, we designed Nova, an automated end-to-end JIT compiler whose defining purpose is to achieve absolute control over this hardware mapping: fusing operations across operation boundaries, optimizing complex memory hierarchies, and tuning execution down to the register level. By capturing eager executions and unifying forward and backward passes into a single value-semantic dialect, Nova unlocks aggressive whole-graph optimizations. It then utilizes an Analytic Configurator to deterministically derive optimal execution schedules based on arithmetic intensity, dropping search time to zero. Backed by a structural hashing runtime, Nova synthesizes fine-grained kernels directly from the computation's structure. In our evaluations on an RTX 3060, Nova matches or modestly exceeds cuBLAS and XLA on TF32 matmuls on most shapes, maintaining a stringent < 5e-4 relative error. At the model level, Nova achieves up to 10.6% greater throughput than PyTorch and 4.4% greater than XLA on a 42-million parameter model, without compromising on numerical fidelity. Crucially, by reducing the memory footprint by up to 29% relative to PyTorch, Nova successfully trains a 144-million parameter model at 17,900 tokens/s where PyTorch encounters Out-Of-Memory (OOM) failures on the same 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [64] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文构建了一个面向消费级硬件本地部署场景的LLM能耗基准，比较9个1B至7B开源模型在RTX 4060 Ti 16GB上的推理功耗、单位提示能耗、单位输出Token能耗与吞吐量。结果表明，能效并不只由参数规模决定，模型架构、量化策略及推理行为同样关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评测通常侧重准确率、能力和速度，而对本地推理的能源成本缺乏系统刻画。随着隐私保护和离线部署需求增长，消费级GPU上的能耗与性能权衡成为模型选型的重要依据，因此需要可复现的硬件级测量基准。

Method: 作者在单张RTX 4060 Ti 16GB GPU上，使用Ollama运行9个参数规模为1B至7B的开源LLM，并针对固定提示集合进行测试。通过nvidia-smi以2Hz频率采样GPU功耗，统计平均功耗、峰值功耗、每个提示的总能耗（J/prompt）、每个输出Token能耗（J/token）以及生成吞吐量（tok/s）。

Result: gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b表现出最佳能效，单位输出Token能耗分别为0.56 J/token和0.65 J/token，吞吐量均超过170 tok/s。相比之下，7B Mistral模型的单位Token能耗最高，可达到最优模型的4.4倍。qwen3.5:2b则因较长的内部推理过程而呈现异常高的单提示能耗，说明仅以表面模型规模或单一能耗指标评估效率可能产生误导。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，本地LLM推理的能源效率由参数量、网络架构、量化方案和Token生成模式共同决定。实际部署时应联合考察J/token、J/prompt和吞吐量，并将可见输出与可能存在的扩展推理Token区分开来；对于能耗敏感的消费级部署，小型高吞吐模型通常具有显著优势。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [65] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CoT-Core，一种免训练的LLM评测核心题选择方法。其利用零样本思维链轨迹表征题目的潜在推理逻辑，以较少样本近似完整评测集得分，从而降低持续评测成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型持续迭代中的全量评测计算开销高。现有核心集方法要么依赖大量历史评测记录，存在冷启动问题；要么主要依据表层文本相似性选题，难以识别措辞不同但推理结构相同的问题，因此无法有效覆盖任务的真实推理空间。

Method: CoT-Core首先让LLM对候选问题生成零样本思维链推理轨迹，再将这些轨迹映射至潜在表示空间，并基于该空间中的聚类或覆盖关系选择核心问题。与直接使用题目文本嵌入不同，该方法试图按内在逻辑等价性而非词汇相似性组织样本；整个流程无需额外训练或依赖历史日志。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA上的实验表明，CoT-Core能够在显著减少评测题量与计算成本的同时，保持对全量评测得分的较高估计保真度。论文还发现，基于推理轨迹的剪枝效果受任务复杂度制约：任务复杂度处于合适范围时收益明显，过于复杂时生成推理轨迹的稳定性与表示质量可能限制方法效果。

Conclusion: CoT-Core将核心集选择从表层语义覆盖扩展到推理结构覆盖，为低成本、无历史数据依赖的LLM持续评测提供了可行方案。其核心贡献在于证明思维链轨迹可作为题目逻辑相似性的有效代理，但实际部署时应结合任务难度验证其适用边界，并关注推理生成本身带来的额外成本与稳定性。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [66] [Assuming You Knew: Fixing an Epistemic Semantics for Flow Policies Using Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00882)
*David A. Naumann*

Main category: cs.AI
