Furthermore, we show that this transition is robust to prompt variations, suggesting that it reflects a general mechanism of model behavior.

</details>


### [131] [No Single Neuron of Failure: Distributed Safety Alignment Against White-Box Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01414)
*Simiao Xie,Chuancheng Shi,Shangze Li,Wenhua Wu,Fei Shen,Ying Zhou,Zhiyong Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出分布式安全对齐（DSA），通过将模型的拒答安全能力冗余地分布到多个神经元中，降低少数关键安全神经元被白盒攻击破坏后导致安全失效的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重基础模型使攻击者能够直接定位并干预与安全行为相关的神经元。现有对齐方法的安全表征可能集中于少量神经元，形成脆弱的单点依赖，因而需要提升安全能力在神经元层面的冗余性与抗干预能力。

Method: 方法在语言侧前馈网络下投影层的输入处进行干预，并将每个特征维度视为一个神经元激活。DSA结合神经元激活值与损失梯度，使用具方向感知的一阶泰勒评分，在全局范围定位当前最支撑拒答行为的神经元；随后联合确定性掩码和随机丢弃，有针对性地扰动这些神经元，迫使模型减少对狭窄安全神经元集合的依赖，并学习由补偿性神经元共同承载的安全表征。

Result: 摘要声称，大量实验表明DSA能显著提升模型抵抗白盒神经元级安全攻击的鲁棒性，同时较好保留通用语言能力与多模态任务效用。

Conclusion: DSA的核心贡献是把安全对齐从依赖少量关键神经元的集中式表征，转向多神经元冗余承载的分布式表征。其潜在价值在于提高开放权重模型面对内部可解释性分析和定向神经元干预时的安全稳定性；但摘要未给出具体攻击设置、指标数值、模型规模及效用损失细节，仍需通过完整实验验证其泛化性和训练成本。

Abstract: With the rapid release of open-weight large foundation models, safety threats are shifting from black-box jailbreaks to neuron-level white-box attacks that directly identify and manipulate safety-related neurons. Existing alignment methods often investigate the safety behavior on a small number of neurons, creating fragile single point of failure with limited redundancy. To address this issue, we propose distributed safety alignment (DSA), which redundantly encodes safety capabilities across multiple computational neurons, ensuring that the model maintains its safety baseline even when critical safety neurons are disrupted. Specifically, we localize the intervention to the inputs of the down-projection layers in language-side feed-forward networks and treat each feature coordinate as the activation of an individual neuron. DSA then combines neuron activations with loss gradients to compute a direction-aware first-order Taylor score that globally identifies the neurons that contribute most to the current refusal behavior of the model. Finally, targeted disruption via deterministic masking and stochastic dropout is coupled, forcing the model to abandon narrow safety neurons and redundantly encode safety behavior across multiple compensatory neurons. Extensive experiments show that DSA substantially improves robustness against white-box neuron-level safety attacks while preserving the model's general language and multimodal utility.

</details>


### [132] [Securing Agentic AI: From Per-Action Checks to Trajectory Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01558)
*Alireza Lotfi,Subangkar Karmaker Shanto,Imtiaz Karim,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自主智能体安全应从单次动作正确性转向系统级行为合规与可验证性，覆盖单智能体、多智能体、控制平面、行为约束及供应链等全栈风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型的智能体获得更高自主性，并在跨组织环境中执行高影响任务，传统依赖提示约束或局部防护的安全机制不足以保证其持续遵守运行规则、系统不变量与责任边界。

Method: 文章以挑战综述和路线图形式分层梳理智能体安全问题：单智能体层关注提示、记忆、检索知识与工具接口的非可信输入；多智能体层关注委派、通信、身份、信任、能力控制及决策透明度；基础设施层关注模型路由、执行控制平面及模型来源验证；系统层关注由合法单步操作组合而成的整体违规行为；生态层关注供应链完整性、溯源、问责和端到端可观测性。

Result: 核心结论是，智能体风险并非仅来自模型输出错误，也来自跨步骤、跨主体和跨组件交互所产生的系统性违规。现有架构在行为隔离、模型来源证明、跨组织信任、全链路审计及安全不变量验证方面仍存在显著缺口。

Conclusion: 可信自主智能体需要将安全设计为架构、通信协议与运行时机制中可验证、可审计和可执行的内生属性，而不是附加性的行为指导。未来应围绕系统级约束验证、最小能力授权、身份与来源证明、运行时监控及全链路责任追踪构建统一安全框架。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly used to execute consequential tasks in environments governed by operational constraints, organizational policies, regulatory requirements, and technical standards. Their safety is therefore determined not by the correctness of individual actions, but by whether their overall behavior remains consistent with the rules and invariants of the systems in which they operate. As large language model (LLM)-based agents become more autonomous and increasingly delegate tasks across organizational boundaries, securing them evolves from a single challenge into a broad and interconnected landscape spanning the entire agentic stack. At the single-agent level, untrusted inputs through prompts, memory, retrieved knowledge, and tool interfaces create attack surfaces. In multi-agent settings, delegation and communication introduce challenges related to identity, trust, capability control, and decision transparency, while the underlying model routing and execution control plane remains vulnerable to manipulation and to unverified model provenance. Perhaps the most fundamental challenge is behavioral containment: sequences of individually permissible actions may collectively violate system-level constraints and safety invariants. At the broader level, supply-chain integrity, provenance, accountability, and end-to-end observability remain largely open problems. A common principle unifies these directions: security must become a verifiable property of the architectures, protocols, and runtimes that govern agent behavior, rather than an optional layer of guidance. Charting these challenges provides a roadmap toward trustworthy autonomous agent deployment.

</details>


### [133] [Neuro-Evolved Heuristics for Variable Gapped Common Subsequence Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00888)
*Marko Djukanović,Christian Blum,Aleksandar Kartelj,Saso Dzeroski,Ziga Zebec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向可变间隔最长公共子序列问题的神经进化启发式方法，通过神经网络学习引导多源束搜索，并与最佳人工启发式融合，在合成与真实数据实例上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多序列可变间隔最长公共子序列问题具有较高求解难度，现有方法主要依赖人工设计的束搜索启发式规则。这类规则通常针对特定数据分布，泛化性与鲁棒性有限，因此需要自动学习更有效的数据驱动搜索策略。

Method: 作者以预定义结构的神经网络表示启发式评分函数，采用遗传算法优化网络权重，形成神经进化框架。训练过程在迭代式多源束搜索中交替进行候选权重优化与搜索效果评估；网络不直接构造解，而是为搜索状态或候选分支提供引导评分。进一步地，作者将学习得到的启发式分数与表现最优的人工启发式分数进行集成，构建混合启发式，并嵌入迭代式多源束搜索框架。

Result: 实验表明，神经进化启发式及其与人工规则结合的集成版本，在合成基准实例和带有数据驱动间隔约束的新真实实例上均优于已有方法。结果说明，学习型搜索引导能够提升多序列问题中的束搜索质量。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了神经进化可用于自动设计组合优化中的搜索启发式。其主要贡献在于：将神经网络评分、遗传权重优化与迭代多源束搜索结合，并通过与人工启发式集成获得更强性能。方法的潜在价值在于减少手工规则设计，并提高针对不同实例分布的适应能力。

Abstract: This study addresses the Variable Gapped Longest Common Subsequence Problem (VGLCSP), a variant of the classical longest common subsequence problem with additional gap constraints and applications in sequence alignment and time-series analysis. While the two-sequence version has been widely studied using dynamic programming, the generalized multi-sequence form is usually solved with beam search-based heuristics, whose hand-crafted designs often lack robustness.
  To overcome this limitation, we propose a learning-based approach for automatically designing more effective data-driven heuristics. The heuristics are represented by a neural network with predefined architecture, whose weights are optimized by a genetic algorithm within a neuro-evolutionary framework. The learning process alternates between weight optimization and evaluation within an iterative multi-source beam search procedure, a state-of-the-art method for the problem. Rather than constructing solutions directly, the neural network learns to guide the search process, producing a neuro-evolved heuristic. We further introduce an ensemble heuristic that combines the scores of learned and the best-performing hand-crafted heuristic. Integrated into the iterative multi-source beam search framework, the resulting hybrid approach outperforms existing methods on both synthetic benchmark instances and newly introduced real-world instances with data-driven gap constraints.

</details>


### [134] [CADIR: A Cross-Backend Editable Intermediate Representation for Agentic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00891)
*Yu Liu,Jingzhe Ni,Yiming Chen,Junqi Huang,Ruofeng Tong,Min Tang,Peng Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CADIR是一种面向智能体的可执行CAD中间表示，通过构造图、几何签名匹配和构造图检索，实现跨CAD后端的可靠生成、重建与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD生成方法通常依赖特定后端的顺序脚本或静态几何，难以同时保留建模历史、稳定的拓扑引用和特征级可编辑性，也难以在不同CAD系统之间迁移。

Method: CADIR基于OCCT和OCP，提供显式、组合式建模操作及细粒度执行诊断。执行过程中记录操作、参数依赖、约束和拓扑选择，形成构造图。提出几何签名匹配方法，以适应参数变化和后端差异，识别对应的边与面，并在FreeCAD、SolidWorks和Fusion 360中重建原生可编辑特征历史。此外，提出面向文本和图像查询的构造图检索，支持完整图和子图检索。

Result: 实验表明，CADIR在几何保真度和执行可靠性方面优于现有CAD表示方法；构造图检索能够进一步提升模型生成质量；跨后端编辑可在多个CAD环境中实现可靠的模型重建及重建后的编辑。

Conclusion: CADIR通过将可执行操作、依赖关系和拓扑信息统一到构造图中，增强了CAD模型的可追溯性、可编辑性与跨系统迁移能力，为基于智能体的跨后端CAD生成和编辑提供了有效基础。

Abstract: Large language models have made it possible to generate executable computer-aided design (CAD) programs from natural-language descriptions or images. However, existing methods represent modeling processes as backend-specific sequential scripts with implicit dependencies or as static geometry, making it difficult to simultaneously preserve construction history, stable topological references, and feature-level editability across different CAD systems. We present CADIR, an agent-friendly executable intermediate representation for CAD generation and cross-backend editing. Built on the OCCT geometry kernel via OCP, CADIR provides explicit, compositional modeling operations and fine-grained execution diagnostics. During program execution, CADIR records modeling operations, parameter dependencies, constraints, and topology selections in a construction graph. To enable reliable cross-backend reconstruction, we introduce Geometric Signature Matching, which identifies corresponding edges and faces despite parameter changes and backend differences, allowing adapters to reconstruct native editable feature histories in FreeCAD, SolidWorks, and Fusion 360. Building on this representation, we further propose a construction-graph retrieval method for text and image queries that supports both full-graph and subgraph retrieval, enabling agents to leverage complete models and modeling substructures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CADIR achieves higher geometric fidelity and execution reliability than existing CAD representations, that construction-graph retrieval further improves model generation quality, and that cross-backend editing enables reliable model reconstruction and post-reconstruction editing across multiple CAD environments.

</details>


### [135] [Modeling Social Dynamics with an LLM-Enabled Agent Based Network-Dynamic (LAND) Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00929)
*Lynnette Hui Xian Ng,Kathleen M. Carley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出GhostField混合式LLM智能体网络动力学（LAND）架构，并在AuraSight虚构场景中模拟大规模社交互动，以检验社会协调、叙事传播与影响力等集体现象是否能够涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会模拟往往难以同时刻画个体异质性、网络结构变化与语言叙事互动。作者旨在验证由大语言模型驱动的智能体社会模拟，能否生成具有分析价值的多层次社会动力学。

Method: 研究构建AuraSight情境，模拟314,244个异质性网络智能体与人类角色围绕虚构歌曲创作竞赛，在30天内交换529,327条消息。分析从四个层面展开：自我网络拓扑、语义网络演化、协调动力学和影响力动力学。

Result: 模拟中观察到可辨识的社会动力学模式，包括网络关系与叙事内容的共同演化、协调行为的形成及影响力分布的变化。结果表明，集体协调和影响力并非单个智能体属性的直接累积，而是网络拓扑与叙事交换递归耦合的产物。

Conclusion: GhostField及其AuraSight实验表明，LLM驱动的智能体网络模拟能够生成一定程度上的涌现社会过程。论文的主要贡献在于提供了一个将网络结构、语言互动和集体行为联合建模的框架；但摘要未说明与真实数据的验证方式、基线比较及生成行为的外部效度，这些将是评估其可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Social dynamics encode the process in which individual network and discourse interactions aggregate into collective influence, narrative dominance and coordinate behavior. This paper uses the the GhostField architecture, a hybrid LLM-Enabled Agent Based Network-Dynamic (LAND) model as a social simulation framework to build the AuraSight scenario. In the AuraSight scenario, 314,244 heterogeneous cyber social agents and human actors exchange 529,327 messages over 30 days surrounding a fictional international song-writing contest. We methodologically examine emergent social dynamics across four analytical layers: ego-network topology, semantic network evolution, coordination dynamics and influence dynamics. Our results show how generated social simulations do also produce social dynamics, and how the dynamics of coordination and influence emerge not from individual agents but from the recursive interaction between network topology and narrative exchange.

</details>


### [136] [PMMC: Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation for Long-Term LVLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00962)
*Jingyu Sun,Yan Lin,Yuyang Xue,Yifan Wang,Zhengtao Yao,Rui Qian,Zefeng Xu,Jiachen Li,Xianyang Liu,Jiancheng Pan,Jingyuan Sun,Syed Murtuza Baker,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出前瞻式多模态记忆编译框架，将部分记忆推理从查询时转移到记忆巩固时，通过预测潜在问题、规划多模态证据路径并验证其可行性，构建结构化问题库，从而提升长期视觉语言代理的回答质量与视觉证据召回率，同时降低查询阶段的令牌消耗和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统通常将视觉经历压缩为文本摘要，或采用静态的“检索后推理”流程，难以处理图像—文本绑定、随时间更新的信息以及细粒度视觉细节，并且在查询时计算开销较高。因此，需要一种能够提前组织多模态记忆、增强证据可用性并提高查询效率的方法。

Method: 框架包含三个核心模块：Questioner 根据累积的多模态交互预测未来潜在问题；Planner 针对问题编译条件化的多模态记忆程序，规划信息检索与证据组合路径；Doubter 对规划出的证据路径进行验证，判断其是否足以支持预测答案。经过验证的问题—程序对被组织成结构化问题库，用于查询阶段的高效路由与证据检索。实验还通过消融研究分析自反馈、动态规划、原始图像访问权限和问题库覆盖率的作用。

Result: 在多模态长期记忆基准上，该方法提升了答案质量和视觉证据召回率，并减少了查询时的令牌使用量与延迟。消融实验表明，自反馈、动态规划、保留原始图像访问能力以及更高的问题库覆盖率会影响系统性能。摘要未提供具体数据、基准名称或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 前瞻式多模态记忆编译能够有效改善长期视觉语言代理的记忆组织与查询效率。通过在记忆巩固阶段预先生成并验证问题相关的多模态程序，系统可以更可靠地完成需要视觉细节、跨模态绑定和时序信息更新的任务。不过，其性能可能依赖问题库覆盖范围及预测问题与真实查询之间的匹配程度。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LVLM agents to maintain consistency and integrate information across extended multimodal interactions. Existing agent memory systems, however, often reduce visual experiences into textual summaries or rely on static retrieve-then-reason pipelines, which are inefficient at query time and brittle when questions require image-text binding, temporal updates, or visual details. We propose Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation, a framework that shifts part of the memory reasoning process from query time to memory consolidation time. Given accumulated multimodal interactions, a Questioner predicts future question candidates, a Planner compiles question-conditioned multimodal memory programs, and a Doubter verifies whether the planned evidence path can support the predicted answer. The verified question-program pairs form a structured question bank for efficient query-time routing and evidence retrieval. Experiments on multimodal long-term memory benchmarks show that our method improves answer quality and visual evidence recall while reducing query-time token and latency costs. Extensive ablations analyze the effects of self-feedback, dynamic planning, raw-image access, and question bank coverage.

</details>


### [137] [TrajWiki: Source-Grounded Memory Trajectories for Long-Horizon Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00967)
*Jingyu Sun,Yuyang Xue,Mingyang Li,Zhengtao Yao,Jiachen Li,Yang Cui,Wenhao Cai,Haozhe Liu,Fangying Wang,Magdalene Katharina Montgomery,Syed Murtuza Baker,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出TrajWiki，一种面向长时程对话智能体的轨迹式外部记忆框架。其将记忆建模为可追溯、可更新且保留演化历史的轨迹，并通过Memory Wiki进行结构化索引，从而提升检索效率、答案证据性与诊断透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型智能体通常将信息存为孤立记录或可覆盖状态，难以保留信息的来源、修改过程、冲突关系及过时状态。这会导致长时程对话中的记忆碎片化、检索成本高、错误难诊断，以及生成回答缺乏可验证依据。

Method: TrajWiki采用两层记忆机制：第一层为记忆轨迹，将每项记忆表示为基于来源的演化过程，并利用不可变的情节快照保存历史；记忆更新通过ADD、REVISE和DEPRECATE等声明级操作执行，以显式记录新增、修订和弃用。第二层为Memory Wiki，将对话历史逐步编译为相互关联的结构化页面，覆盖实体、事件、数值、主题及冲突等关键信息。推理时，系统先定位相关Wiki页面，再访问链接的记忆轨迹、对应快照及原始消息，最终生成具备证据支撑的回答。

Result: 在LoCoMo和MedMT基准上，TrajWiki在开源与闭源大语言模型骨干下均提升了长时程对话表现。除性能改善外，框架还增强了记忆演化、检索失败原因和答案生成依据的可解释性与可诊断性。

Conclusion: TrajWiki的核心贡献在于将记忆从静态存储单元转变为具有来源与版本历史的动态轨迹，并以Wiki式中间层降低复杂对话记忆的组织与检索难度。该设计有助于构建更可靠、可审计且适用于长期交互的对话智能体。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown strong capabilities in generating coherent and contextually appropriate responses, yet robust long-horizon dialogue remains limited by the lack of external memory that is traceable, updatable, and diagnostically transparent. Existing memory-augmented agents often store memories as isolated records or overwritable states, making it difficult to preserve how information originates, evolves, conflicts, or becomes obsolete over time. We propose TrajWiki, a trajectory-based memory framework for long-horizon conversational agents. Instead of treating memory as static entries, TrajWiki represents each memory as a source-grounded evolution trajectory, maintained through immutable episodic snapshots and claim-level operations such as ADD, REVISE, and DEPRECATE. To reduce fragmentation and retrieval cost, TrajWiki further introduces Memory Wiki, a persistent intermediate layer that incrementally compiles dialogue history into structured and interlinked wiki pages capturing salient entities, events, quantities, topics, and conflicts. At inference time, queries are routed hierarchically from relevant wiki pages to linked memory trajectories, then to corresponding snapshots and source messages for evidence-grounded answer synthesis. Experiments on LoCoMo and MedMT show that TrajWiki improves long-horizon dialogue performance across both open-source and closed-source LLM backbones, while providing greater interpretability and diagnostic visibility into memory evolution, retrieval failures, and answer generation.

</details>


### [138] [PROGRESS: Coverage-guided RL to Train Search-augmented LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00969)
*Sudipta Paul,Vijay Srinivasan,Vivek Kulkarni,Aounon Kumar,Yashas Malur Saidutta,Wenbo Li,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PROGRESS，一种面向搜索增强型大语言模型智能体的强化学习训练方法。其核心是在结果级奖励之外，引入教师模型生成的“关键搜索查询”作为覆盖奖励，以显式指导复杂问题的查询分解与搜索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索增强智能体通常依赖结果级奖励训练，虽然能优化最终答案质量，但对中间搜索行为提供的监督较弱，尤其难以约束模型是否将复杂问题拆解为充分、必要且可检索的子查询。因此，模型可能获得正确结果却采用低效或不完整的搜索路径。

Method: PROGRESS使用冻结的教师模型将复杂原始问题分解为一组关键搜索查询，并将这些查询视为理想搜索覆盖目标。策略模型在训练中自行生成分解查询和执行搜索，其奖励不仅考虑最终任务结果，还衡量生成查询对教师关键查询的覆盖程度。该覆盖奖励被整合进R1风格的强化学习框架，以较轻量的方式提供查询分解层面的过程指导，而不需要对每一步推理或搜索轨迹进行稠密人工标注。

Result: 实验表明，加入覆盖引导奖励后的强化学习能够提升整体任务表现。结果支持以下判断：相较于仅使用最终结果奖励，显式监督查询分解和搜索覆盖性能够改善搜索增强智能体的决策质量。摘要未给出具体数据集、指标、基线方法或数值增幅，因此无法进一步评估提升幅度、统计显著性及跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作强调，搜索增强型智能体的训练不应只优化最终答案，还应关注搜索策略本身。PROGRESS通过教师驱动的关键查询覆盖奖励，为复杂查询分解提供可扩展的弱过程监督，是连接结果级强化学习与细粒度过程监督之间的一种折中方案。其潜在局限在于奖励质量依赖教师分解的正确性与完整性，且关键查询覆盖并不必然等同于最优搜索效率或最终答案可靠性。

Abstract: Existing search-augmented LLM agents are trained using Reinforcement Learning to boost its reasoning capabilities. However, these approaches primarily rely on outcome-level rewards, which provide little supervision over search behavior and overlook agent's ability to decompose complex queries properly. To mitigate this issue, we propose PROGRESS which utilizes teacher-guided coverage reward to explicitly shape decomposed query generation of the policy model. During training, frozen teacher models are used to decompose complex queries into essential search queries. These essential search queries are utilized to guide the search behavior of the policy model. Integrated into an R1-style training framework, our approach provides lightweight guidance over query decomposition decisions without dense process-level supervision. Experiments show that coverage-guided RL improves overall task performance, highlighting the importance of explicitly supervising search strategies in agentic LLMs.

</details>


### [139] [Search-GRT: Guided Retrieval Training of Search Agents to Optimize for Complex Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00974)
*Aounon Kumar,Sudipta Paul,Vivek Kulkarni,Vijay Srinivasan,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Guided Retrieval Training（GRT），通过在强化学习训练阶段利用标注相关文档约束检索空间，缓解多跳问答中检索错误级联与奖励稀疏问题，从而提升搜索型语言模型的问答准确率和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答要求模型将复杂问题分解为多个子查询、检索跨来源证据并综合作答。早期检索偏差会逐步传递并放大，导致最终答案错误。现有基于强化学习的搜索训练虽有效，但终局问答奖励通常稀疏，难以为子查询生成和中间检索决策提供稳定、充分的学习信号。

Method: GRT在强化学习训练中引入基于真实标注信息的检索引导：模型的检索过程被限制在经过筛选的相关文档集合内。该设计降低了训练阶段检索空间的噪声，使模型更容易获得与正确证据链相关的反馈，并学习生成更准确的子查询、选择有效证据及完成多文档答案整合。论文将GRT与Search-R1等既有方法进行比较，并在多类问答任务上评测。

Result: 实验显示，GRT在广泛的问答任务上相对现有方法取得稳定提升，其中在多跳问答任务上的性能提升超过40%。此外，GRT能够以更少的训练步数达到更优的问答表现，表明其提高了强化学习训练的样本效率与收敛效率。

Conclusion: GRT表明，利用训练数据中的相关文档标注对检索策略进行受控引导，可有效缓解搜索型语言模型强化学习中的稀疏奖励问题，并显著改善复杂多跳推理场景下的检索与回答质量。其核心价值在于以训练期的受限检索换取更强监督信号；实际部署时仍需关注模型在开放检索环境中的泛化能力，以及对高质量相关文档标注的依赖。

Abstract: The effective use of search engines by large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge, particularly in complex, multi-hop question-answering (MHQA) tasks. These tasks require the model to decompose questions into subqueries, retrieve relevant information, and synthesize answers from multiple sources, often leading to cascading errors due to poor retrieval in early stages. Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise in improving LLMs' search capabilities, but it often suffers from sparse rewards during training, hindering the model's ability to learn effectively. To address these challenges, we introduce Guided Retrieval Training (GRT), a novel method that improves the performance of a search agent by restricting the retrieval process during RL training using ground truth information. By focusing on a curated set of relevant documents, GRT provides the model with a stronger learning signal, mitigating the problem of sparse rewards and improving its ability to generate accurate subqueries and synthesize correct answers. Our experimental results demonstrate that GRT achieves consistent performance improvements over existing methods, such as Search-R1, across a wide range of question-answering (QA) tasks. Notably, GRT excels in MHQA tasks, achieving over 40% improvements in performance. Additionally, GRT enhances training efficiency by achieving better QA performance with fewer training steps.

</details>


### [140] [Passing Coarse Marginal Checks Can Be Cheap: Persona Mixtures and Imprecise Treatment-Response Estimates in an LLM Persona Panel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00979)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文评估一组固定的、人格设定的轻量级 GPT-4.1 配置在重复策略任务中能否复现人类数据的边际模式。结果显示其在多数预注册条件均值标准上达标，但提示词措辞、位置和标签表征可显著改变行为；因此，该面板不能被视为已证明可替代人类研究参与者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是检验将大语言模型作为“合成研究参与者”时，单纯匹配人类边际反应是否足够。作者特别关注模型输出的变异来源、处理效应估计的不确定性，以及提示词表征变化是否会破坏行为测量的稳定性。

Method: 作者使用由16个固定人格提示配置构成的 GPT-4.1 面板，在重复策略任务中进行测试，并以预注册的广义参考条件均值作为验证准则。分析比较了不同不确定性假设下提示词间变异的占比，包括对称 Dirichlet 敏感性分析和有限机会插件估计；同时估计继续概率的总体处理对比及保守的同时95%区间。研究还通过措辞与位置操控、标签冲突操控检验文本表征控制，并对原始人格层面 p13 结果进行事后审查和零调用复分析。

Result: 该面板在4个重复任务单元中的3个满足预注册参考均值标准，唯一未达标项仅比下界低0.011。提示词索引的变异很强：在 Jeffreys α=0.5 下，提示词间变异占比中位数为63%—71%；在 α=1 下为47%—53%；有限机会插件估计则为85%—96%。继续概率的聚合处理差异为+0.083和+0.078，但保守同时95%区间分别为[-0.171,+0.330]和[-0.181,+0.330]，表明处理效应估计并不精确。措辞与位置调整可使基础配置中的合作从0/40变为37/40，标签冲突也显示出行为受表征方式控制。原始 p13 结果未进行前瞻性家族错误控制，而事后精确检验的统计功效不足，故 p13 只能作为后续复现目标。

Conclusion: 边际条件均值的通过不足以证明模型稳定地刻画目标处理反应，也不足以支持其可替代人类参与者。该研究表明，固定模型—提示词面板的结果高度依赖提示词、表征方式、误差控制和不确定性建模。公开复现材料验证了4,916次确认性运行且未进行实时模型调用，但结论仅适用于该固定面板，不能推广为模型具有人类可替代性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as synthetic research participants and are often validated by whether their marginal responses resemble human data. We study a fixed panel of sixteen lightweight persona-conditioned GPT-4.1 configurations in repeated strategic games. The panel met preregistered broad-reference condition-mean criteria in three of four repeated-game cells; the sole miss was 0.011 below the lower reference bound. Variation was strongly prompt-indexed, but its share depended on uncertainty assumptions: fixed-panel symmetric-Dirichlet sensitivities produced median between-prompt shares of 63%-71% under Jeffreys alpha=0.5 and 47%-53% under alpha=1, while finite-opportunity plug-in estimates were 85%-96%. Aggregate continuation-probability contrasts were +0.083 and +0.078, with conservative simultaneous 95% intervals [-0.171, +0.330] and [-0.181, +0.330]. The treatment jointly changed the continuation process and its textual representation. A separate wording-and-position operation shifted cooperation from 0/40 to 37/40 in the bare configuration, and a label conflict also revealed representation control. The original persona-level p13 result was not prospectively family-controlled, while a post-adjudication exact gate was structurally underpowered; p13 is therefore a replication target rather than a finding. External review exposed family-error, dependence, construct, and boundary-uncertainty defects, and zero-call reanalysis changed the interpretation without rewriting the historical record. The registered marginal criteria could be passed without precisely estimating the treatment-response object. A public capsule verifies 4,916 confirmatory Phase 3-5 runs with no live model calls. The results concern one fixed model-prompt panel and do not establish human substitutability.

</details>


### [141] [Auditing Discovery Claims: A Two-Sided Criterion for Agentic Science, with the Negative Side Decidable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00981)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用于审计自改进科学智能体能力声明的“双侧审计”框架：一侧通过形式化证明界定旧验证器无法表达的能力上限，另一侧使用未参与优化的外部判定器检验真实迁移。结果显示，单一可被适配的预测器会严重夸大RNA设计能力；部分智能体生成程序在外部评审下优于人工程序且计算成本更低，但尚未识别出可迁移的因果机制。作者同时发现其自身系统的效果很大部分可能来自任务集的随机可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进系统通常以基准分数提升、描述长度门槛或显著性检验来支持“新能力”声明，但这些证据无法区分真实能力增益、更多搜索预算、验证器变化，以及对存在误差的评估预言机的过拟合。论文旨在建立更严格的审计方法，使能力提升必须相对于智能体自身既有状态定义，并能排除验证器漏洞和计算投入差异造成的表观收益。

Method: 作者构建双侧审计。负侧审计先离线证明：无伪结的RNA结构预言机无法表示交叉碱基对，因此其能力范围存在精确上界。正侧审计则以未被系统优化过的多个预测器和外部裁决器评估候选设计，并采用同一目标上的配对比较、目标簇聚类统计和显著性检验。研究还比较智能体撰写的程序与人工程序的外部泛化表现及预言机调用次数；最后通过匹配的无向搜索、随机序列基线和搜索无关探针，分解作者系统表观提升中由搜索及任务可解性贡献的部分。

Result: 针对60个含交叉配对的RNA目标，一个为特定预测器优化的无求解器算子在该预测器下成功43个，明显高于上下文无关能力下界的0个；但在三个预测器共同检验时仅1个保留。对于原先成功的43个目标，该算子从未见过的预测器仅确认2个设计，而最小自由能求解器确认26个，配对检验p=8e-7，表明内部预言机指标无法识别这一失真。在951个配对单元中，6个前沿模型里有2个产生的算子未超时，并在外部裁决下取得0.293的迁移表现，高于作者人工程序的0.095；目标簇聚类区间为[+0.108,+0.297]，p=5e-5，同时预言机调用量低4.6至10倍。机制层面，7个候选解释均未能改变统计量，故尚无可迁移机制证据。评估面板本身也有限制：3个预测器共享最近邻热力学参数，其中两个的一致性κ=0.673。作者系统的匹配无向搜索效应为零，而搜索无关探针显示其主要效应中84%发生在随机序列本已能解决的目标上。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将能力审计从“内部指标是否提升”转向“是否超出旧验证器的形式化边界、能否通过独立外部裁决、是否并非由额外计算购买、是否存在可迁移机制”四类问题。证据支持前两项中的部分结论：外部可验证的性能差异存在，且可在更低计算成本下实现；但无法支持机制性能力发现。论文也强调，评估预言机共享偏差与任务集随机可解性可显著误导结论，因此自改进系统的能力声明应同时报告验证器边界、跨验证器存活率、计算匹配结果、评估器相关性及随机基线。

Abstract: When a self-improving AI-for-science system claims a new capability, the evidence is usually a benchmark delta, a description-length gate, or a p-value. None separates a real gain from extra search, from a changed verifier, or from adaptation to a fallible oracle. We build a two-sided audit whose negative side is a formal fact: a pseudoknot-free oracle provably cannot represent a crossing base pair, so the prior verifier's range is bounded exactly, offline, before any run. "New" is relative to the agent's prior self, never to the base model. First, how far a single fallible oracle can inflate a capability claim. An invented, solver-free operator solves 43/60 crossing RNA targets under the predictor it optimizes, above a context-free floor of 0/60; under three predictors, 1/60 survives. Paired on the same 43 targets, a predictor the operator never saw confirms 2 of its designs against 26 for a minimum-free-energy solver (p = 8e-7). No statistic computed from the system and its own oracle sees that gap. Second, agent-written procedures can beat a human-written one under a judge no objective can flatter, at a fraction of the compute. Of six frontier models, the two whose operators ran without timeouts carry over at 0.293 against our 0.095 (n = 951 paired units, target-clustered [+0.108, +0.297], p = 5e-5) while spending 4.6-10x fewer oracle calls. Three rungs: difference under an outside adjudicator (reached), not bought with compute (reached, both directions), mechanism identified and transferable (not reached; seven candidates tested, none moves the statistic). The ceiling is the panel itself: its three predictors share nearest-neighbour thermodynamic parameters, two agreeing at kappa = 0.673. The audit is as unsparing about our own system: matched undirected search is an exact zero, and a search-free probe puts 84% of our headline effect on targets a random sequence already solves.

</details>


### [142] [SCHEDBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Constraint Faithfulness in Natural-Language Combinatorial Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00991)
*Shrenil Shaun Sharma,Avi Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCHEDBench是一个用于评估大语言模型在不同自然语言表述下是否保持调度约束忠实性的基准，覆盖四类调度问题、1,132个实例和13个模型。结果显示，语义等价的表述变化会显著降低模型生成方案的可行性，其中约束顺序变化最敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调度评测通常关注模型能否解决单一表述的问题，但较少检验模型是否真正理解约束语义，以及在措辞、句法和约束排列变化后是否保持一致行为。该研究旨在系统评估大语言模型对组合调度约束的语义鲁棒性。

Method: 基于经典调度实例和求解器验证的可行性、最优性结果，构建SCHEDBench。基准覆盖作业车间调度、单模式与多模式资源约束项目调度、护士排班和课程表编排，包含不同难度的1,132个实例。研究通过领域模板、主题实体、词汇与句法改写，以及约束层面的表述变化生成自然语言问题，并使用参考解验证可行性和目标最优性；随后评估13个前沿及开放权重大语言模型在匹配实例上的表现。

Result: 模型对语义等价的自然语言表述并不具有可靠的不变性。表述变化会降低生成调度方案的可行性，并在匹配实例上导致超过噪声水平的硬约束违规变化。在单独考察的变化因素中，约束重排序表现出最明确的超噪声敏感性。

Conclusion: 大语言模型尚未稳定掌握与表面形式无关的调度约束语义，其调度能力容易受到自然语言表达方式影响。SCHEDBench为评估此类组合约束忠实性和表述鲁棒性提供了系统化工具，也表明模型在实际调度应用前需要更严格的约束验证与鲁棒性测试。

Abstract: This paper introduces SCHEDBench, a natural-language benchmark for evaluating combinatorial scheduling constraint faithfulness under surface-form variation. Grounded in canonical scheduling instances and solver-derived feasibility and optimality, SCHEDBench assesses whether large language models (LLMs) generate schedules with the same constraint-feasible behavior across varied natural-language (NL) surface forms. SCHEDBench spans 1,132 instances across job-shop scheduling problems (JSP), single and multi-mode resource-constrained project scheduling problems (RCPSP), nurse rostering/scheduling, and curriculum timetabling problems of varying difficulty. Instances are templated into natural language problems using domain-specific templates, themed entities, lexical-syntactic template rephrasing, and constraint-level surface-form variation, with reference solutions verified for feasibility and objective optimality. Across thirteen frontier and open-weight LLMs, we find that models are not reliably invariant to semantically equivalent renderings of the same scheduling problem. Surface-form variation reduces feasibility and induces above-noise shifts in per-instance hard-constraint violations on matched instances. Among the tested isolated axes, constraint reordering yields the clearest above-noise sensitivity.

</details>


### [143] [Judging Is Not Enumerating: Silent Omissions in LLM-Authored Acceptable Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01000)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Peiji Long,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究语言模型从“答题者”转变为“评测者”时的可靠性，发现模型虽然较擅长判断候选项是否正确，却明显不擅长一次性生成完整、正确的测试集、答案键、评分规则或奖励函数。核心缺陷是系统性遗漏应被纳入正确集合的项目，而非知识不足或无法表达规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型被用于构建自动评测、代码测试、奖励建模和强化学习验证器，其生成的评测标准会直接定义其他系统的“正确性”。论文旨在检验这种评测者角色是否具备足够能力，并识别生成式评测标准失效的具体机制及可行缓解措施。

Method: 作者在四类参考构造上比较“成员资格判断”和“集合/测试集作者生成”两种能力：两个具有完整有限真值的任务、HumanEval+/MBPP+等具有强化可执行参考的代码任务，以及真值明确不完整的WordNet词汇任务。实验覆盖约24倍参数规模范围，并设置“直接输出判定谓词而非枚举其扩展集合”的对照实验，以区分知识与形式化能力不足和集合实例化能力不足。进一步通过植入过度纳入与遗漏错误来测试审计能力，并在43,227项生产部署及RLVR场景中评估下游影响。最后检验探针门控和基于参考执行结果的自动修复策略。

Result: 模型的判别能力显著优于生成完整评测集合的能力：在算法构造中，判别相对作者生成的F1优势为0.29至0.34，且随模型规模增加未消失；在代码任务中，模型判别F1为0.74至0.90，但其生成的测试套件仅接受19%至42%的预言机正确解。若要求模型输出判定谓词，同一模型F1约为0.99，表明问题不在知识缺失或规则描述能力，而在将规则所诱导的集合完整实例化。主要错误为遗漏，且难以被审计：模型识别植入的过度纳入错误的概率是识别遗漏的6至7倍，生产部署中遗漏型失败比例约为10:1。用于RLVR时，作者生成的答案键相对精确预言机造成1.9个百分点准确率损失，相对WordNet参考造成18.5个百分点损失。用已知正确探针筛选可将错误拒绝率从58%至92%降至最多5%，但仅保留5%至39%的套件；将错误期望值重写为参考执行输出，则可使可用产出率提升3.3至10.6倍。

Conclusion: 不应默认语言模型一次性生成的评测器、测试集或答案键可作为可靠真值来源。模型能够理解并表达规范，却常无法穷尽规范所覆盖的正确实例，导致以遗漏为主的隐蔽性假阴性，并可能损害自动评测和强化学习训练。实际部署应优先使用可执行参考、独立验证、已知正确样例门控及基于参考结果的修复流程；相比仅审查显式错误，更需要针对难以观察的遗漏建立系统性覆盖与校验机制。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly promoted from examinees to examiners: they write the test suites, answer keys, rubrics, and reward functions that define correctness for other systems. We measure the capability that role assumes and find it lacking under the protocol the role is usually deployed with, one-shot greedy authoring with no test-time reasoning. Across four reference constructions - two with complete finite truth, one with a hardened executable reference (HumanEval+/MBPP+), one with an explicitly incomplete lexical reference (WordNet) - models judge whether a candidate belongs far better than they author the set itself. On the incompleteness-proof algorithmic construction the gap is +0.34 to +0.29 F1 over a 24x parameter range and does not close; on executable code, models judging at F1 0.74-0.90 author suites admitting only 19-42% of oracle-correct solutions. A control locates the deficit: asked to emit the predicate rather than its extension, the same models reach F1 about 0.99. The failure is not missing knowledge or an inability to specify, but an inability to materialise the region a specification induces. The dominant error is omission, which resists audit: an over-inclusion is a token a reviewer can challenge, a missing member an absence whose discovery is the authoring problem itself. Models detect planted over-inclusions 6-7x more often than planted omissions, and a production deployment of 43,227 items fails omission-first at 10:1. Wired into RLVR, an authored key costs 1.9 points of accuracy against an exact oracle and 18.5 WordNet-relative (six paired seeds, p=0.031). Gating authored verifiers on a known-correct probe cuts false rejection from 58-92% to at most 5%, but keeps only 5-39% of suites. Repairing them instead, by rewriting each wrong expected value to what a reference execution returns, raises yield 3.3-10.6x across four author families.

</details>


### [144] [Toward Fine-Grained Forgetting:Attribute Unlearning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01008)
*Junkai Lin,Junkai Chen,Siqi Hou,Yuhao He,Ruiqi Liu,Chenhan Jin,Shengze Xu,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“属性级多模态大语言模型遗忘”任务与基准，并通过无需训练的CLRP框架，在删除指定属性信息的同时尽量保留同一对象的非目标信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私遗忘评测多以完整档案或整体身份信息为删除单位，难以覆盖实际中仅需删除某一具体属性的需求。属性级删除更困难，因为目标属性与应保留属性常共享身份相关和视觉证据，易导致目标信息残留泄露或非目标能力受损。

Method: 作者构建属性级MLLM遗忘基准，覆盖长文本、数值和短文本目标，不同遗忘比例及多类提问方式。提出Causal Localization and Retain-Aware Projection（CLRP）：先利用激活修补定位对目标属性泄露具有因果作用的网络层；再在该层执行保留感知投影，移除与目标属性相关的表示子空间，同时约束保留同一对象的非目标证据。该方法为轻量级、免训练方案。

Result: 基准评测显示，现有遗忘方法在属性级场景中的遗忘—保留权衡不稳定：要么仍能恢复目标属性，要么显著损害同一对象的保留属性。跨多种主流MLLM架构和不同参数规模的实验表明，CLRP能够更有效地抑制目标属性披露，并较好维持通用能力及同一对象的非目标信息。

Conclusion: 属性级遗忘是比档案级删除更贴近实际需求、也更具挑战性的MLLM遗忘问题。通过因果层定位与保留感知表示投影，CLRP为无需重新训练的细粒度知识删除提供了有效路径；其核心价值在于改善目标删除与同一身份相关信息保留之间的平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exhibit strong vision--language capabilities but may also memorize and disclose sensitive information. Machine unlearning seeks to remove designated knowledge without retraining from scratch while preserving general utility. Existing privacy-oriented benchmarks primarily adopt profile-level deletion, whereas practical requests are often finer grained: a model should forget a specified attribute while retaining non-sensitive information about the same identity. We therefore introduce attribute-level MLLM unlearning as a finer-grained task and construct a benchmark spanning long-text, numeric, and short-text targets, multiple forget ratios, and diverse question types. Our evaluation reveals that target and retained attributes share identity-specific and visual evidence, making selective forgetting susceptible to residual leakage or collateral degradation; accordingly, existing methods exhibit unstable forgetting--retention trade-offs in this setting. To address this challenge, we propose Causal Localization and Retain-Aware Projection (CLRP), a lightweight training-free framework. CLRP uses activation patching to identify the layer that causally mediates target-attribute disclosure, then applies a retain-aware projection that removes the target-attribute subspace while preserving same-identity evidence. Experiments across multiple widely used MLLMs with distinct architectures and parameter scales demonstrate the effectiveness of CLRP.

</details>


### [145] [FactorJEPA: Factorizing Monolithic Futures into Layout-Agent-Interaction Channels for Crowded and Chaotic Global South Urban Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01049)
*Kapil Wanaskar,Gaytri Jena,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Vasu Sharma,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: World models have attracted significant attention for their ability to capture and predict the structure and dynamics of the physical world. In this emerging landscape, Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) offer a particularly compelling direction.
  We study a largely unexplored regime: populous, crowded, and chaotic Global South urban environments, which we call DENSEWORLD. Unlike the lower-density, lane-structured settings that dominate existing evaluations, these scenes exhibit soft spatial boundaries, extreme agent heterogeneity, persistent occlusion, and rapid social negotiation under mixed traffic. We introduce the first large-scale dataset for this regime: 1,000 hours of drive-through, walk-through, and aerial video across 22 cities. Existing JEPA formulations struggle to preserve dense interaction dynamics under heterogeneity and partial observability.
  We introduce FactorJEPA, which makes world structure a first-class predictive primitive. Rather than encoding the future in a monolithic latent, it composes layout, entities, and interactions, using a visibility gate and separated subspaces to preserve partially observed agents and discourage cross-factor shortcuts. FactorJEPA improves (i) future-latent accuracy (Future-frame L1), (ii) intervention-sensitive prediction (Causal L1), and (iii) robustness to reduced visual evidence (Mask-ratio slope), while exposing (iv) a reproducible motion-information trade-off (Motion cosine). Method rankings replicate across 2B and 1B V-JEPA 2.1 backbones, with rho = 0.895 to 0.978.
  We publicly release the DENSEWORLD-115k dataset (https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymousML123/denseworld-115k) and the surgery-trained FactorJEPA checkpoints (https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymousML123/factorjepa-outputs/tree/main/outputs/full/vjepa_2_1_vitg_1B/train/m09c_surgery_3stage_DI_diheavy_encoder).

</details>


### [146] [Control Under Compression: Reliability Frontiers for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01056)
*Yinghan Hou,Zongyou Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CompressAgent 基准，用于评估工具调用语言模型代理的控制上下文（ACC）压缩后是否仍具备可执行的运行可靠性，而非仅衡量文本压缩率或语义相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理的系统侧控制上下文包含工具定义、参数约束、策略、执行流程与恢复机制，压缩虽能降低输入成本和上下文占用，但传统提示词压缩评测无法判断压缩后的控制指令能否稳定驱动代理完成实际任务。

Method: 研究构建环境验证型基准，在9个独立ACC、3类任务、3个固定Qwen API模型标识符、6种上下文保留预算下进行15,525次运行；比较通用改写、分节压缩、义务感知压缩等方法，并以任务执行成功率及失败类型评估运行可靠性。

Result: 完整上下文的成功率为93.8%。保留75%上下文时，通用改写和分节压缩分别达到92.7%和92.4%，接近基线；在50%至35%区间，不同方法出现显著分化，35%时分节压缩、义务感知压缩和通用改写的成功率分别为47.0%、39.0%和19.9%。当保留比例降至25%至10%时，可执行协议普遍变得脆弱。可靠性还因ACC而显著不同，主要失败表现为工具执行错误与动作解析错误。

Conclusion: ACC压缩应被视为运行时可靠性优化问题，而非单纯的令牌削减任务。不存在适用于所有控制上下文的统一最优压缩器；实际部署应对每个ACC进行独立的环境验证与可靠性资格测试。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents are governed not only by task prompts but also by persistent system-side instructions that specify tools, arguments, policies, execution protocols, and recovery. Compressing these agent control contexts (ACCs) can reduce input cost and context use, yet existing prompt-compression evaluations do not reveal whether the resulting control remains operationally reliable. We introduce CompressAgent, an environment-verified benchmark for ACC compression across nine independently constructed ACCs, three task families, three fixed Qwen API model identifiers, six retained-context budgets, and 15,525 runs. We uncover a nonlinear, method-dependent reliability frontier. At 75% retained context, generic rewriting and section-based compression achieve 92.7% and 92.4% success, close to the 93.8% full-context baseline. Between 50% and 35%, methods diverge sharply; at 35%, section-based, obligation-aware, and generic rewriting achieve 47.0%, 39.0%, and 19.9%. At retained-context budgets from 25% to 10%, executable protocols become fragile. Reliability also varies substantially across ACCs, making universal compressor rankings inappropriate and motivating per-context qualification. Failure analysis shows that compression primarily surfaces as tool-execution and action-parsing errors. These findings recast ACC compression from token reduction into a runtime-reliability problem that must be evaluated through executable outcomes.

</details>


### [147] [Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals: Separating Matching and Content Retrieval Across Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01075)
*Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出角色解耦注意力残差（RD-AttnRes），将注意力中的查询-键路由与值路由分离，使“匹配位置”和“提取内容”可从不同深度的残差表示读取。在120M和343M模型、共10组配对实验中，该方法均降低验证负对数似然和困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Block Attention Residuals使用同一内容相关的深度混合同时构造查询、键和值输入，但查询和键负责决定注意力匹配位置，值负责决定检索的内容，两者功能不同。论文研究这两类决策是否应被强制依赖相同的网络深度。

Method: RD-AttnRes保留查询与键共享的深度路由，同时为值学习一个独立的、面向同一残差源集合的深度路由。若将两类路由查询绑定，则可精确退化为原始架构；解耦后每层仅额外增加一个模型宽度的向量，且不增加新的token间注意力计算。实验采用冻结的配对预训练协议，在FineWeb-Edu上以5个匹配随机种子训练120M和343M参数模型，每个模型训练预算为20亿token。

Result: RD-AttnRes在全部10次配对比较中均优于基线。120M模型的平均验证负对数似然下降0.0301，对应困惑度下降2.97%；343M模型平均下降0.0247，对应困惑度下降2.43%。早期训练预算控制实验表明，性能增益不能由额外参数量、重复执行路由或固定值路由单独解释。路由诊断还显示，查询-键与值的深度分布持续存在差异。

Conclusion: 在所评估的训练设置下，注意力匹配与内容检索受益于从残差层级中进行不同的深度读取。RD-AttnRes以极低额外参数和无额外token间注意力开销实现这种功能分离，并稳定改善语言建模性能。

Abstract: Depth-routing residual architectures allow Transformer layers to retrieve earlier representations instead of inheriting only the immediately preceding state. Existing Block Attention Residuals, however, use a single content-dependent depth mixture to construct the inputs to queries, keys, and values. This design couples two functionally different decisions: queries and keys determine where attention matches, whereas values determine what content is retrieved. We therefore ask whether matching and content retrieval should be forced to read from the same depth. We introduce Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals (RD-AttnRes), a minimal extension that shares one depth route between queries and keys while learning an independent value route over the same residual sources. Tying the two routing queries exactly recovers the parent architecture, while decoupling them adds only one model-width vector per layer and introduces no additional token-to-token attention operation. We evaluate RD-AttnRes using a frozen, paired pretraining protocol on FineWeb-Edu with five matched seeds for both 120M- and 343M-parameter models and a 2.0B-token training budget. RD-AttnRes improves validation negative log-likelihood in all 10 matched comparisons. The mean reductions are 0.0301 and 0.0247, corresponding to perplexity reductions of 2.97 percent and 2.43 percent at 120M and 343M parameters, respectively. Early-budget controls indicate that neither the additional parameter count, duplicated routing execution, nor a fixed value route reproduces the improvement. Routing diagnostics further reveal persistent divergence between the query-key and value depth distributions. These results suggest that, within the evaluated training regime, attention matching and content retrieval benefit from distinct reads over the residual hierarchy.

</details>


### [148] [Inter-Residue Geometry Attention for Antibody-Specific Epitope Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01092)
*Chuanliu Fan,Nan Yu,Junjie Wu,Guohong Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出LF3DRoPE，一种将蛋白质折叠后的三维局部几何直接作为注意力位置编码的抗体特异性表位预测方法，并在AsEP基准上取得最佳MCC表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表位预测方法通常以蛋白语言模型表示为基础，再额外引入图、表面或点云结构编码器，但注意力中的位置机制仍主要依赖一维序列顺序。由于抗体识别表位依赖CDR与抗原表面的三维互补性，作者希望以残基间真实的三维相对位移替代或补充序列位置偏移。

Method: 方法设计了Local-Frame 3D Rotary Position Encoding（LF3DRoPE）。该方法基于蛋白质骨架为各残基定义局部坐标系，将残基间位移表示在局部参考系中，并将连续的方向与距离几何信息直接注入Rotary Position Encoding注意力计算。局部坐标表达使模型对全局平移和旋转，即SE(3)变换保持不变，同时保留残基间的定向几何关系。

Result: 在AsEP数据集的ratio划分和epitope-group划分上，LF3DRoPE均获得了最优的MCC指标。消融实验表明，局部三维几何信息能够提供超出序列顺序注意力的有效信息；刚体变换测试验证了模型对任意全局坐标变换的稳定不变性。突变排序实验进一步显示，该方法能够表征抗原与特定抗体之间的结构兼容性。

Conclusion: LF3DRoPE证明了折叠后的残基三维几何可以作为注意力机制本身的位置编码，而非仅作为附加结构特征。该设计兼顾连续三维方向信息、全局SE(3)不变性和预测性能，为面向蛋白质相互作用与抗体表位识别的结构感知注意力建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Antibody-specific epitope prediction aims to identify which antigen residues are recognized by a given antibody, a task that depends on the three-dimensional complementarity between antibody CDRs and the antigen surface. Existing methods usually leverage PLM embeddings and inject structure through additional graph, surface, or point-cloud encoders, where the positional mechanism inside attention remains largely tied to one-dimensional sequence order. For proteins, the analogue of a token offset is not only sequence separation, but also the three-dimensional displacement between residues after folding. This raises a question, can folded residue geometry serve as the positional mechanism of attention itself? We propose Local-Frame 3D Rotary Position Encoding (LF3DRoPE), which expresses inter-residue displacements in backbone-defined local frames and injects them directly into rotary attention. This design preserves continuous directional geometry while ensuring invariance to global $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ transformations. On the AsEP benchmark, LF3DRoPE achieves state-of-the-art $\mathrm{MCC}$ on both ratio and epitope-group splits. Ablations and rigid transformation tests show that local three-dimensional geometry provides information beyond sequence-order attention while preserving invariance to arbitrary global coordinate systems. Mutation ranking results further indicate that LF3DRoPE captures antigen-specific structural compatibility.

</details>


### [149] [Fighting Fire with Fire: On the Feasibility of Protecting Exercises Against AI Cheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01112)
*Tobias Braun,Jonas Grebe,Louis Rethfeld,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用对抗性视觉扰动识别学生是否盲目依赖多模态生成式人工智能的方法，并评估其在黑盒模型条件下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能可能使学生将认知任务外包，造成能力幻觉并削弱独立推理。研究旨在保护教育练习、识别机械复制答案的行为。

Method: 构建带有视觉组件的多项选择题，在图像中加入细微对抗扰动，使多模态模型倾向于选择预先指定的错误选项。利用代理模型优化扰动，并在Claude、Gemini和ChatGPT等黑盒助手上测试。通过比较学生答案与模型诱导答案模式的匹配频率，结合统计假设检验建立识别方法。

Result: 实验表明，视觉扰动能够在一定程度上诱导多模态模型产生一致的指定错误答案，从而形成可用于检测的统计指纹。盲目复制模型答案的学生预计会更频繁地呈现该模式，但方法的有效性受模型差异、扰动迁移性和现实测试条件限制。

Conclusion: 对抗性机器学习可被重新用于教育场景中的机器辅助推理检测，通过利用模型脆弱性识别潜在的机械依赖。该范式具有应用前景，但仍需进一步验证跨模型稳定性、扰动隐蔽性、误报风险及实际部署可行性。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of generative AI enables students to outsource cognitive effort to increasingly capable assistants, creating an illusion of competence while undermining the independent reasoning that education aims to cultivate. We investigate whether adversarial machine learning can be repurposed to protect educational exercises against such corrosive reliance. Our approach uses multimodal multiple-choice questions whose visual components can be protected with subtle visual perturbations that steer AI solvers toward designated incorrect answers. These responses form a statistical fingerprint: students who blindly copy a solver reproduce the induced answer pattern more frequently than genuine students. We study the feasibility of this paradigm under realistic black-box assistant assumptions using three of the most common state-of-the-art multimodal language models: Anthropic's Claude, Google's Gemini, and OpenAI's ChatGPT. By using accessible surrogate models, we optimize adversarial perturbations that induce consistent response patterns. Those patterns enable principled detection through statistical hypothesis testing. These findings establish both the promise and the limitations of fighting machine-assisted reasoning with the vulnerabilities of the machines themselves.

</details>


### [150] [MA-HEAD-Net: Adaptive Rule-Guided Multi-Agent DRL for AoI Minimization in UAV-Assisted Emergency Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01128)
*Yixin Zhang,Zhuohui Yao,Wenchi Cheng,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究无人机辅助应急通信中的信息年龄最小化问题，针对异构业务、突发到达、有限码长传输及无人机移动控制等耦合因素，提出结合规则先验与多智能体深度强化学习的MA-HEAD-Net框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应急通信任务对信息新鲜度高度敏感，过时信息可能降低调度与控制效果。现有方法难以同时处理长短数据包业务的时延差异、突发流量、传输时长与可靠性耦合，以及多无人机轨迹和资源调度的联合决策问题。

Method: 作者以马尔可夫调制泊松过程描述突发数据包到达，以有限码长理论刻画传输时长、成功完成概率和AoI演化之间的关系；设计嵌入微时隙的调度机制，并自适应选择检查点间隔，以协调可容忍时延的长包与紧急短包。随后将无人机轨迹控制、用户调度和检查点间隔选择建模为多智能体决策问题，提出MA-HEAD-Net。该方法通过门控多头策略网络融合通信领域规则先验与数据驱动策略，其中自适应门控针对不同子任务调整规则逻辑与学习逻辑的贡献，并基于多智能体近端策略优化进行联合训练。

Result: 仿真表明，MA-HEAD-Net相较代表性多智能体深度强化学习基线具有更高的策略形成效率；在动态无人机辅助通信场景中，其AoI低于学习型方法和启发式方法。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将通信规则先验显式融入多智能体强化学习，提升了联合轨迹、调度和传输决策的学习效率与信息新鲜度性能。其核心贡献在于面向异构紧急业务构建了有限码长感知的AoI优化模型，并利用自适应门控机制实现规则知识与学习策略的有效协同。

Abstract: In post-disaster scenarios, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are critical for establishing emergency communication networks. For time-critical rescue missions, information freshness is crucial because decisions based on outdated data may lead to ineffective control actions. This paper investigates age of information (AoI) minimization for UAV-assisted emergency communications with heterogeneous emergency services. We model bursty packet arrivals using a Markov-modulated Poisson process and adopt finite blocklength theory to capture the coupling among transmission duration, packet completion, and AoI evolution. To balance delay-tolerant long-packet transmission and urgent short-packet response, we propose a mini-slot-embedded scheduling mechanism with adaptive checkpoint-interval selection. We formulate the joint optimization of UAV trajectory control, user scheduling, and checkpoint-interval selection as a multi-agent decision problem, and develop MA-HEAD-Net, an adaptive rule-guided multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework. MA-HEAD-Net incorporates communication-domain rule priors into a gated multi-head policy, where adaptive gates regulate the contributions of rule-prior and learned-policy logits for different subtasks. The policy and gating components are jointly optimized under multi-agent proximal policy optimization. Simulation results show that MA-HEAD-Net improves policy-formation efficiency compared with representative multi-agent deep reinforcement learning baselines and achieves lower AoI than both learning-based and heuristic methods in dynamic UAV-assisted emergency communication scenarios.

</details>


### [151] [PATH-Bench: Path-Dependent Evaluation of Lifelong Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01149)
*Xidong Yang,Xingyi Zhang,Wenhao Li,Wenyan Liu,Junjie Sheng,Yun Hua,Wei Yin,Tao Fang,Chuyun Shen,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PATH-Bench，用于评估终身学习大语言模型智能体在不同经验积累路径下的迁移、保留与遗忘行为；并提出选择性经验使用机制SEU，以筛选有益经验、抑制干扰经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体通常通过外部记忆或技能库积累经验，但多数基准仅考察任务间的静态迁移，忽略经验到达顺序及历史任务组合对后续能力的影响。因此，难以系统衡量智能体的前向迁移、后向迁移、遗忘，以及有益与干扰经验共同作用下的鲁棒性。

Method: PATH-Bench首先借助多模型上下文学习估计任务之间的有向关系；随后围绕探测任务构造可控的任务序列，其中包含以正向帮助或负向干扰为主的历史经验；最后重复测试探测任务，并以平均性能、前向迁移、后向迁移和遗忘为指标评估。作者在单轮代码生成与多轮工具调用任务上测试八类代表性智能体。基于分析结果，提出SEU框架，在每个新任务到来时选择性纳入可能有帮助的历史经验，并过滤潜在干扰项。

Result: 实验表明，历史经验是否有用不仅取决于记忆或技能的表示方式，也取决于任务之间的交互结构。较强的迁移能力并不必然意味着较好的长期保留；后续经验还可能改变甚至削弱此前获得的收益。SEU在多数实验设置中同时降低遗忘并提升前向迁移，说明针对当前任务进行经验筛选优于无差别地使用累积历史。

Conclusion: PATH-Bench将终身智能体评估从静态任务迁移扩展到路径依赖的经验演化过程，为分析学习顺序、干扰和遗忘提供了受控框架。论文的核心设计启示是：终身智能体不应简单扩大记忆或技能库，而应依据当前任务动态选择和调控历史经验，以提升迁移效率与长期鲁棒性。

Abstract: Lifelong LLM agents increasingly adapt through external learning states that store past interactions as retrievable memories or reusable skills, yet existing benchmarks rarely account for how the path of accumulated experience shapes what agents transfer and retain. In this work, we establish PATH-Bench, a benchmark for path-dependent evaluation of lifelong agents. PATH-Bench estimates directed task relationships via multi-model in-context learning, constructs probe-centered sequences with controlled helpful and interfering histories, and repeatedly evaluates probe tasks to measure average performance, forward transfer, backward transfer, and forgetting. We evaluate eight representative agents on single-turn code generation and multi-turn tool-use tasks under positive- and negative-dominant histories. Benchmark results show that experience utility depends jointly on how experience is represented and on the task's interaction structure, that strong transfer does not ensure retention, and that later experience can reshape gains acquired earlier in the learning path. Based on these findings, we propose Selective Experience Use (SEU), an agent harness that regulates how path-accumulated experience influences each new task, admitting helpful items while filtering out potential interference. SEU consistently reduces forgetting while improving forward transfer in the majority of settings. The PATH-Bench provides both a controlled evaluation framework and actionable guidance for designing more selective and robust lifelong agents.

</details>


### [152] [The Graph Language: How Knowledge Graphs Speak to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01175)
*Giuseppe Pirrò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出GRALAN框架，通过关系标记将知识图谱结构映射到大语言模型的语义空间，使冻结的大语言模型能够利用图结构开展知识密集型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型具备较强的语言理解与推理能力，但在事实知识覆盖、显式关系建模和多跳推理方面仍可受限。现有知识图谱与大语言模型结合方法往往难以同时保留图结构信息并适配语言模型表示空间，因此需要一种结构保真且兼容冻结模型的集成机制。

Method: GRALAN使用可训练的语言中介模块GRALAN-s，为知识图谱中的关系生成结构化关系标记，并将其输入冻结的大语言模型。该方法不要求端到端微调主模型，而是通过关系标记显式保留图中的连接与关系结构。在问答任务中，作者将问题回答重构为围绕问题相关子图的实体分类任务，从候选实体中识别正确答案。

Result: 实验表明，GRALAN在知识图谱问答中优于已有方法，尤其在需要跨越多个关系路径的复杂多跳推理任务上表现突出。结果支持关系标记能够有效传递图结构信息，并帮助语言模型利用其推理能力进行答案判别。

Conclusion: GRALAN提供了一种知识图谱与大语言模型融合的新范式：以可训练中介生成关系结构标记，在保持知识图谱结构保真度的同时，充分利用冻结大语言模型的语义理解与推理能力。该框架具有用于知识密集型问答及相关应用的潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at reasoning but benefit from grounding provided by Knowledge Graphs (KGs). However, integrating these paradigms is challenging. We introduce GRALAN, which enables KGs to speak directly in the LLM's semantic space through relational tokens that preserve graph structure. GRALAN-s trainable language mediator generates structured tokens for any frozen LLM, creating a foundation for knowledge-intensive applications. We demonstrate its effectiveness in question-answering by re-framing the task as entity classification over question-focused subgraphs. Experiments show that GRALAN significantly outperforms existing methods, particularly on complex multi-hop reasoning tasks, establishing a new paradigm for KG-LLM integration that maintains structural fidelity while leveraging LLMs' reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [153] [Co-evolution of social reward and punishment under institutional interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01183)
*Van An Nguyen,Vuong Khang Huynh,Hoai Thuong Nguyen,Duc Tin Duong,An Nguyen Gia,Tat Kien Nguyen,Huu Loi Bui,My Nguyen Tra,Ho Nam Duong,Ba Thanh Phan,Thanh Vo,Dinh Anh Trung Hoang,Adeela Bashir,Zhao Song,Manh Hong Duong,Le Hong Trang,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究同伴激励与制度激励同时存在时，对合作、社会福利和执法效率的影响。核心结论是：同伴惩罚最能提升合作率，同伴奖励更有利于社会福利；制度应优先奖励实施同伴激励的个体，若采取惩罚则应直接针对背叛者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实社会中，个体间的分散式奖励或惩罚与机构的集中式干预常并存，但二者如何协同影响合作演化及总体福利尚不明确。论文旨在区分“提高合作水平”与“最大化社会福利”这两个可能不一致的目标，并提出相应的制度设计原则。

Method: 作者构建包含无条件合作者、背叛者、同伴惩罚者和同伴奖励者四类策略的囚徒困境模型。模型允许分散式同伴激励和集中式制度激励并行，且制度可对任意策略组合实施奖励或惩罚。在无限充分混合群体中，使用四策略复制子动力学分析均衡状态与演化流；在结构化群体中，利用方格晶格上的基于主体仿真考察空间效应与网络互惠。评估指标包括合作水平、演化均衡、总体收益扣除制度成本后的社会福利。

Result: 同伴惩罚在促进合作方面效果最强，而同伴奖励通常带来更高的社会福利。制度性奖励同伴激励策略能够显著改善合作与福利；相较之下，补贴无条件合作者的作用较弱。在制度性惩罚框架下，直接惩罚背叛者是唯一稳定有效的方案。若制度惩罚同伴惩罚者或同伴奖励者，会削弱分散式激励机制，进而降低合作水平和社会福利。

Conclusion: 制度设计不应仅以合作率最大化为目标，因为高合作并不必然对应最高社会福利。更优的政策是支持能够维持分散式激励的同伴激励者；若使用集中式惩罚，应将对象限定为背叛者，避免破坏个体层面的合作维护机制。

Abstract: We investigate how peer and institutional incentives jointly shape the evolution of cooperation, social welfare, and enforcement efficiency in social dilemmas. In a Prisoners Dilemma with four strategies, unconditional cooperators (C), defectors (D), social punishers (SP), and social rewarders (SR), we allow decentralised peer incentives and centralised institutional incentives to act simultaneously, with the institution able to reward or punish any subset of strategies. In infinite well-mixed populations, we analyse the resulting four-strategy replicator dynamics, and in structured populations we use agent-based simulations on square lattices to study spatial effects and network reciprocity. Intervention schemes are evaluated by equilibrium states and evolutionary flow for infinite well-mixed populations, by cooperation levels and social welfare for structured populations, defined as aggregate population payoff net of institutional cost. We find that peer punishment most strongly promotes cooperation, whereas peer reward is more beneficial for social welfare. Institutionally rewarding peer incentive strategies substantially improves both cooperation and welfare, while subsidising unconditional cooperators has little impact. Under institutional punishment, directly penalising defectors is the only consistently effective policy; punishing peer incentive strategies dismantles decentralised incentives, reduces cooperation, and harms social welfare, showing that maximising cooperation does not necessarily optimise overall societal benefit. Our findings provide design principles for institutions seeking to balance cooperation promotion with welfare maximisation.

</details>


### [154] [Humans Are More Diverse: Frontier LLMs Show Extreme Policies in Idealised AI Development Races](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01193)
*Phu Hoa Pham,Duy Minh Dao Sy,Trung Kiet Huynh,Phu Quy Nguyen Lam,Chi Nguyen Tran,Minh Trung Le,Phong Hao Le,Dinh Nam Nguyen,Thien Ky Nguyen Dong,Elias Fernandez Domingos,Le Hong Trang,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型在多主体AI开发竞赛中的安全决策，并强调在将模型行为解释为“战略性”或“安全意识”之前，必须先审计其是否真正理解和稳定执行博弈规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发竞赛可被建模为安全困境：参与者可选择较慢但安全的开发，或选择更快但伴随可能失去最终收益的风险。仅凭模型采取了看似合理的动作，不能证明其理解博弈、收益结构或竞争状态，因此需要建立行为解释前的有效性验证流程。

Method: 作者构建了一个含2至5名参与者的重复博弈，首先验证游戏引擎正确性；随后审计模型的规则回忆、状态追踪、收益计算能力，以及面对语义等价但表述不同任务时的稳定性。通过向模型提供经验证的算术结果、改变回答表示形式等干预，检验其后续行为是否改变。研究还将7个模型端点的行动序列与演化博弈论基准及既有人类实验数据进行比较，并分析模型、风险条件、人格设定和参与者数量的差异。

Result: 结果发现，模型即使具有较强的规则复述能力，也可能在状态追踪和期望收益计算上表现薄弱。即便游戏规则不变，提供正确算术信息或改变输出表示方式也会改变后续决策。跨模型的汇总行动比例掩盖了显著差异：不同模型在行动轨迹、对对手行为的反应、对自身竞赛位置的反应方面均不一致。对于3至5人竞赛，增加竞争者并未产生统一效应，而是呈现模型特异性的模式。

Conclusion: 多主体AI竞赛模拟不能仅依据最终动作频率或表面上的合理选择，就认定模型具有战略理解、人类相似性或安全意识。应在行为解释之前进行游戏引擎与认知能力审计，并采用轨迹层面的分析。研究结论具有探索性，仅适用于所测试的模型、提示词和解码设置。

Abstract: An AI development race creates a multi-agent safety dilemma. Each company can develop slowly and safely, or move faster while taking a risk that may remove its final reward. We use this repeated game to study strategic safety behaviour among large language model (LLM) agents in races with two to five players. However, a valid action does not show that an agent understands the game. We therefore place an audit gate before behavioural interpretation. We first verify the game engine, then test rule recall, state tracking, payoff calculation, and stability under different but equivalent task descriptions. We then compare LLM action sequences with an evolutionary game-theory benchmark and published human data, and explore differences across models, risk conditions, personas, and two- to five-player races. The audit shows that strong rule recall can coexist with weak state tracking and expected-payoff calculation. Providing verified arithmetic and changing the response representation can also change later actions, even when the game rules stay fixed. Across seven tested model endpoints, aggregate rates hide large differences in action sequences, responses to opponents, and responses to race position. Patterns across the tested three- to five-player races are also model-specific rather than a single effect of adding competitors. These results show why multi-agent AI-race simulations need validity checks and trajectory-level analysis before their outputs are described as strategic, human-like, or safety-aware. Our findings are exploratory and apply only to the tested models, prompts, and decoding settings.

</details>


### [155] [Perspectives on Tsallis Statistics for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01223)
*Kleyton da Costa,Bernardo Modenesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理Tsallis统计与人工智能的交叉，核心观点是：参数q可作为控制分布稠密性、稀疏性、峰度和尾部行为的可学习归纳偏置，并已广泛用于注意力、强化学习、概率建模、损失函数与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Boltzmann-Gibbs统计及其对应的softmax、Gaussian假设通常偏向短程相关和轻尾分布，难以充分描述深度学习中常见的稀疏激活、重尾权重、非平稳梯度噪声及探索—利用权衡。本文旨在建立统一框架，解释Tsallis统计为何适合这些现象，并归纳其在AI设计中的共性模式。

Method: 论文首先回顾q-熵、最大熵变分原理、q-指数、q-对数、q-中心极限定理、q-Gaussian及superstatistics等数学基础；随后按应用领域综述softmax泛化、最大熵强化学习、序列与图模型、生成式与概率模型、损失设计和优化方法；最后讨论深度网络权重与梯度统计中的非广延特征、信息几何关联、q-指数族，以及方法使用中的局限与开放问题。

Result: 综述提炼出跨任务一致的设计规律：通过调节q，可在稠密且接近均匀的输出，与稀疏、尖峰或重尾的输出之间连续插值。例如，sparsemax和α-entmax可实现稀疏注意力；Tsallis型熵正则可调节强化学习中的探索强度；q-Gaussian和相关重尾模型可提升对异常值、厚尾数据及复杂不确定性的建模能力。论文还指出，深度网络中经验观察到的重尾权重谱和梯度噪声可能与非广延统计相一致。

Conclusion: Tsallis统计可被视为连接稀疏建模、重尾概率、鲁棒学习和可控探索的统一工具。本文的主要主张是，不应仅将q固定为人工设定的超参数，而应研究其数据驱动或端到端可学习的机制，使其成为表达任务结构与学习动力学特征的归纳偏置；同时需谨慎处理理论适用范围、参数可辨识性及与信息几何方法的关系。

Abstract: Tsallis statistics generalizes Boltzmann-Gibbs statistical mechanics through a single real parameter $q$ that controls the weight assigned to rare and frequent events. Originally proposed to describe physical systems with long-range correlations, multifractal geometry, and heavy-tailed fluctuations, the framework has become a recurring ingredient in modern artificial intelligence (AI): it underlies sparse attention mechanisms (\textsc{sparsemax} and $α$-\textsc{entmax}), maximum-entropy reinforcement learning with controllable exploration, robust and heavy-tailed probabilistic models, and a family of generalized loss functions and regularizers. This paper offers a structured perspective on where Tsallis statistics meets AI. We first review the mathematical core: $q$-entropy and its variational (maximum-entropy) foundation, the $q$-exponential and $q$-logarithm, the $q$-central limit theorem, $q$-Gaussian distributions, and their dynamical origin in superstatistics, emphasizing the properties that matter for machine learning. We then survey applications across softmax generalization, reinforcement learning, sequential and graph neural models, generative and probabilistic modeling, loss design, and optimization, extracting the recurring design pattern in each case: a tunable interpolation between dense/uniform and sparse/peaked behavior governed by $q$. We further argue that the heavy-tailed weight spectra and gradient-noise statistics empirically observed in deep networks are themselves nonextensive signatures, placing modern learning dynamics within the scope of $q$-statistics. Finally, we discuss methodological pitfalls, the relationship to information geometry and $q$-exponential families, and open directions, arguing that $q$ should be treated as a learnable inductive bias rather than a fixed hyperparameter.

</details>


### [156] [CT-PrepAgent: Bounded Policy and Controlled Execution for Adaptive CT Data Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01233)
*Xiaolin Fan,Yue Pei,Yingying Zhang,Haogang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CT-PrepAgent：一种面向异质性CT数据的自适应数据准备框架，通过受限策略与确定性受控执行，自动选择合格的DICOM序列或预定义预处理方案，并在异常情况下进行有限恢复或安全隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同CT采集协议、数据质量和下游任务需求使固定式预处理流程难以跨数据源迁移。现有方法通常依赖人工规则或特定数据集配置，面对采集条件和任务目标变化时需要人工干预。尽管LLM智能体已用于部分医学工作流自动化，其在CT数据准备中的自适应能力仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: CT-PrepAgent首先通过确定性检查提取并构建结构化的数据—任务画像；随后由受限策略基于该画像决定采用可用DICOM序列还是预定义预处理配置。执行模块对决策进行守卫、解析、执行和验证；在启用且安全的条件下实施有界恢复，对于无法安全处理的问题则执行隔离。系统还支持在不重新调用策略的情况下进行回放，以增强可复现性和故障诊断能力。

Result: 在三个公开CT分割任务上，CT-PrepAgent能够生成与数据和任务相适应的预处理决策，并取得最高的宏平均Dice得分。在两个私有原始DICOM队列中，已验证输出的产出率由61.7%提升至70.0%；对于共同通过验证的输出，其配准指标与对照方法相近。受控故障注入和回放实验进一步验证了该框架在所测试故障与分布漂移条件下的有界恢复、安全隔离及策略无关回放能力。

Conclusion: CT-PrepAgent将策略决策限制在可控范围内，并以确定性执行和验证机制保障可靠性，为跨来源、跨任务的CT数据准备提供了更具适应性和可审计性的方案。其核心贡献不只是提高预处理效果，还在于通过有限恢复、异常隔离和可回放执行降低自动化流程的运行风险。

Abstract: Heterogeneous computed tomography (CT) acquisitions and diverse downstream task requirements limit the transferability of fixed data preparation workflows across data sources and tasks. Existing approaches typically rely on manually designed or dataset-specific rules, making it difficult to accommodate changes in acquisition conditions and analytical objectives without manual intervention. Large language model (LLM)-based agents have shown promise for automating medical workflows, yet their potential for adaptive CT data preparation remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose CT-PrepAgent, which enables adaptive CT data preparation through a bounded policy and controlled deterministic execution. Deterministic inspection constructs structured data--task profiles, from which a policy decides an eligible DICOM series or predefined preprocessing profile, while the controlled execution flow guards, resolves, executes, and verifies the decision with bounded recovery when enabled and safe quarantine otherwise. Across three public CT segmentation tasks, CT-PrepAgent derived data-task adaptive preprocessing decisions and achieved the highest macro-average Dice. On two private raw-DICOM cohorts, CT-PrepAgent increased verified output yield from 61.7\% to 70.0\% and yielded similar registration metrics on common verified outputs. Controlled fault and replay tests validate bounded recovery, safe quarantine, and policy-free replay under tested fault and drift settings.

</details>


### [157] [Learning What to Remember and What to Internalize in LLM Self-Evolution via Adaptive Memory-Parameter Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01234)
*Tianyun Ji,Zhenya Huang,Jiayu Liu,Zirui Liu,Yu Su,Hongbin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COVE提出统一的智能体自进化框架，通过协调基于外部工具链和基于模型参数的学习机制，使智能体在动态环境中实现更稳健、高效的适应与能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化方法主要依赖两种单一机制：工具链方法灵活但能力提升有限，参数方法能够实现更深层的能力内化但适应速度较慢。单独使用任一机制都会在灵活性与性能之间产生权衡，因此需要协调两类学习渠道。

Method: COVE采用任务感知路由、阶段感知调度和知识优化三项机制，根据任务特征与知识类型选择合适的学习方式，并在不同进化阶段协调外部记忆或技能更新与模型参数优化，避免无差别累积经验。

Result: 在多类任务上的实验表明，COVE整体优于单一渠道的自进化策略，在环境变化下实现了更稳健且更高效的性能提升。

Conclusion: 智能体自进化应被视为外部工具链学习与模型参数学习之间的协同过程。通过合理匹配任务、知识类型和学习机制，COVE能够兼顾快速适应与深层能力改进。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly operate in dynamic environments where tool interfaces, APIs, and user requirements change after deployment. Existing self-evolution methods mainly follow two paradigms: harness-based approaches, which externalize feedback into editable memories or skills for rapid adaptation, and parameter-based approaches, which internalize experience into model parameters for deeper capability improvement. However, using either mechanism alone creates a trade-off between flexibility and performance. This paper asks how an agent can coordinate both channels to achieve robust self-evolution. We present COVE, a unified agent self-evolution framework that combines harness-based and parameter-based learning through task-aware routing, stage-aware scheduling, and knowledge optimization. Through this design, COVE treats self-evolution not as indiscriminate accumulation of experience, but as a coordinated process that matches tasks and knowledge types to appropriate learning mechanisms. Experiments across multiple task categories show that COVE outperforms single-channel evolution strategies, demonstrating more robust and efficient improvement under changing environments.

</details>


### [158] [Cognitive Demand Steering for Adaptive Meta-Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01319)
*John Scoville,Shengzhuang Chen,Yejin Bang,Stefan Winzeck,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出认知需求引导（CDS）框架：一种无需训练的元推理方法。它在推理过程中持续估计“距离完成问题还缺少哪些认知能力或推理工作”，并据此动态选择指导策略与示例。实验表明，CDS在多个模型和推理基准上优于直接生成与标准链式推理，尤其适用于高难度数学和编程任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元推理方法通常存在三类局限：一是依据已完成步骤的回顾式奖励信号进行控制，难以直接刻画后续推理缺口；二是搜索或控制动作较粗粒度，诊断能力不足；三是往往需要额外训练推理控制器，并依赖大量监督数据。论文旨在构建一种可零样本迁移、无需训练且能细粒度调节推理过程的控制机制。

Method: CDS包含两项核心设计。第一，残余需求评估：每一步由基于大语言模型的进展评估器预测获得最终答案仍需完成的推理需求，而非仅评价上一推理步骤的优劣。第二，认知量表驱动的干预选择：系统使用16个受认知科学启发的维度描述初始问题复杂度和动态残余需求，例如注意与扫描、学习与抽象、空间—物理推理等；元控制器据此选择相应干预，包括通用推理示例及面向特定能力的指导动作。该流程不需要训练专门控制器，可直接跨模型、跨任务使用。

Result: 在3个前沿大语言模型和6个推理基准上的平均结果显示，CDS相较直接调用的准确率提升21.9%，相较标准链式推理提升9%。收益在困难数学与代码推理任务上最显著，说明基于前瞻性认知需求的动态干预对长程、复杂推理更有效。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是将元推理控制从“评估过去步骤”转向“诊断未来剩余推理需求”，并通过多维认知量表实现更细粒度的策略选择。该方法在不增加训练成本的前提下实现了零样本迁移和显著性能提升。不过，摘要未说明残余需求评估的稳定性、干预选择的计算开销，以及不同任务维度标注或量表设计对结果的敏感性；这些将是评估其实用性与可复现性的关键问题。

Abstract: Recent meta-reasoning frameworks improve LLM reasoning by wrapping chain-of-thought generation in an iterative control loop, allowing more effective backtracking, termination of reasoning loops, and injection of promising reasoning patterns, among other strategy adjustments. Despite promising results, methods often rely on backward-looking reward functions, utilize coarse search actions, or require additional reasoning controller training requiring many-shot supervision. We introduce Cognitive Demand Steering (CDS), a training-free meta-reasoning framework equipped with residual demand assessment: at each step, an LLM-based progress evaluator characterizes the residual reasoning required to arrive at a solution rather than merely evaluating the previous step. This allows a meta-controller to select reasoning interventions comprising both general-purpose exemplars and actions (e.g., general guidance for quantitative reasoning) that directly tackle this forward-looking demand signal. This shift eliminates the need for any trained component while enabling zero-shot transfer across models and tasks with no adaptation. Rather than relying on coarse characterizations, we employ cognitive scales to both design interventions as well as profile initial problem complexity and residual demand signal over 16 dimensions motivated by cognitive science (e.g., attention and scan, learning and abstraction, spatio-physical reasoning), giving the controller a fine-grained vocabulary for diagnosing. Averaged across three frontier LLMs and six reasoning benchmarks, CDS improves accuracy by $21.9\%$ over direct calls and $9\%$ over standard CoT reasoning, with the largest gains on difficult mathematics and coding tasks.

</details>


### [159] [G-ReAct: Graph-Guided Deep Search via Structure-State Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01324)
*Shaoxiong Yang,Mengyuan Zhang,Shaojun Lin,Chao Li,Wei Liu,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出G-ReAct，一种基于固定拓扑查询图和状态演化的深度搜索推理框架，用于缓解长程多跳搜索中的上下文遗忘、搜索漂移与探索低效问题。实验表明，该方法以较少训练轨迹即可取得较强性能，并能在推理阶段提升现有大语言模型的搜索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度搜索方法通常采用线性序列进行轨迹生成和推理，难以持续维护中间状态、任务约束与搜索进展，因而容易出现上下文遗忘、搜索方向偏移以及探索效率低下等问题。

Method: G-ReAct将推理过程建模为固定拓扑查询图上的状态演化。图状态显式记录搜索进度，并通过结构化约束指导后续决策，将依赖文本历史的探索式搜索转化为图引导推理。该框架既可生成高质量深度搜索轨迹用于监督微调，也可在无需额外微调的情况下为推理阶段提供结构化搜索指导。

Result: 仅使用1.9K条生成轨迹进行微调时，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507在BrowseComp-ZH和XBench上分别达到52.6%和79.0%的准确率，超过多个使用更大规模数据训练的开源方法，包括强化学习增强方法。在推理阶段应用时，G-ReAct能够稳定提升现有强大语言模型在深度搜索任务上的表现。

Conclusion: 固定拓扑查询图与显式状态演化能够有效增强大语言模型的长程、多跳搜索能力，在较低训练数据成本下实现具有竞争力的性能，并具备良好的即插即用推理增强潜力。

Abstract: Deep search has become a fundamental capability of large language models (LLMs) for solving open-domain complex tasks. However, existing approaches typically rely on linear sequential reasoning for both trajectory generation and inference, making it difficult to consistently preserve intermediate states and constraints throughout long-horizon multi-hop search. Consequently, they often suffer from context forgetting, search drift, and inefficient exploration. To address these limitations, we propose $\textbf{G-ReAct}$, a reasoning framework for deep search that organizes reasoning as $\textbf{state evolution over a fixed-topology query graph}$. The evolving graph state explicitly tracks search progress and guides subsequent decisions, transforming exploratory search driven by textual history into graph-guided reasoning under explicit constraints. G-ReAct supports both training and inference: it generates high-quality deep-search trajectories for supervised fine-tuning and provides structured guidance for inference-time search without additional fine-tuning. Experiments demonstrate that with only 1.9K generated trajectories for fine-tuning, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 achieves $52.6\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-ZH and $79.0\%$ on XBench, outperforming comparable open-source methods trained on substantially larger datasets, including RL-enhanced methods. Furthermore, when applied at inference time, G-ReAct consistently improves the performance of existing strong LLMs on deep-search tasks. We will publicly release all code and model weights.

</details>


### [160] [402Pilot: An x402 Decision Layer for Autonomous Agent Micropayments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01341)
*Yin Li,Yanbo He,Boo-Ho Yang,Rav Lawana,Ziyue Li,Wei Zeng,Jing Tang,Fugee Tsung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出402Pilot：位于自主智能体与支付执行之间的买方决策层，用于在有限钱包约束下选择可付费服务商；其PA-DCT策略在多种市场条件下取得了最优或近优的非预言机综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: x402等可编程支付协议支持按请求微支付，但未解决自主智能体在预算有限、服务质量未知、仅能获得已购买服务反馈且市场价格或性能会变化时，应当购买哪一项服务的问题。论文将其定义为面向智能体的支付决策问题。

Method: 作者构建协议无关的402Pilot框架，并提出PA-DCT策略，即支付感知的折扣式上下文Thompson Sampling。该策略结合上下文信息、预算压力、购买后的选择性反馈和时间折扣，以在探索服务商价值与控制支出之间动态权衡。评测使用402Pilot-Bench冻结回放基准，包含823个任务、5条异构服务商流水线、3种市场状态，并以30组配对随机种子比较学习基线、组件消融和预言机参考。

Result: PA-DCT在固定钱包下展现出最强的非预言机自适应折中：在保持有竞争力服务质量的同时，仅使用39%至43%的钱包，并能随市场状态变化重新分配支出。在价格冲击场景中，其PA-gap/T最佳；在质量、ROI和PA-gap/T共9个场景—指标组合中，获得最佳平均排名和最优最坏情况排名。学习基线比较与消融实验进一步支持支付感知设计和折扣机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将可编程支付从单纯的支付执行扩展为买方侧的价值学习与采购决策。实验表明，面向自主智能体的付费服务调用需要显式纳入预算约束、选择性反馈和市场非平稳性；402Pilot及PA-DCT为此提供了具有实证优势的实现方案。

Abstract: Programmable-payment protocols such as x402 enable per-request micropayments, but they do not determine which payable service an autonomous agent should buy under a finite wallet. We formulate this buyer-side problem as agent-native payment decision-making: contextual provider selection under wallet pressure, chosen-only paid feedback, and changing market conditions. We propose 402Pilot, a protocol-agnostic buyer-side decision layer between autonomous agents and payment execution that implements purchasing policies for selecting among payable providers. We instantiate it with PA-DCT, a payment-aware discounted contextual Thompson-sampling policy that adapts purchasing decisions under wallet pressure while learning from post-payment feedback. To evaluate buyer-side payment policies, we introduce 402Pilot-Bench, a frozen-replay benchmark spanning 823 tasks, five heterogeneous provider pipelines, and three market regimes, each evaluated over 30 paired seeds. PA-DCT achieves the strongest fixed-wallet adaptive trade-off among non-oracle policies: it maintains competitive service quality while spending only 39 to 43 percent of the wallet and reallocates spending as market conditions change. It attains the best non-oracle PA-gap/T under the price shock and the best mean and worst-case ranks across the nine scenario-metric combinations of quality, ROI, and PA-gap/T. Comparisons with learning baselines and component ablations further support the effectiveness and design of the proposed decision policy. These results suggest that programmable payment must be complemented by buyer-side decision-making capable of learning service value and adapting purchasing decisions accordingly.

</details>


### [161] [Agentic Stage-One Stellarator Optimization: Autonomous Multi-Objective Search for Finite-Beta Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01344)
*Tingjia Zhang,Hongke Lu,Zhuoran Meng,Runlai Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由语言模型智能体控制、由确定性DESC求解器执行的仿星器有限β阶段一优化框架，在多目标约束下提升了磁面构型质量，并将优化过程转化为可复用的决策数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿星器阶段一设计涉及高维三维边界、固定边界MHD平衡、约束拓扑、力平衡、稳定性与几何性能。传统迭代优化高度依赖初始构型、傅里叶分辨率、目标权重和计算预算，成本高且需要专家持续干预，难以稳定地产生高质量、可验证的有限β平衡构型。

Method: 采用智能体外循环优化架构：受约束的语言模型智能体分析当前平衡状态并选择下一次局部优化实验；确定性的DESC程序负责固定剖面与磁通、对称性、指标计算、求解器有效性检查和结果验收。系统在共同计算预算下开展有限β多目标搜索，并记录每次局部操作及其结果，形成结构化父构型—动作—结果数据。

Result: 在扩展有限β优化活动的共同预算子集中，通过验收的构型数量由5个增加至19个；Boozer准对称性RMS中位数由2.39×10^-4降至1.07×10^-4，最大主曲率中位数由62.56降至33.00 m^-1。另一条较长优化路径实现了9.10倍的准对称性指标降低，同时修复了磁阱和曲率缺陷。实验共记录734条结构化转移数据。

Conclusion: 结果表明，智能体可以有效承担阶段一优化的外循环决策，在确定性求解与严格验收机制约束下持续推进有限β、多目标构型搜索。该方法不仅改善了平衡构型质量，也将重复优化过程转化为可扩展的构型生成和决策数据积累机制，但目前仍属于概念验证，泛化性和与更大规模设计空间的适应能力有待进一步评估。

Abstract: Stage-one stellarator design searches a high-dimensional family of three-dimensional plasma boundaries and fixed-boundary MHD equilibria for configurations that jointly meet requirements on confinement, field-line topology, force balance, stability proxies, and geometry. These specifications do not provide a general constructive map to a validated finite-beta equilibrium. High-quality targets are commonly developed through iterative numerical optimization whose outcome depends on the initial configuration, active Fourier resolution, objective priorities, and local solver budget. Coordinating this process is computationally costly and expert-intensive, limiting both design throughput and the production of consistently evaluated data. We present a proof of concept for \emph{agentic} stage-one optimization. A bounded language-model agent diagnoses the current equilibrium and selects the next local optimization experiment, while deterministic DESC execution owns prescribed profiles and flux, symmetry, metric evaluation, solver validity, and acceptance. On a common-budget subset from an expanding finite-beta campaign, the number of gate-valid configurations increases from five inputs to nineteen outputs; median Boozer QS RMS decreases from $2.39\times10^{-4}$ to $1.07\times10^{-4}$, and median maximum principal curvature decreases from $62.56$ to $33.00\,\mathrm{m}^{-1}$. A complementary long route achieves a $9.10\times$ QS reduction while repairing magnetic-well and curvature defects. The system also records every attempted local action as transition evidence, yielding 734 structured parent--action--outcome records in the reported experiments. These results show that agentic outer-loop control can sustain finite-beta, multi-objective search and turn repeated optimization into a scalable source of improved equilibria and reusable decision data.

</details>


### [162] [High-Stakes Decisions with Language Models: Insights from Emergency Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01361)
*Khurram Yamin,Christopher Kelly,Bryan Wilder,Eric Horvitz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将语言模型的临床分诊建议理解为概率决策问题：模型不仅需要预测患者结局，还应在明确漏诊与过度升级代价的效用函数下生成推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险临床场景中的决策不能仅依据预测准确率，还必须权衡不同错误及行动后果。现有语言模型用于临床推荐时，通常缺少对决策效用和风险偏好的显式定义，导致其推荐目标不透明。

Method: 研究使用消费者分诊系统的结构化临床病例，构建包含不同效用函数的概率决策框架。效用函数用于设定漏诊紧急情况与不必要转诊或升级处理之间的相对成本，并据此评估语言模型在不同决策目标下的治疗或分诊建议变化。

Result: 结果显示，能力较强的语言模型能够依据所给定的效用设定调整建议。同一组潜在预测结果在不同漏诊成本和过度处理成本设定下，可支持显著不同的分诊策略，说明预测能力与最终行动建议并不等价。

Conclusion: 语言模型在高风险场景中的部署与评估，应从单纯考察预测性能转向概率决策系统视角。模型推荐由预测质量和显式效用共同决定，因此必须明确决策目标、错误代价与风险权衡，才能实现可控、可解释的应用。

Abstract: High-stakes decisions under uncertainty, such as medical emergency triage, require more than accurate predictions. They depend on estimating the likelihood of alternative outcomes while explicitly weighing the consequences of different actions, principles that have long formed the foundation of medical diagnosis and decision making. Yet language models are increasingly used for high-stakes clinical recommendations without explicit specification of the utilities governing these decisions. Here we show that emergency triage with language models can be understood within a probabilistic decision framework, providing a case study of a broader decision-analytic paradigm for steering, evaluating, and deploying language models in high-stakes settings.
