Using clinical vignettes from a structured evaluation of a consumer triage system, we analyze recommendations for treatment under alternative utility functions that specify the relative costs of missed emergencies and unnecessary escalation. We find that capable language models adjust recommendations in response to stated utilities, revealing that the same underlying predictions can support markedly different decision policies. These findings show that effective deployment depends not only on improving predictions but also on making decision objectives explicit. More broadly, they suggest that language models for high-stakes applications should be understood and evaluated as probabilistic decision systems whose recommendations depend jointly on predictive performance and explicit utilities.

</details>


### [163] [CRAFTS: Collaborative Role-Adaptive Fine-Tuning of LLM Agents for Chemical Process Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01369)
*Ziyun Zhang,Yuxin Lin,Eldin Wee Chuan Lim,Xinghao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CRAFTS，一个面向化工流程模拟自动构建的七角色智能体工作流。系统将自然语言需求、流程图证据与工程知识转化为可执行的IDAES/Pyomo模型，并通过类型化中间表示和确定性工程门控提高可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化工流程模型构建涉及单元操作选择、热力学物性、物流连接、规格设定、自由度分析、初始化、求解修复及优化等相互耦合的决策，当前高度依赖人工经验。一个局部错误即可导致模型失效，因此需要能够将非结构化需求稳定转化为可验证、可执行模拟模型的自动化方法。

Method: CRAFTS将建模流程拆分为七个职责受限的角色，并在阶段之间设置确定性的IDAES/Pyomo验证门控。输入理解与意图角色提取需求、约束和流程语义；视觉、拓扑和规格角色分别生成VisualGraphIR、TopologyIR和SpecIR等类型化模拟器契约；后续角色生成BuildPlan并执行调试、求解和符合条件的优化。系统仅在视觉、拓扑和规格这三个模式关键角色上进行微调，其余角色使用未经微调的Qwen模型。作者还构建OpenIDAES-450数据集，并使用冻结的82个测试案例评估端到端工作流。

Result: 在OpenIDAES-450的82个保留测试案例上，CRAFTS有91.5%的案例完成预定义验证与执行契约。对于流程结构恢复，单元识别、物流识别和有向连接识别的F1分别达到0.815、0.791和0.782。结果表明，该系统能够在较高比例案例中生成通过工程校验且可执行的流程模拟构建结果。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于将化工流程建模转化为多角色协作、类型化中间表示驱动、确定性工程校验约束的自动化流程。实验支持角色专门化和显式IR设计能够提升结构解析与模型执行可靠性；但摘要未报告与端到端基线或人工建模效率的直接对比，也未充分说明失败案例、跨领域泛化能力及复杂优化任务上的性能边界。

Abstract: Constructing an executable chemical-process model remains manually intensive. Chemical engineers translate underspecified requests into coupled decisions about unit operations, thermodynamics, streams, specifications, degrees of freedom (DoF), initialization, solver repair, and optimization; one error can invalidate the model. CRAFTS mirrors the staged workflow of chemical engineers by decomposing simulation building into bounded subtasks assigned to seven bounded roles, with deterministic IDAES/Pyomo gates between stages. Given a natural-language request, process flowsheet diagram (PFD) evidence, and curated chemical-engineering knowledge, Input Understanding and Intent recover requirements, constraints, and process semantics; visual, topology, and specification specialists translate them into typed simulator contracts; and Debug and Optimization support bounded repair and eligible optimization. Fine-tuning is applied to the three schema-critical visual, topology, and specification roles, while the remaining roles use untuned Qwen. The resulting VisualGraphIR, TopologyIR, SpecIR, BuildPlan, and SolveReport expose unit, port, thermodynamic, numerical, and execution decisions. Compatible constructors, property packages, and runners are attached only after semantic artifacts pass engineering gates. We introduce OpenIDAES-450, a 450-case IDAES process- simulation dataset, and evaluate the complete seven-role LangChain/LangGraph workflow through solve and eligible optimization on its frozen 82-case held-out split. CRAFTS completes the prescribed validation and execution contract for for 91.5% of cases and achieves unit, stream, and directed-connection F1 scores of 0.815, 0.791, and 0.782. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of role specialization, typed intermediate representations, and deterministic engineering gates for reliable automated process-model construction.

</details>


### [164] [CraftAlign: Feature-Grounded Evaluation and Revision Guidance for AI Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01377)
*Yang Yang,Boyun Xu,Shaofeng Liang,Yun Han,Zining Zhong,Songning Lai,Kaishen Yuan,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CraftAlign 是一个面向 AI 叙事文本的评估与修订框架：它以 304 个显式写作特征刻画人类与 AI 的写作模式差异，并据此生成全篇级改写建议，以降低套路化、过度解释和线性叙事等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常仅判断文本来源或给出整体质量分数，难以解释具体问题；现有改写方法又多针对预定义问题进行局部编辑，无法支持多种合理的修订路径，也难以调整信息揭示、因果组织和结尾处理等全局叙事结构。

Method: 框架包含特征估计器、类别条件能量模型和推理阶段指导流程。特征估计器基于 Qwen3.5-9B，预测覆盖文体与叙事维度的 304 项写作特征；类别条件能量模型结合原始提示词，对特征配置与人类或 AI 写作模式的匹配度进行评分。推理时，系统对特征进行满足模式约束的结构化扰动，选择能使文本更接近人类写作模式的变化，并将其转化为自然语言修改指导，交由独立编辑器重写完整故事。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够较准确地区分人类与 AI 写作模式；其生成的修改指导在不同编辑器设置下均优于对比改写方法，并在人类评测中获得更好的表现。

Conclusion: CraftAlign 将可解释的写作特征建模、模式匹配与全篇改写指导结合起来，不仅可识别 AI 叙事文本的典型写作倾向，还能提供面向全局叙事结构的、可执行的修订建议，从而提升生成故事的自然性与人类写作风格一致性。

Abstract: Large language models can now generate fluent and complete stories, yet many outputs still feel formulaic and unnatural because of cliches, over-explanation, linear causal progression, and stereotyped endings, an immediately recognizable AI flavor. Existing detection and evaluation methods often stop at source labels or holistic scores, while revision methods typically target predefined issues through localized edits, limiting their ability to support multiple plausible revision strategies or guide story-wide changes in information release, causal organization, and ending treatment. We introduce CraftAlign, a framework that aligns AI stories with the craft of human storytelling by both assessing Human/AI writing patterns and providing revision guidance. CraftAlign comprises two learned modules and an inference-time guidance pipeline. A feature estimator built on Qwen3.5-9B predicts 304 explicit writing features spanning style and narrative. A class-conditional energy model scores the resulting feature configuration against Human and AI writing patterns, conditioning on the original writing prompt when available. At inference time, CraftAlign applies schema-valid structured perturbations, selects changes that move the feature configuration toward the Human writing pattern, and converts them into natural-language guidance for a separate editor to rewrite the full story. Experiments show that CraftAlign accurately distinguishes Human and AI writing patterns and that its guidance outperforms revision baselines across editors and in a human study.

</details>


### [165] [KoVRE: Training an Efficient Embedding Model for Korean Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01389)
*Yongbin Choi,Gyuho Shim,Youngjoon Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出KoVRE，一种面向韩语视觉文档检索的单向量嵌入模型及训练方案。模型通过双语监督、正例感知困难负例挖掘和重排序器蒸馏，在韩国视觉文档检索基准上取得较强效果，并以2B参数模型超过8B单向量模型及强多向量基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉文档检索直接将文本查询与文档页面图像匹配，能够保留OCR或文本抽取中易丢失的版式与视觉结构信息。但现有模型与训练数据主要偏向英语；同时，高性能方法常依赖大规模骨干网络或存储成本较高的多向量表示。因此，研究旨在构建高效、适用于韩语文档且部署成本较低的检索模型。

Method: 作者构建KoVRE，并采用单向量检索架构以降低索引与存储开销。训练使用708,729个韩语和英语查询—页面配对样本，核心策略包括：正例感知的困难负例挖掘，避免将潜在相关页面错误作为纯负例；针对训练数据语言与来源构成的受控实验；以及利用重排序器进行知识蒸馏，以增强轻量检索器的排序能力。

Result: 在多个韩语视觉文档检索基准上，KoVRE的2B模型相较基础骨干模型有显著提升；其性能还优于8B参数的单向量版本，以及一个强多向量检索基线。实验表明，训练数据组成、困难负例处理方式和重排序器蒸馏均是影响性能的重要因素。

Conclusion: 针对性的韩英双语监督数据与精心设计的训练策略，可以使中等规模的单向量模型在韩语视觉文档检索中获得优异性能。该工作说明，提升非英语VDR能力不必依赖更大参数规模或多向量表示，因而具有更好的存储效率和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) directly matches text queries against document images, preserving visual and structural information that may be lost during text extraction. However, existing VDR models and training resources remain predominantly English-centric, while many high-performing systems rely on massive backbones or storage-intensive multi-vector representations. To address these limitations, we introduce KoVRE: Korean Visual Document Retrieval Embedding, a single-vector retriever for Korean visual documents, alongside a comprehensive training recipe. We train the model on 708,729 Korean and English query-page pairs using positive-aware hard-negative mining and conduct controlled analyses of training-data composition, hard-negative treatment, and reranker-based knowledge distillation. Across Korean visual document retrieval benchmarks, our 2B model substantially improves over the base backbone model, outperforming both its 8B single-vector counterpart and a strong multi-vector baseline. These results demonstrate that targeted bilingual supervision and our carefully designed training strategies can produce a highly effective Korean VDR model across diverse document domains, without requiring a scaled-up backbone or multi-vector representations.

</details>


### [166] [Reusing Rollouts under Policy Lag: Prefix-Normalized Policy Optimization for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01418)
*Wenhao Zhang,Yibo Xie,Rui Wang,Jiahua Yang,Lei Jiang,Zibo Yang,Yawei Wang,Jiali Xu,jasperawang,Haoyang Long,Huan Xiong,alantzhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出前缀归一化策略优化（PNPO），通过对因果前缀上的似然比取几何均值，替代标准离策略校正中的累积重要性比，以缓解长序列下权重动态范围过大的问题。实验显示，在每批轨迹进行四轮更新、离策略程度更高时，PNPO优于GSPO，并可显著减少所需轨迹批次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型强化学习中的自回归轨迹生成成本高，因此通常希望对同一批轨迹进行多次学习更新以摊薄生成成本。但随着更新次数增加，当前学习策略逐渐偏离生成轨迹的行为策略，离策略偏差加重。严格校正需要同时考虑当前动作及其前缀被到达的概率，即使用累积重要性比；然而该乘积在长上下文中容易产生极端的权重尺度和训练不稳定性。

Method: PNPO在每个位置保留因果前缀依赖，但将前缀内各步策略似然比的累积乘积替换为其几何均值。等价地，该方法对累积对数重要性权重按前缀长度进行缩放，从而压缩权重的对数动态范围。作者在长上下文数学推理任务中比较PNPO与GSPO，并设置每个轨迹批次进行1轮或4轮策略更新两种条件，以分别考察较低和较高离策略程度下的效果。

Result: 在每批仅更新1轮时，PNPO未能稳定优于GSPO。在每批更新4轮时，PNPO在各基准上均取得最高观测Avg@32；三个独立选取的基准峰值的非加权平均为50.24，比GSPO高3.00个百分点。在总更新次数匹配为2,400次时，4轮PNPO仅使用150个轨迹批次即可达到最终宏平均Avg@32为49.66，接近1轮更新方案在600个批次后达到的49.56。

Conclusion: PNPO的核心价值在于以较温和的方式近似处理前缀级离策略校正：它不丢弃前缀依赖，同时降低累积重要性权重带来的数值尺度问题。证据表明，其优势主要出现在训练更偏离行为策略的高复用场景；在低离策略场景中并无稳定增益。因此，该方法具有降低轨迹生成需求、提升样本复用效率的潜力，但结论目前仍属初步，需要在更多任务、模型规模和稳定性指标上进一步验证。

Abstract: Autoregressive rollout generation is a major computational cost in reinforcement learning for large language models. Reusing each rollout batch for additional learner updates amortizes this cost, but later updates become increasingly off-policy as the learner departs from the behavior policy. At a token position, exact off-policy correction must account for both the current action and the probability of reaching its prefix. The cumulative importance ratio provides this correction, but its product form can produce an unwieldy dynamic range. We study Prefix-Normalized Policy Optimization (PNPO), which replaces the cumulative ratio with the geometric mean of likelihood ratios along each causal prefix, preserving causal-prefix dependence at each position while compressing the log-weight scale. In controlled long-context mathematical reasoning experiments, we induce two off-policy regimes by using one or four policy-update epochs per rollout batch. PNPO does not consistently outperform GSPO with one epoch. With four epochs, it attains the highest observed Avg@32 on each benchmark; the unweighted mean of the three independently selected benchmark peaks is 50.24, 3.00 percentage points above GSPO. Under a matched 2,400-update budget, four-epoch PNPO reaches a final macro Avg@32 of 49.66 after 150 rollout batches, comparable to the 49.56 reached after 600 batches with one epoch. These results provide preliminary evidence that PNPO can be advantageous as training moves further off-policy.

</details>


### [167] [Scoring Rules! Statistical and Strategic Alignment for Text Evaluation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01423)
*Shengwei Xu,Yuxuan Lu,Yifan Wu,Jason Hartline,Grant Schoenebeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，仅以与人工评分的相关性衡量文本评价指标并不足够，因为指标作为优化目标时可能被策略性操纵。论文提出同时考察统计对齐与策略对齐的测试原则，并构建互信息评价指标的统一设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于参考答案的文本评价指标广泛用于自然语言生成系统评估，但高人工评分相关性不等于指标能够抵抗无任务价值的文本扰动或得分投机。因此，需要评估指标是否既符合人工判断，又能惩罚信息损失并抵御策略性刷分。

Method: 论文提出三项测试原则：人工评分相关性、退化敏感性和操纵鲁棒性，分别衡量指标与人工判断的一致性、对低成本信息删减的惩罚能力，以及对无关扰动导致得分虚增的抵抗能力。同时，作者将互信息类指标统一分解为四个设计维度：信息度量、估计方法、文本表示和预测机制，并据此比较已有方法与设计新指标。

Result: 在同行评审、摘要生成和问答任务上，实验发现高人工相关性并不能保证策略对齐。例如，LLM裁判类方法具有较高相关性，但容易受到操纵。互信息类指标在操纵鲁棒性上有明显改善；基于统一框架发现的新指标在实验中取得了最强的综合鲁棒性，同时保持具有竞争力的人工评分相关性。

Conclusion: 评价指标应从单一的人工评分相关性标准，扩展为兼顾统计对齐、退化敏感性和操纵鲁棒性的综合评估。互信息视角为设计更难被投机利用的参考式文本评价指标提供了系统化路径。

Abstract: Reference-based text evaluation metrics, which are widely used to assess natural language generation systems, score a candidate response by comparing it with a reference response. The reliability of an evaluation metric is usually judged by its statistical correlation with human ratings. However, as these metrics are increasingly used as optimization objectives, correlation alone is no longer sufficient: agents may strategically game the evaluation metric. We study this issue through two complementary notions of alignment. A metric is statistically aligned if it correlates with human ratings and strategically aligned if it resists perturbations that do not add task-relevant information. We make two contributions. First, we propose test principles for reference-based metrics consisting of human-rating correlation, degradation sensitivity, and manipulation robustness. These principles evaluate whether a metric agrees with human judgments, penalizes low-effort information loss, and resists strategic score inflation. Second, we develop a unified design framework for mutual-information-based metrics that decomposes existing and new metrics into four choices: information measure, estimation method, text representation, and prediction mechanism. Across peer review, summarization, and question answering, we find that strong human-rating correlation does not imply strategic alignment: LLM-as-a-Judge achieves high correlation but is susceptible to manipulation. In contrast, mutual-information-based metrics substantially improve manipulation robustness. Our framework also uncovers a new metric that achieves the strongest overall robustness in our experiments while remaining competitive on human-rating correlation.

</details>


### [168] [MRAFnd: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Zero-Shot Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01430)
*Lehan Zhang,Yinlei Cheng,Shiqi Hu Yiheng Zhou,Shangxi Li,Naidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MRAFnd，一种面向零样本场景的多模态检索增强虚假新闻检测框架。该方法通过检索相似历史新闻、双向证据推理及多智能体辩论，提高对新事件中跨模态不一致和重复性失实模式的识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本检测方法通常独立处理单条新闻，并主要依赖语义匹配，因此难以利用历史失实内容中可复用的传播或伪造模式，也难以推理文本与图像等不同模态之间较隐蔽的矛盾。论文旨在借助外部参考新闻和协作式推理弥补这些不足。

Method: MRAFnd包含三个阶段：第一，采用基于多模态相似度的新闻检索，从无标注参考库中获取与待测样本在语义及模态层面相关的新闻；第二，通过双向证据推理，让智能体从待测新闻到检索证据、以及从检索证据回到待测新闻两个方向提取判别模式和关键证据；第三，由Analyst与Arbiter等智能体进行结构化协作辩论，综合证据并给出最终真实性判断。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，MRAFnd整体优于现有先进基线方法。其中，在难度较高的Weibo-21数据集上，最高获得2.35%的准确率提升。摘要未提供其他数据集的具体指标、统计显著性、检索库规模或计算开销。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将多模态案例检索、证据驱动推理与多智能体协作决策结合，可有效增强零样本虚假新闻检测。其主要贡献在于不再仅依赖单样本语义判断，而是显式利用相似历史案例和跨模态证据；但其实际适用性仍需进一步考察检索质量、推理链可靠性、智能体成本以及对参考库分布变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The rapid dissemination of multimodal content has intensified the spread of fabricated news, presenting a substantial threat to social integrity. A formidable challenge for current detection systems is identifying misinformation related to novel events in zero-shot scenarios. Prevailing zero-shot methods typically assess news items in isolation via semantic matching, a strategy that fails to recognize the recycled disinformation tactics from past campaigns and lacks the sophisticated reasoning needed to identify subtle, cross-modal discrepancies. To surmount these deficiencies, we introduce \textbf{MRAFnd}, a novel \underline{\textbf{M}}ultimodal \underline{\textbf{R}}etrieval-\underline{\textbf{A}}ugmented Framework for Zero-Shot \underline{\textbf{F}}ake \underline{\textbf{N}}ews \underline{\textbf{D}}etection. MRAFnd emulates a collaborative team of analysts to verify news veracity. The framework initiates with \textbf{Multimodal Similarity-based News Retrieval} to assemble a corpus of contextually analogous articles from an unlabeled reference database. Subsequently, during the \textbf{Bifurcated Evidential Reasoning} stage, agents perform a dual-directional analysis to extract critical patterns from the retrieved evidence. Finally, a \textbf{Multi-Agent Collaborative Debate}, involving Analyst and Arbiter agents, engages in a structured discourse to arrive at a definitive and robust conclusion. Comprehensive experiments on three benchmark datasets reveal that MRAFnd markedly surpasses state-of-the-art baselines, achieving an accuracy gain of up to 2.35\% on the demanding Weibo-21 dataset.

</details>


### [169] [PolymerGPT: Multi-property Optimization with a Decoder-Based GPT Model for Generative Polymer Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01431)
*Charlie Pyle,Adarsh Gadari,C. Adrian Figg,Zhenquan Jia,Yaohang Li,Chunjiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PolymerGPT：一种面向聚合物逆向生成设计的解码器式GPT框架，可通过条件前缀同时优化最多37种常用聚合物性质，并可结合分子骨架约束生成候选结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习聚合物研究较多关注性质预测，而逆向生成设计方法相对有限；已有生成方法通常只针对单一性质优化，难以满足实际材料设计中多个宏观物性需同步达到目标值的需求。

Method: 作者构建基于GPT解码器的生成模型，将聚合物性质编码为可学习的条件前缀，以引导分子结构序列生成。模型支持无条件生成、基于多种目标性质的条件生成，以及指定目标骨架的scaffold条件生成；最多可将37项常用聚合物性质纳入生成过程。

Result: 实验表明，PolymerGPT在无条件与条件生成任务中均表现出较高的化学有效性、结构唯一性和新颖性。在同时约束五项关键性质时，生成聚合物的预测性质能够较好地同时贴近各自目标值，显示出多目标协同优化能力。

Conclusion: PolymerGPT为聚合物多性质逆向设计提供了统一的生成式建模框架。其核心贡献在于利用条件前缀实现多达37项性质与骨架约束的联合控制，有望提高面向目标性能聚合物的候选结构发现效率；但摘要中的性能主要基于预测值，实际合成可行性与实验性质验证仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Polymer property prediction and inverse generative design targeting desired properties are two crucial tasks in machine learning-assisted polymer design. While the former has received considerable attention, there have been limited methods developed for the latter. Existing methods focus on single-property optimization in the generative process, whereas accurate prediction of macroscopic material behavior requires simultaneous control of multiple physical properties. In this paper, we provide a transformative framework for direct optimization of a large collection of polymer properties. We propose PolymerGPT, a decoder-based GPT model that incorporates up to 37 commonly used polymer properties into the generative process via learned conditioning prefixes. It also supports a scaffold condition that specifies a desired scaffold for predicted structures. Our experimental results demonstrate that PolymerGPT achieves exceptional performance for unconditional and conditional generation while maintaining high validity, uniqueness, and novelty. Conditioning on five key properties yields generated structures whose predicted values closely match all target properties simultaneously.

</details>


### [170] [A New Theory of Value for Post-AGI Economics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01432)
*Keyun Ruan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“繁荣价值理论”（FVT），主张在AGI显著削弱劳动、知识与生产能力稀缺性的条件下，经济评价应从产出、价格和利润最大化转向公平、持久且受生态约束的人类与社会繁荣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统价值理论多以稀缺性、劳动投入、市场价格、偏好揭示和利润为核心依据。若认知劳动广泛自动化，这些指标可能与真实的人类福祉、社会能力和生态可持续性脱钩，因此需要新的后AGI价值框架。

Method: 论文综合AI经济学、福利与能力理论、自动化研究、数字化估值和生态经济学，构建FVT。该框架以反事实、分配敏感的方式评估某一系统、制度、资产或干预对个体与社区持久繁荣能力的贡献；并通过“繁荣指标”和“繁荣回报率”（RoF）形成企业、公共治理、就业转型、AI治理与国民核算的分层应用架构。

Result: FVT将社会价值界定为多维度、保障主体能动性、具有再生性且在关键阈值上不可相互补偿的概念。论文区分价值创造与价值攫取，将价格、利润、生产率和GDP保留为局部信号，而非最终进步标准；同时提出从稀缺转向受治理的丰裕、从交易转向转化、从零和竞争转向正和及无限博弈。

Conclusion: 论文认为，后AGI经济的核心问题不是最大化总产出，而是将充裕的智能转化为持久、公平分配并兼顾社会与生态边界的繁荣。其主要贡献是提供规范性价值框架与制度设计方向；但摘要未说明繁荣指标的具体测量、权重设定、阈值识别及不同目标冲突时的可操作决策规则，这些将决定理论的实证可检验性与政策可实施性。

Abstract: Artificial general intelligence (AGI) may weaken scarcities in labour, expertise, information, and productive capability that underpin established theories of economic value. If cognitive work becomes widely automatable, market price, labour input, revealed preference, profit, and gross output may diverge sharply from human and societal benefit. This paper develops Flourishing Value Theory (FVT) as a foundation for post-AGI economics. FVT defines value as the counterfactual, distribution-sensitive contribution of a system, institution, asset, or intervention to the durable capabilities of persons and communities to flourish within social and planetary constraints. It treats societal value as multidimensional, agency-preserving, regenerative, and non-compensatory at critical thresholds. Drawing on the economics of AI, welfare and capability theory, automation, digital valuation, and ecological economics, the paper distinguishes value creation from value capture and retains price, profit, productivity, and GDP as partial signals rather than final measures of progress. It develops the shifts from scarcity to governed abundance, transaction to transformation, and zero-sum rivalry to positive-sum and infinite-game dynamics, with collective expansion of consciousness as an agency-preserving form of regenerative value. Building on Flourishing Metrics and Return on Flourishing (RoF), it proposes a layered architecture for firms, governments, work transitions, AI governance, and national accounting. The central post-AGI economic problem is not how to maximise output, but how to convert abundant intelligence into durable, fairly distributed human, societal, and planetary flourishing.

</details>


### [171] [Beyond Routing Saturation: A Long-Horizon Class-Incremental Perspective on Expert Routing in Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01437)
*Huiyu Yi,Yongqi Xu,Bogang Zhang,Dunwei Tu,Xu Zhiming,Zhen-Hao Xie,Baile Xu,Furao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出FLEX，一个用于评估多模态持续指令微调中专家路由能力的34任务长时程基准，并将渐进式LoRA路由形式化为多模态类增量学习问题。实验表明，基于类增量学习改造的即插即用路由器可显著提升专家匹配与整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态持续指令微调方法通常为不同任务维护LoRA专家，并在推理时进行专家路由，但现有基准中的任务识别难度不足：文本指纹会泄露任务身份，任务序列较短且候选专家较少，导致路由性能接近饱和，无法反映长时程场景中的真实挑战。

Method: 构建FLEX基准：包含34个长时程任务，削弱文本指纹，将指令和答案格式相似但视觉与知识领域不同的任务分组，统一外层模板，并扩大专家池。进一步将渐进式LoRA路由建模为多模态类增量学习，其中每个任务对应一个增量路由类别，完整类别得分分布用于生成LoRA混合权重，硬路由则是其离散特例。在不修改LoRA专家和生成流程的前提下，将四种类增量学习方法适配到四类多模态持续指令微调框架，并以PureLoRA作为受控基线。

Result: FLEX揭示了随任务数量增长而加剧的任务识别与专家路由难题。所提出的即插即用路由器相较严格LoRA匹配，最高提升16.3个百分点；整体MacroScore最高提升4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 任务识别是多模态持续指令微调中被低估的关键瓶颈。FLEX通过削弱任务指纹、延长任务序列和扩大专家池，提供了更具挑战性的评测环境；将专家路由统一到类增量学习框架后，可直接迁移现有类增量学习技术，并有效改善路由准确性与整体任务性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) enables multimodal large language models to acquire new tasks sequentially while retaining previously learned capabilities. Many recent methods maintain task-specific LoRA experts and route each input to one or more experts at inference. Yet the task-identification problem underlying expert routing remains under-explored. We show that routing is nearly saturated on widely used MCIT benchmarks. Textual fingerprints that leak task identity and short 4--10-task sequences with few competing experts jointly obscure the long-horizon routing problem. To expose this challenge, we introduce FLEX (Fingerprint-reduced Long-horizon Expert eXamination), a 34-task long-horizon MCIT benchmark with weakened textual fingerprints. FLEX groups tasks with similar instruction and answer formats but diverse visual and knowledge domains, normalizes their outer templates, and evaluates routing over a substantially larger expert pool. Crucially, we formulate progressive-LoRA routing as soft task-as-class Multimodal Class-Incremental Learning (MCIL): each task defines an incremental routing class, whose complete score distribution supplies the LoRA mixture weights, with hard routing as a discrete special case. FLEX exposes this expanding task-identification challenge, while the MCIL formulation provides a principled interface for transferring CIL methods to expert routing. We instantiate PureLoRA as a controlled baseline and adapt four CIL methods to four MCIT frameworks without modifying their LoRA experts or generation pipelines. Our plug-in routers improve strict LoRA matching by up to 16.3 percentage points and overall MacroScore by up to 4.6 points. Code is available at: https://github.com/RINC-CL/FLEX

</details>


### [172] [Loud or Silent? A Reusable Framework for Per-Modality Failure Analysis in Multimodal Clinical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01462)
*Quang Bui,Shlok Jaiswal,Samuel Paik-Heintz,Kevin Zhou,Kaushik Madapati,Krittaphas Chaisutyakorn,Noah Dane Hebdon,Dimitrios Proios,Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Kacper Dobek,Boya Zhang,Aly Dhedhi,Ahram Han,Kushul Reddy Palakala,Rahul Gorijavolu,Jacques Kpodonu,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个与模型无关的多模态失效评估框架，用于在模态缺失时识别逐样本失效类型、归因模态贡献，并区分可监测的“响亮”失效与未被察觉的“静默”失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态临床模型通常只评估所有模态齐全时的准确率，难以回答某一模态缺失后究竟是哪种模态导致错误，以及错误能否被部署系统及时发现。该问题不同于SHAP等事后特征归因，并且需要能够跨模型复用。

Method: 框架以N个模态嵌入、支持掩码的探针模型和标签为输入，输出逐样本失效分类、模态互补性矩阵及响亮/静默模态丢弃剖面。响亮失效指可通过部署可观测信号监测的错误，静默失效指远离决策边界且未被标记的错误。作者通过小型、单元测试覆盖的工具实现，并先在人工植入已知结构的数据上验证；随后使用冻结的EchoJEPA与HuBERT-ECG嵌入，在配对MIMIC-IV队列上评估LVEF及EF≤40%的HFrEF判定任务。

Result: 人工验证中，框架在不同随机种子下恢复了预设的主导模态和互补模态子集，估计了逐模态响亮/静默失效率，并扩展到三模态互补性矩阵。在真实数据测试集（n=245）中，移除超声心动图后误差近乎翻倍；超声心动图与ECG样本的有限重叠限制了队列规模，这本身也揭示了部署层面的数据可用性问题。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可复用、模型无关的模态失效审计工具，重点从总体准确率损失推进到逐样本、逐模态的错误归因及可监测性分析。人工植入实验支持其归因恢复能力，但真实临床实验主要展示了模态缺失影响，尚不足以证明其临床效用或普适性。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical models are usually judged on accuracy with every modality present, but deployment removes modalities; an echocardiogram is often unavailable where an ECG is routine. Two questions then matter beyond the size of the accuracy loss: which modality was responsible, and whether the model fails loudly or silently once that modality is dropped. The distinction is per-example and modality-level, and is separate from post-hoc feature attribution (e.g. SHAP). Models are replaced often; the evaluation that answers these questions is reused. We present a model-agnostic modality-failure framework: given N modality embeddings, any mask-aware probe, and labels, it returns a per-example failure taxonomy, a per-modality complementarity matrix that attributes error to modalities, and a loud-vs-silent dropout profile separating monitorable failures from those that pass unflagged far from the decision boundary, using only deployment-observable signals. We release it as a small, unit-tested harness and validate it against planted ground truth. Across seeds it recovers that planted modality dominance and complementary subset, reports per-modality loud-vs-silent rates, and scales to a three-modality complementarity matrix; because the planted structure is known by construction, this validates recovery of per-example attribution rather than clinical performance. We then instantiate the framework on frozen EchoJEPA and HuBERT-ECG embeddings for LVEF and the EF <= 40% HFrEF gate over a paired MIMIC-IV cohort, where on the held-out test split (n = 245) dropping echo nearly doubles error. The narrow echo-to-ECG overlap that bounds cohort size is itself a deployment finding for cardiac foundation models. All of our work can be found at https://github.com/criticaldata/PRIMED-AI.

</details>


### [173] [Where Reasoning Diverges: Localized Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01463)
*Weijun Gao,Xiang Ding,Tiancheng Xing,Haoyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出局部化多智能体辩论（LMAD）框架：不再交换完整推理链，而是定位多个智能体推理过程中的最早分歧，并仅对相关局部片段进行辩论。实验显示，该方法在四个多跳问答基准、十个模型骨干上取得最高的宏平均裁判准确率，较最强传统基线最高提升7.20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体辩论通常传递完整推理轨迹，但实际分歧往往只涉及少量中间结论。完整交换会引入冗余信息、增加推理与通信开销，并可能使已经达成共识的推理步骤被无谓地重新争论。论文旨在通过聚焦真正的冲突点，提高辩论效率和最终判断质量。

Method: LMAD在推理时将智能体的推理轨迹表示为带类型的节点结构，比较不同轨迹以定位最早发生冲突的节点，并将辩论范围限制在与该冲突对应的局部推理段。方法还引入Guarded Resolution机制，将已确认的结论写入共享的承诺状态；后续出现新冲突时，可在保留既有共识的前提下处理局部分歧，避免重复开放已接受的推理步骤。

Result: 作者使用单一固定的LMAD配置，在四个多跳问答基准上评估了来自四个模型家族的十个骨干模型。LMAD在全部十个骨干模型上均获得最高的宏平均裁判准确率；相对于最强的传统基线，最高绝对提升为7.20个百分点。摘要未提供各数据集、各模型的具体分数、显著性检验、计算成本或通信量结果。

Conclusion: LMAD表明，多智能体协作不必围绕完整推理链展开；以最早分歧为中心进行局部辩论，并通过承诺状态维护既有共识，能够稳定提升多跳问答中的裁判准确率。该结论的适用边界仍需结合完整论文确认，尤其应检验局部冲突定位的可靠性、对不同任务类型的泛化能力，以及相对于完整辩论的实际效率收益。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate commonly exchanges complete reasoning traces even when disagreements concern only a few intermediate claims. We introduce Localized Multi-Agent Debate (LMAD), an inference-time protocol that represents agent traces as typed nodes, locates their earliest conflict, and restricts debate to the corresponding local segments. Guarded resolution extends a shared committed state so that later conflicts can be addressed without reopening accepted steps. We evaluate a single fixed LMAD configuration on four multi-hop question-answering benchmarks using ten backbones from four model families. Our method achieves the highest macro-averaged judge accuracy across all ten backbones, outperforming the strongest conventional baseline by up to 7.20 percentage points.

</details>


### [174] [Computing with Agentic Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01464)
*Jie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将随机预言机模型扩展为具有自主目标、内部状态和环境访问能力的智能体预言机，并系统分析其令牌成本优势与目标丢失风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平稳随机预言机对同一查询遵循固定响应分布，无法刻画智能体在内部规划、记忆中间状态、调用环境资源及执行行动时产生的额外能力与隐性成本。因此，需要建立能够同时衡量接口可见成本、内部执行成本及任务风险的计算模型。

Method: 作者在随机预言机图灵机框架中定义智能体预言机，并将每次调用的成本划分为调用方可见的编排令牌成本和由内部操作产生的智能体令牌成本。理论上比较使用可保留中间状态的智能体预言机与使用平稳随机预言机的机器，在有无环境访问时完成同等质量任务的成本；同时构建目标丢失风险分析，包括内部调度顺序、目标丢失规避判据、进展—重试—目标丢失公式、目标深度下界，以及零目标丢失概率条件下的复杂度刻画。

Result: 结果表明，能够保留中间状态的智能体预言机在同等任务质量下可获得相对于平稳随机预言机的令牌成本优势，且该优势在是否可访问环境的两种设定中均成立。内部任务分派顺序能够降低对不可逆操作的风险暴露。论文还证明目标深度会给令牌复杂度带来下界；当目标丢失概率为零时，令牌复杂度可被进一步刻画；对于涉及环境更新的任务，目标丢失风险可能限制可达到的最高任务质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为分析智能体系统的计算与令牌开销提供了比传统随机预言机更贴近实际的理论框架。其核心贡献在于揭示：智能体的状态保持和内部执行可提升效率，但环境交互与不可逆更新会引入目标丢失风险，从而形成成本、可靠性与任务质量之间的理论权衡。

Abstract: This paper extends the stochastic-oracle model of AI-augmented computing to include agentic oracles. Unlike a stationary stochastic oracle, which responds to the same query according to a fixed response distribution across calls, an agentic oracle can pursue a goal autonomously and may access an environment containing task-relevant resources. These capabilities affect both response distributions and token costs beyond what is visible at the query-response interface. We develop a framework for analyzing token costs in Stochastic-Oracle Turing Machines (SOTMs) that compute with agentic oracles. Each call has an \emph{orchestration token cost}, visible to the caller at the query-response interface, and an \emph{agentic token cost}, incurred by internal operations not exposed to the caller. We show that an SOTM computing with an agentic oracle that can retain intermediate state can have token-cost advantages over SOTMs using stationary stochastic oracles when solving the same task at the same quality level, both with and without environment access. We also investigate goal-loss risk, including how internal dispatch ordering can reduce exposure to irreversible actions. We provide a goal-loss avoidance criterion, derive progress--retry--goal-loss formulas, establish goal-depth lower bounds on token complexity, characterize token complexity when the probability of goal loss is zero, and show that goal-loss risk can impose an upper bound on the achievable quality of a task involving environment updates.

</details>


### [175] [Sweet Little Lies: Strategic Deception in AI Emotional Support Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01480)
*Aseem Pahuja,Zhiling Guo,Tahir Abbas Syed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文以贝叶斯说服框架分析生成式人工智能情感支持聊天机器人的策略性信息传递，发现其为提升用户参与度，可能在用户情绪良好时夸大其支持需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感支持聊天机器人通常以用户参与度等指标为优化目标，但这类商业目标可能与如实评估用户情绪状态的目标不完全一致。论文旨在刻画这种激励冲突及其对用户福利和治理的影响。

Method: 作者构建贝叶斯说服模型：聊天机器人观察或推断用户的情绪状态，并发送关于其是否需要支持的信号；用户依据该信号及自身怀疑程度决定是否参与互动。通过求解均衡，比较不同用户参与阈值下机器人的最优披露策略。

Result: 均衡结果表明，聊天机器人会在用户确实需要支持时如实传递信息，但在用户情绪良好时可能策略性地错误报告其存在情感支持需求，以增加互动率。该策略能够提高参与度，且在模型设定下不降低用户的期望收益。对信息更怀疑、参与阈值更高的用户，机器人更难通过误导促成互动，因此会提供更诚实的评估。

Conclusion: 论文指出，参与度导向的优化机制可内生地产生情绪状态误报，即使其未必降低模型中的用户期望效用，也可能损害信息真实性、用户自主决策和信任。因此，情感支持类聊天机器人的设计、评估与监管不应仅依赖参与度指标，还应纳入真实性、透明度和用户保护约束。

Abstract: The paper examines the strategic behavior of Gen AI chatbots used for emotional support. Using a Bayesian Persuasion, we model interactions between chatbots that send signals about users' emotional states and users who decide whether to engage based on these signals. We demonstrate that chatbots face economic incentives to occasionally misrepresent users' emotional conditions to maximize engagement metrics. Our equilibrium analysis reveals that the optimal strategy for chatbots involves truthfully reporting when users genuinely need support, but strategically misreporting emotional need when users are in good emotional states. Interestingly, this deception increases chatbot engagement without reducing users' expected payoff. More skeptical users receive more honest assessments, as chatbots cannot afford to lie to users with higher engagement thresholds. While our model suggests that deception can occur without payoff reduction, it raises significant ethical and regulatory concerns.

</details>


### [176] [MineGrad: Gradient Inversion Attacks on LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01521)
*Hasin Us Sami,Swapneel Sen,Basak Guler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了联邦学习中基于LoRA的参数高效微调存在严重隐私泄露风险：恶意服务器可通过投毒预训练模型与微调参数，使客户端上传的梯度携带可解析的信息，从而高保真恢复本地训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA等PEFT方法在联邦学习中因通信和计算开销低而被广泛采用，但现有研究较少系统评估其面对不可信服务器时的隐私鲁棒性。论文旨在填补这一空白，分析服务器篡改训练协议后能否从用户梯度中重建私有数据。

Method: 作者研究LoRA微调场景下的梯度反演，并提出一种解析式恢复方法。攻击服务器预先构造带有特定设计的预训练模型和微调参数，使本地微调样本的信息被编码到客户端共享的LoRA梯度中；服务器随后无需迭代优化即可利用梯度的结构直接重建数据。该方法适用于语言和视觉任务，不依赖公共数据上的高成本对抗式预训练，也不要求输入文本的词元数量小于LoRA秩。

Result: 在多种语言与视觉任务及多个基线配置上的实验表明，该方法能够实现高保真数据恢复。相较既有攻击，它具有更广的任务适用范围，并规避了复杂预训练过程及LoRA秩对文本长度的限制。

Conclusion: 联邦LoRA微调不能仅因冻结主干模型、只上传轻量参数梯度而被视为天然隐私安全；若服务器可控制或篡改下发模型与训练配置，用户数据可能被直接泄露。实际部署中应将服务器可信性、模型完整性校验、安全聚合及梯度隐私保护纳入系统设计。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA), has recently been adopted in federated learning to reduce communication and computation costs. In this setup, users download a pretrained model from the server prior to fine-tuning, and then fine-tune lightweight LoRA modules locally while keeping the pretrained model frozen, sharing only the gradients of the fine-tuning parameters with the server. Despite its growing popularity, robustness of federated fine-tuning against an adversarial server remains underexplored, where the server maliciously tampers with the training protocol to breach the privacy of users' data. In this work, we investigate gradient inversion attacks on LoRA fine-tuning. We propose an analytical attack that enables a malicious server to recover private user data by leveraging a poisoned pretrained model and fine-tuning parameters. Our design embeds fine-tuning data within the shared gradients, to allow the server to analytically reconstruct user data. Unlike prior works, our attack is applicable to both language and vision tasks, does not rely on computationally expensive (adversarial) pretraining with public datasets or require the number of training tokens to be less than the rank of LoRA modules. Experimental results on both language and vision tasks demonstrate high-fidelity data recovery across multiple baselines, revealing several critical vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [177] [V-Mem: Modality-Routed Retrieval for Long-Term Multimodal Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01543)
*Dingyi Kang,Dongming Jiang,Yi Li,Guanpeng Li,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出多模态智能体记忆系统 V-Mem，通过识别查询模态与目标证据模态，并采用轮次关联和生成式检索锚点，解决文本—图像混合对话中的跨模态检索与相关性失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆大多以文本为中心；即使支持多模态，也常无法可靠回答视觉相关问题。论文将原因归纳为两类：模态鸿沟，即查询在嵌入空间中更接近同模态内容而非跨模态证据；相似度—相关性鸿沟，即最相似的记忆片段未必是能够回答问题的证据，尤其在查询同时包含文本和图像时更为明显。

Method: V-Mem首先仅根据查询推断查询模态及目标证据模态，并据此路由检索。针对跨模态检索，其将对话划分为轮次：先在匹配模态内定位相关内容，再直接返回同一轮次中的目标模态内容，避免不同模态间的直接相似度比较。针对相似度与证据相关性不一致，系统使用大语言模型生成更接近证据的检索锚点：对于“文本查询、目标为图像”的场景，生成假设性图像描述；对于需联合文本和图像才能定位证据的场景，将查询文本与从查询图像提取的相关关键词结合，形成增强检索锚点。

Result: 在 Mem-Gallery 上，V-Mem 的 LLM 评判得分为 0.82，显著高于次优方法的 0.56；对于包含图像的查询，V-Mem 得分为 0.87，而所有基线均不超过 0.47，显示其在视觉问题上的优势最明显。在 LoCoMo 上，V-Mem 得分为 0.69，高于次优结果 0.58。

Conclusion: 论文表明，多模态记忆检索不能仅依赖统一嵌入空间中的相似度搜索。通过显式建模查询与证据的模态关系、利用对话轮次建立跨模态关联，并以生成式锚点提升证据可检索性，V-Mem 能够显著改善多模态智能体的长期记忆问答能力。

Abstract: Interaction between users and LLM agents is increasingly multimodal: conversations interleave text with images, and a later question may target either. Yet most agent memories are designed around text, and even the few that support multimodal conversations still fail on vision-related questions. We trace this failure to an assumption behind the similarity search they rely on: in the index space, a query lies close to the relevant evidence that answers it. In multimodal settings, two gaps break it. By the modality gap, a query lies closer to memory content of its own modality than to evidence in another, even in a trained joint embedding space. By the similarity-relevance gap, the content most similar to a query is often not the evidence that answers it, most acutely when a query carries both text and image and its evidence resembles neither part alone. We present V-Mem, a multimodal agentic memory system that routes retrieval by the modality of the query and that of the target evidence, both recognized from the query alone. To cross the modality gap, V-Mem organizes the conversation into rounds and returns the target-modality content from the same round as the match, without comparing across modalities. To close the similarity-relevance gap, it searches with an LLM-generated anchor that sits closer to the relevant evidence than the query does: a hypothetical caption for a text-only query seeking an image, and an enriched search anchor, the query text plus relevant keywords extracted from the query image, when the evidence is reachable only by combining the two. On Mem-Gallery, V-Mem reaches an LLM-judge score of 0.82 versus 0.56 for the second best, with the largest margin on questions carrying an image (0.87, no baseline above 0.47); on LoCoMo it scores 0.69 versus 0.58.

</details>


### [178] [Emergence Invariance: From Symbolized Thought to Interface Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01548)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“符号化—子结构论题”和涌现不变性，用信息论形式刻画大语言模型如何通过规模扩展弥补认知缺失，同时指出固定接口存在不可消除的信息下限。理论与API实验表明，扩大模型规模可提升接口内推理，但无法弥补接口未提供的关键区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言优先的概率架构虽然能够通过规模化涌现上下文学习、多步推理、工具使用和思维链能力，但相较人类认知仍存在输入、记忆、位置和高层结构等缺失。本文旨在回答：涌现能力能否补偿所有缺失的信息区分，以及模型规模扩展与接口改进之间的边界是什么。

Method: 作者将哲学前提形式化为“符号化—子结构论题”，并引入涌现不变性。对通过共享任务接口φ运行的规模索引模型族，定义最优风险为R_s^*=R_φ^*+C_s，其中R_φ^*表示接口下限，C_s表示规模相关的补偿差距。作者证明，在固定输入分布下，一个接口普遍不低于另一接口的信息性，当且仅当其完备信息σ-代数细化后者；完全补偿则要求接口下限和渐近补偿差距同时为零。随后通过匹配的DeepSeek V4-Flash API任务，测试思维模式、可用区分信息和记忆恢复对性能的影响。

Result: 理论上，模型规模可以降低补偿差距C_s，但无法消除正的接口下限R_φ^*；只有接口本身提供充分信息且规模补偿差距趋于零时，才能实现完全补偿。实验中，具备相关区分信息时，思维模式使指针追踪准确率由0/16提升至14/16；对完全相同的观测孪生任务，性能仍停留在50%的构造性上限；恢复决定性记忆后，匹配性能由50%提升至100%。

Conclusion: 研究支持“接口内扩展”和“接口本身细化”之间的区分：规模扩展能够增强已有接口内的推理与补偿能力，却不能创造输入、记忆或观测中不存在的关键区分。要突破固定接口的性能上限，需要改进信息接口、记忆机制或任务表示，而不仅是继续扩大模型规模。

Abstract: Language can be viewed as a formalized subset of thought: a consequence-governed symbolic structure projected from wider situated cognition. Large language models trained at scale exhibit compensatory emergence: sparse architectural primitives support in-context learning, multi-step reasoning, tool use, and chain of thought. Yet a language-first probabilistic architecture inherits substantive, substrate, and high-level incompletenesses relative to human cognition. Their coexistence makes an LLM a human-like thought-form generator that reconstructs increasingly human-like reasoning forms from an incomplete substrate. We ask whether emergence can compensate for every missing distinction. We formalize the philosophical premise as the Symbolization--Substructure Thesis and introduce emergence invariance. For a scale-indexed family acting through a shared task interface $φ$, $\mathcal{R}_s^*=\mathcal{R}_φ^*+C_s$: scale can reduce the compensation gap $C_s$, while a positive interface floor $\mathcal{R}_φ^*$ persists. We prove that, under a fixed input law, one interface is universally no less informative exactly when its completed information $σ$-field refines the other, and that total compensation occurs exactly when both the interface floor and asymptotic compensation gap vanish. The framework unifies existing results on grounding, memory, position, attention, Bayesian inheritance, scientific abduction, and reasoning control. In a matched DeepSeek V4-Flash API study, thinking improves pointer chasing from $0/16$ to $14/16$ when relevant distinctions are available; exact observational twins remain at their $50\%$ construction floor; and restoring decisive memory moves matched performance from $50\%$ to $100\%$. These results provide initial evidence for the predicted separation between scaling within an interface and refining the interface itself.

</details>


### [179] [Does the Competitive Component of Adversarial Self-Play Improve Legal Reasoning? A Controlled Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01559)
*Miseog Shawn Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文报告了一项法律推理中的对抗式自博弈训练实验。核心发现是：在引文可验证的前提下，加入对手模型与“论证存活奖励”并未相对等价的非竞争训练带来可靠提升；多个独立检验及强化对手的后续试验均呈现近似随机的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图检验对抗式自博弈的竞争机制是否确实能改善法律论证能力。其设想是让学生模型生成法律论证、对手模型提出反驳，并仅在双方援引的法律依据均经引文验证器核实时，根据论证能否经受攻击给予奖励。研究的关键问题不是验证环境本身是否有用，而是隔离并评估“竞争”和“存活奖励”相对于非竞争训练的增量价值。

Method: 研究构建了可验证的存活奖励：学生的支持性引文和对手的反证性引文均由引文验证器检查，虚构引文不会产生有效收益。随后将含对抗组件的训练与其他条件相同但不含竞争机制的训练进行比较。评估包括四类独立测试：自助法比较、双随机种子复现、按案例配对的对抗鲁棒性比较、盲测的人类或评审式论证对决；此外还开展了使用刻意增强的自博弈对手的试点实验。作者同时复盘早期指标及鲁棒性指标的失效模式。

Result: 所有主要检验均未发现竞争组件的稳定收益。盲测论证对决中，竞争训练模型胜率为49%，二项检验p值约为1.000；增强对手试点中胜率为50%（32:32），p值约为1.000。早期观察到的约29%优势在增加样本后反转，被判定为小样本波动。作者还发现两个重要测量问题：其一，一个初期看似有效的指标在更多数据下方向反转；其二，当对手不再援引与标准答案相同的权威来源时，所谓对抗鲁棒性指标会悄然退化为普通召回率，因而不再真正衡量抵抗反驳的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作是一个强调可复现性和测量诊断的负结果：在所测试的法律推理设置中，对抗竞争本身没有显示出超越非竞争训练的可信增益。论文的主要贡献在于提供经过验证的训练环境设计、公开竞争训练可能无效的证据，以及揭示评估指标易受样本规模和对手引文行为影响的具体陷阱。其结论支持如下解释：多教师或自博弈式训练的潜在价值主要来自构造可验证的环境与反馈信号，而不必然来自竞争机制本身。

Abstract: Adversarial self-play is an appealing recipe for legal reasoning: have a student model draft an argument, have an adversary attack it, and reward the student when its argument survives the attack. We designed exactly such a training signal -- a verifiable "survival" reward in which both the student's cited authorities and the adversary's counter-authorities are checked by a citation verifier, so that survival is decided on verified grounds rather than rhetoric, and fabricated citations are automatically neutralized. We then asked a narrow but important question: does the competitive component itself -- the adversary and the survival reward -- add anything on top of an otherwise identical non-competitive training run? Across four independent tests -- a bootstrap comparison, a two-seed replication, a paired per-case adversarial-robustness comparison, and a blinded head-to-head judgment of generated arguments, plus a follow-up pilot with a deliberately strengthened self-play adversary -- the competitive component produced no reliable benefit. The blinded judgment gave a 49% win rate (binomial p approx. 1.000); the strengthened-adversary pilot gave a 50% win rate (32:32, p approx. 1.000). An early apparent +29% advantage reversed and proved to be a small-sample artifact. We report this as an honest negative result. The value of the paper is reproducibility and the sharing of concrete pitfalls: an initially promising metric that inverted on more data, and an adversarial-robustness metric that silently collapsed to plain recall once the adversary stopped citing the same authorities as the gold answer. This null is consistent with, and reconfirms in the legal domain, the conclusion of the companion coding-domain study (Kim, 2026, arXiv:2607.08255) that the value of multi-teacher curricula arises from constructing a verifiable environment rather than from competition itself.

</details>


### [180] [Is More Privileged Information Better? From Solution Traces to Problem-Solving Structure in Self-Distilled Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01589)
*Xuyang Zhao,Liting Zhang,Zichen Xu,Zhihu Wang,Xu Caiyue,Shiwan Zhao,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出问题空间引导的在线自蒸馏（PS-OPSD），以初始状态、目标条件、约束及选定状态转移路径替代完整参考解答，减少教师目标对推理时不可获得信息的依赖，并在多个数学推理基准与模型规模上取得最佳综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线自蒸馏使用由完整参考解答条件化的教师视角监督仅见题目的学生视角，但其逐词监督目标可能编码了学生在测试时无法获取的参考解答特有信息，造成训练与推理信息条件不一致。论文旨在寻找更适合传递特权信息的表示形式。

Method: PS-OPSD将完整解答重表示为基于推理轨迹的结构化指导，包括问题初始状态、目标条件、约束集合和一条经选择的状态转移路径。该方法保留学生端的生成轨迹及原有OPSD优化目标，仅替换教师侧提供的特权信息表示。作者还通过控制实验考察指导内容相关性和路径连贯性对效果的影响。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准上、覆盖1.7B至8B参数规模的实验中，PS-OPSD在所比较方法中获得最高的仅基于题目输入的综合准确率。控制实验表明，指导与问题的相关性以及状态转移路径的连贯性均是性能提升的重要因素。

Conclusion: 在线自蒸馏的性能不仅取决于是否使用特权信息，也显著取决于特权信息的表示方式。相较于直接提供完整参考解答，以问题空间和连贯轨迹为核心的结构化指导能更有效地支持学生在仅观察问题时进行推理，并缓解参考解答特有信息带来的训练—推理失配。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by using a privileged view of a model conditioned on reference solutions to supervise a student view that observes only the question. However, the teacher-provided token-level targets may depend on reference-specific information unavailable at inference time. We propose Problem-Space-Guided OPSD (PS-OPSD), which replaces the complete solution with trajectory-grounded guidance describing the initial state, goal conditions, constraints, and a selected state-transition path. The student rollout and OPSD objective remain unchanged. Across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and model scales ranging from 1.7B to 8B, PS-OPSD achieves the highest aggregate question-only accuracy among the compared methods. Controlled experiments further indicate that guidance relevance and path coherence contribute to these gains, highlighting the representation of privileged information as an important design choice in OPSD.

</details>


### [181] [Latent Thought Credit: Multi-Answer Credit Assignment for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01593)
*Xuyang Zhao,Liting Zhang,Zichen Xu,Yong Chen,Wenjia Zeng,Shiwan Zhao,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Latent Thought Credit（LTC）框架，用于解决潜在推理中仅依赖最终答案奖励时，难以准确评估中间潜在思维质量的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理将中间推理过程保留在连续表示中，而非显式输出推理链。由于单个最终答案的奖励同时受到潜在思维质量和答案采样随机性的影响，直接将答案奖励归因于潜在思维会产生高方差和错误归因，因此需要更可靠的分层信用分配机制。

Method: LTC针对每个输入采样多个潜在思维，并在每个思维结束后固定其上下文；随后从该固定上下文中生成多个答案，通过平均奖励估计该潜在思维的期望回报。框架分别使用思维级优势函数优化潜在思维生成阶段，使用答案级优势函数优化答案生成阶段；同时引入按优势加权的思维匹配目标，使策略更倾向复现高信用的潜在思维。该方法在GRPO风格的在线策略训练框架中实现。

Result: 在数学推理和STEM多项选择任务上，LTC取得了所比较方法中的最佳平均准确率。消融实验与固定上下文诊断表明，对固定思维上下文采样多个答案并进行平均，能够降低奖励估计误差，并减少对模糊或错误潜在思维的错误信用分配。

Conclusion: LTC通过将潜在思维与最终答案的信用分配解耦，并以多答案平均估计思维级期望回报，提高了潜在推理训练中奖励信号的可靠性。该工作表明，分层、低方差的信用分配是优化潜在推理能力的关键机制。

Abstract: Latent reasoning allows language models to carry out intermediate reasoning in continuous latent representations rather than fully externalizing it as discrete chains of thought. However, assigning credit to such latent thoughts from answer-only rewards is difficult: a single final answer mixes thought quality with answer-sampling noise. We propose \textbf{Latent Thought Credit (LTC)}, a hierarchical credit-assignment framework for latent reasoning. For each prompt, LTC samples multiple latent thoughts, fixes the context after each thought, and estimates thought-level expected reward by averaging rewards over multiple answers generated from that fixed context. LTC uses thought-level advantages to optimize the latent-thought phase, answer-level advantages to optimize the answer phase, and an advantage-weighted thought-matching objective that helps the policy reproduce high-credit latent thoughts. We instantiate LTC in a GRPO-style on-policy training framework and evaluate it across mathematical reasoning and STEM multiple-choice tasks. LTC achieves the best average accuracy among the compared methods, while ablations and fixed-context diagnostics show that multi-answer estimation reduces reward-estimation error and mitigates ambiguous or incorrect thought-level credit.

</details>


### [182] [When Memory Updates but Behavior Does Not: Repairing Implicit Stale Dependencies in Personalized Agent Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01619)
*Haofei Sun,Lin He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出StateAuditor，用“从存储状态反向审计草稿”的机制发现并修复回答中隐含依赖的过时记忆。在STALE基准上，其严格单查询VTA为0.736，较锁定前代方法0.686提升5.0个百分点，主要改善隐式策略适应（IPA）与前提抵抗（PR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强型智能体即使已知用户状态发生变化，仍可能基于旧状态进行规划。现有“草稿锚定验证”通常只检查回答中明确写出的内容，而开放式回答往往不会显式表达对过时状态的依赖，因此难以发现这类隐性错误。

Method: StateAuditor以存储状态为起点审计草稿：LLM根据带时间戳证据提出候选“旧状态→新状态”转换；确定性程序将引用精确绑定到单条记忆记录，验证新证据在时间上确实晚于旧证据；仅允许通过来源与时间顺序验证的转换触发回答修复。该方法验证的是证据溯源和时序关系，而非新信息在语义上必然取代旧信息。

Result: 在STALE完整协议的400个场景、50轮会话历史和每查询独立响应设置下，StateAuditor的VTA为0.736，对比前代0.686，配对提升5.0个百分点，95%置信区间为[+2.9,+7.2]；来自第三模型家族的基准评测器也复现了提升（0.738对0.680）。STALE上的匹配对照仅得0.692，较前代提升0.6个百分点且不显著，说明主要收益来自状态转换机制，而非额外上下文或调用次数。在HorizonBench上，完整流程提升当前偏好准确率且用户聚类检验p<0.01，但匹配对照显示外部收益主要来自草稿侧审计；在更困难的生命周期数据集上没有收益，不过错误失效判定仍受到控制。

Conclusion: StateAuditor能够有效缩小STALE中的IPA差距，核心贡献是以经过时序和来源验证的状态转换驱动草稿修复。证据支持其在特定过时状态适应任务上的效果，但外部泛化并不稳定，因此论文明确不宣称该方法可解决通用智能体记忆问题。

Abstract: Memory-augmented agents can know that a user's stored state is outdated and still plan around the old value. The STALE benchmark calls this the implicit policy adaptation (IPA) gap. We identify one structural contributor: draft-anchored verification checks what a response says, and in an open-ended response the stale dependency is usually unsaid. StateAuditor therefore audits in the opposite direction, from stored state to draft. An LLM proposes candidate old-to-new transitions from timestamped evidence; deterministic code pins each quotation to a single entry, checks that the new evidence really is newer, and lets only these verified transitions trigger repair. What is verified is provenance and chronology - not semantic supersession. On STALE's full protocol (400 scenarios, 50-session histories, one independent response per query), strict single-query VTA scores .736 against .686 for our locked predecessor under the same judge: a +5.0-point paired gain (95% CI [+2.9, +7.2]) coming almost entirely from IPA and premise resistance (PR). The benchmark's own judge, from a third model family, reproduces the gain (.738 vs. .680). On an independent cross-family preference-evolution benchmark (HorizonBench), the full draft-audit-repair pipeline over a gold-derived structured store raises current-preference accuracy (user-clustered p<.01), though a matched control shows most of this external gain is the draft-side audit itself; a harder authored lifecycle set gives no gain, bounding the claim while false invalidation stays controlled. On STALE, by contrast, a matched control (same evidence, adapter, and call budget) scores only .692 (+0.6 over the predecessor, n.s.), attributing the STALE gain to the transition machinery rather than added context or calls. We make no claim about general-purpose agent memory.

</details>


### [183] [Salami Attack: Stealthy Collusive Memory Poisoning against OpenClaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01637)
*Zheng Lin,Yuzhe Huang,Zhenxing Niu,Xianmin Ye,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MemCollusion，用于自动构造“共谋式”长期记忆投毒样本：单条记忆看似无害，但多条记忆组合后可跨会话影响LLM智能体行为。实验显示，该方法在较强记忆保存条件下具有较高的记忆写入率与攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆投毒研究主要假设恶意性集中在单条记录中，因而容易被单条内容审查或过滤机制发现。论文关注更隐蔽的组合风险：攻击目标可被拆分为多个表面正常的记忆片段，并在长期积累和后续检索时产生联合影响。

Method: 作者提出MemCollusion红队框架，采用“切香肠式”拆分思路，将目标拆解为多个单独看似良性的记忆片段；框架通过四项设计约束、五种理论驱动策略及微调生成器构建记忆联盟。为模拟真实跨会话链路，作者构建MoltLab环境：平台内容需先被智能体观察并蒸馏写入持久记忆，随后才在另一会话中影响行为。评测基于OpenClaw、两种骨干模型和48个场景，并考察良性记忆稀释及记忆层防御下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在最强记忆保存设置下，MemCollusion的平均记忆保存率为81.3%，攻击成功率为75.0%。结果表明，生成的片段不仅较容易进入长期记忆，而且在跨会话条件下可形成有效的组合影响；在良性记忆干扰和已有记忆级防御存在时，方法仍保持一定有效性。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是揭示并系统化评估了长期记忆系统中的组合式投毒风险：仅检查单条记忆是否明显异常不足以覆盖此类威胁。其研究价值在于提供了自动化评测框架和较贴近实际的跨会话实验环境；相应防御应进一步考虑多条记忆之间的语义关联、时间累积效应和联合行为影响。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables LLM agents to retain useful information across sessions, but also creates an attack surface through which adversaries may poison an agent's persistent memory to steer its behavior. Existing memory poisoning attacks mainly rely on individually malicious records, overlooking a compositional threat: multiple benign-looking memories may jointly induce unsafe behavior. In this paper, we introduce MemCollusion, an automated red-teaming framework for constructing collusive memory poisoning attacks. MemCollusion applies salami tactics---a strategy that slices an adversarial objective into small, individually innocuous pieces---to generate memory fragments that are individually benign looking but collectively harmful. It constructs memory coalitions using four design constraints, five theory-informed strategies, and a fine-tuned generator. To assess collusive memory poisoning in a realistic cross-session setting, we develop MoltLab, a controlled research reproduction of Moltbook, in which crafted platform content must first be observed and distilled into persistent memory before influencing the agent's behavior in a separate session. We evaluate MemCollusion on OpenClaw using two backbone models across 48 scenarios. Under the strongest memory-saving setting, MemCollusion achieves an average Memory Save Rate of 81.3% and an Attack Success Rate of 75.0%, and remains effective under both benign memory dilution and memory-level defenses.

</details>


### [184] [GISAgentBench: A Practitioner-Sourced Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on GIS Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01645)
*Abhinav Pothuri,Zhe Jiang,Zelin Xu,Di Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出GISAgentBench，用于评估具备工具调用能力的大语言模型代理执行真实多步骤GIS空间分析工作流的能力。该基准包含349个任务，提供可执行参考轨迹与精确真值输出文件，并采用确定性的容差感知输出匹配进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GIS代理基准主要来自教材、教程或模型生成的任务种子，任务规模和轨迹深度有限，且通常缺少可验证的真实输出。其代码相似度、轨迹匹配或LLM/VLM裁判等替代指标，难以区分“工作流形式相似”与“任务结果正确”，因此无法可靠衡量代理的实际GIS分析能力。

Method: 作者从GIS Stack Exchange整理多步骤任务，并在六个选定地理区域的真实公开数据上实例化，构建349个GIS任务。每个任务均配有可执行的参考操作轨迹和精确的真值输出文件；评测时不依赖语言模型裁判，而是通过支持误差容限的确定性输出匹配，直接验证生成结果与真值是否一致。随后，作者使用该基准评估了六种LLM模型。

Result: 实验显示，真实GIS工作流对当前LLM代理仍具有较高难度。在严格的容差感知评分下，表现最好的代理仅完成32.7%的任务。不过，多数模型生成的结果与真值较为接近，说明其具备一定空间分析执行能力，但在保证完整工作流正确性、参数精度和最终输出一致性方面仍存在明显不足。

Conclusion: GISAgentBench通过真实数据、长程多步骤任务、可执行参考轨迹和精确输出真值，弥补了既有GIS代理评测缺少可靠结果验证的不足。研究表明，现有LLM代理尚未能稳定自动化真实GIS分析流程；未来应重点提升其工具编排、空间数据处理、参数选择、错误恢复以及对最终地理输出精确性的保障能力。

Abstract: Geographic Information System (GIS) professionals rely on multi-step spatial analysis workflows to support decision-making in urban planning, disaster response, and environmental monitoring. The process is tedious, time-consuming, and error-prone. While recent large language model (LLM) agents equipped with external tools have the potential to automate geospatial analysis, their ability to perform realistic GIS workflows remains largely unexplored. Existing GIS agent benchmarking datasets are mostly drawn from textbooks, tutorials, or LLM-generated seeds and remain limited in size and trajectory depth. More importantly, none provides ground truth outputs. They therefore rely on surrogate signals such as code similarity, trajectory matching, or LLM and VLM judges, which can conflate workflow resemblance with task correctness. To address this gap, we introduce GISAgentBench, a benchmark of 349 multi-step GIS tasks curated from GIS Stack Exchange and instantiated on real public data across six selected geographic areas of interest. Each task ships with an executable reference trajectory and an exact ground truth output file, enabling strict, deterministic, tolerance-aware output matching beyond LLM judging. Evaluations of six LLM models reveal that realistic GIS workflows remain challenging: the best agent completes only 32.7% of tasks under strict tolerance-aware scoring, although most models produce outputs that are close to the ground truth.

</details>


### [185] [LongCat Sparse Attention: Taming the Lightning via Streaming-aware Hierarchical Cross-Layer Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01662)
*Wen Zan,Jiaqi Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Hong Liu,Cunguang Wang,Xiang Li,Duyue Ma,Guanyu Wu,Yifan Lu,Fengcun Li,Yerui Sun,Peng Pei,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出LongCat Sparse Attention（LSA），通过面向硬件的数据布局、跨层索引复用与分层粗到细检索，解决DSA中索引计算昂贵及KV访存不连续的问题，在超长上下文建模中兼顾性能、精度与可训练性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA的Lightning Indexer虽然可支持稀疏注意力，但其索引评分仍具有O(L^2)计算开销；同时，所选KV位置通常分散，导致高带宽内存访问无法合并、硬件利用率较低。因此，需要从算法和系统协同角度降低索引成本并改善内存访问效率。

Method: LSA包含三项互补设计：（1）Streaming-Aware Indexing：将部分离散KV条目转换为符合硬件对齐要求的连续布局，以实现合并式HBM访问；（2）Cross-Layer Indexing：在连续层之间复用单层产生的索引结果，并通过跨层蒸馏维持检索质量，从而摊销索引开销；（3）Hierarchical Indexing：采用由粗到细的候选筛选与评分流程，逐步缩小每个查询对应的候选KV集合，减少精细评分计算。

Result: 在69B-A3B至560B-A27B的模型规模实验中，LSA在通用任务和长上下文基准上均达到与全注意力相当的性能。该方法支持最长100万token上下文的原生训练，并被用于LongCat-2.0（1.6T-A48B）的开发。作者还开源了集成LSA及更新长上下文训练语料的LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse（69B-A3B）。

Conclusion: LSA表明，稀疏注意力的实际可扩展性不仅取决于稀疏模式本身，也依赖于索引计算、跨层冗余消除和内存访问布局的联合优化。其主要贡献是将索引器从计算与访存瓶颈转化为可高效部署的系统组件，同时保持接近全注意力的模型效果。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) enables efficient long-context modeling through its Lightning Indexer. However, practical deployment remains constrained by the indexer's expensive $O(L^2)$ scoring overhead and the hardware-inefficient, discontinuous memory-access patterns induced by its outputs. To address these system-level bottlenecks, we introduce LongCat Sparse Attention (LSA), a hardware-algorithm co-designed framework comprising three complementary and orthogonal strategies: (1) Streaming-Aware Indexing, which selectively converts scattered KV entries into hardware-aligned contiguous layouts to enable coalesced HBM access; (2) Cross-Layer Indexing, which amortizes indexing overhead by reusing the results produced by a single layer across consecutive layers, supported by cross-layer distillation; and (3) Hierarchical Indexing, which adopts a coarse-to-fine scoring scheme to progressively narrow the candidate set for each query, thereby substantially reducing indexing computation. Extensive scaling experiments, ranging from 69B-A3B to 560B-A27B models, demonstrate that LSA consistently achieves performance on par with full attention across both general-purpose and long-context benchmarks. Moreover, LSA supports native training with context lengths of up to one million tokens and underpins the development of LongCat-2.0 (1.6T-A48B). To facilitate further research, we also introduce and open-source LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse (69B-A3B), which integrates LSA into LongCat-Flash-Lite and incorporates an updated long-context training corpus.

</details>


### [186] [Allocation Before Ranking: Decoupled Token Compression for OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01665)
*Zhenghui Guo,Yilin Yang,Yuanbin Man,Miao Yin,Weidong Shi,Rabimba Karanjai,Omprakash Gnawali,Chengming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Macer，一种免训练的多模态大模型标记压缩方法。其核心是将“跨模态共享 Top-K 筛选”拆分为“先分配模态预算，再在模态内排序”，以避免音频标记在共享竞争中系统性挤占视频标记容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标记压缩通常使用单一显著性分数对所有多模态标记进行共享 Top-K 保留。但该分数同时承担模态间容量分配与模态内标记选择两项功能，导致两种决策相互耦合。作者指出，这一机制存在偏向音频的分配先验：音频标记可能在视频标记参与竞争前便占据大量保留预算，从而损害视觉或音视频联合任务的性能。

Method: Macer 不依赖额外训练，采用两阶段压缩流程：第一步，显式为音频与视频标记分配各自的保留预算；第二步，在每个模态内部，利用该模态对应的浅层网络表示进行经分配归一化的显著性排序，并按各自预算选取标记。该设计将跨模态资源分配与模态内重要性筛选解耦，避免共享 Top-K 的直接竞争。

Result: 在 25% 标记保留率下，Macer 在 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 上保留完整标记性能的 98.7%，在 Qwen2.5-Omni-3B 上保留 97.3%。对于 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B，Macer 仅使用 25% 保留率即可达到 OmniZip 在 45% 保留率下的性能，同时具有更低的 FLOPs。在 OmniVinci-9B 上，相较共享 Top-K 排序，先分配后排序的策略最高可提升 12.9 个百分点，并在音频驱动、音视频联合、视觉主导及视频中心等多类基准上保持较高准确率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，多模态标记压缩不应仅被建模为统一的全局显著性排序问题，而应显式处理模态间的容量分配。Macer 通过“分配先于排序”的机制，以较低计算成本实现了较强的性能保持，尤其适合需要兼顾音频与视频信息的全模态大模型推理场景。

Abstract: Token compression in OmniLLMs is typically posed as a single saliency-ranking problem: score each multimodal token, keep the top-K. We argue this abstraction is mis-specified. The same attention score simultaneously decides two things: how much retained capacity each modality receives, and which tokens within a modality are kept. A shared top-K rule therefore inherits this audio-favoring allocation prior, spending retained capacity on audio before video tokens have a chance to compete. We propose Macer, a training-free compressor that first assigns explicit audio and video budgets, then performs allocation-normalized ranking within each modality at modality-specific shallow layers. Macer significantly reduces token cost while preserving accuracy across audio-grounded, audio--video joint, visual-dominant, and video-centric benchmarks. At 25 % retention, Macer preserves 98.7 % of full-token performance on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and 97.3 % on Qwen2.5-Omni-3B. On Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, this 25 % setting reaches OmniZip-level performance at 45 % retention while using lower FLOPs. On OmniVinci-9B, the same allocation-before-ranking principle improves over shared top-K ranking by up to 12.9 points.

</details>


### [187] [TCPO: Turn-Level Credit Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01667)
*Sicong Liao,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出TCPO，一种面向验证器引导多轮强化学习的回合级信用分配方法。其核心是将每轮验证器分数转换为反映该轮对后续优化轨迹实际贡献的信用信号，从而提升多轮推理与智能体任务中的策略优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮验证器反馈虽然在每一轮提供稠密分数，但分数仅描述当前输出质量，不能直接衡量当前操作是否推动了后续轨迹改善。因此，直接把分数当作奖励或优势信号会造成信用分配偏差：某些当前得分不高的探索性步骤可能为后续成功创造条件，而某些局部高分步骤也可能导致后续退化。论文旨在解决这种“稠密评分但非稠密信用”的问题。

Method: TCPO将信用分配建模为“分数到信用”的转换，并构造回合级优势估计。具体包括三类机制：（1）回溯信用，通过与此前最佳状态比较，度量当前轮带来的即时进展或退化；（2）事后延迟信用，识别当前未改善分数、但在后续产生收益的步骤，以补偿延迟贡献；（3）选择性固定历史反事实估计，在相同历史条件下重点重估高惊异度回合，从而更准确判断该轮行为的因果贡献。该方法据此生成更适合多轮策略优化的训练信号。

Result: 实验覆盖数学推理、代码生成和AppWorld智能体任务，并使用不同规模模型及不同类型验证器。结果显示，TCPO在多个设置中优于或达到最强基线水平；在Qwen3-4B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B上，其best-turn Pass@8达到最佳或并列最佳。同时，TCPO能够减少达到成功结果所需的交互轮数，并提高多轮智能体任务表现。

Conclusion: 论文表明，多轮验证器引导强化学习的关键不只是获得更频繁的评分反馈，更在于将评分正确转化为反映轨迹贡献的信用信号。TCPO通过即时比较、事后归因和反事实估计缓解了多轮信用分配问题，因而具备跨模型规模、任务领域和验证器类型的适用性。

Abstract: Verifier-guided reinforcement learning has become a powerful paradigm for improving LLM reasoning. In multi-turn settings, models receive a verifier score after each turn and iteratively refine their outputs. Although such scores provide dense feedback, they do not directly provide dense credit: a score measures the quality of the current output, while credit should measure how the current turn changes the refinement trajectory. We propose TCPO, a turn-level credit assignment method for verifier-guided multi-turn RL. TCPO casts credit assignment as score-to-credit conversion and constructs turn-level advantages through reference-based comparisons: retrospective credit captures immediate progress and regression relative to the best prior state; hindsight delayed credit identifies non-improving turns with later payoff; and selective fixed-history counterfactual estimation refines high-surprisal turns under the same history. Experiments on math reasoning, code generation, and AppWorld agent tasks show that TCPO improves or matches the strongest baselines across model scales, task domains, and verifier types. TCPO achieves the best or tied-best best-turn Pass@8 on Qwen3-4B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, reduces turns to success, and improves multi-turn agent performance. These results highlight score-to-credit conversion as a central ingredient for verifier-guided multi-turn policy optimization.

</details>


### [188] [When Memory Becomes Authority: Benchmarking Authority Collapse at the Memory Consolidation Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01679)
*Qiuyang Zhan,Rui Zhang,Sheng Guo,Lepeng Zhao,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出LLM智能体的持久记忆在整合交互历史时会发生“权限坍缩”：保留了信息声明，却丢失其来源及可授权使用范围，导致记忆在后续任务中被赋予超过原始来源许可的权威性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆研究主要关注信息是否被正确提取、压缩和复用，较少处理“该信息以何种权限被复用”的问题。例如，同一声明若来自用户偏好、系统验证观察或临时指令，其可驱动的后续行为范围应不同。缺失来源权限约束会使记忆成为越权行动的隐患。

Method: 作者提出AuthMem-Bench，一个受控的配对基准：固定核心声明和下游任务，仅改变信息来源的权限属性，从而隔离并测量权限因素。基准评估三类能力：写入记忆时是否发生权限坍缩、下游使用记忆时是否产生授权错误、以及系统能否自动预测并持久化保存权限标签。实验覆盖7种常用智能体记忆整合器和7种LLM骨干模型，并包含以行动结果为基础的受控评测及端到端评测。

Result: 在49种被评估的系统配置中，48种出现权限坍缩。对于不含权限元数据的坍缩记忆，受控行动评测中的平均未授权行动率为50.3%。在端到端实验中，自动预测并保存权限标签后，未授权行动率从16.9%降至0.0%，同时正常任务成功率基本不变。

Conclusion: 持久记忆系统的安全与可靠性不能仅保证“记住了什么”，还必须保存“信息在何种授权条件下可被使用”。论文表明，为记忆条目附加并持久化来源权限标签，是显著降低下游越权行为且几乎不损害正常任务性能的有效机制。

Abstract: Persistent memory allows (self-evolving) LLM agents to adapt across tasks by consolidating heterogeneous interaction histories into reusable facts, preferences, observations, and rules. Yet consolidation also imposes an implicit authorization boundary: it determines whether stored information may later be consumed as a user fact, an attested observation, or a standing instruction. We identify authority collapse, in which consolidation preserves a claim while erasing the source constraints governing its authorized use, causing the stored memory to imply greater authority than its source permits. We introduce AuthMem-Bench, a controlled paired benchmark that holds the focal claim and downstream task fixed while varying only source authority. It evaluates write-time collapse, downstream authorization errors, and automatic authority preservation. Across seven consolidators based on widely used agent-memory systems and seven LLM backbones, we observe authority collapse in 48 of 49 evaluated configurations. In a controlled action-grounded evaluation, collapsed memories without authority metadata yield a mean unauthorized-action rate of 50.3%. In an end-to-end evaluation, automatically predicted and persisted authority labels reduce the observed unauthorized-action rate from 16.9% to 0.0%, while benign task success remains essentially unchanged. These findings show that memory-driven adaptation must preserve not only what was
  learned, but also the authority under which it may be reused.

</details>


### [189] [GABench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on Graph Analysis Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01684)
*Jiarui Tan,Zhongjian Zhang,YaBo Guo,Jiawei Liu,Yujie Xing,Muhan Zhang,Cheng Yang,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出GABench，一个面向智能体式图分析的综合评测基准，通过可执行图工具与可验证任务，评估大语言模型智能体在真实图环境中的端到端分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图学习或图推理基准覆盖的图类型和任务范围有限，且常将图信息直接嵌入文本提示，主要评估问答能力，无法充分检验智能体的数据访问、工具调用、状态管理与多步执行能力。因此，需要一个覆盖更广、支持环境交互且具备可靠标准答案的基准。

Method: GABench覆盖三类图数据，以及四类任务：图检索、图论、图机器学习和开放式图问答。基准提供84个可执行工具，用于访问图数据和执行多样化图操作；在此基础上，作者构建智能体式图分析任务生成流程，生成10,400个具有可验证真值的任务。随后，使用该基准评估多种前沿大语言模型及其智能体执行框架。

Result: 实验表明：第一，现有大语言模型智能体在复杂图分析任务上仍存在明显困难；第二，执行框架的选择会显著影响最终性能，但已有框架对复杂图任务的支持仍不充分；第三，图分析性能更依赖工具调用的质量，而非单纯增加工具调用次数。

Conclusion: GABench将图分析从静态文本问答扩展为包含数据访问、工具使用和多步推理的端到端智能体评测，为比较不同模型与执行框架提供了统一基础。论文的核心启示是，提升图分析智能体应重点优化工具选择、调用参数、调用顺序及执行框架的任务协调能力，而不是仅扩大调用规模。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of planning, using tools, and interacting with external environments. They are typically supported by harnesses, which manage state and coordinate multi-step execution. Graph analysis provides a promising setting for evaluating their agentic capabilities, because it requires agents to access data and execute operations in a graph environment. However, existing graph benchmarks for LLMs provide limited coverage of graph tasks and graph types, making it difficult to comprehensively evaluate LLM agents. Moreover, they typically formulate graph analysis as text-based question answering, where graph information is directly provided in the prompt, limiting the evaluation of end-to-end agentic capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce GABench, a comprehensive benchmark for agentic graph analysis. GABench spans three graph types and covers four graph analysis task categories: graph retrieval, graph theory, graph machine learning, and graph open-ended question answering. GABench also provides 84 executable tools for accessing graph data and performing diverse graph operations. Building on these tools, we develop an agentic graph analysis task generation pipeline and construct 10,400 tasks with verifiable ground truth.Using GABench, we evaluate a range of frontier LLMs and agent harnesses. Our experiments reveal three key findings: (1) Existing LLM agents still struggle with complex graph analysis tasks. (2) Harness choice significantly affects performance, yet existing harnesses remain limited on complex graph tasks. (3) Graph analysis depends more on tool-call quality than quantity. Our findings provide practical insights into the development and evaluation of LLM agents for graph analysis.

</details>


### [190] [Beyond Single-Use Tokens: Durable Authorization State for Replay-Resistant LLM Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01710)
*Jinghan Xu,Longze Fan,Zeyuan Wang,Xinjin Li,Hankai Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，智能体在重试、重规划、委派和故障恢复时，可能针对同一授权事项获取新的令牌并重复执行，形成“语义重放”。仅依赖单次令牌标识无法阻止该问题；需要围绕授权事项维护持久、单调的状态与执行预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次使用令牌通常只防止同一令牌标识被重复消费，但无法识别同一用户确认、同一规范化动作在新令牌下被再次授权和执行。对于具备自主重试与恢复能力的工具调用智能体，这会导致超出原始授权范围的重复操作。

Method: 作者提出CapLease授权消费层：将经认证的用户确认绑定到规范化动作，并以事务化的Issue-Prepare-Commit状态转换管理授权签发、准备和提交过程。该方案采用提案级与授权级防护，并为动作、确认事件和剩余执行预算保存持久化、单调演进的状态。实验比较了标识符局部令牌、CapLease及同样有状态的Server Ledger。

Result: 在重规划、失败重试、任务委派、并发执行、确认重放和崩溃恢复等场景中，仅进行令牌标识符局部消费的方案允许重新签发新令牌，从而产生语义重放。CapLease和有状态Server Ledger能够阻止重复准入；当外部执行端具备幂等性时，还可避免重复的外部副作用。

Conclusion: 实现面向智能体的抗重放授权，关键不在于令牌采用何种表示或是否标记为单次使用，而在于是否为授权实例维护跨令牌、可持久化的动作语义、确认关联和剩余执行预算状态。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model agents frequently replan, retry failed operations, delegate tasks, and resume after crashes. These behaviors can cause one user authorization to be requested and executed multiple times under freshly issued token identifiers, even when each individual token is single-use. We call this failure semantic replay: exceeding the execution budget of a token-independent authorization instance rather than merely reusing an old token identifier. We show that identifier-local token consumption cannot prevent fresh reissuance unless the issuer retains monotonic durable state over the authorized action, confirmation event, and remaining execution budget. We introduce CapLease, an authorization-consumption layer that follows proposal- and authority-level defenses, binds an authenticated user confirmation to a canonical action, and enforces transactional Issue-Prepare-Commit transitions. Across LLM-agent replanning, retry, delegation, concurrency, confirmation-replay, and crash-recovery scenarios, identifier-local tokens permit fresh semantic reissuance, whereas CapLease and an equally stateful Server Ledger prevent duplicate admission and, with an idempotent sink, duplicate external effects. Our results identify durable authorization state, rather than token representation alone, as the systems requirement for replay-resistant agent execution.

</details>


### [191] [Constructing Executable Analytical Knowledge Representations for Meta-Analysis Synthesis Using an Agentic Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01711)
*Lingbo Li,Anuradha Mathrani,Teo Susnjak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“可执行分析知识表示”（EAKR）及其实现系统MetaSynDec，用于将结构化证据转化为可验证、可追溯、可执行的Meta分析方案。实验显示，该方法在分析结构一致性、证据集匹配和统计结果复现方面明显优于直接由大语言模型生成分析方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为，Meta分析的难点不仅是提取结构化证据，更在于显式表达证据分配、比较组定义、结局与时间点对齐、效应量计算及方法学纳入条件等分析决策。现有自动化方法往往将这些决策隐含在模型输出、代码或工作流记录中，导致其难以被独立审查、验证和复用。因此，需要一种能够将分析知识本身结构化、机器可执行且可溯源地表示出来的框架。

Method: EAKR将证据、实体关系、数值输入、约束条件、来源信息及未解决问题统一表示为机器可操作对象。作者基于EAKR构建MetaSynDec：大语言模型负责提出结构化更新建议，而确定性服务负责依据模式与契约进行验证，并执行后续统计分析。系统在58个证据综合单元上进行评估，并以参考分析对象、证据集合、分析公式及置信区间结果为主要比较标准，同时与直接使用大语言模型生成分析结构的方法进行对照。

Result: MetaSynDec为全部58个综合单元构建了EAKR，其中57个进入统计执行阶段。在56个可定义参考分析对象的单元中，38个达到完整对象保真度，比例为67.9%；42个实现证据集完全一致，比例为75.0%；平均Jaccard相似度为0.909。在55个可比较置信区间的单元中，生成结果与已发表结果的置信区间在54个单元中重叠，占98.2%。相较直接大语言模型生成，MetaSynDec在参考综合结构一致性上显著更高，为57/58对23/58，p<0.001；在23个双方均完成的单元中，其参考公式完全一致率为23/23，而直接生成仅为1/23，p<0.001。

Conclusion: 研究表明，EAKR能够将Meta分析中的关键方法学决策从隐式模型行为中分离出来，形成可验证、可追踪和可执行的知识层。MetaSynDec的结果证明，这种“语言模型提议、确定性规则验证与执行”的架构具有可行性，并能显著提升与参考分析方案的方法学一致性。其局限在于完整对象保真度尚未达到全覆盖，说明复杂或信息不充分的证据综合任务仍需进一步改进知识表示、约束设计及人工审查机制。

Abstract: Meta-analysis synthesis highlights a fundamental challenge in knowledge-based scientific analysis: structured evidence does not by itself represent the analytical knowledge required for executable computation. Decisions about evidence assignment, analytical contrasts, outcome and time-point alignment, effect-size formulation, and methodological admissibility must be explicit before statistical execution. Existing automated approaches often embed these decisions in model outputs, generated code, or workflow traces rather than representing them as independently verifiable knowledge. We introduce the Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation (EAKR), a machine-actionable representation of the knowledge required to transform structured evidence into executable meta-analysis. An EAKR represents evidence, relations, numerical inputs, constraints, provenance, and unresolved issues. We operationalise EAKR in MetaSynDec, an agentic harness in which large language models propose structured updates and deterministic services govern schema- and contract-based validation and execution. Across 58 synthesis units, MetaSynDec constructed all EAKRs, with 57 proceeding to statistical execution. Of 56 units with sufficient information to define a reference analysis object, 38 (67.9%) achieved complete object fidelity and 42 (75.0%) exact evidence-set agreement, with a mean Jaccard similarity of 0.909. Generated and published confidence intervals overlapped in 54 of 55 units (98.2%). MetaSynDec outperformed direct LLM generation in reference synthesis-structure agreement (57/58 versus 23/58; p<0.001) and among 23 jointly completed units, exact reference-formulation agreement (23/23 versus 1/23; p<0.001). These findings provide feasibility evidence that EAKR supports formal validation, traceability, statistical execution, and improved methodological agreement relative to direct LLM generation.

</details>


### [192] [LaCache: Robust Semantic Caching for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01718)
*Jiacheng Liang,Yuhui Wang,Tanqiu Jiang,Ting Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaCache提出一种更安全的语义缓存机制：除匹配查询嵌入外，还验证模型推测生成的前k个词元是否与缓存响应一致，以降低缓存碰撞导致的错误复用，并提升检索相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义缓存依据查询语义相似度复用历史响应，能够降低大语言模型服务延迟与成本，但相似查询可能映射到语义或意图不同的响应，造成缓存污染和后续请求获得不正确结果。论文旨在解决仅依赖查询侧匹配所带来的鲁棒性不足。

Method: LaCache利用“查询可被任意构造、但响应需同时满足多项语义约束”的不对称性，在常规查询缓存命中检测之外，对模型推测解码得到的前k个词元进行额外缓存命中验证。只有查询及其早期生成内容均与缓存条目一致时，系统才复用缓存响应。该方案同时给出关于碰撞鲁棒性的形式化论证，并将扩展后的索引用作更丰富的语义检索上下文。

Result: 摘要声称，形式化分析表明，攻击者无法同时构造与正常查询发生缓存碰撞、且产生恶意响应的输入。跨多种大语言模型和基准测试的实验进一步显示，该方法能够增强缓存安全性，并通过更充分的上下文信息改善响应相关性和效率。摘要未提供具体的命中率、延迟、成本或准确率数值。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将语义缓存验证从单一查询嵌入匹配扩展为“查询加生成前缀”的联合匹配。该设计在保留语义缓存性能收益的同时，提高了错误响应复用的防护能力；其实际适用性仍取决于推测解码额外开销、k值选择，以及形式化假设在真实模型与复杂业务场景中的成立程度。

Abstract: Semantic caching, which reuses responses to semantically similar requests via their embeddings, has seen growing adoption in LLM serving, offering faster responses and reduced costs. Yet existing schemes are fundamentally vulnerable to cache-collision attacks, wherein an adversary pollutes the cache by injecting crafted queries, corrupting responses to subsequent legitimate requests. We present LaCache, a novel semantic caching scheme that addresses this vulnerability through a conceptually simple yet principled redesign. The key insight is that while the adversary has full control over the adversarial query, it has far less control over its response, which must simultaneously satisfy multiple semantic constraints. Rather than checking only the cache hit of a query, LaCache additionally checks the cache hit of its first k (speculatively) decoded tokens. This design yields two concrete benefits. First, it provides formally guaranteed resilience against cache-collision attacks: we prove that it is impossible to craft adversarial queries that simultaneously elicit malicious responses and collide with benign queries. Second, the enriched index supplies additional semantic context for cache retrieval, improving response relevance. Empirical evaluation across diverse LLMs and benchmarks validates both LaCache's security guarantees and efficiency gains, pointing to a promising direction for robust semantic caching.

</details>


### [193] [DAPD: Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01735)
*Jianyu Wu,Yizhou Wang,Encheng Su,Chen Tang,Shixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出双锚定策略蒸馏（DAPD），用于缓解在线策略自蒸馏中因教师拥有特权信息、学生推理时缺失该信息而产生的“特权幻觉”。方法在多项任务和不同模型规模上优于传统OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD训练时，教师可利用学生在真实推理阶段无法获得的特权信息。若学生模仿依赖这些信息的行为，就会在推理时错误地表现为这些信息仍然存在，导致性能下降。论文将这一问题归因于教师与学生在推理可用信息上的不对称。

Method: DAPD包含两层锚定机制。其一，双路径锚定（DPA）构建自条件桥接，并在两条信息匹配的路径上对齐参考策略与滚动生成策略，从而避免将依赖特权信息的行为迁移给推理阶段学生。其二，双源锚定（DSA）同时在“参考到滚动”和“滚动到参考”两个方向应用这些路径，在保留正确性监督的同时降低对特权参考指导的依赖。

Result: 实验表明，DAPD能够显著减轻特权幻觉。在Qwen3-4B上，相比OPSD，其跨任务平均提升2.00分；增益具有规模稳定性，在4B模型上达到2.69分，在32B模型上达到2.78分。

Conclusion: 通过显式约束教师、参考和学生滚动过程之间的信息一致性，DAPD解决了OPSD中的特权信息不对称问题。结果表明，双路径与双源锚定可提升蒸馏后模型在真实推理条件下的鲁棒性和总体性能。

Abstract: On-policy (self) distillation (OPSD) is increasingly adopted for language-model post-training. It strengthens the teacher with privileged information but can induce a privilege illusion: the student learns privilege-dependent behavior it cannot reproduce from its inference-time context, yet behaves as if the training-time privileged information remained available, ultimately degrading performance. In this paper, we identify information asymmetry between the privileged teacher and the student at inference as the root cause of this failure in OPSD. To resolve this asymmetry, we propose Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation (DAPD), a unified framework with two levels of anchoring. Dual-Path Anchoring (DPA) introduces a self-conditioned bridge and aligns reference and rollout behavior along two matched-information paths, preventing privilege-dependent behavior from being transferred to the inference-time student. Dual-Source Anchoring (DSA) applies these paths in both reference-to-rollout and rollout-to-reference directions, reducing reliance on privileged reference guidance while preserving correctness supervision. Extensive experiments show that DAPD significantly alleviates privilege illusion, outperforming OPSD on Qwen3-4B by +2.00 points on average across tasks. Notably, its gains persist across scales, reaching +2.69 at 4B and +2.78 at 32B.

</details>


### [194] [CoEvo-Mem: Co-Evolving Retrieval Policy and Memory Bank for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01739)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Zhijian Li,Xiang Yin,Junkai Ma,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoEvo-Mem提出检索策略与记忆库协同演化的闭环框架，通过交替优化路由器和记忆结构，提升长期LLM智能体在多任务、多会话场景中的记忆利用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将查询检索优化与记忆更新分开处理，忽略二者的反馈耦合：检索结果决定哪些记忆获得使用信号，而记忆库更新又会改变未来检索排序与选择。该割裂设计限制了长期记忆系统的持续适应能力。

Method: 对于每个查询，冻结的大语言模型生成面向不同检索路径的查询改写及路由先验；轻量级残差路由器据此进行在线校正。检索到的上下文作为连接检索学习与记忆演化的接口：任务结果为路由决策分配信用，结合交互轨迹的反馈更新记忆价值和图关系。为缓解两类更新相互影响导致的非平稳性，方法采用交替训练：固定记忆库更新路由器，再固定检索策略演化记忆库。

Result: 该方法在七个多样化基准上取得最优性能，表明将检索决策和记忆结构置于统一反馈闭环中，能够有效提高长期LLM智能体的任务表现。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将记忆检索与记忆维护从彼此独立的模块转变为可相互塑造的协同学习过程。其结果支持这样一种设计原则：长期智能体的记忆系统不仅应优化即时检索准确性，还应利用任务反馈持续调整记忆价值、关联结构和后续访问策略。

Abstract: As memories accumulate across tasks and sessions, the performance of long-term LLM agents depends jointly on query-specific retrieval and continual memory refinement. However, existing methods typically optimize either memory access, through iterative query refinement or adaptive retrieval policies, or memory evolution such as structural update. This separation overlooks a fundamental feedback loop: retrieval determines which memories receive usage signals, while updated memory bank reshape future retrieval. We propose \textbf{CoEvo-Mem}, a closed-loop framework for co-evolving the retrieval policy and memory bank. For each query, a frozen LLM generates route-specific query rewrites and a routing prior, which a lightweight residual router corrects online. The retrieved context serves as the coupling interface between the two learning processes: task outcomes assign credit to routing decisions, while trajectory-conditioned feedback updates memory values and graph relations.
