These updates alter how memories are ranked and selected for subsequent queries, thereby closing the feedback loop. To mitigate coupling induced non-stationarity, CoEvo-Mem alternates between updating the router with the memory bank fixed and evolving the memory bank with the retrieval policy fixed. Across seven diverse benchmarks, \textbf{CoEvo-Mem} achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the importance of retrieval-memory coevolution.

</details>


### [195] [MemSIF: From Structured Interactions to Dual-Track Fact Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01742)
*YuFei Luo,Xiucheng Xu,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MemSIF长时记忆框架，通过结构化交互记忆与双轨事实记忆，缓解LLM智能体在长期交互中的时间—结构错位和延迟效用显现问题，并在LoCoMo与LongMemEval-S上取得最佳准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统通常按时间邻近性组织信息，并在写入时依据即时显著性决定保留内容。但时间相近的对话不一定主题或事件相关，当前看似不重要的信息也可能在未来查询中变得关键。论文将这两类问题分别定义为Temporal-Structural Misalignment（TSM）和Delayed Utility Manifestation（DUM）。

Method: MemSIF包含两部分：其一，Structured Interaction Memory将原始交互组织为保持局部主题一致性的Topical Segments，以及跨时间维持事件连续性的Event Trajectories；其二，Dual-Track Fact Memory包含CoreFact与ActiveFact。CoreFact在写入阶段按预定义模式沉淀稳定信息；ActiveFact则按需生成事实，并根据多历史来源支持及重复查询需求，将高价值事实提升为可复用记忆。

Result: 作者在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上，使用五种骨干LLM进行评测。MemSIF在所有设置中均获得最高Total ACC：相较最强基线，在LoCoMo上提升2.29%至8.79%，在LongMemEval-S上提升2.87%至6.15%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，将交互上下文按主题和事件结构化，并将稳定事实写时固化、潜在高价值事实按需形成和动态晋升，能够有效提升长期记忆检索与问答表现。该工作贡献在于明确刻画两种长期记忆失配机制，并给出对应的结构化与双轨式解决方案。

Abstract: Long-term memory is critical for LLM agents operating over long-horizon interactions. However, several persistent limitations of existing memory systems can be traced to two recurring misalignment patterns in long-term interaction settings: Temporal-Structural Misalignment (TSM) and Delayed Utility Manifestation (DUM). TSM arises when temporal proximity does not reliably align with topical or event-level relatedness, whereas DUM arises when write-time salience does not reliably predict future query utility. To mitigate these misalignment patterns, we propose MemSIF (Memory with Structured Interactions and Facts), a structured interaction-to-fact memory framework. Structured Interaction Memory organizes raw interactions into Topical Segments that preserve local topical coherence and Event Trajectories that maintain cross-time event continuity. Dual-Track Fact Memory uses two complementary tracks: CoreFact memory consolidates stable, schema-guided information at write time, whereas ActiveFact memory forms facts on demand and promotes those supported by multiple historical sources and recurring query demand for reuse. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S across five backbone LLMs show that MemSIF achieves the highest Total ACC in all settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.29%-8.79% on LoCoMo and 2.87%-6.15% on LongMemEval-S. These results support the effectiveness of combining Structured Interaction Memory with Dual-Track Fact Memory to mitigate TSM and DUM. Code is available at https://github.com/luoyufeihaha/MemSIF.

</details>


### [196] [RL-Lock: Reinforcement Learning for Generating Interlocking Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01744)
*Xuyang Ma,Chaewoon Kim,Haonan Zhang,Rulin Chen,Ziqi Wang,Peng Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RL-Lock：一个用于生成三维互锁装配结构的强化学习框架。该方法将体素化目标形状分解为指定数量的互锁部件，并通过结构化动作分块与蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）引导的策略—价值学习，在大规模组合搜索空间中高效寻找可行解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统互锁装配生成通常依赖人工设计的搜索启发式规则，面对复杂形状、较多部件或约束较强的实例时，搜索时间可能过长，甚至无法找到有效分解方案。作者指出，逐步为每个体素决定其所属部件本质上是序贯决策过程，因此适合用强化学习替代手工启发式搜索。

Method: RL-Lock将目标三维体素网格的分解建模为多步决策任务：智能体在每一步决定体素应分配给哪个部件。为降低逐体素决策导致的动作序列过长和分支空间过大的问题，方法采用结构化动作分块（structured action chunking），以更具结构性的方式批量执行相关分配决策；同时结合MCTS引导的策略—价值学习，通过树搜索评估候选决策，并用搜索结果训练策略网络和价值网络。该框架的目标是在满足互锁约束的前提下完成指定数量部件的形状分解。

Result: 实验表明，RL-Lock能够有效生成互锁装配结构。相较于已有方法，其优势尤其体现在高难度案例：当传统方法需要过长计算时间或无法获得有效解时，RL-Lock仍能找到有效的互锁分解结果。摘要未提供具体数据集、成功率、运行时间或消融实验指标，因此无法进一步量化其性能增益。

Conclusion: 该工作将互锁装配生成从依赖手工启发式的组合搜索问题，转化为由强化学习与MCTS协同处理的序贯决策问题。其核心贡献是提出首个面向该任务的强化学习框架，并利用动作分块和搜索引导学习改善复杂实例上的求解能力。潜在局限在于摘要未说明训练成本、泛化能力、互锁约束的精确定义，以及相对于基线方法的定量优势。

Abstract: An interlocking assembly is an assembly in which component parts are connected purely through their geometric arrangement, without relying on external connectors such as glue and nails. Such assemblies have been widely used in a variety of real-world applications due to their structural stability. The problem of generating interlocking assemblies is generally formulated as a shape decomposition problem, where a target 3D object represented as a voxel grid is partitioned into a prescribed number of interlocking pieces. We observe that generating interlocking assemblies is inherently a sequential decision-making problem, where an agent repeatedly decides which piece each voxel should be assigned to. Inspired by the observation, we propose the first reinforcement learning framework RL-Lock for generating interlocking assemblies, without relying on handcrafted search heuristics as existing works did. RL-Lock combines structured action chunking with MCTS-guided policy-value learning to efficiently navigate the large combinatorial search space for interlocking assembly generation. We demonstrate through experiments that RL-Lock allows effective generation of interlocking assemblies, especially for challenging cases in which existing approaches take too long or even fail to find a valid solution.

</details>


### [197] [Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for Verifiable Reasoning in Autonomous Driving VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01755)
*Zixuan Huang,Yang Zhou,Kaixuan Wang,Guli Zhang,Hongyan Xie,Yakun Zhu,Hao Geng,Yikun Ban,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AD-MCQ与DEFT-RLVR框架，旨在避免自动驾驶视觉语言模型因预先看到真实未来轨迹而产生“轨迹锚定偏差”，从而提升可验证的驾驶推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶VLA模型常使用包含真实未来轨迹的监督数据生成思维链。教师模型在已知结果后容易对结果进行事后合理化，而非依据场景证据进行因果决策，导致推理忠实性下降及幻觉增多。直接移除真实轨迹虽可消除该捷径，但会将高层决策与高精度轨迹几何和车辆动力学生成耦合，增加学习难度。

Method: 作者将规划任务重构为自动驾驶多选题AD-MCQ：模型不开放式生成轨迹，而是在显式给出的候选轨迹中选择合理方案，使轨迹级决策可被直接验证。进一步提出DEFT-RLVR训练机制，将未来轨迹从决策前可见的锚点改为决策后的验证目标；模型先根据场景完成推理与选择，再利用未来轨迹评估其决策正确性。候选轨迹构造还允许控制任务难度并扩展评测场景。

Result: 实验表明，DEFT-RLVR能够改善自动驾驶场景中的推理表现，并保持甚至增强模型的一般视觉能力。相较于暴露真实未来轨迹的思维链标注方式，该方法预期可降低结果导向的事后解释和因果困难场景中的幻觉问题。

Conclusion: 该工作指出真实未来轨迹在推理生成阶段的提前暴露会造成关键监督偏差，并通过“决策前隐藏、决策后验证”的训练范式加以缓解。AD-MCQ结合仅依赖VLM的推理方式、可验证的候选选择机制和可控难度设计，为可扩展的自动驾驶推理训练与评测提供了基础。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving (AD) increasingly utilize chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision to enhance the reasoning capabilities of their Vision-Language Model (VLM) components, yet existing annotation pipelines commonly expose the teacher model to the logged ground-truth (GT) future trajectory. We empirically show that this induces trajectory anchoring bias: teacher models rationalize the revealed outcome rather than infer a decision from scene evidence, producing less causally faithful CoTs and substantially more severe hallucinations, especially in causally challenging scenes. Removing the GT trajectory eliminates this shortcut, but open-ended trajectory generation entangles high-level decision-making with precise geometric synthesis and low-level dynamics. To make trajectory-level driving decisions verifiable without requiring open-ended trajectory synthesis, we introduce Autonomous-Driving Multiple-Choice Question (AD-MCQ), which casts planning as selection among explicit trajectory candidates. Taking this a step further, we propose Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for RLVR (DEFT-RLVR) to transform future trajectories from pre-decision anchors into post-decision verification targets. Experimental results show that DEFT-RLVR improves AD reasoning while preserving or even enhancing general visual capabilities. With VLM-only inference and controllable difficulty through candidate construction, AD-MCQ provides a flexible, scalable, and extensible foundation for future research on verifiable AD reasoning.

</details>


### [198] [Leveraging AI for fine-grained food safety risk forecasting in sparse data conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01767)
*Dongqi Wang,Weiwei Chen,Han Zhou,Weihua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基于Transformer的城市级食品安全风险预测框架，融合千万级抽检记录与统计年鉴多源指标，通过Wilson区间监督和半监督标签优化缓解区域样本稀疏问题，并在离线评估及实际监管试验中验证了更高的风险识别与资源配置效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食品安全抽检资源有限，部分城市或区域历史样本不足，传统依赖局部抽检频率或人工经验的方案难以稳定识别细粒度风险。研究旨在利用跨区域、大规模历史数据与辅助社会经济环境变量，实现更早、更精准的城市级风险预警。

Method: 方法以Transformer为核心建模多源特征和历史抽检信息。首先整合超过1100万条抽检记录，以及统计年鉴中的人口、经济和环境指标；其次采用三阶段预训练：利用Wilson区间构建同时反映风险排序与统计置信度的部分监督信号，再通过半监督标签细化提高低样本地区标签质量；最后输出城市级食品安全风险分数，用于指导抽检资源分配。

Result: 在2022年数据上的实验表明，该方法显著优于比较基线。与浙江省市场监管部门合作开展的现场试验进一步显示，相比人工制定的抽检计划，该框架可获得更高的问题检出率，并提升检查资源配置效率。研究还发现，监管人员在决策中常使用阈值式启发规则，这可能限制风险分数的充分利用。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，大规模公共抽检数据、Wilson区间置信度建模与深度学习的结合，能够提升城市级食品安全风险预测的精细度和前瞻性。其实际应用价值不仅在于提高检出效率，也在于推动监管从事后响应转向数据驱动的主动预警；后续应加强风险分数解释、决策支持界面和使用者培训，以促进模型结果进入实际工作流程。

Abstract: Ensuring food safety represents a critical public health challenge, particularly when inspection resources are limited and regional sampling data are sparse. This study proposes a Transformer-based framework capable of forecasting fine-grained, city-level food safety risks by unifying over 11 million inspection records with supplemental demographic, economic, and environmental indicators extracted from the Statistical Yearbook. A three-stage pretraining design leverages partial supervision from the Wilson interval (capturing both safety and risk rankings), together with semi-supervised label refinement, to effectively utilize historical records even when local sample sizes are insufficient. Experimental evaluations on data from 2022 show that the proposed approach outperforms baselines significantly. A subsequent field experiment in collaboration with the Zhejiang Provincial Administration for Market Regulation further demonstrates improved detection rates and more efficient allocation of inspection resources compared to a manually developed plan. Observations of regulatory decision-making reveal a threshold-based heuristic employed by inspectors, hinting that additional training or decision-support interfaces could further enhance the impact of AI-generated risk scores. Overall, these findings underscore that a rigorous integration of large-scale public inspection data, Wilson interval-based confidence modeling, and advanced deep learning can facilitate earlier and more granular identification of food safety threats. By reducing reliance on reactive measures alone, the proposed framework has the potential to advance proactive, data-driven oversight of the global food supply.

</details>


### [199] [FRAMES: Guarded and Dual-Objective Skill Evolution for Agents in Policy-Governed Enterprise Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01772)
*Xuhui Wang,Ruoqi Shu,Chen Dan,Tianhua Xu,Mengxi Luo,Yanming Mai,Bo Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FRAMES是一种面向企业级LLM代理的闭环优化框架，旨在在保持可审计性的前提下，以较低推理成本持续提升规则驱动工作流的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业文档审计等任务要求代理稳定执行规则、为每个结论提供依据并可追溯，但实际运行反馈通常稀疏且缺乏标注；局部规则修改也可能导致无关案例性能退化。因此，需要一种能够利用现有资产冷启动、持续改进且避免回归的方法。

Method: FRAMES先从既有资产中冷启动可部署技能，随后通过基于共识的变异生成候选改进方案；利用同时考虑准确率与成本的Pareto选择保留有效候选；并通过反回归机制约束更新，确保新修改不会损害既有能力。整个过程强调结果依据与决策链路的可审计性。

Result: 在内部生产系统中，FRAMES相较各类基线方法取得了最优的准确率—成本权衡；论文还报告称，该改进趋势在tau-bench基准上得到复现。摘要未给出具体准确率、成本数值、统计显著性或基线配置。

Conclusion: FRAMES表明，LLM代理可通过闭环、共识驱动和多目标优化机制，在不牺牲审计要求的条件下实现稳定迭代。其核心贡献在于将冷启动、成本感知选择与反回归保障结合，以适应低标注、规则频繁调整的生产环境。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly run policy-bound enterprise workflows such as document auditing, where they must apply rules consistently, ground every value, and stay auditable. Improving these agents is hard: operational feedback is sparse and unlabeled, edits to one rule can regress unrelated cases, and accuracy must improve without inflating inference cost or losing auditability. We present FRAMES, a closed-loop framework that cold-starts deployable skills from existing assets and then evolves them through consensus-based mutation, Pareto selection over accuracy and cost, and an anti-regression guarantee, all while preserving auditability. Deployed on our internal production system, FRAMES attains the best accuracy-cost trade-off among baselines, with the same gains reproduced on tau-bench.

</details>


### [200] [REFLEX: Rethinking MoE Inference as Refinement-Aware Compute Allocation in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01784)
*Xiang Xia,Cheng Yan,Yiming Zhang,Jiazheng Liu,Hongyu Zhang,Wuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出REFLEX，一种面向扩散语言模型中MoE推理的免训练专家计算分配方法。它根据不同词元在去噪过程中的细化需求动态重组专家预算，在平均减少15%专家计算分配的同时，通常保持或提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE路由通常为各词元采用固定的专家选择预算，但扩散语言模型每次去噞会同时处理所有位置，而不同位置处于不同的细化阶段、计算需求显著不同。因此，统一的专家预算会导致计算资源与实际细化需求不匹配。

Method: REFLEX不修改或重新训练默认路由器，而是在推理阶段围绕逐步演化的细化过程重分配专家计算。方法采用由粗到细的分层专家预算分配机制，将计算与块级相对细化角色对齐；同时使用Frontier-Progress Score对当前活跃块进行优先级排序，从而优先为更需要细化的部分配置专家计算。

Result: 在LLaDA-MoE和LLaDA2.0-mini两类MoE扩散语言模型及多个常用基准上，REFLEX平均减少15%的已分配专家计算量，并在大多数基准上相对默认路由保持或改善生成质量。相较于自回归模型风格的可变专家路由方法，其质量—计算权衡更稳定一致。

Conclusion: 论文表明，扩散语言模型中的MoE推理不应仅视为逐词元的固定路由问题，而应视为针对异质化去噪细化状态的计算资源分配问题。REFLEX以无需训练、无需修改原路由器的方式验证了细化感知型专家预算分配的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models increase parameter capacity by activating only a small subset of experts for each token. This conditional-computation paradigm has enabled autoregressive language models to scale model capacity without a proportional increase in per-token computation. In diffusion language models (DLMs), however, each denoising forward jointly revisits all token positions despite their sharply different refinement demands, while the default fixed token-choice routing assigns them a uniform expert budget, creating a mismatch between expert computation and refinement demand. We argue that MoE inference in DLMs should therefore be viewed as refinement-aware compute allocation across heterogeneous token refinement states. We propose REFLEX (\textbf{RE}finement-aware \textbf{FLEX}ible expert allocation), a training-free method that keeps the default router unchanged while reorganizing expert computation around the evolving refinement process. Specifically, REFLEX introduces a coarse-to-fine hierarchy for expert-budget allocation that aligns computation with block-relative refinement roles while using the Frontier-Progress Score to resolve active-block priorities. Across multiple widely used benchmarks on two representative MoE-based DLMs, LLaDA-MoE and LLaDA2.0-mini, REFLEX reduces allocated expert computation by 15\% on average while preserving or even improving generation quality on most benchmarks relative to default routing. Compared with autoregressive-style variable-expert routing methods, REFLEX also yields a more consistent quality--computation trade-off, further supporting the importance of allocating expert computation according to the heterogeneous refinement demands exposed within each denoising forward.

</details>


### [201] [Can You Trust the Confidence? ConfBench for Vision-Language Models on Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01792)
*Priyashree Roy,Sujitha Martin,Mohammad Rostami,Spencer Romo,Renhao Xue,Bob Strahan,Diego A. Socolinsky,Boyi Xie,Md Mofijul Islam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ConfBench，一个面向文档关键信息抽取（KIE）置信度校准的基准，用受控退化生成低质量文档变体，以系统评估视觉语言模型在自动处理与人工复核路由中的置信度可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档理解基准主要包含清晰、高质量样本，模型低准确率区域覆盖不足，因而无法充分测试置信度估计和校准能力。实际智能文档处理需要可靠置信度来决定哪些结果可自动通过、哪些应交由人工复核。

Method: 作者对多样化文档集应用20种受控退化流水线，构造1,346个文档变体和超过7万次实体级评估，覆盖完整准确率范围。研究比较4个闭源与3个开放权重视觉语言模型，在三种输入模态下采用口头化置信度和基于log-probability的置信度估计；同时比较首token、平均token及边际聚合策略，并引入ECARB指标，将区分能力提升映射为人工复核预算节约。

Result: OCR与图像联合输入获得更准确的置信度估计。模型能力是置信度质量的主要决定因素：在Claude系列内，能力提升与置信度质量单调对应；但跨模型家族时，参数量不能有效预测校准质量。不同模型的校准程度差异显著，从接近完美到严重过度自信。针对单个模型的事后校准可重缩放绝对置信度，使其适用于阈值路由，但不会改变依赖排序的运营指标。基于log-probability的首token聚合持续优于平均token和边际聚合。

Conclusion: ConfBench填补了文档KIE中置信度校准评测不足的问题，并表明部署可信IDP不能仅依赖模型规模或原始置信度。实践中应优先采用OCR+图像输入、能力更强且经实测校准的模型，并进行模型特定的事后校准；首token log-probability是更优的置信度估计选择。公开ConfBench和ECARB有助于后续研究将校准性能转化为可量化的人工审核成本收益。

Abstract: Intelligent document processing (IDP) with vision-language models (VLMs) hinges on confidence scores trustworthy enough to route extractions between automation and human review. Existing document benchmarks are dominated by clean, high-quality samples, leaving low accuracy regions too sparse for calibration assessment. We introduce ConfBench, the first calibration-specific benchmark for key information extraction (KIE), built by applying 20 controlled degradation pipelines to a diverse document set, yielding 1,346 variants and 70K+ entity-level evaluations spanning the full accuracy spectrum. We evaluate four proprietary and three open-weight VLMs under verbalized and log-probability confidence estimation methods across three input modalities, and find: (i) OCR+Image modality results in more accurate confidence estimates; (ii) model capability is the dominant factor: within the Claude family confidence quality scales monotonically with capability, while across families parameter count is a poor predictor; (iii) calibration quality varies widely across models, from near-perfect to severely overconfident, and per-model post-hoc correction rescales these absolute confidence values for threshold-based routing without altering ranking-based operational metrics; and (iv) log-probability with first-token aggregation consistently outperforms mean-token and margin aggregations. We also introduce ECARB, a review-budget metric translating discriminative gains into operational savings. We release ConfBench publicly to enable systematic study of confidence estimators and calibration methods for trustworthy IDP application deployment.

</details>


### [202] [CoNav-UAV: Cooperative Dual-Altitude Aerial Navigation via Stackelberg Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01802)
*Junru Song,Wenhao Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuekai Qiu,Feifei Wang,Weien Zhou,Tingsong Jiang,Ying Wen,Yang Li,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CoNav-UAV：一个由高空领导者与低空跟随者组成的双无人机视觉-语言导航框架，通过Stackelberg博弈和迭代式联合学习，实现目标搜索、语义定位与近距离避障接近的协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中目标导向视觉-语言导航方法大多直接迁移地面VLN范式，探索效率有限，常需外部辅助或特权信息。已有双机方案虽利用高度互补性，但独立训练两个智能体，缺乏相互适应机制，难以形成有效协作。

Method: CoNav-UAV将高空无人机建模为领导者，负责全局视觉-语言推理、区域探索与任务规划；低空无人机建模为跟随者，负责局部环境感知、精细运动控制和无碰撞接近。作者提出Iterative Stackelberg Learning：领导者利用基于记忆的上下文学习迭代改进高层决策，跟随者通过DAgger式专家蒸馏更新控制策略；二者交替优化，以逼近Stackelberg均衡。系统仅依赖机载视觉和语言输入。

Result: 在AerialVLN基准的三个高保真城市场景中，CoNav-UAV稳定优于单智能体及双智能体基线。在训练场景中，成功率最高提升30.8个百分点；在跨场景迁移中提升9.0个百分点，同时所需适应数据约减少至原来的三分之一。消融分析表明，领导者更新和跟随者更新均带来互补收益；不同视觉语言模型骨干均表现出稳定增益，但学习动态存在差异。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将双无人机协作显式建模为领导者—跟随者博弈，并通过交替式联合学习实现策略互适应，能够有效兼顾全局语义探索与局部精确导航。其主要贡献在于无需特权信息的协同框架、面向Stackelberg均衡的训练机制，以及在场景内和跨场景条件下的显著性能与数据效率提升。

Abstract: Target-oriented vision-and-language navigation (VLN) on aerial platforms is attracting growing attention for missions such as disaster rescue, infrastructure inspection, and security patrol. In this task, an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) needs to locate targets given only a concise description of their appearance and surroundings. This requires global exploration and grounding as well as collision-free close-range approach, two interleaved processes difficult to reconcile within a single agent. Most existing methods transfer the ground VLN paradigm to a low-altitude UAV and compensate for its inefficient exploration with external assistance. A recent attempt deploys two UAVs at complementary altitudes yet still relies on privileged information and trains its two agents independently, precluding any mutual adaptation essential for cooperation. Here we propose CoNav-UAV, which explicitly models the task as a Stackelberg game between a high-altitude leader and a low-altitude follower, with the system operating on onboard visual and linguistic inputs alone. To solve this game, we introduce Iterative Stackelberg Learning. The leader's high-level vision-language reasoning is refined via memory-based in-context learning, while the follower's precise motion control is updated via DAgger-style expert distillation. The alternation drives both agents toward a Stackelberg equilibrium. CoNav-UAV consistently outperforms single- and dual-agent baselines across three high-fidelity urban scenes from the AerialVLN benchmark. Success rate improves by up to 30.8 points on the learning scene, and 9.0 points under cross-scene transfer while using about 3x less adaptation data. Further analyses validate the complementary gains of the leader and follower updates and reveal robust gains yet distinct learning dynamics across VLM backbones.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Skillsets on the Chain: A Blockchain-based Zero-Trust Framework for Agentic AI Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00104)
*Yayu Gao,Yong Xiao,Hao Hu,Xubo Li,Zhiwei Liu,Yingyu Li,Guangming Shi,Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出TrustAgentNet：一个面向智能体网络的双层区块链零信任框架，用于解决第三方技能声明与实际能力不一致、以及多智能体协作中的可信验证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体网络通常采用“声明即信任”模式，第三方技能实现可能夸大能力、隐藏风险或在协作过程中失效。论文旨在建立可审计、可验证且资源开销可控的技能生命周期管理与动态协作信任机制。

Method: 框架包含两类链：全局“技能链”（CoS）管理技能元数据、注册、审计和验证生命周期，并通过专用智能体执行链下审计、链上保留轻量密码学共识；临时“协作链”（CoC）按任务动态建立，用于无信任多智能体协作。论文还分析安全性、任务性能和资源开销之间的三方权衡，并在硬件原型上评估账本-IPFS存储及链上链下协同设计。

Result: 实验显示，相较于无区块链的默认信任基线，TrustAgentNet的额外开销主要来自链下推理，区块链账本成本较小。在50个AI模型测试中，验证流程准确识别40个正常技能并拦截10个对抗性技能，准确率为100%。在非AI技能场景中，针对171个ClawHub技能、1478项特征，取得83.91%准确率和0.85的F1分数。对抗实验还表明系统能够针对多类恶意行为实现技能的自主恢复。

Conclusion: TrustAgentNet通过“全局技能治理+任务级协作信任”的双层架构，将技能能力验证、审计和协作可信性纳入统一的零信任流程。结果表明，该设计在原型条件下具备较强的检测效果和可接受的链上成本；但非AI领域性能低于AI测试集，说明其跨领域泛化、特征鲁棒性及大规模部署成本仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Agentic AI networking (AgentNet) systems rely heavily on third-party skillset implementations and distributed multi-agent collaboration, yet they face major claim-to-capability inconsistencies and security vulnerabilities under trust-by-declaration assumptions. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes TrustAgentNet, a dual-tier blockchain-secured zero-trust framework. Specifically, a global Chain of Skillsets (CoS) governs the lifecycle of skillset metadata with protocols empowered by specialized agents to enforce off-chain auditing while maintaining lightweight on-chain cryptographic consensus. Furthermore, transient, task-oriented Chains of Collaboration (CoC) are dynamically established to enable trustless distributed multi-agent collaboration. Theoretical analysis of the three-way trade-off among security level, task performance, and resource overhead is provided and empirically validated. Experimental results on a hardware prototype demonstrate that compared with no-blockchain trust-by-default baselines, the zero-trust overhead of TrustAgentNet is dominated by off-chain inference, while the blockchain layer incurs minor ledger costs via the ledger-IPFS storage and on/off-chain integration design. Crucially, the proposed verification pipeline achieves a flawless 100% accuracy across 50 AI models, correctly validating 40 honest skillsets and intercepting 10 adversarial ones, and generalizes to non-AI domains with an 83.91% accuracy and a 0.85 F1-score across 1478 features from 171 ClawHub skills. Adversarial experiments further show that TrustAgentNet enables autonomous skillset self-recovery against various malicious attacks.

</details>


### [204] [Deep Learning for Cyber Threat Detection and Mitigation in Healthcare-IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00118)
*Mirza Akhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究面向医疗物联网可穿戴设备的网络安全问题，构建了三类贴近真实场景的数据集，并提出轻量级TCN与Res-TCN模型，用于攻击检测、缓解及树莓派边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网设备资源受限，安全失效会影响患者安全；现有深度学习防御模型通常参数量较大，且可用数据集缺少质量评估、协议与攻击场景覆盖不足，难以满足实时边缘防护需求。

Method: 研究在Cooja和ns-3仿真环境中生成两个二分类数据集UL-ECE-MQTT-DDoS-H-IoT2025和UL-ECE-UDP-DDoS-H-IoT2025，以覆盖MQTT与UDP协议下的传输行为及DDoS特征；另构建融合生理与网络特征的多分类数据集UL-ECE-MultiAttack-H-IoT2025，用于识别选择性转发、中间人和DDoS等多类威胁。模型方面采用TCN和Res-TCN进行轻量化时序特征学习，并为DDoS检测设计基于监测频率的判别机制和动态阈值缓解策略；随后将模型量化并转换为TFLite，部署于Raspberry Pi 4。

Result: 摘要表明，所提方案实现了针对MQTT和UDP场景的DDoS实时检测与缓解，并通过TFLite量化部署在树莓派4上，具备较低延迟和较高能效。摘要未提供准确率、召回率、模型规模、推理时延、功耗或与基线方法的定量对比，因此性能增益尚无法独立评估。

Conclusion: 该研究形成了“数据集构建—轻量时序模型—检测与缓解—边缘部署”的医疗物联网安全防御链路。其主要贡献在于协议感知的数据集、融合生理与网络信息的多攻击场景，以及面向受限设备的可部署模型。后续应补充数据集质量统计、类别分布、跨环境泛化能力、误报漏报代价及与现有方法的完整量化比较。

Abstract: Cybersecurity is a fundamental requirement for protecting wearable devices used in healthcare Internet of Things (H-IoT) systems. Security failures in these resource-constrained systems directly compromise patient safety. Physiological data and network traffic are frequent targets of cyberattacks in H-IoT environments. To address these risks, deep learning-based cybersecurity mechanisms for H-IoT often involve complex architectures with large parameter counts. Existing datasets are also rarely assessed for quality, limiting their applicability. However, this research addresses these challenges by developing multiple realistic datasets and proposing lightweight deep learning models, namely the Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) and Residual TCN (Res-TCN), for H-IoT. It includes two binary classification datasets for Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS) attacks and a multiclass dataset representing Selective Forwarding (SF), Man-in-the-Middle (MITM), and DDoS attacks. The datasets UL-ECE-MQTT-DDoS-H-IoT2025 and UL-ECE-UDP-DDoS-H-IoT2025 are generated in Cooja and ns-3 to capture transmission behaviours and protocol variations. The third dataset, UL-ECE-MultiAttack-H-IoT2025, integrates physiological and network features to represent multiple cyber threats in H-IoT. Building on this, the TCN model is designed to detect and mitigate DDoS attacks over the MQTT and UDP-based datasets. It incorporates a monitoring frequency-based detection mechanism and a dynamic threshold-based mitigation strategy. To enable edge deployment, the model is quantised and converted into TensorFlow Lite (TFLite) for real-time DDoS detection on Raspberry Pi 4, achieving low latency and power-efficient operation in H-IoT. This thesis establishes a deep learning-based cybersecurity defence mechanism encompassing realistic dataset generation, lightweight model design, and edge deployment for securing H-IoT systems.

</details>


### [205] [Enforcing Access Control in Distributed Version Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00132)
*Xin Xu,Quanwei Cai,Jingqiang Lin,Shiran Pan,Liangqin Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向分布式版本控制系统（DVCS）的分布式访问控制方案Disac，利用属性基加密（ABE）和属性基签名（ABS）分别实现读、写权限控制，并以Git客户端为例说明其可集成性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式版本控制系统依赖服务器统一分发数据和执行访问控制，而DVCS中的各实体通常保存完整仓库副本，能够自主获取和分享仓库，缺乏可信中心的持续协作。因此，传统面向CVCS的访问控制机制难以直接适用于DVCS环境。

Method: Disac将访问控制逻辑分散至各个实体：数据拥有者独立管理自身数据及访问策略；使用ABE对仓库数据或相关内容进行加密，使只有属性满足策略的用户可读取；使用ABS生成可验证且与属性关联的签名，以控制和验证写入、提交等操作的权限。方案旨在不依赖中心服务器的条件下实现独立执行的权限管理。

Result: 论文声称Disac能够使每个实体保持对自身数据的完整控制，并在各实体本地独立执行访问控制。对Git客户端的分析表明，该方案可较容易地与Git集成。摘要未提供具体的性能测试、通信开销、密钥管理成本或安全实验结果。

Conclusion: Disac为DVCS提供了一种基于密码学机制的去中心化读写访问控制思路。其核心价值在于适应DVCS的副本分散与自主共享特性；但方案实际部署效果仍需结合属性撤销、密钥分发、历史副本泄露、策略更新及性能开销等问题进一步验证。

Abstract: Version control systems (VCS), including central VCS (CVCS) and distributed VCS (DVCS), are widely adopted to manage the changes to various types of data. Unlike the CVCS where all the entities obtain the data from the server and the access control is enforced with the cooperation of the server, each entity in the DVCS stores the entire repository, obtains the repository shared by any entity and is free to share its own repository. Therefore, existing access control schemes for CVCS are not suitable for DVCS. In this paper, we present a distributed access control scheme (Disac) for DVCS. Disac makes each entity have full control over its data, while the access control is enforced at each entity independently. We adopt Attribute-based Encryption (ABE) and Attribute-based Signature (ABS) to achieve the read and write permission control. The analysis of the Git client demonstrates that Disac can be integrated easily.

</details>


### [206] [Stateful Cooperative Agents Safeguarding LLMs Against Evolving Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00134)
*Siyuan Li,Zehao Liu,Haoyu Li,Xi Lin,Ning Liu,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Mohsen Guizani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出CoopGuard，一种面向持续演化的多轮对抗攻击的主动式LLM防御框架，通过多智能体协作实现扰动、误导与自适应防御，并构建EMRA评测数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM防御方法多为被动响应，难以应对攻击者在多轮交互中持续调整策略的情形。作者旨在从单次拦截转向主动干预，通过提高攻击成本、诱导无效策略并持续学习攻击模式，增强系统长期鲁棒性。

Method: 框架采用协作式多智能体架构：其一，以受控响应节奏延缓交互并增加攻击资源消耗；其二，生成策略性模糊回复以误导攻击者；其三，对交互日志进行取证分析，识别攻击模式并更新防御规则。自适应协调机制根据威胁升级程度动态调整各类防御策略。作者同时提出EMRA数据集，包含8类多轮攻击中的5,200个对抗样本。

Result: 在EMRA及多个LLM骨干模型上的实验表明，相较所评估的先进基线，CoopGuard平均将攻击成功率降低69%；其欺骗性持续交互指标DR达到最强基线的6倍以上；同时，攻击者的token消耗平均增加198.83%。

Conclusion: CoopGuard表明，多智能体协作、动态策略编排和交互日志反馈能够有效提升LLM对多轮自适应攻击的防御能力。其核心贡献包括主动防御范式、联合扰动与误导的策略设计，以及支持多轮演化攻击评测的EMRA数据集。仍需进一步验证该方法在真实开放环境中的泛化能力、对正常用户体验的影响及计算开销。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly integrated into complex applications, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks has raised significant concerns. However, existing defenses remain reactive in nature. This limitation makes it difficult for them to counter sophisticated threats, as adversaries continuously adjust their strategies across multi-turn interactions. In this paper, we present a proactive defense framework for securing LLMs against evolving multi-turn adversarial attacks that combines disruption, misdirection, and adaptation across successive interaction turns. In particular, it employs a cooperative multi-agent architecture in which specialized agents execute complementary defense strategies. These strategies include controlled response pacing to increase attack costs, strategically ambiguous outputs to mislead adversaries into ineffective strategies, and forensic analysis of interaction logs to identify attack patterns and refine defenses. These agents are coordinated by an adaptive mechanism that dynamically adjusts the defense strategy in response to escalating threats. To facilitate comprehensive evaluation, we present the EMRA dataset designed to simulate evolving strategies across multi-turn attacks, including 5,200 adversarial samples across eight attack types. Experimental results on EMRA across multiple LLM backbones show that the proposed framework reduces ASR by 69% on average relative to evaluated state-of-the-art baselines. Beyond suppressing harmful outputs, it sustains deceptive engagement, achieving an average DR more than six times that of the strongest baselines and increasing attacker-token consumption by 198.83% on average relative to evaluated baselines. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/SiyuanLi00/CoopGuard.

</details>


### [207] [Symbolic Attack Chain Generation from Atomic Red Team Techniques: An Empirical Study of Predicate Representation Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00143)
*Ramya Varunsegar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文比较九类与五类谓词表示在自动化攻击链规划中的效果，发现较低粒度表示在计划有效性和成本上基本不逊于高粒度表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于PDDL的攻击链生成依赖将技术准确映射为符号谓词，但AURORA使用九类攻击动作链接模型的粒度是否必要尚未得到验证。研究旨在评估谓词分类粒度对规划质量的实际影响。

Method: 研究采用“LLM负责将技术翻译为PDDL表示、Fast Downward负责确定性规划推理”的流程。以16种技术构成的语料为实验对象，对比完整九类AALM与基于Atomic Red Team执行证据经验归纳出的五类简化方案，并从计划有效性、规划成本和攻击链保真度进行评估。

Result: 两种粒度方案在81.3%的实验结果中产生完全相同的规划结论。计划是否可生成及其成本对谓词粒度总体不敏感，说明五类方案在多数测试情形下能够保留与九类方案近似的规划能力。

Conclusion: 高粒度谓词表示的主要价值不在于提高攻击链生成的可行性或降低成本，而在于提供更细致的计划内部结构与因果依据解释。对于以链路可达性和规划效率为主要目标的系统，可考虑采用较低粒度表示；若强调可解释性、技术关联细节或审计追踪，则高粒度模型仍具优势。

Abstract: Automated attack chain generation is critical for modern cybersecurity, yet manual construction fails to scale as adversary behaviors expand. While classical AI planning using PDDL offers a formal method to automate this process, it relies on the accurate translation of techniques into symbolic predicates. Current state-of-the-art systems like AURORA employ a nine-category Attack Action Linking Model (AALM), but the necessity of this specific granularity remains unvalidated. This work investigates the impact of predicate representation granularity on plan validity, cost, and fidelity. Utilizing a pipeline where a Large Language Model (LLM) performs translation and the Fast Downward engine performs deterministic reasoning, the study compares the full nine-category AALM against a reduced five-category scheme derived empirically from Atomic Red Team (ART) execution evidence. Results from a sixteen-technique corpus demonstrate that plan validity and cost are largely insensitive to granularity, with 81.3% identical outcomes across both schemes. The findings suggest that higher granularity primarily enhances the internal structural resolution of a plan's justification rather than the viability of the generated attack chain itself.

</details>


### [208] [Exposed by Design: A Dynamic Security Assessment of Internet-Facing MCP Servers at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00150)
*Nicolás Padilla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对互联网暴露的Model Context Protocol（MCP）服务器开展动态安全评估，结合多源被动发现与Corvus主动测试框架，在确认的生产服务器中发现了多类可报告安全漏洞及显著的认证缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP服务器快速普及并广泛暴露于互联网，但其运行时行为、安全配置和漏洞状况缺乏系统性测量，因此有必要建立面向MCP特性的动态安全评估方法。

Method: 研究从11个数据源进行被动资产发现，并使用自研Corvus框架对服务器实施主动动态测试。该框架包含34个测试模块，覆盖10类MCP特有漏洞；研究进行了4轮测量，确认生产服务器后对其中部分实例开展审计。

Result: 研究确认640个生产MCP服务器，并动态审计414个，发现68个可报告漏洞，包括SQL注入、针对云元数据服务的SSRF、提示模板注入和通过游标操纵实现的路径遍历。91.8%的受审计服务器未启用OAuth认证；确认服务器中的687个工具实例暴露了缺乏访问控制的Shell执行能力；41.6%的服务器在相邻测量轮次之间三天内消失，显示出较高的部署变动率。研究还介绍了负责任披露流程，并发布了开源Corvus框架。

Conclusion: 互联网暴露的MCP生态存在较高的认证、访问控制和输入验证风险，快速部署与缺乏安全审查可能进一步放大风险。动态、面向协议特性的持续测量对于发现和缓解MCP安全问题具有重要价值；不过，摘要未提供漏洞严重性分布、样本代表性及修复验证等更深入分析。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has seen rapid adoption since its November 2024 launch, with over 21,000 server instances detectable on the public internet. We present the first dynamic behavioral security assessment of internet-facing MCP servers, combining passive discovery across eleven data sources (crt.sh, HuggingFace, GitHub, npm, Smithery, PyPI, Censys, FOFA, Shodan, glama.ai, and pulsemcp.com) with active dynamic testing using Corvus, a purpose-built framework implementing 34 test modules covering 10 MCP-specific vulnerability classes. Across four measurement runs spanning July 2026, we confirm 640 production MCP servers and dynamically audit 414, uncovering 68 reportable vulnerabilities including SQL injection, SSRF targeting cloud metadata services, prompt template injection, and path traversal via cursor manipulation. We find that 91.8% of dynamically audited servers lack OAuth authentication, 687 tool instances across confirmed servers expose shell execution capabilities without access controls, and 41.6% of confirmed servers disappear within three days between consecutive measurement runs---indicating rapid deployment cycles without security review. We report on our responsible disclosure pipeline and release Corvus as an open-source framework for MCP security evaluation.

</details>


### [209] [From Monoliths to Swarms: A Study of Attack Surface Evolution in the Transition to Multi-Agent Web Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00202)
*Yashaswi Malla,Sandra Siby*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究基于大语言模型的网页智能体从单智能体系统向多智能体系统演进后新增的安全风险，提出面向网页多智能体系统的攻击分类体系与测试平台WebMASLab，并发现跨智能体委派可导致单智能体中不存在的“电话循环”攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体通过角色分工提升复杂任务性能，但也因代理间的委派、协作和信息传递扩大了攻击面。现有研究对这类由系统架构本身引入或放大的漏洞缺乏系统化分类与可控实验评估。

Method: 作者构建多智能体网页攻击分类法，并设计WebMASLab测试平台，以纯外部网页攻击者为威胁模型。在保持用户任务、工具接口和浏览器环境不变的条件下，对单智能体与多智能体架构进行对照；评估基线、提示词加固和推理增强三种防护条件下的三类对抗场景，其中包括利用跨代理委派形成循环任务链的Telephone Loop攻击。

Result: Telephone Loop攻击对单智能体系统无效，却能在多智能体系统中成功触发循环委派。四个受测前沿模型中，三个模型在基线条件下会被该攻击攻破，平均攻击成功率为80%；另一模型表现出较强鲁棒性，检测率为92%。其余模型在基线下的检测率为0%，提示词加固后仅有一个模型提升至33%。提示词加固的效果也不稳定：某模型的攻击成功率由100%降至8%，但对其他模型仅带来有限改善。

Conclusion: 多智能体网页系统的角色专业化不仅是性能优化机制，也会产生单智能体架构中不存在的结构性安全风险。防御措施不能仅依赖通用提示词加固，而应针对代理委派关系、跨代理状态传播、任务循环和协作协议设计专门的检测、约束与隔离机制。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based web agents are increasingly evolving from single-agent systems (SAS) to multi-agent systems (MAS). While MAS can lead to improved task performance by decomposing complex tasks across specialized sub-agents, such role decomposition introduces new structural attack surfaces that are absent in SAS. This expanded attack surface remains poorly understood and inadequately categorized.
  To address this, we propose a taxonomy to categorize attack vectors specific to web-based MAS, accounting for vulnerabilities introduced or amplified by the involvement of multiple agents. We further present a test-bed WebMASLab to analyze web agent security against a fully external, web-only adversary. To isolate the effect of architecture, we keep the user task, tool surface, and browser substrate fixed, and compare single- and multi-agent setups. We evaluate three adversarial scenarios, across three conditions (baseline, prompt-hardened, and reasoning-enabled), including a novel MAS-specific Telephone Loop attack that exploits cross-agent delegation to create cyclical task loops. The attack is inert against SAS but compromises MAS when powered by three of the four frontier models evaluated (Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-5.2, GPT-5.4), averaging 80% across them at baseline. Only the fourth model, Claude Sonnet 4.6, resists the attack with a 92% detection rate. For the rest, the detection is 0% at baseline, reaching 33% with prompt-hardening for one model. We also show that obvious defenses do not generalize; prompt-hardening collapses one model's ASR from 100% to 8% while providing only modest reduction to the others. Our findings demonstrate that the transition from single- to multi-agent web systems changes the security landscape. Role specialization may not only lead to performance optimization but also introduce new architectural risks that require further study and defenses.

</details>


### [210] [Reflection, Education, Consistency: Towards Best Ethics Practices At Security And Privacy Conferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00282)
*Florian Hantke,Rafael Mrowczynski,Til Dralle,Ben Stock*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究考察安全与隐私领域顶级会议的研究伦理政策及其实践效果。作者通过政策回顾和20位社区成员访谈发现，当前伦理机制的主要目标是提升伦理意识、避免不伦理研究发表，但其效果受限于教育不足、政策复杂且不一致、规则频繁变化等问题。论文建议开展社区级协同治理，并提出开放式伦理知识库（wiki）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全与隐私研究中的伦理问题争议较大，顶级会议近年来陆续设置强制伦理声明等机制，但社区对这些措施的接受度不一。现有会议程序缺少明确、公开的目标，也缺乏关于其实际效果的实证评估。因此，研究旨在厘清伦理程序存在的理由、预期目标、现实挑战及改进方向，为会议层面的制度设计提供依据。

Method: 研究采用定性研究方法：首先回顾安全与隐私领域四个顶级会议既往的伦理政策；随后对20名资深和初级社区成员开展深入半结构化访谈，受访者包括部分现任或前任程序委员会主席及研究伦理委员会主席。访谈围绕伦理程序的动机与目标、现有干预措施的评价，以及研究团队提出的新方案展开。

Result: 研究识别出两个受到社区普遍认可的核心目标：提高研究者伦理意识，以及阻止不符合伦理要求的工作发表。尽管社区整体正向这些目标发展，但缺乏社区内部的系统性伦理教育被认为是关键障碍，伦理教育责任常被转移给高校。受访者还指出，不同会议之间的政策复杂度和一致性不足、政策逐年变动，会增加研究者理解与遵循规则的成本。部分参与者警示，过度监管可能引发反弹，使作者将伦理要求视为形式化合规任务，例如借助大语言模型生成伦理章节，而非进行实质性伦理反思。

Conclusion: 单纯增加强制性伦理审查或论文声明要求不足以实现有效伦理治理。更可行的方向是建立跨会议、社区范围内协调一致的伦理治理与教育机制，明确政策目标、稳定规则并降低制度碎片化。作者建议成立社区级伦理指导力量，并以开放伦理wiki作为促进知识共享、政策透明和持续教育的初步实践。

Abstract: Research ethics is a controversial and emotionally charged topic in the security and privacy community, sparking discussions at conferences and on social media. In recent years, some of the leading conferences have introduced interventions such as mandatory ethics sections, with mixed reactions within the community. Program committee chairs and steering committees increasingly emphasize ethics, yet there is limited empirical validation on ethics procedures and interventions, as well as no explicitly communicated goals.
  To support a shared understanding in our community and guide informed decisions at the conference level, we examined past ethics policies at the top-four conferences and conducted in-depth semi-structured interviews with senior and junior (n=20) community members, including some (former) chairs of program and research ethics committees. In these, we explored reasons for and goals of ethics procedures and discussed existing ethics interventions as well as possible new approaches proposed by our team.
  While the community appears to be evolving toward the identified goals, i.e., raising ethical awareness and preventing the publication of unethical work, we identified the lack of ethical education within the community itself as a major obstacle to further progress on this matter. Ethical education is often passed to the universities. Interviewees also mentioned challenges with the complexity of ethics policies and the lack of consistency across different conferences, as well as policies changing year-to-year. Several participants warned that over-regulation may lead to backlash, encouraging mere compliance, such as people turning to LLMs for ethics sections rather than undertaking in-depth consideration. In response, we suggest a coordinated, community-wide ethics steering effort and take a first step by introducing an open ethics wiki.

</details>


### [211] [Trimming: Decoupling Multiplicative Depth from Modulus Chains in RNS-CKKS via Rational Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00375)
*John Chiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 Trimming：一种面向 RNS-CKKS 的细粒度层级管理机制。其通过额外的小型 NTT 友好模数链实现部分模数转换，以“有理层级”替代传统按完整模数因子丢弃的整数层级，从而尝试解除乘法深度对模数链结构的强约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Grafting 方法已将缩放因子与密文模数解耦，改善了精度管理灵活性，但乘法深度仍受传统模数链逐层下降机制限制。传统 RNS-CKKS 每次重缩放通常需要丢弃整个模数因子，导致层级调整粒度粗、深度资源利用不充分。因此，论文旨在解决 RNS-CKKS 中的“深度瓶颈”。

Method: 方法核心是在原有模数链之外引入由较小 NTT 友好模数因子构成的辅助 trimming 模数链。系统不再仅能执行丢弃完整主模数因子的离散层级转换，而可借助辅助链执行部分模数转换和模数因子细化。论文据此定义有理层级抽象，使层级消耗可以具有更细的粒度；同时设计目标是保持与既有 RNS-CKKS 算术操作兼容，并可与 Grafting 形成互补。

Result: 摘要未提供实现数据、复杂度分析或实验结果，因此尚不能量化 Trimming 对可支持乘法深度、运行时间、密钥切换开销、模数转换成本、噪声增长及安全参数的实际影响。当前结果主要是机制层面的设计主张：该方法理论上可实现更灵活的深度管理和自适应模数转换。

Conclusion: Trimming 将 RNS-CKKS 的层级管理从传统整数模数链推进到细粒度的有理层级模型，目标是将乘法深度与固定模数链进一步解耦。其贡献定位为与 Grafting 的精度解耦相对应的深度解耦组件，并朝向完全解耦的 RNS-CKKS 架构发展。论文后续价值取决于具体实现和实验验证，尤其需要证明细粒度转换带来的深度收益能够覆盖额外模数、NTT 与基转换操作的开销。

Abstract: Recent work on Grafting decouples scale factors from ciphertext moduli, enabling more flexible precision management in RNS-CKKS. However, the multiplicative depth remains fundamentally constrained by the modulus chain structure.
  In this paper, we propose \emph{Trimming}, a novel fine-grained level management mechanism that decouples multiplicative depth from modulus chains in RNS-CKKS via rational levels. The key idea is to introduce an auxiliary trimming modulus chain composed of smaller NTT-friendly modulus factors, which enables partial modulus transitions instead of directly discarding an entire modulus factor. By replacing conventional discrete level reductions with fine-grained modulus factor refinement, Trimming provides a rational-level abstraction beyond the traditional integer-based modulus chain representation.
  Our approach preserves the compatibility with existing RNS-CKKS arithmetic while enabling more flexible depth management and adaptive modulus transitions. Similar to Grafting, which addresses the precision bottleneck, Trimming targets the depth bottleneck in RNS-CKKS and contributes toward a fully decoupled RNS-CKKS architecture.
  The proposed framework will be further validated through concrete implementation and experimental evaluation to investigate its practical performance and computational overhead in real-world homomorphic encryption applications.

</details>


### [212] [TI-StegoAlign: Channel-Guided Post-Training for Generative Text Steganography under Tokenization Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00382)
*Jiuan Zhou,Yuhao Xue,Yu Cheng,Yuan Xie,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TI-StegoAlign是一种面向接收端信道的生成式文本隐写后训练框架，通过BCSO增强编码稳定性、CCPO优化接收端恢复与文本质量，在无需通信阶段分词修正的情况下，实现了100%的接收端比特准确率，并改善困惑度偏差和抗隐写分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依据发送端生成的token评估恢复率，但接收端只能看到表面文本；去分词与重新分词可能改变token边界、导致编码状态失步，从而高估实际恢复效果。已有方法主要依赖推理时过滤或逐条验证，未从生成策略层面适配接收端信道。

Method: TI-StegoAlign采用信道引导的后训练框架。首先，Bit-Consistent Supervised Objective（BCSO）在实际发送端嵌入位置扩大局部编码裕量，以提升跨分词过程的比特一致性。随后，Channel-Conditioned Preference Optimization（CCPO）利用接收端真实恢复结果、文本质量和抗隐写分析反馈，对完整隐写文本进行偏好对齐。训练仅更新LoRA参数，通信阶段无需依赖特定分词修正。

Result: 实验显示，TI-StegoAlign达到100%的接收端比特准确率。相较最强基线，归一化困惑度偏差降低21.6%，抗隐写分析性能相对提升6.3%。这些结果表明，该方法同时改善了接收端可靠性、文本质量保持和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 该工作将优化目标从发送端token恢复扩展到接收端实际信道，证明通过局部编码稳定性增强与接收端反馈驱动的偏好优化，可以有效缓解分词失步问题。其优势是参数高效且无需通信时额外校正，但摘要未说明数据集、信道设置、评测规模及100%准确率的适用范围，实际泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Generative text steganography enables LLM agents to exchange secret information through task-relevant messages. Yet most methods evaluate recovery on sender-side tokens, whereas the receiver observes only surface text. Detokenization and receiver-side retokenization can alter token boundaries, desynchronize coding states, and cause such evaluation to overestimate receiver-side recovery. Existing remedies rely on inference-time filtering or verification, correcting individual outputs without adapting the generation policy to the receiver-side channel. To address these limitations, we propose TI-StegoAlign, a channel-guided post-training framework. The Bit-Consistent Supervised Objective (BCSO) enlarges local coding margins at realized sender-side embedding positions. Channel-Conditioned Preference Optimization (CCPO) then aligns complete stegotexts using receiver-realistic recovery, text quality, and anti-steganalysis feedback. TI-StegoAlign updates only LoRA parameters and requires no tokenization-specific correction during communication. Experimental results show 100% receiver bit accuracy. Compared with the strongest baselines, TI-StegoAlign achieves a 21.6% reduction in normalized perplexity deviation and a 6.3% relative improvement in anti-steganalysis performance.

</details>


### [213] [Auditable Release Control for Pedagogical Leakage in LLM Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00515)
*Nizam Kadir*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“教学泄漏”概念：模型在未获授权前泄露答案或关键推理。作者构建带披露合同、可信策略门控、语义验证与回退机制的发布边界，并通过固定样本和外部复现实验显示，该机制可显著降低泄漏，但会牺牲部分帮助性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型导师即使回答正确、有帮助，也可能过早给出解答或决定性推理，破坏分步教学、提示式学习等交互目标。论文旨在将这一问题形式化为状态与动作相关的安全失败，并建立可审计、可归因的控制机制。

Method: 系统由五类披露合同的选择器、控制特权模式的可信策略门、生成文本的渲染器及统一发布函数组成。发布函数执行可检查规则、可选累积验证和按动作配置的回退。系统记录可重放轨迹，以区分选择、生成、验证和执行环节的失败。实验使用599个固定Gemini 3.5回答，并以40个未见问题簇和480条攻击序列进行外部时间戳复现；安全性由盲测的三模型评审面板判定，兼顾帮助性指标。

Result: 严格完全中介将599个回答中的面板多数泄漏标记从181降至0，配对问题簇差异为-30.22个百分点，95%置信区间为[-35.00,-25.72]，但替换了581个回答并降低帮助性。仅使用检查器回退时有11个多数泄漏标记；加入语义验证后为14个，未显示可靠的边际改进。全局A1脚手架实现0个多数标记和54个任一评审标记，在自动安全性和效用上优于拟合Q。外部复现中，高保障发布将多数标记从42降至8，差异为-7.08个百分点，95%置信区间为[-13.13,-2.29]；仍有7例失败且新增1例失败，平均帮助性下降0.192。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，基于明确披露合同的授权感知发布边界能够显著抑制教学泄漏，并提供可审计的失败归因能力。然而，其保证范围是发布控制而非普遍语义安全，也未证明学习效果提升；安全收益与帮助性损失之间存在明确权衡，语义验证组件的额外价值尚不稳定。

Abstract: Large language model tutors can be correct and helpful yet disclose an answer or decisive reasoning before that disclosure is authorized. We formalize this state- and action-dependent failure as pedagogical leakage and introduce an authorization-aware complete-mediation boundary. A selector emits one of five disclosure contracts, trusted policy gates privileged modes, and a renderer proposes language. A single release function applies inspectable checks, optional cumulative verification, and action-specific fallback; replayable traces separate selection, generation, verification, and enforcement failures.
  Matched component attribution exposes a safety-utility frontier. On 599 fixed Gemini 3.5 proposals, strict mediation reduces blinded three-model panel-majority leakage flags from 181 to 0 (paired problem-cluster difference -30.22 points, 95% CI [-35.00,-25.72]), while replacing 581 responses and lowering helpfulness. Checker-triggered fallback alone yields 11 majority flags; adding the semantic verifier yields 14 and no reliable marginal gain. A global A1 scaffold yields 0 majority and 54 any-judge flags, outperforming fitted Q on automatic safety and utility. In an externally timestamped replication over 40 unseen problem clusters and 480 attack sequences, high-assurance release reduces majority flags from 42 to 8 (-7.08 points, 95% CI [-13.13,-2.29]); seven failures persist, one is introduced, and mean helpfulness falls by .192. These results establish an auditable release boundary and failure attribution under declared contracts, not universal semantic safety or learning gains.

</details>


### [214] [Exploring Usability and Legal Practice: Insights from German Judicial Users of Digital Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00541)
*Tobias Hoppmann,Leona Lassak,M. Angela Sasse,Zinaida Benenson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究将数字取证置于刑事程序中的社会技术系统框架下，基于对101名司法实践者的调查，识别出流程整合、跨领域沟通、证据访问和培训方面的显著改进需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究主要关注数字取证工具与技术方法，对其在司法程序中的组织协作、角色关系和实际工作流整合关注不足。随着数据规模增长、技术持续演进及技术与法律人员协作复杂度提高，仅优化工具难以解决实际应用障碍。

Method: 研究采用社会技术系统视角，并借鉴可用性研究与以人为中心的安全理念。作者对德国北莱茵-威斯特法伦州司法系统中的101名从业者开展问卷调查，受访者包括检察官、法官和数字取证专家，以考察不同利益相关者对数字取证应用、协作与可用性的看法。

Result: 调查显示，受访者普遍认为数字取证需要更有效地融入现有工作流；技术与法律领域人员之间需要加强沟通；不同角色对数字取证能力、结果形式及使用方式的预期需要进一步协调。研究还发现，可通过强化跨学科合作、提升证据数据和分析结果的访问速度与易用性、改进相关培训和教育来增强数字取证的可用性。

Conclusion: 数字取证的改进不应局限于工具性能和分析方法，还应面向司法程序中的人员、组织、流程与沟通机制进行系统设计。社会技术系统视角能够为提升数字取证在司法实践中的可用性、协同性和实际效能提供重要依据。

Abstract: Digital forensics has become an integral part of modern criminal proceedings, yet its effective integration remains challenging because of increasing data volumes, evolving technologies, and complex interactions between technical and legal stakeholders. Although prior work has focused primarily on digital forensic tools and methods, its broader procedural and organizational context has received limited attention. Building on emerging perspectives inspired by usability research and human-centered security, we conceptualize digital forensics as part of a socio-technical system within criminal proceedings. We consequently investigate this perspective through a survey of 101 practitioners from the judiciary of the German federal state of North Rhine-Westphalia, including public prosecutors, judges, and digital forensic experts. The results indicate a strong demand for improved integration of digital forensics into workflows, enhanced cross-domain communication, and a closer alignment of stakeholder expectations. They also uncover great potential for the improvement of digital forensics usability, e.g., through stronger interdisciplinary cooperation, easier and faster access to evidential data and results, or improvement of stakeholder training and education.

</details>


### [215] [Vulnerability Detection in AArch64 Machine Code Using a Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02125)
*Oleksandr Mostovyi,Denys Symonov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向AArch64二进制机器码的可解释数字孪生系统，在无源代码条件下通过执行轨迹与符号规则检测漏洞，并输出可复现的具体状态证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二进制漏洞检测往往难以同时覆盖跨多条指令的攻击模式、保留完整运行时上下文并提供清晰解释。该工作旨在将具体执行语义、状态追踪和规则化检测结合，以提升检测结果的可解释性与可复现性。

Method: 系统构建程序具体执行的数字孪生，持续维护寄存器、处理器标志位、内存及存活堆分配块状态。每条指令被转化为包含指令名称、操作数值和执行后状态的轨迹事件。漏洞模式以带测试的Kleene代数符号规则描述，规则定义事件序列及机器状态谓词，并被编译为有限自动机在线扫描轨迹，因此无需SMT求解器。

Result: 实验覆盖整数溢出、空指针解引用和堆缓冲区溢出三类预定义缺陷。系统在相应不安全轨迹中均成功触发检测，在安全轨迹中未产生误报。每个报告提供被触发规则、轨迹位置及相关具体状态值。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了基于具体执行轨迹和有限自动机的规则检测可用于AArch64无源码分析，尤其适合识别依赖多步执行上下文的缺陷模式。其主要价值在于不依赖SMT求解器，同时为检测结论提供细粒度、可验证的执行证据；但摘要中的评估范围仅涵盖三类预定义规则，泛化能力、性能开销和对复杂真实程序的适用性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper proposes an explainable digital twin for vulnerability detection in AArch64 machine code without access to source code. The digital twin reproduces the concrete execution of a program and preserves the state of registers, processor flags, memory, and live allocated blocks. Each instruction is transformed into a trace event containing the instruction name, operand values, and the post-instruction state. Vulnerabilities are represented as symbolic rules in Kleene algebra with tests: each rule specifies an event sequence and predicates over the machine state. This approach enables the detection of not only isolated unsafe instructions but also multi-step execution patterns. The rules are compiled into finite automata that scan the trace without using an SMT solver. The experimental evaluation covers three CWE classes: integer overflow (CWE-190), null pointer dereference (CWE-476), and heap buffer overflow (CWE-122). The system detected all three predefined vulnerabilities and produced no report on the safe trace. Each detection result includes the triggered rule, the trace position, and the concrete state values, thereby providing a reproducible explanation.

</details>


### [216] [Robust Watermarks Meet Backdoored Models: Evading Diffusion Semantic Watermarks via Stealthy Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00543)
*Jinyuan Liu,Tianshuo Cong,Pei Li,Tianrui Wang,Xinlei He,Anyu Wang,Xiaoyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了潜在扩散模型语义水印检测链路中的神经网络后门风险，并提出GhostVAE：通过在VAE编码器中植入隐蔽后门，使带触发器的生成图像能够规避水印检测，同时尽量保持正常图像上的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义水印研究通常关注水印嵌入与鲁棒性，却较少审视检测流程所依赖的神经网络模块是否会成为安全薄弱点。论文旨在系统评估VAE编码器被植入后门后，对语义水印可信性的影响。

Method: GhostVAE包含两个阶段：第一阶段利用功率谱正则化构造通用触发器，以提升触发器在不同图像条件下的稳定性；第二阶段采用参数对齐目标训练带后门的VAE编码器，使其在常规输入上维持原有编码行为，而在触发器激活时生成有利于绕过水印检测的潜变量表示。作者在三种语义水印方案和三种主流潜在扩散模型上进行评测，并从输入空间、参数空间和潜变量空间检验其隐蔽性及防御有效性。

Result: 实验显示，GhostVAE在正常图像上仍能保持较高的水印检测能力，平均真阳性率为94.4%；在触发器激活的情况下，平均规避成功率达到94.6%。针对17种代表性防御措施的分析表明，该方法在多个观测维度下仍具有较强隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 论文表明，语义水印的安全性不能仅由水印算法本身保证；若检测所依赖的VAE等神经网络组件缺乏供应链、参数完整性和端到端安全验证，水印系统可能被后门破坏。因此，安全部署应将模型组件审计、完整性验证、异常行为检测与鲁棒检测机制纳入整体设计。

Abstract: Although semantic watermarking is considered a promising safeguard for images generated by Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs), the reliance of the watermark detection pipeline on neural networks introduces a critical yet underexplored backdoor attack surface. To systematically study this vulnerability, we propose GhostVAE to plant a stealthy backdoor into the encoder of Variational Autoencoder (VAE), enabling reliable evasion of watermark detection. GhostVAE operates in two stages: it first constructs a universal trigger via power spectrum regularization to improve the trigger robustness, and then trains a backdoored VAE encoder with a parameter-aligned objective. Through extensive evaluations across three state-of-the-art semantic watermarking schemes and three widely adopted LDMs, we show that GhostVAE preserves watermark detection performance on benign images (achieving an average true positive rate of 94.4%), while simultaneously enabling highly effective evasion under trigger activation (achieving an average attack success rate of 94.6%). Moreover, we comprehensively analyze seventeen representative defenses and demonstrate that GhostVAE remains stealthy across the input space, parameter space, and latent space. Our work fundamentally undermines the trustworthiness of semantic watermarking systems and highlights that secure deployment of semantic watermarks requires end-to-end security considerations, particularly for neural network components.

</details>


### [217] [A False Average: Chain-of-Thought Monitors Collapse Where They Are the Only Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00583)
*Shikhar Shiromani,Leo Richter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究表明，若智能体能够重写其思维链而保留全部实际操作与输出，基于思维链的监控器可能近乎失效：在特定子集上的检出率由约95%降至不足11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估思维链监控能否识别仅在推理中暴露、但行为表面正常的奖励投机行为，并检验其面对恶意推理重写时的稳健性。

Method: 攻击者仅重写智能体的推理文本，使其呈现善意工程意图，同时逐字保留命令和输出；研究在一次无梯度攻击、不同监控器与智能体模型、在线智能体及校准监控器上测试，并比较基于轨迹的防御、激活探针和因果控制。

Result: 重写后，留出监控器在相关子集上的检出率从约95%降至11%以下；总体准确率因大量可由行为直接识别的样本而产生误导。攻击可跨监控器和模型迁移，并在真实智能体上复现。仅依赖轨迹的防御只能部分恢复检测能力，即使针对该攻击进行提示也有限；激活探针可区分监控器漏判的奖励投机，但因果实验表明这只是额外检测器，而非监控器隐含掌握了相关信息。

Conclusion: 思维链监控高度依赖推理文本的真实性，一旦推理可被重写且行为不变，其在关键样本上的检测能力会接近崩溃。可靠防御需要引入轨迹之外的独立信息，而不能仅依赖更强的轨迹分析或提示。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is meant to catch the reward hacks that look clean in the actions and betray themselves only in the reasoning. We show that this is exactly where an adversary who controls the reasoning can defeat it. Rewriting only an agent's reasoning to read as good-faith engineering, while copying every command and output verbatim so the exploit is unchanged, drops a held-out monitor's catch rate on that subset from about 95% to under 11% in one gradient-free shot. A monitor's aggregate accuracy is a false average: dominated by hacks the actions give away, it hides the near-total collapse this rewrite produces on the subset where CoT monitoring is the only signal. The attack transfers across monitor families and agent models, reproduces with live agents, though against a calibrated monitor evasion concentrates in the strongest agent. Trace-only defenses recover it only partially, even one primed on the attack, because the rewrite stays truthful about what happened and lies only about intent; only information from outside the trace helps substantially. A probe on an open-weight surrogate monitor's activations separates the hacks its verdict misses, but a causal control shows this is a detector, not evidence the monitor secretly knows.

</details>


### [218] [Domain Decoupling Attack: Exploiting the Validation Gap Between Protective DNS and Shared Edge Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00643)
*Weizhe Wang,Minhong Dong,Jinhao Li,Yao Zhang,Hao Liu,Qiang Hu,Tao Luo,Guangquan Xu,Bin Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出域解耦攻击（DDA）：攻击者利用DNS授权与共享IP之间的绑定缺陷，先通过允许域名获得共享边缘IP的访问权限，再以隐藏域名同时设置TLS SNI和HTTP Host，从而绕过部分基于DNS的访问控制。大规模测量显示，该问题在CDN和非CDN共享主机环境中均较普遍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CDN的流量隐藏或访问控制方案通常依赖SNI与Host不一致、域名所有权验证不足或特定服务商的路由改写，难以适用于现代CDN环境。论文旨在揭示DNS授权映射到共享IP后可能产生的跨租户访问风险。

Method: 研究分析DNS授权、共享IP与主机名验证之间的关系，设计DDA流程：解析允许域名获取共享IP后，连接同一IP并在TLS SNI和HTTP Host中一致呈现目标隐藏域名。随后对来自六大洲的1,069,048个域名开展大规模探测，并通过实验室实验验证DNS绑定访问控制在共享地址上的结构性局限。

Result: 研究共完成18,025,068次成功探测，发现总体暴露率为95.8%；CDN域名为99.26%，非CDN域名为92.75%，非CDN跨租户IP为97.7%。实验结果进一步表明，仅依赖DNS解析结果进行IP级授权，无法可靠区分共享地址上的不同租户。

Conclusion: DNS派生的IP授权在共享主机环境中存在系统性安全缺陷，单纯验证允许域名与IP之间的关系不足以防止跨租户访问。CDN及非CDN服务应加强租户级身份绑定、主机名与证书校验及请求路由隔离，以提升共享基础设施中的访问控制能力。

Abstract: Network attackers often conceal malicious communication within legitimate Internet traffic. Existing CDN-based evasion techniques rely on SNI--Host inconsistency, insufficient domain ownership verification, or provider-specific routing rewrites, which limit their applicability in modern CDN environments. We identify a validation gap in DNS-based authorization, where permission derived from an allowed domain applies to a shared IP and can be reused to reach another tenant in both CDN and non-CDN shared-hosting environments. This paper presents the Domain Decoupling Attack (DDA), which resolves an allowed domain to obtain permission for a shared edge IP and subsequently connects to the same address while presenting the hidden domain consistently in both TLS SNI and HTTP Host. Measurements of 1,069,048 domains across six continents produce 18,025,068 successful probes and identify exposure rates of 95.8% overall, 99.26% for CDN domains, 92.75% for non-CDN domains, and 97.7% for non-CDN cross-tenant IPs, while laboratory experiments reveal a structural limitation of DNS-bound access control on shared addresses. These results clarify the security risks of DNS-derived IP authorization and support the evaluation and improvement of access-control mechanisms in CDN and non-CDN shared-hosting environments.

</details>


### [219] [What Syntax Cannot See: The Dynamic Syntactic Invariance Principle and Several Instances of the Same Hidden Assumption, and a Contradiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00958)
*Fabio F. G. Buono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 摘要声称提出一种可跨领域复用的方法：识别既有结论的隐含静态假设，将其变量化并研究假设移除后的后果。核心主张是“动态句法不变性原理”，并进一步宣称可导出滚动密钥保密性、物理理论可达性、可计算但对观察者似噪声的输出，以及最终的P≠NP结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图解决静态理论结果在规则随时间演化时是否仍成立的问题，并将“完整描述层面的真实差异在受限观察层面不可见”作为统一结构，解释多个看似无关领域中的不可达性或不可区分性现象。

Method: 方法论上，作者先定位已接受结果中未明说的前提，再把该前提形式化为可变条件，给出在条件放宽后结论存续的充要条件。形式核心是动态句法不变性原理；随后将该原则分别映射到滚动密钥更新、相对论、物理定律形式理论及可计算输出等场景，并声称在SAT上构造矛盾以推出P≠NP。

Result: 摘要宣称获得一个关于静态不可达性在动态规则下保持成立的尖锐必要条件；证明滚动密钥方案可跨会话维持保密；在若干领域发现共同的“全局可区分、局部不可见”结构；并据称由SAT推导出P≠NP。

Conclusion: 从摘要本身无法验证任何关键定理。最大的问题是核心对象、形式语言、动态更新模型、“必要条件”的精确表述、各领域映射及SAT矛盾链条均未给出。尤其P≠NP属于极高门槛结论，摘要中的“标准理论已承认”与“有一项关于证明的保留”不能替代可逐步审查的证明。该工作可被视为一个雄心较大的统一框架提案，但其可信度完全取决于正式定义、定理陈述、独立证明和同行审查。

Abstract: This paper develops a single method, find what an accepted result silently assumed, make it a variable, and prove what follows once it is dropped, and shows it keeps working across domains with nothing in common. Its formal core is the Dynamic Syntactic Invariance Principle: a known static inaccessibility result survives when a system's rules evolve in time, under one sharp necessary condition. A direct cryptographic payoff follows: the secrecy of a rolling-key scheme persists across sessions under a structural update, strengthening a static guarantee from earlier work. The same move is then carried into settings far from each other, special relativity, the reach of a formal theory of physical law, and computable output that is fully meaningful yet indistinguishable from noise to every observer, each established on its own terms, so the recurring structure beneath them is found, not imposed: a distinction real at a full level of description can be invisible at a restricted one. Applied to \textsc{SAT}, this yields a contradiction from which coherence forces $\mathsf{P}\neq\mathsf{NP}$, derived from what standard theory already admits rather than assumed,offered with one reservation, about the proof, not the answer.

</details>


### [220] [Improving the Security of Containerized Workloads using Transparency and Traceability Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00660)
*Nikos Fotiou,Lefteris Georgiadis,Ignacio Lacalle,George C. Polyzos,Vasilios A. Siris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可验证的容器镜像分发架构：通过透明度服务、一次性签名密钥、追加式透明注册表和准入阶段策略校验，确保只有满足身份、签名与审计要求的镜像能够被部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容器镜像通常经由CI/CD流水线构建并在云边异构环境中运行。构建环节或凭证一旦失陷，可能导致不可信镜像被大规模传播。因此，需要在镜像生产、发布和部署之间建立可验证的完整性、可审计的透明度以及强制执行的部署准入控制。

Method: 架构中的透明度服务为经认证身份生成与单次签名事件绑定的一次性密钥；每次签名事件被写入仅追加的透明注册表，并产生可密码学验证的包含证明。系统将包含证明及签名者身份属性附加到镜像元数据中。在部署准入阶段，策略即代码组件验证签名、证明、身份属性及其是否符合预定义规则。作者实现了与GitHub Actions和GitLab Runners集成的原型，并在现实威胁模型下评估其供应链防护能力。

Result: 摘要表明，该原型能够将构建身份、镜像签名记录和透明日志证明关联起来，并在准入时自动拒绝不符合策略的镜像。与GitHub Actions和GitLab Runners的集成说明方案可嵌入常见CI/CD环境；但摘要未提供性能开销、扩展性、策略误拒率或具体对比实验数据。

Conclusion: 该工作将短生命周期或一次性签名密钥、透明日志审计和准入控制结合，为容器软件供应链提供端到端的可验证分发机制。其主要价值在于把镜像可信性从构建后的静态签名检查扩展为可追溯、可证明且可由部署策略强制执行的流程；后续仍需通过量化实验验证其在大规模多注册表、多集群场景中的性能、可用性与密钥服务可靠性。

Abstract: Containerized workloads are commonly built via CI/CD pipelines, stored in registries, and executed across heterogeneous infrastructures, including cloud and edge environments. A single compromised build step or credential can turn routine automation into large-scale distribution of malicious artifacts, motivating integrity, transparency, and enforceable deployment-time checks. In this paper, we present an architecture for verifiable container image distribution that addresses key-management challenges and enables policy-enforced admission-time verification. A transparency service generates one-time signing keys bound to authenticated identities, records signing events in an append-only transparency registry, and returns cryptographically verifiable proofs of inclusion. These proofs and identity attributes are attached to image metadata and evaluated by policy-as-code at admission time, so only compliant artifacts are deployed. We implement a proof-of-concept integrated with GitHub Actions and GitLab Runners and evaluate how the resulting pipeline mitigates common supply-chain attacks under a realistic threat model.

</details>


### [221] [EntailLLM: Verifying LLM-Generated Vulnerability Discovery Paths with Domain Knowledge via Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01763)
*Kaustuv Mukherji,Jaikrishna Manojkumar Patil,Colton Payne,Paulo Shakarian,Dana Warmsley,Nigel Stepp,Evelyn Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出EntailLLM，一种利用独立领域知识图谱和时间标注逻辑，对大语言模型生成的二进制漏洞分析路径进行蕴涵验证的框架。实验显示，其将总体蕴涵率从78%提升至98%，且可直接应用于真实医疗设备二进制文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在软件漏洞推理中可能生成看似合理但违背领域知识的分析结论，尤其会降低安全关键软件分析的可信度。既有方法通常仅评估输出预测准确性，或限制模型在单一知识图中搜索，缺乏利用独立领域知识检验推理一致性的机制。

Method: EntailLLM将模型提出的分析路径表示为二进制函数调用图上的遍历序列，并将漏洞相关领域知识编码为独立知识图谱。系统通过时间标注逻辑对两类图结构进行对齐与蕴涵检验，从而判断分析路径是否符合领域规则；该验证过程提供可解释的逻辑依据，并继承广义标注逻辑的形式化语义与保证。

Result: 作者在3类CWE、4种大语言模型、3种提示策略及7个二进制程序上开展评估，程序函数调用图规模介于405至12,696个节点。引入领域知识后，汇总蕴涵率由78%提高至98%；仅3%的实验设置出现蕴涵率下降。系统在真实医疗设备二进制文件上的端到端部署同样达到98%的汇总蕴涵率，且无需针对单个设备进行微调。

Conclusion: EntailLLM通过将模型生成的漏洞分析路径与独立领域知识进行形式化蕴涵验证，显著提高了推理结果的领域一致性与可信度。其核心贡献在于从单纯预测评估转向可解释、语义明确的逻辑验证，为安全关键二进制漏洞分析中的大语言模型输出校验提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to reason about software vulnerabilities, but their outputs can silently violate domain knowledge, limiting their reliability in safety-critical settings such as medical devices. Prior work either treats that output as a prediction to be scored or constrains it to walks within a single knowledge graph; neither checks whether reasoning over a binary is consistent with an independent body of domain knowledge. We present EntailLLM, which validates each LLM-proposed analyst path by entailment: the path is a traversal of the binary's function call graph, the domain knowledge is represented in a separate graph, and verification aligns the two under temporal annotated logic. Across three CWE classes, four LLMs, three prompting strategies, and seven binaries varying in size from 405 to 12,696 function call-graph nodes, domain knowledge raises pooled entailment from 78% to 98%, with entailment decreasing in only 3% of the experiments. EntailLLM is deployed end-to-end on real medical-device binaries, reaching 98% pooled entailment without per-device tuning. Our system inherits the formal guarantees of generalized annotated logic, providing logical verification of LLM output that is both explainable and grounded in well-defined semantics.

</details>


### [222] [From Chasing Ghosts to Missed Attacks: Perspectives and Perceptions of SOC Practitioners on LLM Integration, Risks, and Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00672)
*Jonas Thurner,Nadine Jost,Stefan Albert Horstmann,Fabian Ising,Lea Groeber,Alena Naiakshina,Sebastian Schinzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过访谈SOC从业者，评估大语言模型在安全运营中的实际适用性，发现其适合报告生成等重复性低风险任务，但尚不足以可靠承担事件分析等高影响职责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC需在高压环境下处理海量安全事件。尽管LLM可能提升效率，其在真实SOC工作流中的能力边界、风险来源及安全集成方式仍缺乏来自从业者的实证认识。

Method: 研究者对25名具有LLM使用经验的SOC从业者开展半结构化访谈，并结合交互式情景讨论，识别潜在应用、实施障碍、人员因素及负责任集成需求。最终归纳出15项用例，并将其划分为6类功能类别。

Result: 受访者认可LLM在报告自动化、信息整理等重复性和低层级任务中的价值；但认为事件分析等高影响任务暂不可行。主要问题包括技术深度不足、上下文理解有限、缺乏组织特定知识，以及用户过度依赖模型的风险。研究还指出，限制因素不仅来自模型能力，也来自SOC自身的数据、流程和治理准备度。

Conclusion: LLM在SOC中的近期定位应是受人监督的辅助工具，而非自主决策者。安全部署需要围绕组织知识接入、上下文管理、结果验证、权限与责任边界、过度依赖防护及工作流整合进行人本化设计。尽管存在风险，从业者因效率诉求和竞争压力普遍愿意采用LLM。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) process large volumes of security events, requiring analysts to accurately detect and assess ongoing cyberattacks under time pressure. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest potential benefits for security operations, yet their practical suitability for real-world SOC workflows remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we conducted 25 semi-structured interviews with SOC practitioners who had prior experience with LLMs, complemented by interactive scenarios to anticipate challenges and identify opportunities for the responsible integration of LLM-based tools into SOC workflows. We identified 15 LLM use cases grouped into six functional categories. While LLMs are valued for automating repetitive, low-level tasks such as report automation, practitioners rate high-impact tasks such as incident analysis as not yet feasible, reporting limitations in technical depth, context awareness, and organization-specific knowledge. They locate these limitations less in the models than in the readiness of their SOCs and human factors driving over-reliance. Despite concerns, practitioners express a strong willingness to adopt LLMs, describing competitive pressure that leaves few alternatives. This work contributes an empirical, practitioner-driven analysis of LLM use across SOC roles and organizations and derives concrete design and integration requirements for human-centered, operationally safe LLM-assisted security operations.

</details>


### [223] [Supporting Cybersecurity Risk Management for Medical Devices via the SECUMAN Ontology and Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00698)
*Martin Diller,Anne Esslinger,Piotr Gorczyca,Evi Hartig,Lia Kacholdt,Hannes Strass*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SECUMAN本体及SHACL约束，用于以机器可读方式表示、检查和关联医疗器械网络安全风险管理文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联网医疗器械的网络安全风险可能影响患者安全，但现有风险管理文件多为半结构化自然语言文本，难以进行一致性检查、认证审查、追溯和复用。因此需要统一的形式化语义模型与自动化验证机制。

Method: 作者构建基于OWL的SECUMAN本体，覆盖安全风险环境、风险评估、控制措施和残余风险评价；同时定义SHACL形状约束，以验证文档结构完整性及其对预期模型的符合性。该方案与VDE Spec 90025以及RISKMAN本体和形状对齐，并在安全风险管理模型基础上扩展威胁场景、保护目标、攻击者画像、暴露等级、资产和安全设计论证等网络安全相关概念。

Result: SECUMAN提供了面向医疗器械网络安全风险文档的统一语义词汇表和结构化校验机制，可将原本难以自动处理的文档内容映射为可验证、可关联的数据模型。其预期能力包括自动化初步验证、风险项与控制措施之间的可追溯性，以及网络安全与功能安全风险管理文档的集成。

Conclusion: 该工作将医疗器械网络安全风险管理从以文本为中心的文档维护方式推进到基于本体和约束的形式化管理方式。其主要价值在于增强文档一致性、审查效率和跨领域整合能力；摘要描述的是提出的框架及预期用途，尚未给出具体数据集、实证评估指标或与其他方案的定量比较结果。

Abstract: We propose the SECUMAN ontology and shapes for representing and analysing cybersecurity risk-management documentation for medical devices. Cybersecurity risks are increasingly relevant for connected medical devices and may have direct consequences for patient safety. Current risk-management files are often maintained as semi-structured natural language text, which makes consistency checking, certification review, and reuse difficult. SECUMAN provides a formal OWL-based vocabulary for modelling security-risk context, assessment, control measures, and residual-risk evaluation, and uses SHACL constraints to check structural completeness and conformity with the intended documentation model. The ontology is aligned with VDE Spec 90025 and the related RISKMAN ontology and shapes, while extending their safety-oriented approach to concepts relevant to cybersecurity risk documentation such as threat scenarios, protection goals, attacker profiles, exposure levels, assets, and secure design arguments. SECUMAN is intended to support automated first-pass validation, traceability, and integration of cybersecurity and safety risk-management documentation.

</details>


### [224] [Adversarial Attacks in Multi-Agent LLM Pipelines: Unveiling Structural Vulnerabilities in Agentic AI Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00718)
*Faisal Haque Bappy,Tahrim Hossain,Tarannum Shaila Zaman,Raiful Hasan,Kamrul Hasan,Tariqul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出，多智能体大语言模型流水线因缺少跨代理边界验证而存在系统性安全缺口：恶意内容一旦被某个代理接受，便可能作为可信输入在后续环节传播。实验表明，风险主要由流水线架构决定，而非单个模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体安全机制通常假设模型输入边界相对明确，但多智能体系统包含多次代理间的信息、身份、执行意图和状态传递。现有框架往往隐含信任这些传递过程，导致内容注入、代理冒充、计划偏离和记忆污染等风险难以被既有评测发现。因此，作者旨在解释此类结构性脆弱性的根源，并推动从模型级防护转向流水线级防护。

Method: 作者提出“边界验证”作为核心安全原语，要求在代理间边界处显式校验传递数据的内容、身份、执行意图及状态完整性。论文基于GAIA和SWE-Bench的标注生产轨迹考察良性部署中的风险表现，并在受控多智能体环境中，以相同流水线配置测试GPT-5-mini、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Kimi K2.5，对不同失效模式及攻击成功情况进行比较。

Result: 研究发现，多智能体流水线中的安全缺陷即使在良性任务轨迹中也会出现，且大多无法被现有评测体系有效覆盖。跨模型对比显示，攻击成功率与流水线的结构和信任关系更一致，而不单纯取决于模型能力强弱。这支持了内容注入、身份伪造、任务计划偏离和记忆污染属于架构层攻击面的判断。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：多智能体大语言模型的对抗脆弱性本质上是架构属性，而非仅靠替换或增强单一模型即可解决的问题。未来防护应在代理边界部署可验证的内容过滤、身份认证、意图约束和状态完整性检查，并建立面向端到端流水线的安全评测标准。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines orchestrate multiple specialized language model agents into structured workflows where intermediate outputs are passed across agents to solve complex tasks. This design introduces a security gap absent in single-agent settings: once an agent accepts adversarial content, it is propagated as trusted input throughout the pipeline. We argue that this vulnerability stems from the absence of boundary verification, a security primitive that enforces explicit validation of data as it crosses inter-agent boundaries, including content, identity, execution intent, and state integrity. Without such verification, modern pipelines embed implicit trust assumptions that are not adversarially robust, giving rise to structurally distinct attack surfaces (e.g., content injection, agent impersonation, plan deviation, and memory poisoning). Leveraging annotated production traces from the GAIA and SWE-Bench benchmark, we show that these vulnerabilities arise in benign deployments and largely evade existing evaluation frameworks. We further operationalize these failure modes within a controlled multi-agent setting and evaluate them across GPT-5-mini, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and Kimi K2.5 under identical pipeline configurations. The results reveal that attack success aligns with pipeline structure rather than model capability, indicating that adversarial vulnerability is fundamentally an architectural property and motivating a shift toward pipeline-level defenses.

</details>


### [225] [Safety Invariants for Agents Orchestrating Irreversible State Transitions: A Four-Dimensional Formalism Evaluated on Public Ledgers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00783)
*Zhaoming Yin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“执行保真性（execution fidelity）”安全保证，用于约束自主智能体对不可逆外部系统的写操作：一次会话在账本上的实际效果必须是“完全无操作”或“恰好一次执行此前向用户展示的状态转移”。作者以跨公共账本的价值转移为例，给出形式化模型、七项安全不变量与实证评估，并声称该框架可推广至其他不可逆外部状态操作场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架主要优化任务成功率，通常未系统处理资金转移、持久化写入和硬件执行等不可逆副作用。在规划器映射错误、执行结果不明确、重试、至少一次投递及委托调用等故障条件下，智能体可能执行错误目标、重复执行或在用户未知情况下产生外部影响。论文试图将“用户意图是否正确”这一难以由运行时判定的问题，与“系统是否忠实执行已展示操作”这一可验证问题分离，从而使预览机制成为实质性的安全控制。

Method: 作者将公共账本上的价值移动建模为由（钱包、链、地址、协议）四维索引的状态转移。围绕执行保真性目标，论文证明在给定故障模型下，实际账本效果只能为零效果或与用户预览完全一致且仅发生一次的转移。该保证不验证预览是否符合用户真实意图，而是将该问题收敛为对有限预览对象的单一谓词判断。为实现该性质，系统从保真性条件推导出七项安全不变量，而非依赖经验罗列规则；随后在N=60的对抗测试集上，与朴素ReAct基线比较，并在不同写入倾向的底层模型上测试。

Result: 在两种“写入激进型”底层模型上，所述安全栈相对朴素ReAct基线将通过率提高约74个百分点；在一种“写入谨慎型”模型上，仅提高约3个百分点。作者据此指出，仅使用单一模型评估智能体安全方案的区分度较低，甚至接近不可证伪。系统已部署，报告了覆盖8条链的108次生产写操作，并据此支持其故障分类。结果表明，该方法对高风险、倾向直接执行写操作的模型更具增益；但摘要未提供置信区间、失败案例分布、基线具体配置或七项不变量的细节，因而难以独立判断统计稳健性与工程覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是把不可逆操作安全从笼统的“智能体是否可靠”转化为可形式化验证的“展示内容与实际执行是否一致且至多一次”。这一界定清晰、工程价值较高，尤其适用于资金转移等重复执行代价高的场景。其主要边界也很明确：执行保真性不能保证用户预览本身正确、用户理解预览、授权关系合理，亦不能消除预览生成阶段的语义错误。因此，该框架应被视为不可逆执行层的强一致性与防重放控制，而非端到端意图对齐方案。其跨领域推广主张在概念上合理，但仍需要在持久化存储、硬件控制等具有不同确认语义、回滚机制和并发模型的环境中进一步验证。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly asked to produce irreversible effects on external systems - transferring funds, writing to durable storage, actuating hardware. Existing agent frameworks (ReAct, Reflexion, MCP) optimize task success on benchmarks and give little attention to the safety of irreversible side-effects. We formalize one such setting, movement of value across public ledgers, as state transitions in a four-dimensional space indexed by (wallet, chain, address, protocol), and use that formalism to state and prove a guarantee we call execution fidelity: under a fault model admitting planner mis-mapping, ambiguous outcomes, retries, at-least-once delivery, and delegated non-human callers, a session's realized effect on the ledger is either nothing at all or exactly the transition that was rendered to the user, exactly once. The theorem deliberately does not claim that the rendered transition matches the user's intent - no runtime layer can decide that - but it confines that unbounded question to a single predicate over a finite object, which is what makes a preview a sufficient control rather than a formality. Seven safety invariants, derived from the fidelity condition rather than enumerated from experience, discharge the guarantee. Empirically, on a controlled N=60 adversarial suite the stack lifts pass rate by ~74 percentage points over a naive-ReAct baseline on two write-aggressive backing models, but by only ~3 points on a write-cautious one - evidence that single-model evaluations of agent safety stacks are close to unfalsifiable. The system is deployed; 108 production write operations across 8 chains back the failure taxonomy. Although the evaluation setting is public ledgers, the formalism and invariants apply to any probabilistic agent acting on irreversible external state.

</details>


### [226] [Hardware-rooted attestation for AI-agent evidence: composing IETF RATS with action evidence packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00801)
*Anton Sokolov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出将软件层的行动证据包（AEP）与基于硬件信任根的远程证明结合，以验证AI代理输出是否确由声明的模型版本在未被篡改的平台上生成；其在swtpm模拟器上的实验表明该组合方案可识别正常、模型被替换、证明过期及伪造输出绑定等情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AEP能够记录AI代理声称执行的操作、授权信息和结果，但仅反映代理运行时的自述，无法回答特定模型版本是否确实在可信、未修改的硬件与运行环境中产生某一输出。为弥补软件声明与底层执行环境真实性之间的证据缺口，论文引入硬件远程证明。

Method: 方案采用IETF RATS（RFC 9334）远程证明架构及Veraison验证器思路，构造复合证明：先对硬件证据进行RATS评估，再将其与AEP绑定。具体地，平台寄存器携带模型工件度量值；证明协议将AEP输出哈希和验证方新鲜随机数共同纳入由证明密钥签名的TPM Quote。论文还将“已授权、未授权、不确定、已证明、受争议、已过期”等验证词汇映射至RATS评估结果，并通过一个最小化RATS验证器替身进行检验。

Result: 在软件TPM（swtpm）模拟环境中，验证器能够按预期判定：对于模型度量正确且Quote新鲜的情况给出“已证明”；模型度量被替换时给出“受争议”；重放陈旧Quote时给出“已过期”。此外，即使攻击者使用有效Quote，也无法通过验证来伪造与该Quote绑定的AEP输出。

Conclusion: AEP与RATS硬件证明的绑定可显著增强AI行动证据的可验证性：AEP说明代理报告了什么，硬件证明则支持平台状态和模型工件与批准参考值一致的判断。该工作证明了协议与验证逻辑的可行性，但实验仅基于TPM模拟器，因此不能构成真实硬件信任根下的安全保证；后续需在实体硬件、完整供应链度量和生产级验证服务中评估。

Abstract: An action evidence package (AEP) is a signed, append-only record of what an AI agent did, who or what authorised the action, and what the outcome was. It is a software-layer artefact: it tells a verifier the story of an action as the agent's own runtime reports it. This note argues that software attestation of this kind is necessary but not sufficient. When a verifier's question shifts from "what does the agent claim it did?" to "did the specific model version the operator claims to have deployed actually produce this output, on unmodified hardware?", the AEP alone cannot answer. The missing element is a hardware root of trust: an attestation that the measured boot and runtime state of the platform match an endorsed reference. The IETF Remote Attestation Procedures (RATS) architecture (RFC 9334) and Veraison, an open-source RATS Verifier implementation (Confidential Computing Consortium / Linux Foundation), supply exactly this. We propose a composite attestation: hardware Evidence appraised under RATS, bound to a software AEP. We map a small verifier vocabulary (Authorised / Unauthorised / Indeterminate / Attested / Contested / Expired) onto RATS appraisal outcomes, and demonstrate feasibility with a small executed experiment: on a software Trusted Platform Module (TPM; the swtpm emulator), an output-binding protocol folds the hash of an AEP outcome and a fresh appraiser nonce into an attestation-key-signed quote, with a model-artefact measurement carried in a platform register. A minimal RATS-Verifier stand-in resolves the three platform outcomes as designed -- Attested for a good, fresh quote; Contested when the model measurement is swapped; Expired when a stale quote is replayed -- and rejects a forged AEP outcome bound to a valid quote. The result is a feasibility demonstration on emulated hardware, not a hardware-rooted guarantee.

</details>


### [227] [XR-PRISM: Data-Driven Privacy and Risk Impact Scoring Metric for Extended Reality in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00826)
*Nafisa Anjum,M. Rasel Mahmud*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述2017—2024年间65篇医疗扩展现实（XR）安全与隐私研究，提出涵盖设备、网络、用户和云端四层的威胁分类体系，并构建六因素加权风险评分方法XR-PRISM，以支持风险比较、排序与缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗XR依赖广泛的传感器、复杂的数据处理链路及远程交互，面临安全、隐私与患者安全风险。现有研究缺乏统一且量化的风险评估框架，导致不同威胁和防御措施难以比较及优先级排序。

Method: 研究者系统综述65篇同行评议论文，归纳设备、网络、用户和云端四个威胁层，并整理相应防御措施。在此基础上提出XR-PRISM，将威胁发生可能性、系统脆弱性、攻击面、安全影响、隐私影响和控制措施有效性六项因素进行加权整合，形成单一风险评分。

Result: 超过70%的已识别防御措施缺乏标准化风险评估；不足15%的文献攻击需要较高专业技能才能实施，表明许多威胁具有较低的实施门槛。XR-PRISM能够为医疗XR风险提供透明、数据驱动的量化比较依据。

Conclusion: 本文建立了医疗XR安全与隐私风险的统一分类和评估框架。XR-PRISM有助于研究人员和实践者识别高优先级风险、比较防御方案并优化缓解措施，但其实际应用仍需进一步验证权重设定、评分一致性及不同医疗场景下的适应性。
