Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) technologies are transforming healthcare through immersive training, remote consultation, and patient rehabilitation. However, their extensive sensing capabilities and complex data pipelines introduce distinct security, privacy, and safety risks. Existing research lacks a unified quantitative framework for assessing and prioritizing these risks. We review 65 peer-reviewed studies on XR security and privacy published from 2017 to 2024, synthesizing a four-layer threat taxonomy consisting of Device, Network, User, and Cloud layers, along with a corresponding catalog of defenses. Building on this analysis, we introduce XR-PRISM, a six-factor weighted Privacy and Risk Impact Scoring Metric that integrates threat likelihood, system vulnerability, attack surface, safety impact, privacy impact, and control effectiveness into a single actionable risk score. Our analysis shows that more than 70% of the identified countermeasures lack standardized risk evaluation, while fewer than 15% of the documented attacks require a high level of expertise to execute. XR-PRISM provides researchers and practitioners with a transparent, data-driven method for comparing, prioritizing, and mitigating security and privacy risks in healthcare XR deployments.

</details>


### [228] [Explainable Hybrid Feature Selection for Intrusion Detection in Internet of Medical Things Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00869)
*Amira Berrezzek,Hayet Djellali,Giulio Mallardi,Lamia Mahnane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向医疗物联网网络的轻量级、可解释入侵检测方案：通过相关性过滤与模型重要性、SHAP相结合的混合特征选择，将特征数量最多缩减88%，同时基本保持随机森林和LightGBM的检测准确率与F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网设备类型多样、算力和存储资源受限，且网络流量检测通常要求实时响应。使用完整高维特征训练入侵检测模型会增加计算与部署开销，也降低模型决策的可解释性，因此需要在尽量不损失检测性能的前提下筛选紧凑特征集。

Method: 方法包含三个阶段：首先使用Pearson相关系数过滤高度冗余的特征；其次结合模型驱动的特征重要性评分与SHAP归因结果，选择更小且更具判别力的特征子集；最后在筛选后的特征上训练随机森林与LightGBM分类器。模型解释层面同时采用SHAP和LIME，说明保留特征对单次或整体预测结果的贡献。实验使用CIC-IoMT 2024与CIC-IDS 2017数据集，并将紧凑特征模型与全特征模型进行性能比较。

Result: 实验表明，特征空间可由40项压缩至最少5项，最大降幅为88%。在此压缩程度下，模型的准确率和F1分数与全特征训练模型相比仅相差几个百分点，说明多数原始特征可能存在冗余，少量关键特征已能保留主要检测能力。随机森林和LightGBM均被用于验证该特征选择策略的适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，融合相关性去冗余、模型重要性与SHAP解释的特征选择方法，能够构建计算负担更低且具有一定可解释性的入侵检测器，适合资源有限、强调实时性的医疗物联网部署场景。摘要的局限是未报告具体类别级指标、推理延迟、内存消耗、跨数据集泛化结果及不同攻击类型下的性能，因此实际部署价值仍需通过更完整的效率、安全性和鲁棒性评估确认。

Abstract: Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) networks are hard to protect: devices are heterogeneous, computing resources are scarce, and traffic must be analyzed in real time. We present an intrusion detection system that addresses these constraints through feature selection. A Pearson correlation filter first removes redundant attributes; a hybrid strategy then combines model-based feature importance with SHAP attribution to pick a compact subset, on which we train Random Forest and LightGBM classifiers. SHAP and LIME explain what each retained feature contributes to the decisions. On CIC-IoMT 2024 and CIC-IDS 2017, the method cuts the feature space by up to 88% - from 40 to as few as 5 features - and accuracy and F1-score stay within a few points of models trained on all features. Compact, interpretable detectors of this kind are practical candidates for deployment on resource-limited medical networks.

</details>


### [229] [Multi-LLM Consensus Framework for Evaluating Banking-Sector NIDS Dataset Coverage of MITRE ATT&CK Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00895)
*Sanjida Khanom,Sadia Afrin Khan,Adrita Rahman Tory,Md. Ahsan Habib,Khondokar Fida Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向银行业的NIDS基准数据集评估方法，通过衡量数据集对银行特有网络攻击行为的覆盖度，弥补实验室高准确率与真实部署效果之间的验证鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用NIDS数据集通常缺乏银行基础设施中的行业特征与威胁模式，例如支付报文系统、自动柜员机相关入侵活动。因此，即使模型在标准基准上表现良好，也可能无法有效识别真实银行环境中的关键风险。

Method: 研究将MITRE ATT&CK中的已记录对手行为映射至NIDS基准数据集，并依据NIST SP 800-94规定的传感器可观测性约束筛选网络层面可检测的行为。作者使用由四个先进大语言模型组成的共识引擎，对210项银行相关对手技术进行评估，得到68项网络可观测行为，并据此计算五个基准数据集的加权覆盖率及直接技术级证据比例。

Result: 在五个数据集中，UNSW-NB15的综合效用最高，加权覆盖率为82.2%；但其具有直接技术级证据的覆盖仅为18.4%，表明高覆盖中相当部分属于间接或推断性匹配。CIC-DDoS2019对核心银行行为存在89.9%的覆盖盲区，显示其对于银行场景的适配性较弱。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了可复现的行业感知型NIDS评估框架，强调评估不应仅依赖统计准确率，而应考察数据集对目标行业关键行为的可观测覆盖。结果表明，现有通用基准难以充分支持银行业NIDS验证，亟需构建包含银行原生业务流程、资产环境与攻击行为的数据集。

Abstract: The systemic criticality of global banking networks has ren-dered them high-priority targets for advanced persistent threats, neces-sitating Network Intrusion Detection Systems (NIDS) whose operational effectiveness must extend beyond statistical accuracy. However, a signif-icant validation gap persists between experimental NIDS performance and real-world effectiveness: NIDS models that achieve high accuracy on standard benchmarks often fail in operational banking environments because generic datasets lack sector-specific patterns, such as SWIFT and ATM-related intrusions, that characterize real financial threats. To address this, the paper investigates a sector-aware evaluation method-ology that systematically assesses how well existing NIDS benchmark datasets cover the attack behaviors most relevant to banking infrastruc-ture. The methodology maps documented adversary behaviors from the MITRE ATT&CK knowledge base to NIDS benchmarks while enforcing the realistic sensor limitations defined by NIST SP 800-94. Leveraging a multi-LLM consensus engine with four state-of-the-art models, we evalu-ated 210 banking-specific adversary techniques to derive a baseline of 68 network-observable behaviors for systematic coverage analysis. Results across five benchmark datasets demonstrate that UNSW-NB15 achieves the highest utility with an 82.2% weighted coverage score (though only 18.4% reflects direct, technique-level evidence), while CIC-DDoS2019 re-veals an 89.9% blind spot for core banking behaviors. These findings es-tablish a reproducible foundation for sector-aware NIDS evaluation and highlight the urgent need for banking-native datasets.

</details>


### [230] [A Decade of Healthcare Cyber Threats: Empirical Analysis, Evidence-Based Prioritisation, and AI Threat Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00901)
*Sadia Mehrin Rahi,Ayesha Siddika,Istiyak Hasan Maruf,Adrita Rahman Tory,Muhammad Aminur Rahaman,Khondokar Fida Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文基于2017—2024年医疗行业的1,214条多源威胁记录，刻画攻击行为演化、检测覆盖缺口及漏洞—行为关联，并提出42项优先检测技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗系统持续遭受网络威胁，但既有研究缺少跨MITRE ATT&CK、CISA已知被利用漏洞目录和NIST漏洞数据库的实证整合，难以系统判断攻击者战术迁移与防御资源错配。

Method: 研究筛选44个经验证的医疗行业威胁实体，整合三类权威数据源；按年度统计ATT&CK战术与技术使用变化；比较攻击技术关注度与现有检测指导覆盖度；将679个已确认遭利用的漏洞与主导行为技术进行收敛映射；据此筛选高优先级检测对象，并评估其与AI集成临床系统新兴威胁的对应关系。

Result: 防御规避在整个观察期始终是最主要战术，占技术使用量的15%—20%；持久化战术由11.2%降至零，初始访问由9.0%降至零，显示攻击活动向隐蔽化明显转移。检测覆盖最薄弱的技术反而获得最多攻击者关注，表明防御指导与实际威胁重心存在结构性失配。679个已利用漏洞可收敛至一个主导行为技术，形成可同时覆盖漏洞与行为层面的防御瓶颈点。研究最终识别42项可立即部署检测的高优先级技术，且其与AI集成临床系统面临的新兴攻击具有直接映射关系。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是用多源数据证明医疗网络攻击正从传统入口与驻留手段转向规避检测，并指出防御应从平均化覆盖转为优先补足高对手关注、低检测覆盖的技术缺口。漏洞利用与攻击行为之间的收敛关系可用于建立统一检测控制点；42项优先技术可作为医疗机构，尤其是部署AI临床系统机构的近期检测工程路线图。

Abstract: Healthcare systems face persistent and evolving cyber threats, yet how adversarial tactics and techniques have shifted over time has not been systematically characterised using empirical, multi-source data. This paper analyses 1,214 threat records drawn from three authoritative sources: the MITRE ATT&CK behavioural framework, the CISA Known Exploited Vulnerabilities catalogue, and the NIST vulnerability database, covering 44 validated healthcare-targeting threat entities from 2017 to 2024. We show that attacker behaviour has shifted measurably toward stealth-oriented tactics: defense evasion remained the dominant tactic throughout the observation period, consistently accounting for 15-20% of observed technique use from 2017 to 2024, while persistence declined from 11.2% to zero and initial access from 9.0% to zero over the same period. We further demonstrate that existing detection guidance is structurally misaligned with where attacker effort is concentrated, with the least-covered techniques receiving the most adversarial attention. A convergence analysis links 679 confirmed exploited vulnerabilities to a single dominant behavioural technique, identifying a common addressable chokepoint across the vulnerability and behavioural surfaces. Finally, we identify 42 high-priority techniques representing immediate detection opportunities and show that this set of techniques maps directly to emerging threats against AI-integrated clinical systems.

</details>


### [231] [Neuro-Symbolic Participation Governance for Verifiable AI Agents in Open Digital Twin Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00937)
*Juan Li,Wei Cai,Yan Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向协作式数字孪生环境的神经符号去中心化治理框架，用于验证自治AI智能体的身份、能力与策略合规性，并通过区块链智能合约实现可审计、隐私保护的跨机构协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统在跨机构、去中心化的高风险决策支持场景中，通常缺乏对智能体身份真实性、能力边界和机构政策遵循情况的可靠验证。这会削弱人机协作中的信任、问责与有效人工监督。

Method: 框架采用多层语义画像描述智能体，将神经网络的概率推理能力与符号化、确定性的机构治理规则结合；以形式化领域本体对智能体能力进行语义锚定，使其具备机器可解释、策略感知和上下文敏感的参与能力；由组织机构签发凭证，并通过基于区块链的智能合约验证凭证与访问规则，在不暴露敏感数据的条件下记录和审计参与过程。

Result: 在包含诊所、数字孪生和可穿戴设备提供方智能体的决策支持原型中，该方法能够阻止未授权交互并执行机构策略，且运行开销处于可管理范围。摘要未提供具体实验规模、性能指标、对比基线或安全攻击评测结果。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法与去中心化凭证治理相结合，可为跨机构数字孪生中的可信智能体协作提供可扩展路径。其主要价值在于把能力验证、政策执行、隐私保护和审计机制统一起来；但其实际普适性仍需通过更大规模部署、量化性能对比、凭证撤销机制及复杂策略冲突处理实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents, increasingly empowered by large language models, are becoming important components of human-machine systems for high-stakes decision support in digital twin ecosystems. However, existing multi-agent systems often lack robust verification for identity, capability, and policy compliance, especially in decentralized environments spanning multiple institutions. This paper proposes a neuro-symbolic decentralized governance framework for verifiable agents in collaborative digital twin environments. By representing agents through multi-layer semantic profiles, the framework bridges probabilistic neural reasoning with deterministic institutional governance, thereby supporting trustworthy human-AI collaboration and meaningful human oversight. Capabilities are grounded in formal domain ontologies to enable machine-interpretable, policy-aware, and context-sensitive participation. These credentials, issued by organizational authorities, are validated via blockchain-based smart contracts, ensuring auditable participation without exposing sensitive data. We demonstrate the framework using a decision-support prototype with clinic, digital twin, and wearable provider agents effectively prevents unauthorized interaction and enforces institutional policies with manageable overhead. Our findings suggest that neuro-symbolic decentralized governance provides a scalable and trustworthy pathway for safe human-machine collaboration across institutional boundaries.

</details>


### [232] [NoisePQC++: A Unified NIST-Compliant PQC and Hybrid-PQC Implementation of the Noise Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00954)
*Nadeem Ahmed,Aryya Gangopadhyay,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出NoisePQC++：一个基于C++23的Noise协议框架扩展实现，将NIST标准化的ML-KEM后量子密钥封装机制与传统ECDH结合，支持纯后量子、混合式及经典握手，以提升Noise生态在量子计算威胁下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Noise协议通常依赖ECDH密钥交换，而其安全性可能受到量子计算能力发展的影响。由于Noise被广泛用于安全通信系统，作者旨在为既有协议提供可部署、兼容性较强且具备前向安全性增强能力的后量子迁移方案。

Method: 作者实现统一的C++23库NoisePQC++，在Noise握手框架中集成ML-KEM和经典ECDH。该实现覆盖57种经典Noise握手模式、13种后量子握手及相应混合变体；支持纯PQC握手、ECDH与PQC结合的混合握手，以及混合前向保密机制。论文通过正常与不利网络条件下的性能测试，评估引入后量子机制后的开销。

Result: 实验表明，在常规网络环境中，后量子和混合握手带来的额外开销较小；在不利条件下开销仍处于可接受范围。相较既有工作，该方案的协议模式覆盖更全面、实现支持更完整，并能显著提升对量子攻击者的抵抗能力。

Conclusion: NoisePQC++说明，将NIST标准化后量子算法及混合密钥交换机制引入Noise协议具有工程可行性。其广泛的握手模式支持和可接受的性能代价，为Noise相关系统逐步部署后量子安全通信提供了可信基础；但摘要未给出具体延迟、带宽、计算成本及安全证明细节，实际选型仍需结合完整论文数据评估。

Abstract: The threat of quantum computers to classical public-key cryptography has created an urgent need to evolve secure communication protocols with post-quantum cryptographic (PQC) primitives. The Noise Protocol Framework, widely used in systems such as WireGuard and WhatsApp, traditionally relies on the Elliptic Curve Diffie-Hellman (ECDH) public-key exchange scheme, which is vulnerable to quantum threats. In this paper, we present NoisePQC++, a unified C++23 implementation of the Noise Protocol framework augmented with post-quantum Key Encapsulation Mechanisms and Hybrid Forward Secrecy. Our design integrates the National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) standardized ML-KEM algorithm alongside classical ECDH, enabling full PQC, hybrid ECDH+PQC handshakes, and unified support for all 57 classical Noise handshake pattern variants, 13 post-quantum Noise handshakes, and their hybrid variants. Compared with prior work, NoisePQC++ offers broader protocol coverage, more complete implementation support, and greater flexibility. Our evaluation shows minimal overhead under normal network conditions and acceptable overhead in adverse cases, while significantly improving resistance against quantum adversaries. These results indicate that NIST-standardized post-quantum and hybrid Noise handshakes are practical and provide a credible basis for future deployment.

</details>


### [233] [An AI Approach to Verified Production Cryptographic Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00965)
*Chuyue Sun,Su Fong,Zhiyi Kuang,Yizheng Jiao,Nina Narodytska,Haoze Wu,David L. Dill,Clark Barrett*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CryptoProver 是一个面向生产级密码库验证的 AI 系统，可在不修改可执行代码的前提下，从高层 API 契约自动合成内部规格与经 Verus 检查的证明，并已用于 curve25519-dalek 与 RustCrypto chacha20 的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的证明系统通常依赖人工预先提供完整规格、前提和局部证明目标，难以覆盖真实生产密码库中跨模块、内部不变量和实现细节复杂的验证需求。密码代码作为关键基础设施，对正确性要求极高，因此需要降低端到端形式化验证的人工成本。

Method: 系统以高层 API 契约和固定可信库为输入，自动生成内部函数规格、辅助引理及 Verus 证明。其采用“信任优先”设计：通过机械检查防止规格被弱化、禁止虚构公理、检测跨模块证明失效；同时以隔离机制阻止从代码仓库历史或既有参考证明中检索答案。可信基包括域规格、算术事实、公理与 Verus 标准库。

Result: CryptoProver 独立构造了 curve25519-dalek 的新证明，并依据 RFC 8439 验证了此前未验证的 RustCrypto chacha20 实现。对于 curve25519-dalek，在给定 API 契约和固定可信库的条件下，系统在 11.4 小时内完成内部规格与证明合成，记录的 API 成本为 466.99 美元。相比之下，公开进行的人类主导独立验证历时约八个月，由五名主要贡献者完成。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，经过严格可信约束的 AI 证明合成能够显著缩短生产密码库形式化验证的周期，并可从局部证明自动化扩展到端到端的库级验证。其核心贡献不仅是生成证明，还包括通过反规格弱化、反虚构公理和反参考检索机制，增强自动生成验证结果的可信度。

Abstract: Cryptographic code is critical infrastructure that must be correct, yet formally verifying production libraries remains difficult. Existing language-model proof systems solve isolated obligations with specifications and premises already given, leaving production-library verification unresolved.
  We present CryptoProver, an AI-based system that synthesizes internal specifications and Verus-checked proofs from high-level API contracts. Without changing executable code, CryptoProver constructs a new independent proof of curve25519-dalek and verifies RustCrypto's previously unverified chacha20 implementation against an RFC 8439 specification. These cryptographic lineages underpin deployed systems including Signal and Shadowsocks; Signal has an estimated 218M global downloads. The independent, human-led curve25519-dalek verification was developed publicly over eight months by five main contributors. Given the API contracts and a fixed trusted library of field specifications, arithmetic facts, axioms, and vstd, CryptoProver synthesizes the internal specifications and proofs in 11.4 hours with USD 466.99 in recorded API cost. CryptoProver follows a trust-first design principle: mechanical gates reject specification weakening, invented axioms, and cross-module breakage, while isolation blocks reference proof retrieval, including from git history.

</details>


### [234] [Inverting the Hidden: Unveiling Multimodal Privacy Leakage in Collaborative LVLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01020)
*Shuaifan Jin,Zhibo Wang,Qiyuan Wang,Yiting Han,Yajie Zhou,Yuanfan Zhang,Jiahui Hu,Xiaoyi Pang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示协同推理中传输的LVLM深层隐藏状态存在显著隐私泄露风险：攻击者可从这些中间表示中高精度恢复图像与文本信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同推理通常认为不传输原始输入即可保护隐私，但LVLM将视觉信息映射到语言嵌入空间后，其深层隐藏状态是否仍保留可恢复的敏感语义尚不明确。论文旨在验证这一风险并量化其严重性。

Method: 作者首先在正则性假设和正语义—干扰间隔条件下，理论证明与隐私相关的视觉语义在隐藏状态中仍具有可辨识性和稳定可恢复性。随后提出RASR攻击：通过分别逆向遵循图像和文本前向处理路径，获得粗粒度初始重建；再以隐藏状态一致性约束联合优化图像与文本重建，实现由粗到细的多模态恢复。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct和LLaVA-1.5-7B、共五个数据集上的实验显示，RASR相对最强基线将图像重建MSE降低约50%，文本恢复的最高词元准确率达到99%。结果表明，即使是深层LVLM隐藏状态，也包含足以恢复视觉和文本内容的信息。

Conclusion: 论文表明，协同LVLM推理中仅隐藏原始输入并不足以保证隐私；中间隐藏状态本身应被视为高风险泄露载体。该工作为隐藏状态保护、分割层选择、安全传输及隐私防御机制的设计提供了明确的实证与理论依据。

Abstract: Collaborative inference deploys Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) by partitioning computation between edge devices and the cloud. While withholding raw inputs supposedly ensures privacy, transmitting intermediate hidden states exposes a critical attack surface. However, it remains unclear whether deep-layer LVLM hidden states retain recoverable private information, given that visual content has been projected into the language embedding space. To address this concern, we theoretically analyze LVLM hidden-state recoverability and show that, under regularity assumptions and a positive semantic--nuisance margin, privacy-relevant visual semantics remain identifiable and stably recoverable. Motivated by this analysis, we propose RASR, a novel coarse-to-fine multimodal reconstruction attack. RASR obtains initial image and text reconstructions through modality-specific inverse paths that follow their respective forward processing pipelines in reverse, and then uses hidden-state consistency to refine both reconstructions. Evaluations on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and LLaVA-1.5-7B across five datasets demonstrate that RASR reduces image reconstruction MSE by \(\sim\)50\% compared to the strongest baselines, while achieving up to 99\% token accuracy for text recovery. These results show that privacy-sensitive visual and textual information can be recovered even from deep-layer LVLM hidden states, exposing the privacy risks of collaborative inference.

</details>


### [235] [CallScreenBench: Benchmarking On-Device Models as Phone Secretaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01033)
*Simiao Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出CallScreenBench，用于评估运行在手机端的小型量化语言模型充当“电话秘书”时的表现。核心发现是：模型能力提升会改善部分对话质量，但“来电分流/筛查”指标的表面提升主要是测量假象；加入脚本化退化基线并校正后，在预注册工作点上，15组模型比较中原本11组可区分的分流表现降为0组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准通常假设存在明确任务、合作用户和可验证的任务结果，但电话秘书面对的是未知来电者，其意图可能不可靠，系统必须在开场信息有限且没有外部真值的情况下代替机主作出回应。此场景的关键不在于完成任务，而在于机主是否认可代理的处置方式。因此，需要避免把单一自动指标误当作可靠的代理质量，并特别检验指标是否会被简单、无效的策略“刷高”。

Method: 作者构建CallScreenBench，从五个彼此独立的质量维度评估电话代理；每个维度均同时展示一个可能诱导错误优化的反向指标，且不将各维度平均为单一总分。论文还报告无工具、无凭据的代理的“谨慎性画像”。实验覆盖6个可在设备端运行的模型，参数规模为0.6B至4B，均采用4比特量化。为检验测量有效性，作者额外设计脚本化退化代理作为性能下限，例如直接挂断或机械复述来电者内容，并分析这些策略对各项指标及模型间比较结论的影响。

Result: 整体对话质量随模型能力增强而提高，但分流能力并未呈现可靠的能力扩展趋势。若直接采用原始指标，15个模型对中有11个在预注册操作点上显示出可分离的分流性能；在引入退化代理并进行校正后，可分离的模型对数量降至0。尤其是，简单地挂断或复述来电者的话也可获得满分的“留言保真度”，说明该指标无法独立代表有效的电话处置。结果表明，某些看似合理的自动化评分会奖励逃避、无响应或机械重复等非期望行为。

Conclusion: CallScreenBench的主要贡献不仅是提出电话秘书评测任务，更是揭示了此类开放式代理评估中的指标失效问题。论文主张以多维、非聚合式报告和退化基线压力测试替代单一总分或简单阈值判定，并明确不设定“通过/失败”门槛。对于后续研究，任何声称提升电话筛查能力的结果都应证明其指标不能被挂断、复述等退化策略获得高分。

Abstract: Language models small enough to run on a handset, quantized to a few bits, are increasingly capable of acting for their user, making on-device task automation newly plausible. One such task is answering the phone. A phone secretary takes an unknown inbound call on its owner's behalf. Unlike the agents evaluated by most benchmarks, it has no task to complete and no cooperative user: the caller holds the goal, may be an adversary, and must be judged from the opening turn with no oracle. What matters is not task success, but whether the owner would endorse how their proxy handled the call.
  We present CallScreenBench, which scores this setting on five quality dimensions. Each dimension is printed beside the counter-metric that bills it and is never averaged into a single number. We also report a guardedness profile for a toolless proxy that holds no credentials and calls no tools. Across six on-device models (0.6-4B parameters, 4-bit quantization), quality scales with capability, but triage does not. The appearance that it does is an artifact of measurement. Scripted degenerate agents supply the missing floors: after correcting for them, the number of model pairs whose triage performance separates falls from 11 of 15 to zero at the preregistered operating point. An agent that simply hangs up and echoes the caller also scores perfect message fidelity. We report which of our own metrics these floors defeat and declare no pass/fail threshold.

</details>


### [236] [Decoy Images Amplify Caption-Mediated Defenses Against Encoded Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01043)
*Haoyu Zhang,Xiangchen Guan,Shibo Zheng,Mohammad Zandsalimy,Shanu Sushmita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文发现：在视觉语言模型中，给编码型越狱文本附加一张与内容无关的诱饵图像，可能显著降低攻击成功率。该现象主要源于黑盒防御流水线在“是否存在图像”条件下发生分支，而非图像语义本身；但无条件添加图像会增加正常请求误拒，需依赖检测器进行门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将图像视为攻击载体，但本文关注图像输入与既有黑盒安全防御之间的非直观交互。作者旨在解释为何同一编码攻击文本在加入无关图像后会更难绕过防御，并评估这一机制能否形成实际可用的安全缓解措施。

Method: 作者在五个前沿视觉语言模型上，测试两类编码攻击与三种黑盒防御，重点考察基于图像描述的ECSO防御。实验比较纯文本编码输入与“文本加无内容诱饵图像”的攻击成功率，并以精确McNemar检验评估配对差异。控制实验包括：空白画布与自然照片诱饵、无额外审核层的三个开源权重模型，以及非符号化的语义编码器。进一步测量无条件加图像对良性请求拒答率的影响，并测试由轻量级编码输入检测器门控的图像附加策略；最后评估针对描述重检环节的自适应攻击。

Result: 对于文本单独输入几乎不改变攻击成功率的ECSO防御，在附加无关诱饵图像后，攻击成功率最高下降73个百分点；所有未饱和的比较均达到统计显著。空白图与自然照片均复现该结果，说明关键因素是图像存在而不是图像内容。该现象也出现在没有独立审核层的开源模型上，排除了单纯厂商过滤器造成该效应的可能；语义型编码器的复现则表明结果不依赖特定符号混淆方式。代价是，无条件附加图像会使良性拒答率升至20%至79%，较文本基线增加10至67个百分点。使用检测器仅对疑似编码攻击输入附加图像，可使良性拒答回落至文本基线，同时在检测器命中的样本上保留安全收益；其主要瓶颈是检测器召回率。面对针对描述重检的自适应攻击，效果虽减弱但仍存在。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是揭示视觉语言模型安全流水线存在由图像输入触发的条件性行为：图像本身即使不携带相关语义，也可改变编码攻击与防御机制的交互结果。证据支持“图像存在触发描述型防御分支”及“图像侧内在安全机制参与”的解释，但由于黑盒设定，作者无法直接验证厂商内部流水线。该方法不应被视为稳健、可直接部署的防御；在实践中，其安全效益高度依赖编码攻击检测器的召回能力，并可能被自适应攻击削弱。

Abstract: We report a counter-intuitive interaction between image inputs and existing black-box defenses on Vision--Language Models (VLMs): pairing an encoded jailbreak prompt with an unrelated decoy image can sharply lower attack success rate (ASR). The operative change is in the defense pipeline, not in the image. Across five frontier VLMs, two encoded-attack families, and three black-box defenses, a caption-mediated defense (ECSO) that leaves ASR essentially unchanged on text-only encoded input drops it by up to $73$pp once a content-free decoy is attached; every non-saturated contrast is significant under exact McNemar tests. We advance two hypotheses for this pattern, supported by indirect evidence rather than pipeline introspection, since a black-box threat model precludes inspecting vendor internals: caption-mediated defenses branch on image presence, and intrinsic image-side safety engages on image-resident content. Three controls constrain the explanation. Blank-canvas and natural-photograph decoys reproduce the effect on every model, implicating image presence rather than content; the effect replicates on three open-weight VLMs served with no moderation layer, so it is not a vendor-filtering artifact; and a non-symbolic, meaning-based encoder reproduces it, so it is not specific to symbolic obfuscation. Attaching a decoy unconditionally is not deployable --- it raises benign refusal to $20$--$79\%$, an inflation of $+10$ to $+67$pp --- but gating attachment on a lightweight encoded-input detector returns benign refusal to the text baseline while preserving the safety gain wherever the detector fires, making detector recall the binding constraint. Under adaptive attacks that target the caption-mediated re-check, the effect degrades but holds. We frame this as an observation about pipeline interaction, not as a robust defense.

</details>


### [237] [SoK: Intent-Oriented Systematization of Multi-Turn LLM Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01117)
*Siyuan Li,Aodu Wulianghai,Zehao Liu,Xi Lin,Qinghua Mao,Haoyu Li,Xiang Chen,Siyuan Liang,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向意图结构的多轮越狱攻击分类法，表明攻击效果主要取决于有害意图在多轮交互中的组织方式，而非上下文长度或查询次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将多轮越狱视为松散的提示模式集合，缺乏对攻击者如何跨轮次组织、推进和隐藏有害意图的系统分析；同时，基于单轮或单点的安全评估难以覆盖这类攻击。

Method: 作者构建了一个由四部分组成的意图导向分类体系，并通过受控消融实验比较不同意图组织方式的影响，进一步分析有害意图在不同交互范围内的可检测性。

Result: 实验显示，多轮越狱的有效性主要由意图是否被有策略地跨轮组织决定，而不是由对话上下文长度或查询数量决定。意图组织方式还决定了检测所需的范围：检测面可能从单轮扩展到整个会话，甚至跨越多个会话。

Conclusion: 仅依赖轮次级别的安全机制在结构上不足以应对多轮越狱，单点安全评估也会忽视意图的跨轮组织过程。因此，安全检测与评估协议应根据有害意图的可观测层级，覆盖单轮、会话级及跨会话级范围。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in interactive settings, where user intent commonly unfolds through multi-turn dialogue. Multi-turn jailbreaks exploit this pattern by advancing a harmful intent across turns, so that no single message exposes the full objective. However, existing work treats these attacks as a loose collection of prompt patterns and does not analyze how the adversary organizes and advances harmful intent across an interaction. We develop a four-part, intent-oriented taxonomy that organizes multi-turn jailbreaks by adversarial intent structure. Through controlled ablations, we find that effectiveness is driven by how deliberately intent is organized across turns rather than by context length or query count. We further show that the way intent is organized determines the level at which it becomes detectable, pushing the required detection surface outward from the turn level to the session level to the cross-session level. These findings indicate that turn-local safety mechanisms are structurally insufficient and that single-point evaluation overlooks how intent is organized, motivating evaluation protocols aligned to the level at which harmful intent becomes observable. The code is available at: https://github.com/SiyuanLi00/INTACT.

</details>


### [238] [Latency-Optimal Adaptive Split Inference for Privacy-Preserving Cloud-Edge-End Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01148)
*Yi Li,Peng Zhang,Man Ho Au*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向物联网终端的自适应分割推理框架：终端执行明文网络前缀并加密中间激活值，边缘与云端仅在全同态加密密文上执行后续网络，以在隐私保护条件下优化端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备算力和存储受限，难以在本地完整执行卷积神经网络；而将全部推理置于云端的全同态加密推理延迟较高。论文旨在利用终端、边缘和云的协同资源，在不泄露原始输入及中间数据、且私钥始终留在终端的前提下，降低加密推理时延。

Method: 方法将协同加密推理建模为分割点对选择问题：确定终端侧明文执行的切分位置，以及边缘侧密文执行结束、转交云端继续执行的位置。规划器联合估计终端明文前缀计算、激活加密、网络通信、边缘侧FHE计算和云侧FHE计算的开销，并在卷积层级与网络块级两种粒度下搜索延迟最优方案。终端作为信任锚点保留私钥，边缘和云均不接触明文数据。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和PathMNIST实验中，卷积层级协同分割方案相对于全云FHE推理实现约12.9倍的摊销端到端加速，相对于块级分割方案实现约3.9倍加速，并保持与对应明文模型一致的准确率。纳入建模通信开销后，其摊销延迟分别为CIFAR-10上的1033.279秒/样本和PathMNIST上的1023.429秒/样本。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，按卷积层进行细粒度自适应切分可显著优于粗粒度块级切分和全云FHE执行，为资源受限终端的隐私敏感推理提供了可行的端—边—云协同路径。但绝对延迟仍约为千秒量级，说明方案主要验证了相对性能优势，距离严格实时或低时延物联网应用仍需进一步降低FHE计算与通信成本。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) end devices are increasingly expected to support privacy-sensitive batch inference, yet their limited computational resources often make full local execution of convolutional neural networks impractical. This paper presents a latency-optimal adaptive split inference framework for privacy-preserving cloud-edge-end collaboration. The end device acts as the trust anchor, executes the plaintext model prefix, encrypts the split activation using fully homomorphic encryption (FHE), and keeps the secret key locally, while the edge and cloud execute assigned model segments only on FHE ciphertexts. We formulate collaborative encrypted inference as a split-pair selection problem over an end-side split point and an edge-side termination point. The proposed planner jointly models plaintext prefix execution, encryption, communication, edge-side FHE execution, and cloud-side FHE completion, and supports both convolution-level and block-level split granularities. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and PathMNIST show that the proposed convolution-level collaborative scheme achieves amortized end-to-end speedups of approximately 12.9 times over full-cloud FHE and 3.9 times over the block-level alternative, while preserving the corresponding plaintext-model accuracy. Including modeled communication, the amortized latencies are 1033.279 s/sample on CIFAR-10 and 1023.429 s/sample on PathMNIST.

</details>


### [239] [A Unified Benchmark for Privacy-preserving Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01192)
*Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Rafael Pires,Mathis Randl,Martijn de Vos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文对明文向量检索与四种隐私保护检索方案（SAP、EMVP、BNTM、Tiptoe）进行统一基准评测，表明不同方案在隐私、吞吐量、延迟和召回率之间形成帕累托前沿，无法简单视为统一的性能折损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加密向量检索方案通常在不同数据集、威胁模型、参数设置、硬件环境和指标口径下独立评估，导致性能数据不可直接比较。实践者因此难以依据可靠证据选择适合当前部署需求的方案。

Method: 作者在相同工作负载、硬件平台和度量定义下，比较Plaintext基线与SAP、EMVP、BNTM、Tiptoe四种密码学后端。评测维度覆盖隐私属性、吞吐量、查询延迟、召回率及GPU加速收益，并公开实验工件以支持复现。

Result: SAP的性能接近Plaintext；EMVP提供密码学不可区分性，但在CPU上吞吐量代价约为Plaintext的4倍；BNTM在EMVP基础上增加对恶意服务器的可验证性，但中位查询延迟进一步增加约22倍；Tiptoe能够隐藏簇选择信息，但单查询成本约为Plaintext的190倍。GPU对Plaintext和SAP有效，但对EMVP和BNTM的加速收益不明显。

Conclusion: 隐私保护向量检索方案不存在单一最优解：SAP适合强调接近明文性能的场景，EMVP适合需要较强保密性且可接受适度性能代价的场景，BNTM适合需要额外可验证性的场景，Tiptoe则适用于连访问的簇选择信息也必须隐藏的高隐私需求。统一基准为实际部署中的方案选型提供了可比较、可复现的依据。

Abstract: Vector search powers semantic search, recommendation systems, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). By design, the service answering a query sees both the query embedding and, usually, the corpus against which it is matched. This is a privacy breach for both the user issuing the query and the owner of the corpus. A family of cryptographic schemes (e.g., SAP, EMVP, BNTM, Tip-toe) addresses that leak. However, as each scheme is published and evaluated on its own corpus, threat model, parameter choices, hardware, and metric conventions, the numbers cannot be compared directly. Consequently, a practitioner asking which one to deploy today has no defensible way to choose. We close that gap with a uniform experimental comparison, including a Plaintext baseline and four cryptographic backends running over the same workload, hardware, and metric definitions. Under that ruler, the schemes spread across a Pareto frontier in privacy, performance, and recall rather than imposing a flat penalty on performance. We find that the performance of SAP matches Plaintext, EMVP delivers cryptographic indistinguishability at a 4x throughput cost on CPU, BNTM adds malicious-server verifiability at a further 22x median-latency cost, and Tiptoe hides the cluster choice itself, but incurs a 190x per-query cost compared to Plaintext. GPU acceleration pays off for Plaintext and SAP but not for EMVP or BNTM. All our experiment artifacts are publicly available for reproducibility

</details>


### [240] [The Boy Who Cried Wolf: Adversarial Misclassification of Safe Inputs as Unsafe in Multimodal Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01373)
*Shuo Shi,Rui Yin,Naen Xu,Jiahao Chen,Chunyi Zhou,Tianyu Du,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Zhaoxiang Wang,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“非安全诱导攻击”，研究攻击者通过对安全图像施加难以察觉的扰动，使多模态安全审查模型将正常请求误判为违规，从而造成服务可用性下降与用户信任受损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态安全研究主要关注攻击绕过审查、导致漏检的问题，而对攻击者刻意制造误报的威胁关注不足。作者指出，大规模误报会形成“狼来了”效应：合法请求频繁被拒绝，降低系统可用性，并削弱用户对安全机制的信任。

Method: 作者提出非安全诱导攻击框架，并设计Unsafe Semantic Distillation（USD）方法。USD不针对某一具体提示词优化扰动，而是使扰动在表征空间中接近非安全内容的分布特征，以提升其面对不同用户提示和现实使用场景时的泛化能力。

Result: 在四个先进多模态安全审查模型及模拟用户交互场景上的实验显示，USD的攻击成功率达到84%，优于现有攻击方法。结果表明，当前多模态安全架构对由视觉扰动引发的误报较为脆弱。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了多模态安全过滤器中此前被忽视的可用性失效模式：安全机制不仅可能被绕过，也可能被诱导为过度拦截。其核心贡献在于定义误报诱导威胁、提出具备跨提示词泛化能力的USD方法，并通过实验说明现有模型需要加强对对抗扰动和分布级语义偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal guard models have emerged as critical safety components for screening content in vision-language systems. While adversarial research has extensively studied jailbreaking attacks that produce false negatives, the inverse threat of inducing false positives on benign inputs remains unexplored. We introduce Unsafe Induction Attacks, where adversaries distribute imperceptibly perturbed safe images that trigger guard models to reject legitimate user requests, causing a "Boy Who Cried Wolf" effect that degrades service availability and erodes trust. This reveals an availability failure mode in deployed safety filters. To realize this threat under diverse user prompts, we propose Unsafe Semantic Distillation (USD), which aligns adversarial perturbations with distributional representations of unsafe content rather than prompt-specific instances. Evaluated on four state-of-the-art guard models across realistic user simulation scenarios, USD achieves 84% attack success rates, outperforming existing methods and exposing fundamental vulnerabilities in current multimodal safety architectures.

</details>


### [241] [AdaptoNet: Modular Foundation-Adaptive Neural Networks for Cyber-Physical Attack Detection in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01375)
*Anissa Elias,Jennifer Rogers,Hui Lin,Yan,Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究电力系统中的数据拒绝攻击场景，并提出AdaptoNet以提升测量缺失条件下的攻击检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动检测方法依赖完整测量数据。当攻击者通过线路故障与区域测量阻断相结合时，检测性能会显著下降，因此需要一种能够适应动态测量可用性的模型。

Method: AdaptoNet采用模块化神经网络结构：先在完整数据上训练并冻结基础模块，再训练自适应模块；该模块以二值测量可用性向量为条件输入，从而区分正常缺失、被拒绝数据与异常数据，无需重新训练基础模块。模型在IEEE 30、39、57和118节点系统上进行评估，并模拟区域内最多阻断20%测量值的条件。

Result: 数据拒绝与线路故障联合发生时，标准方法的检测性能下降超过86%，F1值低于12%。AdaptoNet可将F1恢复至81%以上，约为原方法的7倍，并接近完整测量条件下89%至99%的基线性能。

Conclusion: AdaptoNet通过显式建模测量可用性，显著增强了检测模型在部分数据不可用场景下的鲁棒性。其冻结基础模块加轻量自适应模块的设计降低了适配成本，适合处理电力系统中动态缺失测量造成的性能退化问题。

Abstract: Cyber attacks on the power grid combine physical disruptions with compromised data to destabilize cyber-physical systems. We demonstrate that data denial attacks, where adversaries block measurements in a targeted region while triggering a line outage, reduce detection performance by more than 86\%, rendering standard data-driven methods ineffective. We propose AdaptoNet, a modular neural network that adapts to measurement availability through conditional controls. AdaptoNet pairs a frozen foundational module trained on complete data with a trainable adaptive module, conditioned on a binary measurement-availability vector, enabling the model to distinguish between denied and anomalous data without retraining the foundational module. Evaluated across four IEEE test systems (30-, 39-, 57-, and 118-bus) under in-region attacks blocking up to 20% of measurements, AdaptoNet recovers F1 from below 12\% to above 81\%, an approximate sevenfold improvement approaching the 89%-99% baseline with complete measurements.

</details>


### [242] [Why Formal Monitors Fail: Attack Distribution Entropy as a Coverage Bound for LTL-Based LLM Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01388)
*Ruiyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出：基于固定不变式的有限状态自动机（FSA）运行时监控器，其攻击召回率主要受攻击序列模式分布的集中度限制，而非仅由模型能力、训练数据或提示词决定。攻击模式低熵且集中时，少量规则可获得较高覆盖；高熵且分散时，任何可处理规模的固定规则集都难以实现高覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LTL/FSA安全监控器在不同大模型架构上的攻击覆盖率差异显著，但既有解释无法由能力评分、训练数据或提示设计充分说明。论文旨在建立一个可解释这种跨架构性能差异的理论框架，并在部署前预测监控器是否适合特定模型后端。

Method: 作者将攻击表示为离散工具调用序列的“触发—完成”模式，并证明固定不变式FSA监控器的召回率上界由攻击分布中出现频率最高的k个模式所占质量决定，其中k对应监控器可容纳的规则或模式规模。论文以香农熵衡量模式分布的集中程度：低熵意味着少数模式占据大部分攻击，高熵意味着攻击分散于大量结构不同的模式。随后在八种前沿LLM架构上验证熵—覆盖关系，并提出利用小规模攻击样本估计熵、预测部署前监控覆盖率的测试方法。

Result: 实验显示，GPT类和DeepSeek后端的攻击分布高度集中，报告熵约为0.24比特，单一模式可覆盖96%的攻击，对应监控器召回率约68%至75%。Gemini变体的攻击分布更分散，熵约为2.81比特，存在7个簇且每个簇占比不超过7%，对应召回率仅约6%至13%；架构匹配的再训练并未改变这一现象。攻击熵可解释覆盖率方差的76%，相关系数为r=-0.87，p=0.005，95%置信区间为[-0.98,-0.78]；留一验证中相关系数处于[-0.91,-0.82]。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是给出固定不变式FSA监控器的熵—覆盖上界，并将监控失败归因于攻击模式的结构性多样性。其实际含义是：对高熵后端，不应预期通过增加少量固定规则或常规再训练获得高召回，而应采用更大规模、可适应或多样化的监控策略；对低熵后端，则可优先使用轻量级固定规则监控。需要注意的是，摘要中的结论依赖于攻击模式定义、样本代表性、k的实际可处理约束及八个架构上的评估设置，泛化到真实开放环境仍需完整论文中的理论假设与实验细节支持。

Abstract: Runtime safety monitors based on Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) and finite automata (FSA) are increasingly deployed to intercept unsafe tool-call sequences in LLM agents. Yet the same monitor achieves 68-75% attack coverage on some model architectures and near-zero on others, with no explanation from capability scores, training data, or prompt design. We provide the missing theory. We prove that the recall of any fixed-invariant FSA monitor is bounded above by the concentration of the attack distribution: the fraction of attacks covered by the k most frequent trigger-completion patterns. When attacks concentrate (low Shannon entropy), a small fixed invariant set achieves high recall; when they disperse across many structurally distinct patterns (high entropy), no fixed invariant set of tractable size can, regardless of how the invariants were derived. We validate this entropy-coverage bound across eight frontier LLM architectures. GPT-class and DeepSeek backends yield highly concentrated attacks (H ~ 0.24 bits; one pattern covers 96%), explaining 68-75% recall; Gemini variants yield high-entropy distributions (H ~ 2.81 bits; 7 clusters each <= 7%), explaining near-zero recall (6-13%), invariant to architecture-matched retraining. Entropy accounts for 76% of variance in coverage (Pearson r = -0.87, p = 0.005, 95% CI [-0.98, -0.78]), holding under leave-one-out (r in [-0.91, -0.82]). We introduce a pre-deployment entropy test that predicts monitor coverage from a small attack sample, enabling architecture-aware monitor selection before deployment. The bound and test are architecture-agnostic and apply to any FSA-based runtime monitor over discrete action sequences.

</details>


### [243] [Pivot: Proactive and Verifiable Threshold Oblivious Pseudorandom Functions From Isogeny Group Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01390)
*Abhinav Sharma,Vikas Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PIVOT提出一种无可信经销商的主动式同态门限可验证盲PRF框架，通过周期性刷新服务器份额来抵抗移动攻击者，同时保持主密钥、公钥及历史OPRF输出不变，并支持委员会重共享和公开可验证追责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于同源映射的门限OPRF多面向静态腐化模型。若服务器份额长期不变，移动攻击者可在不同时段逐步获得足够多的同一轮份额，从而恢复主密钥。因此，需要一种适用于长期运行服务的主动安全机制，在不影响既有输出和公开参数的情况下更新份额。

Method: PIVOT基于有效同源映射群作用构建无经销商门限VOPRF。其核心结合Shamir秘密共享、加法同态系数承诺、按拉格朗日权重顺序执行的群作用，以及将认证份额与相应同源映射动作关联的联合零知识关系。系统通过协调的纪元切换完成主动份额刷新，并提供安全擦除、公开可验证归责，以及可改变门限参数的委员会重共享。论文进一步定义长期主动门限VOPRF功能，并在向量化假设和one-more hidden-group-action假设下进行基于模拟的安全证明。

Result: 该方案证明了分布式密钥生成、门限求值、主动刷新和委员会重共享的正确性与安全性。份额刷新不会改变主密钥、公钥或已生成的OPRF输出，因此依赖既有输出的应用状态无需重新计算。作为应用，论文给出了分布式隐私查询服务，其中加密数据库可在多次份额更新和委员会迁移后持续有效。

Conclusion: PIVOT将主动安全机制引入基于同源映射的门限VOPRF，使长期部署能够抵抗跨时期累积份额的移动攻击。其主要贡献是同时实现份额无关的长期稳定输出、可验证刷新与追责、以及灵活的委员会迁移；实际性能、通信开销和具体参数下的实现效率仍需结合全文实验进一步评估。

Abstract: Oblivious pseudorandom functions (OPRFs) allow a client to evaluate a keyed pseudorandom function on a private input without revealing that input to the server. In a threshold OPRF, the secret key is distributed among (n) servers so that any qualified set of at least (t) servers can complete an evaluation, while fewer than (t) shares reveal no information about the key. Existing isogeny-based threshold OPRFs, however, are primarily designed for static corruption models. If the same shares remain valid throughout the lifetime of the service, a mobile adversary can compromise different servers over time, accumulate (t) shares from the same sharing state, and eventually recover the master key. We introduce PIVOT (Proactive Isogeny-based Verifiable Oblivious Threshold PRF), a dealerless threshold VOPRF framework based on effective isogeny group actions. PIVOT periodically refreshes the server shares without changing the master key, public key, or previously generated OPRF outputs. The construction combines Shamir secret sharing, additively homomorphic coefficient commitments, sequential Lagrange-weighted group actions, and joint zero-knowledge relations that link certified shares to their corresponding isogeny actions. It also supports coordinated epoch transitions, publicly verifiable blame, secure erasure, and committee resharing under a possibly different threshold. We formalize the functionality of a long-lived proactive threshold VOPRF, prove the correctness of distributed key generation, threshold evaluation, proactive refresh, and committee resharing, and provide a simulation-based security analysis under the vectorization and one-more hidden-group- action assumptions. As an application, we describe a distributed private lookup service whose encrypted database remains valid across repeated share renewals and committee migrations.

</details>


### [244] [How Benchmarks and Evaluation Protocols Shape Conclusions in Provenance-Based Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01454)
*Lorenzo Guerra,Thomas Chapuis,Guillaume Duc,Pavlo Mozharovskyi,Van-Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统复评了多种基于溯源的入侵检测系统，发现其性能结论高度依赖数据集与评测协议；在多数测试集上，基于可执行文件名和路径的简单允许列表可匹配或超过学习式基线，说明既有指标可能主要反映词汇新颖性，而非溯源架构能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIDS常报告较强效果，但不同工作采用的数据划分、阈值设定、检查点选择及标注质量不一致，使架构优劣难以公平比较。研究旨在通过经过审计、标注和校准要求筛选的公开数据集，检验代表性PIDS的架构性主张是否具有稳健的实证支持。

Method: 作者重点使用经审计的DARPA TC E3数据集，制定统一评测流程：测试时间段与训练阶段严格分离，模型检查点与告警阈值仅可通过验证集校准。评估同时覆盖告警层面的排序/操作点指标，以及进程级上下文和节点恢复等调查实用性指标；并以训练集中可执行文件名称和路径构建简单允许列表作为非学习基线。最后，利用特征完整性与字段熵衡量数据集的语义信号质量。

Result: 告警成功率与调查价值并不一致：部分系统能够发现攻击，但无法提供足够的进程级上下文以支持取证。在四个主要数据集中的三个上，简单允许列表在关键操作点指标上达到或优于所选学习式基线。特征完整性和字段熵分析表明，若数据集语义信号不足，其更容易揭示是否存在异常告警，却难以有效区分不同模型架构。Theia数据集具有最强的语义信号质量，并使参考模型在排序和节点级恢复方面呈现最清晰的改进。

Conclusion: PIDS的架构性能主张不能脱离基准数据属性和评测协议单独解释。未来评测应采用时间隔离的测试集、仅基于验证集的校准策略，并同时报告告警性能与调查/取证效用；还应设置简单词汇或路径基线，以确认模型收益确实来自溯源语义建模而非对名称、路径等新颖性的利用。

Abstract: Provenance-based intrusion detection systems (PIDS) frequently report strong performance, but the conclusions drawn from these results can be highly sensitive to benchmarking choices and evaluation protocols. We investigate this dependency by re-evaluating representative PIDS on public datasets that meet our audit, labeling, and calibration requirements. Focusing primarily on the audited DARPA TC E3 datasets, we apply a unified protocol with temporally separated test periods and validation-only checkpoint and threshold calibration, and ask which architectural claims are empirically supported. We find that alerting success and investigation utility can diverge sharply, as several systems surface attacks without providing enough process-level context to support forensic investigation. On three of the four primary datasets, a simple allowlist built from training executable names and paths matches or exceeds the selected learned baselines on key operating-point metrics, suggesting that much of their measured performance reflects lexical novelty rather than richer provenance modeling. To explain why only some datasets expose architectural differences, we measure semantic signal quality through feature completeness and field entropy. This analysis helps explain why several audited E3 datasets can expose alerting behavior without reliably separating model architectures, while Theia pairs the strongest semantic signal quality with the clearest improvements in ranking and node-level recovery by our reference model. These results show that architectural claims in PIDS should be interpreted together with the benchmark properties and evaluation protocol that produced them.

</details>


### [245] [SP2UBI: Secure and Privacy-Preserving Usage-Based Insurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01539)
*Mobin Aghamirkarimi,Matin Aghamirkarimi,Farid Zaredar,Morteza Amini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SP2UBI，一种面向使用型车险的隐私保护方案，通过粗粒度统计数据、环上全同态加密和ISAC辅助测速验证，实现投保人与保险机构之间的双向机密性及传感器数据完整性保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统车险依赖静态参数，而使用型车险需要分析高分辨率车联网数据，容易泄露驾驶习惯和移动轨迹。现有方案还存在数据生命周期泄露、传感器欺骗或故障难以检测、审计过程损害隐私以及预言机问题等不足。

Method: SP2UBI仅收集不含时空标识符的粗粒度统计远程信息，降低重构细粒度轨迹的可能性；利用Torus全同态加密直接在密文上计算风险因子，同时隐藏用户数据和保险公司的风险模型参数；结合集成感知与通信技术构建测速验证机制，以隐私保护方式检测传感器层面的数据操纵。

Result: 实验表明，SP2UBI单轮协议执行时间为41.2毫秒，具有较低计算开销。与现有方案相比，该方法在保持轻量化的同时提供了更强的隐私保护和数据完整性保障。

Conclusion: SP2UBI有效融合了隐私保护计算与传感器数据验证，能够在不暴露敏感驾驶信息及保险定价模型的情况下支持使用型车险风险评估，并提升对传感器异常和欺诈行为的抵抗能力。

Abstract: The transition from traditional auto insurance, whose basis is defined by a set of static parameters, like the age of driver and number of accidents, to Usage-Based Insurance, whose pricing is based on driving behavior, was boosted by Intelligent Transportation Systems. At the same time, the analysis of high-resolution telematics data might reveal users' behavior and habits, which is associated with significant privacy concerns. The majority of privacy-preserving UBI systems are subject to policyholders' data leaks at some point during the life cycle of their protocol. Moreover, it is challenging to ensure data integrity against possible intentional or unintentional sensor anomalies (like sensor spoofing or malfunctioning) since the approaches used in this case require privacy-compromising audits and do not consider the oracle problem. In order to address the issues, we propose SP2UBI, a privacy-preserving UBI solution which guarantees mutual confidentiality. SP2UBI collects the telematics data in coarse-grained, statistical form without any spatiotemporal identifiers so that it is impossible to reconstruct fine-grained mobility traces. By utilizing the Torus Fully Homomorphic Encryption scheme, which incurs low computational overhead, computations are performed directly over encrypted data. This way, the insurer calculates risk factors without accessing any sensitive information, while parameters of its risk model are confidential. In order to protect data integrity against sensor level manipulation, SP2UBI incorporates a speed verification system assisted by Integrated Sensing and Communications (ISAC) technology which is capable of detecting fraud while preserving user privacy. Experimental evaluation shows that one round of protocol execution takes 41.2 ms, showing that our framework is lightweight and preserves more privacy guarantees compared to state-of-the-art solutions.

</details>


### [246] [From Viral to Void: Multi-Dimensional Behavioral and Contractual Analysis for Rug Pull Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01609)
*Jinyin Song,Hongping Wang,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向以太坊 Meme 币跑路诈骗的多维风险检测框架，并以 MLP 为核心模型结合类别不平衡处理与阈值优化，实现链上风险识别和可视化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meme 币跑路诈骗具有部署成本低、隐蔽性强、资金转移快和人工审查困难等特点。现有方法常依赖单一维度特征，且在样本类别不平衡、泛化能力和结果可解释性方面存在不足，难以满足实时预警需求。

Method: 研究首先界定诈骗的定义、类型及危害机制；随后从恶意合约设计、链上交易异常、流动性操纵和社交媒体披露等维度构建特征体系，并通过 BOBU 案例复盘攻击路径、提取量化指标。模型部分采用多层感知机，结合 SMOTE 过采样和 Focal Loss 缓解正负样本不均衡，利用动态阈值搜索平衡精确率与召回率，并通过梯度裁剪和早停提高训练稳定性。最终基于 Flask 开发支持批量检测、风险排序和结果导出的网页系统。

Result: 测试集上，模型取得 0.927 的准确率、0.787 的 F1 值和 0.952 的 AUC-ROC，摘要声称其性能优于传统方法。系统层面实现了检测结果可视化及高风险对象排序，具备一定实际应用形式。

Conclusion: 该工作将合约、交易、流动性及外部信息结合，为 Meme 币跑路风险识别提供了较完整的端到端方案。其主要贡献在于多维特征设计、面向类别不平衡的训练策略和工程化系统实现。摘要仍未说明数据集规模与时间跨度、标签获取方式、基线模型及统计显著性、跨市场或跨时期验证结果，以及社交媒体特征的采集可靠性；因此，模型泛化能力、真实实时性和可解释性仍需通过更严格的外部验证加以确认。

Abstract: As the blockchain and decentralized finance (DeFi) ecosystems continue to expand and mature, rug pull scams involving meme coins are occurring with increasing frequency, posing a threat to the security of investors' assets and the healthy development of the industry. Rug Pull scams are characterized by extremely low deployment costs, covert execution, rapid fund transfers, and high detection difficulty. Traditional manual reviews or fixed rules struggle to meet real-time early warning requirements, and existing detection methods generally suffer from issues such as a single feature dimension, inadequate handling of class imbalance, and weak model generalization and interpretability. To address these shortcomings, this paper focuses on the detection of Ethereum-based rug pull scams. First, we clarify their definitions, types, and harm mechanisms, and construct a multi-dimensional feature system based on dimensions such as malicious smart contract design, on-chain transaction anomalies, liquidity manipulation, and social media disclosures. Next, using the "Second Uncle Coin"(token symbol: BOBU) case as an example, we reconstruct the attack process and derive quantitative detection metrics. Subsequently, a risk detection model based on a Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) is designed. We employ a combined strategy of SMOTE oversampling and Focal Loss to address the issue of sample imbalance, dynamically search for optimal thresholds to balance precision and recall, and incorporate gradient pruning and early stopping to enhance training stability. Experiments show that the model achieves an accuracy of 0.927, an F1 score of 0.787, and an AUC-ROC of 0.952 on the test set, outperforming traditional methods. Finally, a visualizable web-based detection system is developed using the Flask framework, enabling batch risk assessment, high-risk ranking display, and result export functions.

</details>


### [247] [Mutate to Bypass: Autonomous Endpoint Evasion via Knowledge-Driven Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01639)
*Weifeng Yuan,Wenbo Guo,Qingyun Du,Jun Chen,Feng Dong,Haoyu Wang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出AutoBypass，一个基于知识库与多智能体闭环推理的自动化框架，用于评估商业EDR产品对公开已知规避技术的韧性。实验表明，该系统在七个商业终端安全平台上均实现规避，并显著提升了开源小模型的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开报告、威胁情报和开源概念验证代码中已包含大量EDR规避知识，但这些信息通常零散、难以直接执行。现有自动化工具也缺少从检测告警、执行失败和遥测反馈中持续修正策略的能力，因此难以系统化评估EDR对已公开攻击技术的实际防护强度。

Method: AutoBypass构建检测感知知识库，将威胁情报、专家分析及开源概念验证材料组织为规避技术与操作约束。多智能体据此进行攻击规划、生成多态代码并编译可执行文件；随后，遥测驱动推理模块根据告警和失败信号诊断原因，将修正证据反馈至后续策略选择，形成“知识检索—生成执行—遥测诊断—策略迭代”的闭环。

Result: 在七个商业终端安全平台的测试中，AutoBypass对所有目标均成功实现规避。文中报告其针对Windows Defender的规避率为90%，针对Trend Micro AV为86.7%。消融实验显示，引入知识库后，8B参数级开源模型的成功率由27%–53%提升至43%–83%，性能接近大型专有模型。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，公开安全知识可以通过结构化知识表示、智能体协作和遥测反馈，被转化为持续化、自动化的EDR韧性评估能力。其主要贡献在于证明了知识库对较小开源模型的显著增益，以及闭环机制对评估流程可执行性和适应性的提升；但结果也表明，终端防护产品需要针对公开规避知识、代码变体及行为链路加强持续检测与验证。

Abstract: Public reports and open-source resources expose many EDR evasion techniques, but it remains unclear whether commercial Endpoint Detection and Response (EDR) systems can withstand these documented attacks. Evaluating them requires turning fragmented security knowledge into working payloads and refining those payloads from opaque alerts, tasks that existing automation does not address. We present AutoBypass, a knowledge-grounded, closed-loop multi-agent framework for automated EDR resilience assessment. A Detection-Aware Knowledge Base structures threat intelligence, expert analyses, and open-source proofs of concept into evasion techniques and operational constraints. Agents use this knowledge to plan attacks, generate polymorphic code, and compile binaries, while a telemetry-driven reasoning engine diagnoses failures and feeds corrective evidence back into the strategy. Across seven commercial endpoint security platforms, AutoBypass bypassed every target, reaching 90% evasion against Windows Defender and 86.7% against Trend Micro AV. Ablations show that the knowledge base raises the success rates of 8B open-weight models from 27--53% to 43--83%, bringing them close to large proprietary models. These results demonstrate a systematic way to operationalize public security knowledge for continuous, automated assessment of EDR resilience.

</details>


### [248] [Performance analysis of Machine learning algorithms for predicting malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01642)
*ABM. Adnan Azmee,Pranto Protim Choudhury,Md. Aosaful Alam,Orko Dutta,Muhammad Iqbal Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习的恶意软件检测框架，并比较ANN、SVM、XGBoost和Extra Trees等分类算法。实验表明，XGBoost表现最佳，准确率达到98.62%，同时实现了基于Flask的实时客户端-服务器检测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于特征码的杀毒软件难以及时识别新型恶意软件，通常需要等待特征码更新，因此存在检测滞后和系统暴露风险。研究旨在利用机器学习提升恶意软件检测的准确性、及时性与可扩展性。

Method: 使用基准恶意软件数据集，训练并比较人工神经网络、支持向量机、XGBoost和Extra Trees Classifier等模型，以区分恶意软件与良性应用程序。在此基础上，采用Flask开发实时客户端-服务器检测系统，用于对可执行文件进行在线分类。

Result: XGBoost取得最佳检测效果，准确率为98.62%，优于其他评估模型。研究还完成了可实际运行的实时检测系统，能够对可执行文件进行恶意或良性判定。

Conclusion: 先进机器学习算法能够有效提升恶意软件检测性能，其中XGBoost展现出较强的分类能力。结合实时服务架构，该方法为构建智能化、可扩展的恶意软件检测解决方案提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Malware poses a persistent and evolving threat to modern computing systems, making accurate and timely detection a critical cybersecurity challenge. Traditional signature-based antivirus solutions often fail to identify newly emerging malware, leaving systems vulnerable until updated signatures become available. To address this limitation, this study proposes a machine learning-based malware detection framework capable of distinguishing malicious software from benign applications with high accuracy. Several state-of-the-art classification algorithms, including Artificial Neural Networks (ANN), Support Vector Machines (SVM), XGBoost, and Extra Trees Classifier, were evaluated and compared using a benchmark malware dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that XGBoost achieved the best performance, attaining an accuracy of 98.62%, outperforming the other evaluated models. To demonstrate the practical applicability of the proposed approach, a real-time client-server malware detection system was also developed using the Flask framework, enabling efficient classification of executable files as malicious or benign. The findings highlight the effectiveness of advanced machine learning techniques for enhancing malware detection and contribute toward the development of intelligent and scalable cybersecurity solutions.

</details>


### [249] [MNC: Scope-Bound Semantic Declassification for Private LLM-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01719)
*Jinghan Xu,Longze Fan,Zeyuan Wang,Xinjin Li,Hankai Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“最小必要通信”（MNC）协议，用于约束多智能体大语言模型系统中敏感信息的语义化披露及其后续使用。其核心贡献是将信息披露与接收者、用途、转发、保存期限、日志和记忆等可执行范围绑定，并通过参考监控器实施约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私提示、脱敏和访问控制主要限制表层文本或原始数据访问，难以解决“已获合法信息的智能体应披露多少，以及接收方可如何继续使用”的问题。多智能体系统中的内部消息、工具参数、日志和长期记忆均可能形成隐私泄露路径；即使公开输出安全，重复通信也会累积推断风险。

Method: 作者设计了带类型的语义降密协议MNC：从应用预先定义的候选披露集合中选择满足任务需求的最小披露，并为该披露附加接收者、目的、可否转发、生命周期、日志记录和记忆存储范围。参考监控器在后续通信、工具调用、日志操作和记忆检索中执行这些范围限制；历史感知扩展则考虑多次披露所导致的累积推断风险。实验覆盖语义连接、记忆、探测和长期交互场景，并在两个骨干模型的MAGPIE执行环境中验证信息在规划、工具使用、协作和记忆检索中的传播。

Result: 实验显示，传统防护方案虽然能够维持协议层面的任务效用，但仍会暴露明显的额外推断信号。在接收文本完全相同的条件下，MNC可保证授权接收，同时阻止仅依赖文本语义降密方法无法阻止的未授权转发、日志写入、持久化存储以及过期后的记忆检索。多智能体执行结果进一步表明，受控披露会实际影响后续规划、工具调用、协同过程与记忆使用。

Conclusion: 论文认为，隐私保护不应仅依赖文本内容过滤或数据访问限制，而应将语义披露视为具有用途和生命周期的受控能力。MNC通过“任务充分但最小”的披露选择及跨操作范围强制执行，为私有多智能体大语言模型系统提供了更具实践性的通信边界；其有效性依赖于候选披露集合的正确设计、参考监控器的完整覆盖以及对长期推断风险的持续建模。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems can expose protected state through internal messages, tool arguments, logs, and persistent memory even when their public outputs appear innocuous. Existing privacy prompts, redaction methods, and source-level access controls restrict surface content or data access, but do not specify what a legitimately informed agent should disclose or how that disclosure may be reused downstream. We introduce Minimum-Necessary Communication (MNC), a typed semantic-declassification protocol that selects a task-sufficient disclosure from an application-authored candidate family and binds it to explicit recipient, purpose, forwarding, lifetime, logging, and memory scopes. A reference monitor enforces these scopes across subsequent operations, while a history-aware extension accounts for inference risk accumulated over repeated disclosures. Controlled semantic-join, memory, probing, and longitudinal experiments show that conventional defenses can preserve protocol-level utility while exposing substantial additional inference signal. Under identical receipt text, MNC preserves authorized delivery while blocking unauthorized forwarding, logging, durable storage, and retrieval after expiration that a text-only semantic declassifier permits. Two-backbone MAGPIE executions further show that mediated disclosures propagate through subsequent planning, tool use, coordination, and memory retrieval. These results support scope-bound semantic declassification as a practical communication boundary for private LLM-agent systems.

</details>


### [250] [Benign Alone, Harmful Together: Exploiting Experience Composition in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01759)
*Bingyu Yan,Xiaoming Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Ziyi Zhou,Yirui Qi,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出EvoBreak，一种针对自进化大语言模型智能体的序列化经验攻击方法。其核心发现是：单条经验看似无害，但跨会话积累、组合后可能共同削弱智能体的安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆攻击通常依赖直接访问智能体记忆，或写入显式恶意内容，因此隐蔽性和现实适用性有限。作者关注一种更隐蔽的风险：攻击者通过正常任务交互诱导智能体形成多条良性经验，并在后续利用这些经验的组合效应触发不安全行为。

Method: EvoBreak以经验为条件进行多阶段自适应攻击：首先观察受害智能体从交互中蒸馏出的经验；随后识别目标需求中尚未被覆盖的部分；再通过表面良性的阶段性任务诱导补充经验；最后重写查询，使已积累的互补经验被联合激活。为训练攻击策略，论文构建BreakGym，采用结构优先的数据合成流程生成具有不同依赖关系、可分解的安全敏感目标；优化方面结合拒绝采样监督微调与Hint-guided GRPO。

Result: 实验覆盖多种自进化框架、不同受害模型骨干、预演化领域及安全基准。结果表明，EvoBreak相较已有攻击方法具有更高的攻击效果，同时能保持较高的阶段任务良性程度，说明其不依赖明显恶意输入或直接篡改记忆。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了自进化智能体中的“良性经验组合”攻击面：安全评估不能只检查单条记忆或单轮输入是否有害，还应评估经验之间的长期依赖、跨会话组合和最终查询对历史经验的联合激活。该工作强调应加强经验写入审计、跨经验关联风险检测、持久记忆隔离及长期安全评测。

Abstract: Self-evolving large language model agents improve their capabilities by distilling interaction trajectories into persistent experiences. Yet this mechanism introduces a new safety risk: experiences that are benign in isolation may jointly weaken an agent's safety boundary when accumulated and reused across sessions. Existing memory attacks typically require direct memory access or induce explicitly malicious records, limiting their stealthiness and applicability. We propose EvoBreak, an experience-conditioned sequential attack that operates through individually benign attack-stage tasks and induced experiences. EvoBreak repeatedly observes the experiences distilled by the victim, identifies uncovered target-relevant requirements, and adaptively acquires complementary experiences before reformulating the final query to activate them jointly. To support training, we introduce BreakGym, a structure-first synthesis pipeline that generates decomposable safety-sensitive targets with diverse dependency structures. EvoBreak is optimized using rejection-sampling supervised fine-tuning and Hint-guided GRPO. Experiments across self-evolving frameworks, victim backbones, pre-evolution domains, and safety benchmarks demonstrate that EvoBreak consistently outperforms existing attacks while maintaining high benignness. These results reveal benign experience composition as a persistent attack surface in self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [251] [GalSAS-SDR-SIM: An End-to-End Simulation Platform for Galileo Signal Authentication Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01901)
*Haiyang Wang,Yuanyu Zhang,Wensen Du,Ji He,Pinchang Zhang,Ning Xi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出开源SDR仿真平台GalSAS-SDR-SIM，用于模拟Galileo信号认证服务（SAS），支持E1频段OSNMA与E6频段扩频码认证（SCA）的协同生成、验证及性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实且可控的Galileo SAS信号目前获取有限，制约了兼容接收机的早期研发，以及认证机制的可复现实验与性能研究。

Method: 平台将E6扩频码加密过程与E1 OSNMA密钥披露机制耦合，模拟SAS完整认证流程；支持灵活配置码加密参数，以适配不同算力的接收机；可按用户定义的位置和时间并发生成Galileo E1、E5b和E6信号，并支持OSNMA跨卫星配置。实现遵循公开的官方技术规范。

Result: 实验表明，平台能够实现导航电文认证与扩频码认证的同步验证。作者还在不同SAS配置下评估了认证性能及计算资源开销，证明该平台可用于比较配置策略与接收机实现代价。

Conclusion: GalSAS-SDR-SIM为Galileo SAS兼容接收机开发提供了可控、开放且可重复的测试环境，也为研究人员评估和改进卫星导航信号认证技术提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Galileo is developing a Signal Authentication Service (SAS) that integrates Open Service Navigation Message Authentication (OSNMA) on the E1 band with Spreading Code Authentication (SCA) on the E6 band to strengthen its resilience to spoofing attacks. As Galileo SAS is still under development, access to realistic and controllable SAS signals remains limited, hindering both the early development of compatible receivers and reproducible research on signal authentication. To bridge this gap, this paper presents GalSAS-SDR-SIM, an open-source software-defined radio (SDR) simulation platform that emulates the SAS workflow by coupling E6 code encryption with the OSNMA key-disclosure process. The platform allows flexible SAS configuration of code encryption parameters to accommodate receivers with different computational capabilities. It also supports the concurrent generation of Galileo E1, E5b, and E6 signals for user-defined locations and times, and OSNMA cross-satellite configurations. Experimental results demonstrate simultaneous verification of navigation messages and spreading codes. We further evaluate SAS authentication performance and computational resource costs under different SAS configurations. Implemented according to publicly available official specifications, GalSAS-SDR-SIM provides a practical tool for accelerating SAS-capable receiver development and supporting the research community in evaluating and improving Galileo signal-authentication techniques.

</details>


### [252] [D-MUTRA: DLT-based MUTual Remote Attestation for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01938)
*Adam Zahir,Vincent Lefebvre,Mark Angoustures,Milan Groshev,Carlos J. Bernardos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出D-MUTRA，一种面向多智能体系统的区块链互认证远程证明框架，使智能体在运行期间相互验证完整性，并通过智能合约实现去中心化、透明且可审计的证明协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统远程证明通常仅覆盖启动或应用加载阶段，难以持续发现运行期的软件篡改；同时其依赖中心化可信验证者或专用安全硬件，且证明记录缺乏透明审计能力。这些局限不适合需要持续协作与相互信任的分布式多智能体环境。

Method: D-MUTRA采用纯软件实现，由两部分构成：其一，Security-as-a-Service为各智能体注入轻量级运行时测量与验证能力；其二，区块链智能合约负责协调互认证流程、记录证明状态并提供可审计性。每个智能体同时承担证明者与验证者角色，在测量自身运行时完整性的同时验证其他智能体。作者基于Hyperledger Besu私有以太坊网络实现原型，并在ROS与Gazebo构建的机器人群体仿真场景中评估。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能够支持智能体持续相互证明，识别恶意软件修改，并可扩展至较大规模部署。摘要声称其对受保护应用的性能开销可以忽略，但未给出具体的延迟、吞吐量、资源消耗或规模上限等量化指标。

Conclusion: D-MUTRA将运行时完整性验证、对等互认证和区块链审计机制结合，为多智能体系统提供了不依赖专用安全硬件的去中心化信任建立方案。其主要贡献在于将远程证明从中心化、阶段性检查扩展为透明的持续协作过程；后续仍需通过真实硬件环境、对抗性网络条件及更完整的性能数据验证实际适用性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) comprise autonomous software agents that collaborate to perform complex tasks in critical cyber-physical domains, including multi-robot coordination and the Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT). In such distributed environments, a compromised agent may execute modified software while appearing trustworthy, causing other agents to act on false information and corrupting the mission. Agents must therefore establish and maintain mutual trust throughout operation. Remote attestation (RA) is a well-established technique for this purpose, enabling a remote verifier to assess the integrity of a potentially compromised prover device. However, conventional RA approaches face significant limitations in MAS: integrity guarantees are restricted to boot or application-load time, designs rely on centralized trusted verifiers or security hardware, and attestation records lack transparency and auditability. To address these limitations, this paper presents D-MUTRA, a blockchain-based framework that introduces a mutual RA protocol in which agents measure their runtime integrity while verifying that of their peers, acting as both prover and verifier. The framework operates entirely in software and relies on two components: a Security-as-a-Service that instruments agents with lightweight measurement and verification capabilities, and a smart contract that coordinates the attestation protocol in a decentralized and transparent manner. We implement a proof-of-concept on a private Ethereum blockchain using Hyperledger Besu and evaluate it in a swarm robotics scenario built with Robot Operating System (ROS) and the Gazebo simulator. Results show that D-MUTRA enables agents to continuously attest one another, detects malicious software modifications, and scales to large deployments with negligible overhead on protected applications.

</details>


### [253] [Measuring Post-Quantum TLS Deployment Across UK Internet Sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02147)
*Konstantinos Loizou,Essam Ghadafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究测量了4,665家组织在HTTPS与SMTP STARTTLS端点上的可观测后量子密码（PQC）部署情况。结果表明，PQC公开部署主要由少数基础设施提供商驱动，且Web与邮件系统之间存在显著不均衡，因此外部可见的PQC支持不能直接等同于组织整体迁移就绪度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着PQC成为长期网络信任的重要组成部分，公开可测的PQC支持常被用作组织迁移进度的代理指标。然而，面向互联网的端点配置可能仅反映云服务、CDN或托管服务商的默认部署，而非组织自身完成了全面迁移。研究旨在量化这种可观测部署，并检验协议、行业和基础设施提供商对部署差异的影响。

Method: 研究对十个行业中的4,665家组织进行测量，扫描其HTTPS及SMTP STARTTLS可达端点，检测是否支持预先评估的后量子密钥交换组。同时，研究尽可能将端点归因至底层基础设施提供商，并采用统计分析比较不同协议、行业和提供商的部署模式；对于同时具有两类可达服务的组织，使用匹配分析评估HTTPS与SMTP支持差异。

Result: 在可达端点中，44.0%的HTTPS服务支持至少一种被评估的PQC密钥交换组，而SMTP服务的比例仅为6.4%。对同时可通过两种协议访问的组织，HTTPS支持PQC显著更常见，匹配优势比为16.89。虽然行业间存在部署差异，但基础设施提供商身份对PQC支持的预测能力明显强于组织所属行业，且部署高度集中于少数提供商。仅144家组织在Web和电子邮件基础设施中同时支持PQC，显示迁移状态碎片化且不均衡。此外，所有测量端点均未观察到后量子证书签名。

Conclusion: 可观测PQC部署目前主要体现基础设施提供商的部署决策，而非组织端到端的后量子迁移完成度。将公网HTTPS或SMTP端点的PQC支持作为组织迁移准备度、合规状态或截止日期进展的唯一指标，可能导致高估。后续评估应区分协议层支持、证书签名迁移、邮件与Web等业务系统覆盖范围，以及组织自有系统与第三方基础设施所贡献的部署。

Abstract: Post-quantum cryptography (PQC) is becoming an important component of long-term trust in Internet-facing infrastructure. Publicly observable PQC support provides evidence of externally visible deployment, but does not necessarily reflect the overall progress of an organisation's post-quantum migration. This distinction matters when observable deployment is used as an indicator of organisational readiness or progress towards migration deadlines.
  We present a measurement study of observable PQC deployment across 4,665 UK organisations spanning ten sectors. We measure post-quantum key-exchange support across HTTPS and SMTP STARTTLS endpoints, attribute reachable endpoints to their underlying infrastructure providers where possible, and statistically examine protocol-, sector-, and provider-level deployment patterns. Among reachable endpoints, 44.0\% of HTTPS services supported at least one evaluated PQC key-exchange group, compared with 6.4\% of SMTP services. Among organisations reachable over both protocols, HTTPS support was significantly more common than SMTP support (matched odds ratio 16.89). Although deployment varied across sectors, infrastructure provider identity was substantially more predictive than organisational sector, and observable deployment was highly concentrated among a small number of providers. Only 144 organisations supported PQC across both web and email infrastructure, highlighting an uneven and fragmented migration landscape. No post-quantum certificate signatures were observed across the measured endpoints. These findings show that observable PQC deployment is currently shaped predominantly by infrastructure-provider deployment decisions and should not be interpreted as a complete measure of organisational migration readiness.

</details>


### [254] [TurboRetry: Mitigating Large-Scale QUIC Handshake Floods with Off-the-Shelf DPU Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02264)
*Jiahao Wu,Heng Pan,Kai Lv,Zhenyu Li,Yanbiao Li,Gaogang Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出TurboRetry，将QUIC Retry防御机制拆分并卸载至DPU，以缓解大规模握手洪泛导致的主机CPU瓶颈，实验显示吞吐量较纯主机实现提升10—20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUIC握手洪泛会诱使服务器重复执行高开销密码学操作，耗尽CPU资源。虽然QUIC的Retry机制可过滤部分异常请求，但其本身在高并发场景下仍具有显著计算开销，可能成为性能瓶颈。

Method: TurboRetry采用主机与DPU协同的分离式架构，将Retry相关任务划分为适合DPU处理与必须由主机处理的两类。系统通过扩展Retry Token格式维持QUIC协议语义，并设计低协调开销的协作机制；同时将连接授权任务卸载到路径中的DPA，以进一步提升处理效率与防护能力。

Result: 评估结果表明，TurboRetry相较于传统主机侧Retry实现具有明显性能优势，吞吐量提升约10—20倍，说明DPU卸载能够有效降低主机在大规模QUIC握手请求下的计算负担。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将QUIC Retry机制部分卸载到DPU是一种有效的系统设计路径。其关键贡献在于任务拆分、扩展Token设计及主机-DPU协作方案，可在保持QUIC语义的同时显著提升大规模握手防护场景下的吞吐性能。

Abstract: The modern transport protocol QUIC is designed to enhance network performance and security, but it remains vulnerable to handshake flooding attacks. Such attacks exhaust CPU resources by forcing the server to perform expensive cryptographic operations via a large number of handshaking requests. QUIC provides a built-in defense mechanism, the Retry mechanism, to mitigate these attacks. However, our experiments reveal that it can still become a performance bottleneck under large-scale QUIC handshake floods due to substantial computational overhead. In this paper, we design and implement TurboRetry, a split design, that offloads the Retry mechanism onto DPUs to efficiently mitigate QUIC handshake floods. TurboRetry partitions the tasks of the Retry into two categories, and then assigns them to the DPUs and the host, respectively. To preserve QUIC semantics and reduce the coordination overhead, TurboRetry designs an extended Retry token format and an efficient cooperation scheme. In addition, TurboRetry offloads the connection authorization task to the on-path DPA to further improve both performance and security. Our evaluation shows that TurboRetry outperforms the host-side implementation by a wide margin, improving throughput by 10-20$\times$.

</details>


### [255] [A Multi-Objective AutoML-based Efficient Intrusion Detection System for EV Charging Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02274)
*Li Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向电动汽车充电系统与物联网安全的多目标自动机器学习入侵检测框架，在保持较高检测准确率的同时，降低推理延迟和模型体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车充电系统接入大量物联网设备后，攻击面扩大，需通过入侵检测系统保障安全。现有基于机器学习的方案通常依赖人工设计模型，且主要关注检测性能，未充分兼顾实际部署所需的低延迟与小模型规模。

Method: 方法采用轻量化训练策略，并以LightGBM累计特征重要性为依据进行自动特征选择，获得紧凑特征子集。随后使用NSGA-III联合优化特征选择阈值及LightGBM关键超参数，以加权F1分数最大化、99百分位推理延迟比最小化和模型大小比最小化为三个优化目标。

Result: 在CICEVSE2024和CICIDS2017数据集上的实验表明，该方法相较对比方法能够取得有竞争力的加权F1分数，并实现更低的P99推理延迟和更小的模型体积。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，多目标自动机器学习能够有效平衡检测精度、尾部推理时延和模型资源开销，适合资源与实时性受限的电动汽车充电系统及物联网入侵检测部署场景。

Abstract: Electric Vehicle Charging Systems (EVCSs) are increasingly connected with Internet of Things (IoT) devices, which improves charging intelligence but also expands their exposure to cyber-attacks. Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs) are essential for securing EV charging networks; however, conventional Machine Learning (ML)-based IDSs often rely on manual model design and mainly optimize detection performance without fully considering inference latency and model size. In this paper, a Multi-Objective Automated ML (MOO-AutoML)-based efficient IDS is proposed for EVCS security. The proposed framework uses a lightweight training strategy and a LightGBM-based automated feature selection method to select compact feature subsets based on accumulated feature importance. Then, Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm III (NSGA-III) jointly optimizes the feature selection threshold and key LightGBM hyperparameters under three objectives: maximizing weighted F1-score, minimizing 99th percentile inference latency ratio, and minimizing model size ratio. Experiments on CICEVSE2024 and CICIDS2017 show that the proposed MOO-AutoML IDS achieves competitive weighted F1-scores, lower P99 inference latency, and smaller model sizes than the compared methods. Overall, the results indicate that the proposed method can support accurate and efficient intrusion detection for EVCS and IoT security under practical deployment constraints.

</details>


### [256] [TrainShield: Targeted Awareness for Cybersecurity Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02296)
*Giovanni Pizzenti,Alberto Verna,Nikhil Jha,Giuseppe Tipaldo,Stefano Traverso,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出TrainShield，一种将网络安全培训嵌入用户日常工作流的情境化、事件驱动交互范式：系统在检测到钓鱼、数据泄露等风险时，立即提供与当前任务和用户能力匹配的微学习内容与反馈。初步研究显示，该方法被认为比传统冗长、异步培训更有用且更受偏好，但生成内容与用户期望的一致性仍是挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络安全意识培训通常脱离真实使用场景，难以在用户实际面临风险时影响其决策。随着威胁更多利用人的行为而非单纯技术漏洞，研究旨在缩小“培训发生时刻”与“风险发生时刻”之间的差距，将安全事件转化为即时学习机会，并促进用户从自动化反应转向反思性决策。

Method: TrainShield整合实时风险检测能力，例如钓鱼识别与数据泄露防护；当检测到风险事件时，系统通过事件触发的超媒体覆盖层，在浏览工作流中呈现上下文相关的学习节点。其设计模型将检测事件映射为自适应培训实例，综合用户建模、上下文提取和基于大语言模型的内容生成，为不同知识水平和当前任务情境的用户提供个性化微学习材料及结构化反馈。

Result: 初步研究表明，参与者普遍认为该系统有助于提升风险意识，并相较于篇幅较长、非同步的传统培训形式更受欢迎。研究同时发现，LLM生成的培训内容未必总能准确满足用户期望，说明个性化生成内容的相关性、表达方式和质量控制仍需进一步优化。

Conclusion: 将网络安全教育嵌入日常交互、由风险事件即时触发，是面向行为的网络安全培训的可行且有前景的方向。该研究的核心贡献是提出并初步验证了“检测—情境理解—自适应干预—反馈”的训练框架；后续工作应重点评估其对实际安全行为和长期学习效果的影响，并改善生成内容与用户需求之间的对齐。

Abstract: In recent years, cybersecurity threats have increasingly exploited human behaviour rather than purely technical vulnerabilities, exposing the limits of traditional awareness programmes delivered outside real-world contexts. To bridge this gap, we introduce TrainShield, an interaction paradigm for contextual cybersecurity training that embeds adaptive learning interventions directly within user workflows. The system integrates real-time risk detection (e.g., phishing and data loss prevention) with event-triggered hypermedia overlays that dynamically connect users to context-specific learning nodes embedded within their browsing workflow to deliver personalised micro-learning content and structured feedback tailored to the user's knowledge level and current context. This approach operationalises behavioural theories by transforming security incidents into immediate learning opportunities, shifting users from automatic to reflective decision-making at critical moments. We further formalise a design model that maps detected events to adaptive training instances, combining user modelling, context extraction, and large language model (LLM)-based content generation.
  A preliminary study indicates that the approach is perceived as useful in increasing risk awareness and is preferred over lengthy and asynchronous traditional training formats, while also highlighting challenges in aligning generated content with user expectations. Overall, the results suggest that embedding contextual, event-driven training within everyday interactions is a promising direction for behaviour-oriented cybersecurity education.

</details>


### [257] [Lost in Permissions: Exploring the Microsoft 365 App Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02336)
*Vincenzo Longo,Alberto Verna,Nikhil Jha,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文首次对 Microsoft 365 第三方应用生态进行隐私与安全测量，发现应用权限披露严重不一致，且大量应用请求与其声明功能不匹配的高权限、租户级访问范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M365 第三方应用可通过 OAuth 权限访问企业邮件、文件、日历、聊天记录和用户目录等敏感资源，但该生态此前缺乏系统性的安全与隐私研究。论文旨在评估应用权限透明度，并识别违反最小权限原则的潜在高风险应用。

Method: 研究结合公开应用市场 API 与自动化租户侧部署，爬取超过 8,000 个应用的信息。随后使用神经主题模型按应用功能描述进行聚类，并在每个主题内基于同类应用的权限画像实施无监督异常检测。研究进一步使用大语言模型辅助分析最异常的案例，并通过盲法人工检查验证异常权限与风险之间的关系。

Result: 超过 8,000 个应用中，只有 1,069 个同时公开了应用描述和权限集合，表明不同官方分发渠道的透明度存在显著差异。异常检测显示，许多应用请求了超出声明功能所需的广泛权限，尤其是目录级读写等租户范围权限。人工检查与辅助分析表明，权限画像越偏离同类应用，其请求权限的潜在风险通常越高。

Conclusion: M365 第三方应用生态存在系统性信息不透明和权限治理不成熟的问题，过度授权较为普遍，并会扩大企业攻击面。该研究提出的主题感知异常检测流程可帮助租户管理员识别可疑应用，以及导致其异常的关键权限，从而支持更有效的权限审计与风险处置。

Abstract: The Microsoft 365 (M365) ecosystem hosts thousands of third-party applications that integrate with enterprise tenants via fine-grained OAuth permissions, potentially granting access to sensitive organisational resources such as emails, files, calendars, chats, and user directories. Despite the security implications of these permission grants, the M365 ecosystem has not been systematically studied.
  We present the first privacy- and security-oriented measurement of M365 third-party applications. By combining public marketplace APIs with automated tenant-side deployment, we crawl over 8,000 applications. We find that only 1,069 of them expose both descriptions and permission sets, with significant inconsistencies in transparency across official distribution channels.
  Next, we leverage a topic-aware anomaly detection framework to assess whether requested permissions align with declared functionality. We cluster applications via Neural Topic Modelling and apply unsupervised anomaly detection within each topic to identify deviations from peer permission profiles. LLM-assisted analysis of the most anomalous cases and blind manual inspection reveal a correlation between anomalous permission profiles and the risk associated with the requested permissions. We find that many applications request overly broad tenant-wide scopes (e.g., directory-wide read/write access), violating least-privilege principles and increasing the organisational attack surface. Our pipeline provides tenant administrators with actionable insights by identifying anomalous applications and the permissions that most contribute to their anomalousness.
  Overall, our findings expose systemic opacity and structural immaturity in the M365 app ecosystem, where permission disclosure is inconsistent and over-privileged access is common.

</details>


### [258] [Self-Supervised Representations for Binary Program Clustering: From Empirical Study to Retrieval-Augmented Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02348)
*Martin Mocko,Daniela Chudá*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了自监督学习与表格表示学习在二进制程序聚类中的效果，并提出检索增强方法VIME-R，在Ember和Bodmas数据集上取得最佳聚类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制程序聚类对发现未知样本、追踪家族演化和自动化分析具有重要价值，但现有研究较少系统探索自监督学习和表格表示学习在“无论标签均需聚类”的二进制程序场景中的适用性。

Method: 研究分为两阶段：第一阶段将BYOL、SimSiam、Barlow Twins和VICReg等视觉自监督方法适配到表格特征，并通过监督式样本对生成估计性能上限；第二阶段比较纯无监督表格表示学习方法与PCA、Autoencoder、UMAP等基线。基于VIME的结果，作者提出VIME-R：以检索相似样本构造增强对，替代VIME中随机边缘分布扰动生成训练对的方式。

Result: 在性能上限实验中，BYOL和SimSiam的聚类性能接近全监督模型，而Barlow Twins和VICReg明显较弱。在纯无监督比较中，VIME优于PCA、Autoencoder和UMAP等强基线，成为最佳方法。VIME-R进一步超过VIME，在两个数据集上的Homogeneity提高2.7%至5.8%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，表格表示学习能够有效提升二进制程序聚类，其中VIME具有较强无监督性能；利用检索机制构造信息量更高的增强样本对可进一步改善表示质量。检索增强表格表示学习因此是提升自动化恶意软件分析能力的有前景方向。

Abstract: Malware clustering is a critical task in cybersecurity that helps discover threats and analyze evolving malware families. While self-supervised learning (SSL) and tabular representation learning (TRL) have achieved breakthroughs in other domains, their application to binary program clustering (the task of clustering all incoming samples regardless of label) remains largely unexplored. This study presents the first systematic investigation of SSL and TRL methods for binary program clustering, conducted in two phases on the public Ember and Bodmas datasets. In Phase 1, we establish a performance ceiling by adapting prominent vision-based SSL models (BYOL, SimSiam, Barlow Twins, VICReg) for tabular data with supervised pair generation, finding that BYOL and SimSiam achieve performance comparable to fully supervised models, while Barlow Twins and VICReg significantly underperform. In Phase 2, we evaluate purely unsupervised TRL methods against strong baselines (PCA, Autoencoder, UMAP), demonstrating that VIME establishes a new state of the art for binary program clustering. Informed by these findings, we propose VIME-R, a retrieval-augmented extension of VIME that replaces random marginal-distribution corruption with retrieval-based augmentation to generate more informative training pairs. VIME-R further improves upon VIME, achieving 2.7\%-5.8\% higher Homogeneity on both datasets. Our results highlight retrieval-augmented tabular representation learning as a promising direction for enhancing automated malware analysis. Code will be made available.

</details>


### [259] [Antares: Foundation Models for Agentic Vulnerability Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02407)
*Supriti Vijay,Aman Priyanshu,Didier Chapoteau,Arthur Goldblatt,Jianliang He,Kimia Majd,Fraser Burch,Baturay Saglam,Takahiro Matsumoto,Zhuoran Yang,Amin Karbasi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Antares，一组面向智能体式软件漏洞定位的紧凑语言模型，参数规模为350M、1B和3B。其目标是在大型代码仓中通过迭代推理和仓库探索定位脆弱实现，并强调以较低部署成本获得接近大型闭源模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统漏洞定位需要理解跨文件代码关系、追踪调用链并在大型仓库中反复探索，计算与人工成本较高。现有高性能模型通常参数规模大、推理昂贵，难以支持本地、快速和低成本的安全分析，因此作者尝试构建小型但具备仓库级推理能力的专用模型。

Method: Antares基于IBM Granite基础模型，采用两阶段训练流程：第一阶段使用网络安全推理与代码仓探索数据进行监督微调，使模型学习漏洞分析和导航行为；第二阶段在包含漏洞的代码仓上使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习，直接优化漏洞定位任务的可验证结果。模型覆盖350M、1B和3B三个规模，面向智能体式、多步仓库探索场景。

Result: 摘要声称，在广泛评测中，Antares-3B的表现接近GPT-5.5，并超过参数量大200倍以上的开放权重模型。效率方面，作者报告可在单张H100 GPU上约15分钟完成500个任务的完整评测，折合单任务平均评测时间不足2秒、成本低于0.002美元。该结果表明，针对任务设计的数据与强化学习目标可能比单纯扩大模型参数更有效。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是证明小型专用模型可通过“安全推理监督微调+可验证奖励强化学习”获得较强的仓库级漏洞定位能力，并具备本地部署优势。摘要中的性能结论仍需结合完整论文核查评测基准、任务定义、奖励构造、比较模型配置、重复实验及GPT-5.5访问条件，以判断其可复现性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Vulnerability localization is a fundamental step in software security, requiring models to reason over large codebases and iteratively identify vulnerable implementations. We present Antares, a family of compact language models (350M, 1B, and 3B parameters) for agentic vulnerability localization. Based on IBM Granite base models, Antares is trained through a two-stage pipeline that combines supervised fine-tuning on cybersecurity reasoning and repository exploration data with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards over vulnerable repositories. Across extensive evaluations, Antares-3B approaches GPT-5.5 while outperforming open-weight models over 200x larger in size. The Antares family further enables fast, low-cost local inference, completing a full 500-task evaluation sweep in approximately 15 minutes on a single H100 GPU, corresponding to an amortized evaluation time of under 2 seconds and less than $0.002 per task.

</details>


### [260] [Agentic Incident Response through Digital Twin-Enhanced Multiscale Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02422)
*Yiran Gao,Tao Li,Kim Hammar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向自动化事件响应的分层智能体框架，将决策理论规划与大语言模型生成的可执行响应命令结合，并借助数字孪生实现仿真规划和仿真执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事件响应依赖人工依据预定义剧本进行决策，效率低且人力成本高。现有基于控制、优化和强化学习的方法虽具理论性能保障，但通常依赖抽象模型，难以直接部署于真实运维环境；纯LLM智能体则因反复调用模型进行规划而容易产生幻觉，并受限于较短的有效规划范围。

Method: 方法采用战术层与操作层分离的两级架构：战术层使用rollout规划器，在数字孪生环境中通过模拟计算资源分配与高层响应策略；操作层由轻量级LLM智能体将高层策略转换为具体、可执行的响应命令。数字孪生同时支持战术规划阶段的仿真，以及操作执行阶段的仿真/模拟验证。

Result: 在三个攻击场景中，该方法相较于先进LLM基线，平均将恢复执行时间缩短15.1%，并将恢复率提高33.6%。结果表明，显式决策规划能够降低LLM在长程响应规划中的不可靠性，同时保留其将策略转化为运维命令的能力。

Conclusion: 该研究的核心贡献是以决策理论规划负责高层策略搜索，以LLM负责低层命令生成，从而构建更可靠、可执行的自动化事件响应系统。数字孪生是连接策略规划和实际执行的重要基础设施；不过，摘要未说明测试环境与真实生产系统之间的迁移效果、LLM命令安全约束，以及面对未见场景时的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Incident response is currently managed by security operators using predefined playbooks, resulting in slow, labor-intensive security decision-making processes. Consequently, there is a growing need for automated incident response planning. Decision-theoretic approaches based on control, optimization, and reinforcement learning have been proposed to automate such planning tasks with well-grounded approaches, yet most of which, while guaranteeing strong performance, are limited to abstract models and cannot be directly applied to operational systems. A promising approach to mitigate this limitation is to use the security knowledge embedded in large language models (LLMs) to develop agentic response systems. However, current agentic approaches rely on repeated invocations of the LLM to generate a response plan, which is unreliable and limits the planning horizon due to hallucination. In this paper, we develop a principled LLM-based planning method by combining decision-theoretic planning with LLM-generated response commands. The proposed agentic incident response approach uses a rollout planner to compute a high-level response strategy that allocates security resources (the tactical scale), which is then translated into executable commands by a lightweight LLM agent (the operational scale). Within this architecture, we use a digital twin that supports tactical planning through simulation and operational execution through emulation. Across three attack scenarios, our agentic approach reduces recovery execution time by 15.1\% on average and increases the recovery rate by 33.6\% over frontier LLM baselines.

</details>
